/*
WorldLedgerV0.15.2 — Core MVP Foundation Script / Code Pass 1 CanonValidator Shell Lock + Support Layer Schema Completion
Made by: JustSomeBobby

WorldLedger is a freeform RPG truth engine for AI Dungeon. It is built to preserve
canon, consequences, state, and memory without turning AI Dungeon into a rigid command game.

Credit and direct inspiration:
- RPGUI by @RonenNess — state-first RPG/HUD presentation discipline and clean helper separation, reinterpreted for AI Dungeon as text-first state and command readouts. No HTML UI code is used.
- TAS / True Auto Stats by @Yi1i1i — freeform action reading, emergent growth ambition, and structured stats / inventory / abilities, rebuilt as proposal -> validation -> reducer ownership.
- Auto-Cards by @LewdLeah — object permanence, entity detection, Story Card drafting, and memory maintenance ideas, rebuilt so cards mirror canon instead of creating it.
- Inner Self by @LewdLeah — segmented long-term NPC memory and major-character persistence, rebuilt as selective reducer-owned memory rather than universal NPC simulation.

Not direct foundations for this build: LoLa, Hashtag-DnD, AID-dice-rolling, and Gnurro AIDscripts. They may remain general research references, but they are not credited here as active functional inspirations unless a future build directly borrows from them.

Design doctrine:
Input may intend. Context may remind. Output may propose. ParserHits are observations.
EventProposals are structured claims. CanonValidator decides eligibility. Reducers commit.
OperationLedger dedupes. DirtyQueue marks refresh. Context reads. Cards mirror. ReleaseCheck tells the truth.

Code Pass 1 scope:
This is the foundation build with the Code Pass 1 Section 15 CanonValidator Shell lock applied against the active control map, plus a V0.15.2 support-layer schema completion pass for the support layers that were too shallow in the first V0.15 pass. It keeps old scripts and previous WorldLedger/AIDRPG versions as evidence and salvage sources, not as paste-in architecture, and makes CanonValidator the strict truth gate between EventProposals and reducer commits. It initializes, inspects, audits, validates, and commits only limited MVP-safe events while enforcing safe state creation, structural repair, preservation of valid truth, quarantine of unsafe legacy data, richer dirty/context/card/migration schemas, and no hook-wrapper leakage inside the Library bundle.

Project naming bridge: AIDRPG is the broader RPG-engine project goal. WorldLedger is the script-engine implementation name for the AIDRPG Core MVP / Code Pass 1 rebuild.
Deferred systems remain disabled: generated Story Cards, full economy, crafting, investigation, reputation/faction
automation, domain/labor, absorption/evolution, advanced combat, warfare, multi-protagonist, and manual mutation commands.
*/

    { source: 'RPGUI', creator: '@RonenNess', usefulRule: 'state-first RPG presentation and clean helper separation', absorbedInto: 'CommandRenderer, ContextBuilderLite, Player/Inventory readouts', status: 'active_in_foundation', noPaste: true },
    { source: 'TAS / True Auto Stats', creator: '@Yi1i1i', usefulRule: 'freeform action reading, stats/inventory ambition, duplicate-sensitive RPG state', absorbedInto: 'EventScannerLite, ProposalBuilder, CanonValidator, Body/Inventory/Ability/Progression owners', status: 'active_as_rules_not_old_script', noPaste: true },

    { source: 'AIDRPG v1.8.x old patch pile', creator: 'JustSomeBobby + prior build iterations', usefulRule: 'large feature intent and known failure cases including economy/crafting/domain/investigation/scale', absorbedInto: 'Deferred gates, owner map, releasecheck requirements, future reducer specs', status: 'evidence_only_not_pasted', noPaste: true },

    CommandOutputFormatLock: 'Owns command response headers, status labels, non-canon warnings, disabled-feature honesty, and hook-wrapper leakage prevention.',

    AuditLogger: 'Owns capped diagnostics and black-box recorder entries only.',

    LegacySalvageLedger: 'Records direct inspiration, older-version salvage rules, and deferred evidence; never mutates gameplay state.',

    ReducerRegistry: 'Routes accepted validations to exactly one owner reducer.',
    Reducers: 'Only owner reducers may commit gameplay truth after validation and ledger checks.',
    DirtyQueue: 'Marks mirror/context targets only; never creates canon.',
    ContextBuilderLite: 'Reads state and builds bounded context packets only.',
    CardApiAdapter: 'Wraps story card API access safely; live writes are disabled by default.',
    CardSyncLite: 'Dry-run protected mirror shell only; generated cards are disabled.',
    MigrationManager: 'Repairs/quarantines safely; never imports old prose as canon.',
    CleanupManagerLite: 'Caps logs/queues only; never deletes active truth.',
    Hooks: 'Orchestrates Input, Context, Output entry points without bypassing the pipeline.'
  });
  WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS = Object.freeze([
    'help','version','layout','schema','schemaaudit','owners','sheet','status','inventory','abilities','quests','actors','location','time',
    'debuglast','parser','parsercheck','proposal','proposalcheck','validator','validatorcheck','resolver','resolvercheck','controlmap','salvage','borrowledger','audit','auditlog','cardaudit','contextaudit','stateaudit','statemanager','integrity','repairlog','runtime','hookaudit','runtimecheck','failsoft','ledger','ledgercheck','commandrouter','commandcheck','dirty','migration','quarantine','startupaudit','commandformat','commands','releasecheck'
  ]);
  WL.MVP_DISABLED_SYSTEMS = Object.freeze([
    'generated cards','live protected card writes','manual mutation commands','full economy','crafting',
    'investigation','reputation/factions','domain/labor','absorption/evolution','advanced combat','warfare','multi-protagonist'
  ]);
  WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS = Object.freeze([
    'WorldLedger Player','WorldLedger Inventory','WorldLedger Abilities','WorldLedger Quests',
    'WorldLedger Actors Places','WorldLedger Time','WorldLedger Audit','WorldLedger Settings'
  ]);

  WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS = Object.freeze([
    'meta','features','runtime','startup','player','body','progression','inventory','abilities','quests',
    'actors','places','time','cards','operationLedger','audits','migration','quarantine','dirty','cleanup'
  ]);
  WL.SUPPORT_ROOT_BUCKETS = Object.freeze(['layout','commandFormat','pending']);
  WL.ROOT_BUCKETS_ALL = Object.freeze(WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS.concat(WL.SUPPORT_ROOT_BUCKETS));
  WL.SCHEMA_CAPS = Object.freeze({
    hookTrace: 30, runtimeWarnings: 25, runtimeErrors: 20, diagnostics: 40, parser: 40, proposals: 40,
    validations: 40, reducers: 40, cards: 40, context: 40, migration: 40, quarantine: 24, dirtyTargets: 40, recentLedger: 180, dryRunWrites: 20
  });
  WL.SCHEMA_OWNER_MAP = Object.freeze({
    meta: 'StateManager', features: 'StateManager / ReleaseCheckLite', runtime: 'Runtime', startup: 'StateManager / StartupImporterFuture',
    player: 'StateManager / PlayerReducerFuture', body: 'BodyReducerLite', progression: 'ProgressionReducerLite', inventory: 'InventoryReducerLite',
    abilities: 'AbilityReducerLite', quests: 'QuestReducerLite', actors: 'ActorReducerLite', places: 'PlaceReducerLite', time: 'TimeReducerLite',
    cards: 'CardSyncLite', operationLedger: 'OperationLedger', audits: 'AuditLogger', migration: 'MigrationManager / StateManager',
    quarantine: 'MigrationManager / StateManager', dirty: 'DirtyQueue', cleanup: 'CleanupManagerLite', layout: 'LayoutLock', commandFormat: 'CommandOutputFormatLock', pending: 'Runtime / PipelineDiagnostics'
  });
  WL.MUTATION_BOUNDARIES = Object.freeze({
    parser: 'ParserHits and EventProposals only; parser may not mutate canon buckets.',
    validator: 'May accept/reject/pending/soft_fail/hard_deny proposals; may not write gameplay state.',
    reducer: 'Only owner reducers commit accepted events to owned buckets.',
    context: 'Read-only; may render compact truth but cannot write canon.',
    cards: 'CardSync mirrors stable state only; Story Card text never becomes canon.',
    commands: 'Inspect-only in Code Pass 1; command output is non-canon and suppressed from parsing.',
    cleanup: 'May cap logs/queues; may not invent or delete active gameplay truth.'
  });

  // ============================================================
  // 02. Feature Flags and Defaults
  // ============================================================
  WL.FeatureDefaults = Object.freeze({
    commandsEnabled: true,
    releaseCheckEnabled: true,
    auditEnabled: true,
    operationLedgerEnabled: true,
    parserShellEnabled: true,
    maxInputParserHits: 12,
    maxOutputParserHits: 20,
    maxParserWindows: 16,
    proposalShellEnabled: true,

    canonValidatorLockEnabled: true,

    commandFormatLockEnabled: true,

    // Deferred / blocked in Code Pass 1.
    generatedCardsEnabled: false,
    protectedCardWritesEnabled: false,
    manualMutationCommandsEnabled: false,
    economyEnabled: false,
    craftingEnabled: false,
    investigationEnabled: false,
    reputationEnabled: false,
    domainEnabled: false,
    absorptionEnabled: false,
    advancedCombatEnabled: false,
    warfareEnabled: false,
    multiProtagonistEnabled: false,
    partyAdvancedEnabled: false,

    cardSyncDryRun: true,
    cardSyncSafeMode: true,
    generatedCardCreatesPerTurn: 0,
    generatedCardUpdatesPerTurn: 0,
    contextBudget: 1800,
    debugMode: false
  });

  // ============================================================
  // 03. Utility Helpers
  // ============================================================
  WL.U = {
    nowTurn(root) {
      return Number(root && root.runtime && root.runtime.currentTurn || 0);
    },
    hasGlobalState() {
      return typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' && typeof globalThis.state === 'object' && globalThis.state !== null;
    },
    ensureGlobalState() {
      if (typeof globalThis.state !== 'object' || globalThis.state === null) globalThis.state = {};
      return globalThis.state;
    },
    norm(value) {
      return String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/[\u2018\u2019]/g, "'")
        .replace(/[\u201C\u201D]/g, '"')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
    },
    lower(value) { return this.norm(value).toLowerCase(); },
    title(value) {
      const raw = this.norm(value).replace(/^the\s+/i, '');
      if (!raw) return '';
      const small = { a: 1, an: 1, and: 1, at: 1, by: 1, for: 1, from: 1, in: 1, of: 1, on: 1, or: 1, the: 1, to: 1, with: 1 };
      return raw.split(/\s+/).map((w, i) => {
        const clean = w.toLowerCase();
        if (i > 0 && small[clean]) return clean;
        return clean.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + clean.slice(1);
      }).join(' ');
    },
    slug(value) {
      return this.lower(value).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '').slice(0, 80) || 'unknown';
    },
    clamp(n, min, max) {
      n = Number(n);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) n = min;
      return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
    },
    hash(value) {
      const s = String(value == null ? '' : value);
      let h = 2166136261;
      for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
        h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
        h += (h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24);
      }
      return ('00000000' + (h >>> 0).toString(16)).slice(-8);
    },
    capArray(arr, max) {
      if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return [];
      while (arr.length > max) arr.shift();
      return arr;
    },
    uniquePush(arr, value, max) {
      if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return false;
      const v = this.norm(value);
      if (!v) return false;
      const k = this.slug(v);
      for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) if (this.slug(arr[i]) === k) return false;
      arr.push(v);
      this.capArray(arr, max || 20);
      return true;
    },
    ensureObj(parent, key, factory) {
      if (!parent || typeof parent !== 'object') return factory ? factory() : {};
      if (!parent[key] || typeof parent[key] !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parent[key])) parent[key] = factory ? factory() : {};
      return parent[key];
    },
    ensureArray(parent, key) {
      if (!parent || typeof parent !== 'object') return [];
      if (!Array.isArray(parent[key])) parent[key] = [];
      return parent[key];
    },
    safeJson(value) {
      try { return JSON.stringify(value); } catch (e) { return '[unserializable]'; }
    },
    unquoted(text) {
      return String(text || '').replace(/["“][\s\S]*?["”]/g, ' ').replace(/'[^']{1,240}'/g, ' ');
    },
    sentenceSplit(text) {
      const t = this.norm(text);
      if (!t) return [];
      return t.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+|\n+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 16);
    },
    startsCommand(text) {
      return /^\s*\/\w+/.test(String(text || ''));
    },
    commandName(text) {
      const m = String(text || '').match(/^\s*\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
      return m ? m[1].toLowerCase() : '';
    },
    commandArgs(text) {
      return String(text || '').replace(/^\s*\/\S+\s*/, '').trim();
    },
    getInfoActionCount() {
      try {
        if (typeof info === 'object' && info && typeof info.actionCount === 'number') return info.actionCount;
      } catch (e) {}
      return null;
    },
    getStoryCards() {
      try {
        if (Array.isArray(globalThis.storyCards)) return globalThis.storyCards;
        if (globalThis.state && Array.isArray(globalThis.state.storyCards)) return globalThis.state.storyCards;
      } catch (e) {}
      return [];
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 03B. Layout Lock and Module Boundary Inspector
  // ============================================================
  WL.LayoutLock = {
    sectionOrder() { return WL.SECTION_ORDER.slice(); },
    contracts() { return Object.assign({}, WL.MODULE_CONTRACTS); },
    moduleNames() {
      return ['StateManager','Runtime','CommandRouter','CommandOutputFormatLock','CommandRenderer','AuditLogger','OperationLedger','ReleaseCheckLite','Sanitizers','Resolver','EventScannerLite','ProposalBuilder','LegacySalvageLedger','CanonValidator','ReducerRegistry','Reducers','DirtyQueue','ContextBuilderLite','CardApiAdapter','CardSyncLite','MigrationManager','CleanupManagerLite'];
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const out = [];
      const modNames = this.moduleNames();
      out.push({ status: 'PASS', label: 'layout version locked: ' + WL.LAYOUT_VERSION });
      out.push(WL.SECTION_ORDER.length === 25 ? { status: 'PASS', label: '25-section Code Pass 1 assembly order registered' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'section order count mismatch' });
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('layout') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: '/layout command registered as inspect-only' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: '/layout command missing' });
      out.push(root && root.layout && root.layout.locked === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'state.layout lock bucket present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'state.layout lock bucket missing' });
      for (let i = 0; i < modNames.length; i++) {
        const name = modNames[i];
        out.push(WL[name] ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'module present: ' + name } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'module missing: ' + name });
      }
      const forbidden = ['tasis','patchData','cardSync','aidrpgOld'];
      for (let j = 0; j < forbidden.length; j++) {
        let present = false;
        try { present = !!(globalThis.state && globalThis.state[forbidden[j]]); } catch (e) {}
        out.push(!present ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'forbidden state root absent: state.' + forbidden[j] } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'forbidden state root present: state.' + forbidden[j] });
      }
      if (root && root.layout) {
        root.layout.lastAudit = out.map(r => ({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), status: r.status, label: r.label }));
        WL.U.capArray(root.layout.lastAudit, 80);
      }
      return out;
    },
    render(root) {
      const rows = this.inspect(root);
      const bad = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      const lines = ['Layout: ' + WL.LAYOUT_VERSION + (bad ? ' / FAILURES: ' + bad : ' / locked'), 'Section order:'];
      WL.SECTION_ORDER.forEach(sec => lines.push('- ' + sec));
      lines.push('Module check:');
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      lines.push('Deferred systems: ' + WL.MVP_DISABLED_SYSTEMS.join(', '));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 03C. State Schema Lock and Ownership Inspector
  // ============================================================
  WL.StateSchemaLock = {
    canonicalBuckets() { return WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS.slice(); },
    supportBuckets() { return WL.SUPPORT_ROOT_BUCKETS.slice(); },
    allBuckets() { return WL.ROOT_BUCKETS_ALL.slice(); },
    owner(bucket) { return WL.SCHEMA_OWNER_MAP[bucket] || 'unowned'; },
    ensureSchemaMetadata(root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return;
      if (!root.meta || typeof root.meta !== 'object') root.meta = {};
      const m = root.meta;
      if (!m.projectName) m.projectName = WL.PROJECT_NAME;
      if (!m.buildName) m.buildName = WL.BUILD_NAME;
      if (!m.buildStage) m.buildStage = WL.BUILD_STAGE;
      m.schemaVersion = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      m.schemaId = WL.SCHEMA_ID;
      m.schemaLockVersion = WL.SCHEMA_LOCK_VERSION;
      if (!m.packageAuthority) m.packageAuthority = 'AIDRPG Final Build Planning Package v1.4.1 / Code Pass 1';
      if (!m.createdBy) m.createdBy = 'WorldLedger script';
      if (typeof m.createdTurn !== 'number') m.createdTurn = 0;
      if (typeof m.lastUpdatedTurn !== 'number') m.lastUpdatedTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      if (!m.lastReleaseCheckResult) m.lastReleaseCheckResult = 'unknown';
      if (!Array.isArray(m.notes)) m.notes = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(m.schemaRepairs)) m.schemaRepairs = [];
    },
    ensurePlayerShape(root) {
      const p = root.player || (root.player = {});
      if (!p.id) p.id = 'player';
      if (!p.name) p.name = 'Unknown';
      if (p.raceSpecies == null) p.raceSpecies = p.species || p.race || 'unknown';
      if (p.species == null) p.species = p.raceSpecies || 'unknown';
      if (p.race == null) p.race = p.raceSpecies || 'unknown';
      if (p.age == null) p.age = 'unknown';
      if (p.appearance == null) p.appearance = 'unspecified';
      if (p.bodyType == null) p.bodyType = 'humanoid';
      if (p.buildIdentity == null) p.buildIdentity = 'balanced';
      if (p.specialization == null) p.specialization = 'none';
      ['titles','nicknames','notes','identityWarnings'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(p[k])) p[k] = []; });
      ['origin','temporaryAppearance','activeDisguise','activeIllusion'].forEach(k => { if (p[k] == null) p[k] = ''; });
    },
    ensureBodyShape(root) {
      const b = root.body || (root.body = {});
      // Preserve older flat resources while adding locked ResourceTrack objects.
      if (!b.hpTrack || typeof b.hpTrack !== 'object') b.hpTrack = { current: Number(b.hp == null ? 100 : b.hp), max: Number(b.maxHp == null ? 100 : b.maxHp) };
      if (!b.mpTrack || typeof b.mpTrack !== 'object') b.mpTrack = { current: Number(b.mp == null ? 50 : b.mp), max: Number(b.maxMp == null ? 50 : b.maxMp) };
      if (!b.epTrack || typeof b.epTrack !== 'object') b.epTrack = { current: Number(b.ep == null ? 100 : b.ep), max: Number(b.maxEp == null ? 100 : b.maxEp) };
      b.hpTrack.max = WL.U.clamp(b.hpTrack.max || b.maxHp || 100, 1, 999999);
      b.hpTrack.current = WL.U.clamp(b.hpTrack.current == null ? b.hpTrack.max : b.hpTrack.current, 0, b.hpTrack.max);
      b.mpTrack.max = WL.U.clamp(b.mpTrack.max == null ? (b.maxMp || 50) : b.mpTrack.max, 0, 999999);
      b.mpTrack.current = WL.U.clamp(b.mpTrack.current == null ? b.mpTrack.max : b.mpTrack.current, 0, b.mpTrack.max);
      b.epTrack.max = WL.U.clamp(b.epTrack.max == null ? (b.maxEp || 100) : b.epTrack.max, 0, 999999);
      b.epTrack.current = WL.U.clamp(b.epTrack.current == null ? b.epTrack.max : b.epTrack.current, 0, b.epTrack.max);
      b.hp = b.hpTrack.current; b.maxHp = b.hpTrack.max;
      b.mp = b.mpTrack.current; b.maxMp = b.mpTrack.max;
      b.ep = b.epTrack.current; b.maxEp = b.epTrack.max;
      if (!Array.isArray(b.injuries)) b.injuries = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(b.conditions)) b.conditions = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(b.statusNotes)) b.statusNotes = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(b.bodyTraits)) b.bodyTraits = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(b.bodyWarnings)) b.bodyWarnings = [];
      if (!b.fatigue) b.fatigue = 'normal';
      if (!b.deathBoundary) b.deathBoundary = 'alive';
    },
    ensureRuntimeShape(root) {
      const r = root.runtime || (root.runtime = {});
      if (!r.currentHook) r.currentHook = 'none';
      if (typeof r.currentTurn !== 'number') r.currentTurn = 0;
      if (typeof r.hookDepth !== 'number') r.hookDepth = 0;
      if (!Array.isArray(r.hookTrace)) r.hookTrace = [];
      ['lastInputHash','lastContextHash','lastOutputHash','lastAppliedOutputHash','lastCommandHash','lastCommandName','pendingInputIntentId'].forEach(k => { if (r[k] == null) r[k] = ''; });
      if (typeof r.commandActive !== 'boolean') r.commandActive = false;
      if (typeof r.commandSuppressed !== 'boolean') r.commandSuppressed = false;
      if (typeof r.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput !== 'boolean') r.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput = !!r.commandSuppressed;
      if (typeof r.lastCommandTurn !== 'number') r.lastCommandTurn = 0;
      if (typeof r.outputReplayDetected !== 'boolean') r.outputReplayDetected = false;
      if (!Array.isArray(r.runtimeWarnings)) r.runtimeWarnings = r.warnings || [];
      if (!Array.isArray(r.runtimeErrors)) r.runtimeErrors = r.errors || [];
      if (typeof r.failSoftCount !== 'number') r.failSoftCount = 0;
      if (!Array.isArray(r.warnings)) r.warnings = r.runtimeWarnings;
      if (!Array.isArray(r.errors)) r.errors = r.runtimeErrors;
    },
    ensureStartupShape(root) {
      const s = root.startup || (root.startup = {});
      if (!s.setupStatus) s.setupStatus = s.startupLocked ? 'locked' : 'not_started';
      if (typeof s.setupImported !== 'boolean') s.setupImported = !!s.identityImported;
      if (typeof s.gameplayStarted !== 'boolean') s.gameplayStarted = !!s.firstGameplayInputSeen;
      if (typeof s.setupLockedTurn !== 'number') s.setupLockedTurn = 0;
      if (!s.setupSourceHash) s.setupSourceHash = '';
      ['importedFields','rejectedFields','warnings','errors','startupOperationKeys'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(s[k])) s[k] = []; });
    },
    ensureLockedShapes(root) {
      this.ensureSchemaMetadata(root);
      this.ensureRuntimeShape(root);
      this.ensureStartupShape(root);
      this.ensurePlayerShape(root);
      this.ensureBodyShape(root);
      if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== 'object') root.cards = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.protectedTitles)) root.cards.protectedTitles = WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.slice();
      root.cards.protectedTitles = WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.slice();
      if (!root.operationLedger || typeof root.operationLedger !== 'object') root.operationLedger = {};
      ['permanentKeys','rewardKeys','cardKeys','sourceUseKeys'].forEach(k => { if (!root.operationLedger[k] || typeof root.operationLedger[k] !== 'object') root.operationLedger[k] = {}; });
      if (!Array.isArray(root.operationLedger.recentKeys)) root.operationLedger.recentKeys = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips)) root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips = [];
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return [{ status: 'FAIL', label: 'root missing' }];
      rows.push(root.schemaVersion === WL.SCHEMA_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'root schemaVersion locked: ' + WL.SCHEMA_VERSION } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'root schemaVersion mismatch' });
      rows.push(root.meta && root.meta.schemaId === WL.SCHEMA_ID ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'meta schemaId locked: ' + WL.SCHEMA_ID } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'meta schemaId missing/mismatch' });
      WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS.forEach(k => rows.push(root[k] && typeof root[k] === 'object' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'canonical bucket present: ' + k + ' / owner: ' + this.owner(k) } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'canonical bucket missing: ' + k }));
      WL.SUPPORT_ROOT_BUCKETS.forEach(k => rows.push(root[k] && typeof root[k] === 'object' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'support bucket present: ' + k + ' / owner: ' + this.owner(k) } : { status: 'WARN', label: 'support bucket missing: ' + k }));
      const b = root.body || {};
      rows.push(b.hp >= 0 && b.hp <= b.maxHp ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'HP invariant locked' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'HP invariant broken' });
      rows.push(b.mp >= 0 && b.mp <= b.maxMp ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'MP invariant locked' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'MP invariant broken' });
      rows.push(b.ep >= 0 && b.ep <= b.maxEp ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'EP invariant locked' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'EP invariant broken' });
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'generated cards disabled by schema lock' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'generated cards enabled too early' });
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.manualMutationCommandsEnabled === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'manual mutation commands disabled by schema lock' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'manual mutation commands enabled too early' });
      return rows;
    },
    render(root) {
      const rows = this.inspect(root);
      const fail = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      const lines = ['Schema Lock: ' + WL.SCHEMA_LOCK_VERSION + (fail ? ' / FAILURES: ' + fail : ' / locked'), 'Schema ID: ' + WL.SCHEMA_ID, 'Canonical root buckets: ' + WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS.join(', '), 'Support buckets: ' + WL.SUPPORT_ROOT_BUCKETS.join(', '), 'Ownership:'];
      Object.keys(WL.SCHEMA_OWNER_MAP).forEach(k => lines.push('- ' + k + ': ' + WL.SCHEMA_OWNER_MAP[k]));
      lines.push('Boundary rules:');
      Object.keys(WL.MUTATION_BOUNDARIES).forEach(k => lines.push('- ' + k + ': ' + WL.MUTATION_BOUNDARIES[k]));
      lines.push('Audit:');
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 04. State Schema Defaults
  // ============================================================
  WL.Schema = {
    features() { return Object.assign({}, WL.FeatureDefaults); },
    root() {
      return {

  // ============================================================
  // 07. AuditLogger
  // ============================================================
  WL.AuditLogger = {
    caps: Object.freeze({
      debug: 50, warnings: 50, parser: 40, proposals: 50, validations: 50, reducers: 40,
      cards: 50, context: 50, cleanup: 50, migration: 50, releasecheck: 50,
      lastTurnSummary: 24, recentProposals: 50, recentValidationResults: 50,
      debugMessages: 50, runtimeWarnings: 25, runtimeErrors: 20, duplicateAudit: 50,
      cardAudit: 50, contextAudit: 50, cleanupAudit: 50, migrationAudit: 50,
      repairAudit: 50, auditLog: 80
    }),
    safeLine(value, maxLength) {
      const max = WL.U.clamp(maxLength || 240, 24, 1000);
      let s = WL.U.norm(value);
      if (s.length > max) s = s.slice(0, max - 3) + '...';
      return s;
    },
    ensure(root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return null;
      if (!root.audits || typeof root.audits !== 'object' || Array.isArray(root.audits)) root.audits = {};
      const buckets = Object.keys(this.caps);
      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) if (!Array.isArray(root.audits[buckets[i]])) root.audits[buckets[i]] = [];
      if (root.runtime) {
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.runtimeWarnings)) root.runtime.runtimeWarnings = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.runtimeErrors)) root.runtime.runtimeErrors = [];
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.hookTrace)) root.runtime.hookTrace = [];
      }
      root.audits.auditLoggerLockVersion = WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION;
      return root.audits;
    },
    capBucket(root, bucketPath, max) {
      if (!root) return;
      const parts = String(bucketPath || '').split('.').filter(Boolean);
      let obj = root;
      for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
        obj = obj && obj[parts[i]];
        if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') return;
      }
      const key = parts[parts.length - 1];
      if (!obj || !key) return;
      if (!Array.isArray(obj[key])) obj[key] = [];
      WL.U.capArray(obj[key], max || this.caps[key] || 40);
    },
    capAll(root) {
      const audits = this.ensure(root);
      if (!audits) return;
      const caps = this.caps;
      Object.keys(caps).forEach(k => this.capBucket(root, 'audits.' + k, caps[k]));
      if (root.runtime) {
        this.capBucket(root, 'runtime.runtimeWarnings', WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings || 25);
        this.capBucket(root, 'runtime.runtimeErrors', WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors || 20);
        this.capBucket(root, 'runtime.hookTrace', WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.hookTrace || 30);
        this.capBucket(root, 'runtime.suppressionAudit', 30);
        this.capBucket(root, 'runtime.returnAudit', 30);
      }
    },
    row(root, family, outcome, reason, extra) {
      return Object.assign({
        turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root),
        family: family || 'system',
        outcome: outcome || 'info',
        reason: this.safeLine(reason || '', 220),
        sourceEventKey: '',
        preview: '',
        notes: []
      }, extra || {});
    },
    append(root, bucket, entry, max) {
      const audits = this.ensure(root);
      if (!audits) return null;
      if (!Array.isArray(audits[bucket])) audits[bucket] = [];
      const row = Object.assign({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), at: bucket }, entry || {});
      if (row.message) row.message = this.safeLine(row.message, 360);
      if (row.reason) row.reason = this.safeLine(row.reason, 360);
      if (row.preview) row.preview = this.safeLine(row.preview, 360);
      audits[bucket].push(row);
      WL.U.capArray(audits[bucket], max || this.caps[bucket] || WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.diagnostics || 40);
      return row;
    },
    debug(root, subsystem, message, data) {
      if (arguments.length === 3) { data = null; message = subsystem; subsystem = 'debug'; }
      const row = this.append(root, 'debugMessages', { subsystem: subsystem || 'debug', message: this.safeLine(message, 300), data: data || null }, this.caps.debugMessages);
      this.append(root, 'debug', { subsystem: subsystem || 'debug', message: this.safeLine(message, 300), data: data || null }, this.caps.debug);
      return row;
    },
    warn(root, subsystem, message, data) {
      if (arguments.length === 3) { data = null; message = subsystem; subsystem = 'warning'; }
      return this.append(root, 'warnings', { subsystem: subsystem || 'warning', message: this.safeLine(message, 300), data: data || null }, this.caps.warnings);
    },
    runtimeWarning(root, message, data) {
      const msg = this.safeLine(message, 300);
      const row = this.append(root, 'runtimeWarnings', { message: msg, data: data || null }, this.caps.runtimeWarnings);
      if (root && root.runtime) {
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.runtimeWarnings)) root.runtime.runtimeWarnings = [];
        root.runtime.runtimeWarnings.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), message: msg, data: data || null });
        WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.runtimeWarnings, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings || 25);
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.warnings)) root.runtime.warnings = [];
        root.runtime.warnings.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), message: msg });
        WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.warnings, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings || 25);
      }
      return row;
    },
    runtimeWarn(root, message, data) { return this.runtimeWarning(root, message, data); },
    runtimeError(root, a, b, c, d, e) {
      let rec;
      if (typeof a === 'object' && a !== null) rec = a;
      else rec = { hook: root && root.runtime ? root.runtime.currentHook : 'unknown', phase: 'runtime', message: a, data: b || null };
      rec = Object.assign({
        turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), hook: rec.hook || 'unknown', phase: rec.phase || 'runtime',
        message: this.safeLine(rec.message || rec.error || 'runtime error', 300),
        safeTextReturned: !!rec.safeTextReturned, partialCommitBlocked: rec.partialCommitBlocked !== false
      }, rec);
      const row = this.append(root, 'runtimeErrors', rec, this.caps.runtimeErrors);
      if (root && root.runtime) {
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.runtimeErrors)) root.runtime.runtimeErrors = [];
        root.runtime.runtimeErrors.push(rec);
        WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.runtimeErrors, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors || 20);
        if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.errors)) root.runtime.errors = [];
        root.runtime.errors.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), message: rec.message });
        WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.errors, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors || 20);
      }
      return row;
    },
    proposal(root, record) {
      const rec = this.row(root, (record && record.family) || 'proposal', (record && record.outcome) || 'created', (record && record.reason) || 'proposal recorded', record || {});
      this.append(root, 'recentProposals', rec, this.caps.recentProposals);
      return this.append(root, 'proposals', rec, this.caps.proposals);
    },
    validation(root, record) {
      const rec = this.row(root, (record && record.family) || 'validation', (record && (record.result || record.outcome)) || 'checked', (record && record.reason) || 'validation recorded', record || {});
      this.append(root, 'recentValidationResults', rec, this.caps.recentValidationResults);
      return this.append(root, 'validations', rec, this.caps.validations);
    },
    duplicate(root, key, reason) {
      const rec = this.row(root, 'ledger', 'duplicate_skip', reason || 'duplicate key skipped', { sourceEventKey: WL.OperationLedger ? WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey(key) : WL.U.slug(key), preview: String(key || '').slice(0, 120) });
      return this.append(root, 'duplicateAudit', rec, this.caps.duplicateAudit);
    },
    card(root, message) { return this.append(root, 'cardAudit', { message: this.safeLine(message, 300) }, this.caps.cardAudit); },
    context(root, message) { return this.append(root, 'contextAudit', { message: this.safeLine(message, 300) }, this.caps.contextAudit); },
    cleanup(root, message) { return this.append(root, 'cleanupAudit', { message: this.safeLine(message, 300) }, this.caps.cleanupAudit); },
    migration(root, message) { return this.append(root, 'migrationAudit', { message: this.safeLine(message, 300) }, this.caps.migrationAudit); },
    release(root, releaseCheckRecord) {
      const rec = Object.assign({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), result: 'WARN', passes: [], warnings: [], failures: [], blockers: [] }, releaseCheckRecord || {});
      return this.append(root, 'releasecheck', rec, this.caps.releasecheck);
    },
    turnSummary(root, summaryRecord) {
      const rec = Object.assign({
        turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root),
        inputHash: root && root.runtime ? root.runtime.lastInputHash : '',
        outputHash: root && root.runtime ? root.runtime.lastOutputHash : '',
        proposalsCreated: 0, accepted: 0, rejected: 0, duplicateSkipped: 0,
        dirtyTargets: root && root.dirty ? root.dirty.targets.slice(0, 12) : [],
        notes: []
      }, summaryRecord || {});
      return this.append(root, 'lastTurnSummary', rec, this.caps.lastTurnSummary);
    },
    repair(root, message) {
      const row = this.append(root, 'repairAudit', { message: this.safeLine(message, 300) }, this.caps.repairAudit);
      this.warn(root, 'repair', message);
      return row;
    },
    summarizeLastTurn(root, message) { return this.turnSummary(root, { notes: [this.safeLine(message, 240)], message: this.safeLine(message, 240) }); },
    inspect(root) {
      this.ensure(root);
      const rows = [];
      rows.push({ status: 'PASS', label: 'AuditLogger lock present: ' + WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION });
      ['debugMessages','runtimeWarnings','runtimeErrors','recentProposals','recentValidationResults','duplicateAudit','lastTurnSummary','releasecheck'].forEach(k => rows.push(Array.isArray(root.audits[k]) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'audit bucket present/capped: ' + k } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'audit bucket missing: ' + k }));
      rows.push(typeof this.safeLine === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'safeLine present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'safeLine missing' });
      rows.push(typeof this.capAll === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'capAll present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'capAll missing' });
      return rows;
    },
    runBC7SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const st = {}; const r = WL.StateManager.init(st);
      this.debug(r, 'test', 'debug message'); add('BC7-A01', r.audits.debugMessages.length === 1, 'debug audit records');
      this.runtimeWarning(r, 'runtime warning'); add('BC7-A02', r.audits.runtimeWarnings.length >= 1 && r.runtime.runtimeWarnings.length >= 1, 'runtime warning surfaces to audits and runtime');
      this.runtimeError(r, { hook: 'output', phase: 'forced', message: 'boom', safeTextReturned: true, partialCommitBlocked: true }); add('BC7-A03', r.audits.runtimeErrors.length >= 1 && r.runtime.runtimeErrors.length >= 1, 'runtime error structured');
      this.proposal(r, { family: 'inventory', reason: 'proposal test', preview: 'test' }); add('BC7-A04', r.audits.recentProposals.length === 1, 'proposal audit records');
      this.validation(r, { family: 'inventory', result: 'reject', reason: 'validation test' }); add('BC7-A05', r.audits.recentValidationResults.length === 1, 'validation audit records');
      this.duplicate(r, 'same_key', 'duplicate test'); add('BC7-A06', r.audits.duplicateAudit.length === 1, 'duplicate audit records');
      for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) this.debug(r, 'spam', 'line ' + i);
      add('BC7-A07', r.audits.debugMessages.length <= this.caps.debugMessages, 'audit caps applied');
      add('BC7-A08', !r.inventory.itemsById.same_key, 'AuditLogger does not mutate gameplay');
      return results;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 05. StateManager
  // ============================================================
  WL.StateManager = {
    clone(value) {
      try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)); } catch (e) { return value; }
    },
    root() {
      return this.init(WL.U.ensureGlobalState());
    },
    getRoot(rawState) {
      return this.init(rawState || WL.U.ensureGlobalState());
    },
    init(rawState) {
      const st = rawState && typeof rawState === 'object' ? rawState : WL.U.ensureGlobalState();
      const root = this.ensureRoot(st);
      this.ensureAllBuckets(root);
      this.ensureMeta(root);
      this.ensureFeatures(root);
      this.ensureRuntime(root);
      this.ensureStartup(root);
      this.ensurePlayer(root);
      this.ensureBody(root);
      this.ensureProgression(root);
      this.ensureInventory(root);
      this.ensureAbilities(root);
      this.ensureQuests(root);
      this.ensureActors(root);
      this.ensurePlaces(root);
      this.ensureTime(root);
      this.ensureCards(root);
      this.ensureOperationLedger(root);
      this.ensureAudits(root);
      this.ensureMigration(root);
      this.ensureQuarantine(root);
      this.ensureDirty(root);
      this.ensureCleanup(root);
      this.repairKnownFieldTypes(root);
      this.migrateIfNeeded(root);
      this.protectOldState(root);
      this.applySafeCaps(root);
      const integrity = this.validateIntegrity(root);
      root.meta.lastIntegrityOk = integrity.ok;
      root.meta.lastIntegrityWarnings = integrity.warnings.slice(-8);
      return root;
    },
    createFreshRoot(reason) {
      const root = WL.Schema.root();
      root.meta.stateManagerLockVersion = WL.STATE_MANAGER_LOCK_VERSION;
      root.meta.createdReason = reason || 'fresh_root';
      root.migration.migrationStatus = 'fresh';
      return root;
    },
    ensureRoot(rawState) {
      const st = rawState && typeof rawState === 'object' ? rawState : WL.U.ensureGlobalState();
      const existing = st[WL.ROOT_KEY];
      if (!existing || typeof existing !== 'object' || Array.isArray(existing)) {
        const fresh = this.createFreshRoot(existing == null ? 'missing_root' : 'malformed_root');
        if (existing != null) {
          const preview = String(existing).slice(0, 180);
          fresh.quarantine.records.push({ turn: 0, source: 'state.' + WL.ROOT_KEY, sourceKey: 'malformed_root', originalPreview: preview, reason: 'WorldLedger root was not an object; rebuilt safely.', severity: 'high', suggestedAction: 'Do not import automatically; review manually if needed.', unresolved: true });
          fresh.migration.warnings.push('Malformed state.' + WL.ROOT_KEY + ' was quarantined as preview only; no gameplay truth imported.');
          st[WL.ROOT_KEY + '_malformed_preview'] = preview;
        }
        st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = fresh;
        return fresh;
      }
      return existing;
    },
    ensureAllBuckets(root) {
      const fresh = WL.Schema.root();
      const buckets = WL.ROOT_BUCKETS_ALL || ['meta','features','layout','commandFormat','runtime','startup','player','body','progression','inventory','abilities','quests','actors','places','time','cards','operationLedger','audits','migration','quarantine','dirty','cleanup','pending'];
      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) this.ensureBucket(root, buckets[i], () => this.clone(fresh[buckets[i]]) || {});
      root.schemaVersion = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      return root;
    },
    ensureBucket(root, bucketName, defaultFactory) {
      if (!root[bucketName] || typeof root[bucketName] !== 'object' || Array.isArray(root[bucketName])) {
        root[bucketName] = defaultFactory ? defaultFactory() : {};
        this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: ' + bucketName + ' bucket was missing or malformed and has been recreated with safe defaults.');
      }
      return root[bucketName];
    },
    ensureMeta(root) {
      const m = this.ensureBucket(root, 'meta', () => WL.Schema.root().meta);
      if (!root.build || typeof root.build !== 'object') root.build = WL.Schema.root().build;
      root.build.projectName = WL.PROJECT_NAME;
      root.build.buildName = WL.BUILD_NAME;
      root.build.buildVersion = WL.BUILD_VERSION;
      root.build.buildStage = WL.BUILD_STAGE;
      root.build.lastTouchedTurn = Number(m.currentTurn || root.runtime && root.runtime.currentTurn || 0);
      m.projectName = WL.PROJECT_NAME;
      m.buildName = WL.BUILD_NAME;
      m.buildStage = WL.BUILD_STAGE;
      m.schemaVersion = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      m.schemaId = WL.SCHEMA_ID;
      m.schemaLockVersion = WL.SCHEMA_LOCK_VERSION;
      m.stateManagerLockVersion = WL.STATE_MANAGER_LOCK_VERSION;
      if (!m.packageAuthority) m.packageAuthority = 'AIDRPG Final Build Planning Package v1.4.1 / Code Pass 1';
      if (!m.createdBy) m.createdBy = 'WorldLedger script';
      if (typeof m.createdTurn !== 'number') m.createdTurn = 0;
      if (typeof m.currentTurn !== 'number') m.currentTurn = 0;
      if (!Array.isArray(m.notes)) m.notes = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(m.schemaRepairs)) m.schemaRepairs = [];
      return m;
    },
    ensureFeatures(root) {
      const defaults = WL.FeatureDefaults;
      const f = this.ensureBucket(root, 'features', () => ({}));
      Object.keys(defaults).forEach(k => { if (typeof f[k] === 'undefined') f[k] = defaults[k]; });
      const forceFalse = ['generatedCardsEnabled','protectedCardWritesEnabled','manualMutationCommandsEnabled','economyEnabled','craftingEnabled','investigationEnabled','reputationEnabled','domainEnabled','absorptionEnabled','advancedCombatEnabled','warfareEnabled','multiProtagonistEnabled'];
      for (let i = 0; i < forceFalse.length; i++) {
        const k = forceFalse[i];
        if (f[k] !== false) {
          f[k] = false;
          this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: unsafe/deferred feature flag ' + k + ' was forced false for Code Pass 1.');
        }
      }
      f.cardSyncDryRun = true;
      f.cardSyncSafeMode = true;
      f.commandsEnabled = true;
      f.stateManagerLockEnabled = true;
      return f;
    },
    ensureRuntime(root) {
      const r = this.ensureBucket(root, 'runtime', () => WL.Schema.root().runtime);
      if (!r.currentHook) r.currentHook = 'none';
      if (typeof r.currentTurn !== 'number') r.currentTurn = 0;
      if (typeof r.hookDepth !== 'number') r.hookDepth = 0;
      ['hookTrace','runtimeWarnings','runtimeErrors','warnings','errors'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(r[k])) r[k] = []; });
      ['lastInputHash','lastContextHash','lastOutputHash','lastAppliedOutputHash','lastCommandHash','lastCommandOutputHash','lastCommandName','pendingInputIntentId','lastInputText','lastReturnStatus','lastReturnShape','lastSuppressionReason','lastDuplicateOutputHash','lastFailSoftHook','lastFailSoftPhase','activeTraceId'].forEach(k => { if (r[k] == null) r[k] = ''; });
      ['commandActive','commandSuppressed','suppressEventScanForCommandOutput','outputReplayDetected','reentryBlocked','mutationBlocked'].forEach(k => { if (typeof r[k] !== 'boolean') r[k] = false; });
      if (typeof r.lastCommandTurn !== 'number') r.lastCommandTurn = 0;
      if (typeof r.duplicateOutputSkipped !== 'number') r.duplicateOutputSkipped = 0;
      if (typeof r.failSoftCount !== 'number') r.failSoftCount = 0;
      if (!Array.isArray(r.suppressionAudit)) r.suppressionAudit = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(r.returnAudit)) r.returnAudit = [];
      r.runtimeLockVersion = WL.RUNTIME_HOOK_CONTRACT_VERSION;
      return r;
    },
    ensureStartup(root) {
      const s = this.ensureBucket(root, 'startup', () => WL.Schema.root().startup);
      ['firstHookSeen','firstGameplayInputSeen','startupLocked','identityImported','oldStateQuarantined','setupImported','gameplayStarted'].forEach(k => { if (typeof s[k] !== 'boolean') s[k] = false; });
      if (!s.setupStatus) s.setupStatus = s.startupLocked || s.gameplayStarted ? 'locked' : 'not_started';
      if (typeof s.setupLockedTurn !== 'number') s.setupLockedTurn = 0;
      if (!s.setupSourceHash) s.setupSourceHash = '';
      ['importedFields','rejectedFields','warnings','errors','startupOperationKeys','lockedFields'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(s[k])) s[k] = []; });
      return s;
    },
    ensurePlayer(root) {
      const p = this.ensureBucket(root, 'player', () => WL.Schema.root().player);
      if (!p.id) p.id = 'player';
      if (!p.name) p.name = 'Unknown';
      if (p.raceSpecies == null) p.raceSpecies = p.species || p.race || 'unknown';
      if (p.species == null) p.species = p.raceSpecies || 'unknown';
      if (p.race == null) p.race = p.raceSpecies || 'unknown';
      if (p.age == null) p.age = 'unknown';
      if (p.appearance == null) p.appearance = 'unspecified';
      if (p.bodyType == null) p.bodyType = 'humanoid';
      if (p.buildIdentity == null) p.buildIdentity = 'balanced';
      if (p.specialization == null) p.specialization = 'none';
      p.level = WL.U.clamp(p.level || 1, 1, 1000);
      p.xp = WL.U.clamp(p.xp || 0, 0, 999999999);
      p.xpToNext = WL.U.clamp(p.xpToNext || 100, 1, 999999999);
      ['titles','nicknames','notes','identityWarnings'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(p[k])) p[k] = []; });
      ['origin','temporaryAppearance','activeDisguise','activeIllusion','locationId'].forEach(k => { if (p[k] == null) p[k] = ''; });
      return p;
    },
    ensureBody(root) {
      const b = this.ensureBucket(root, 'body', () => WL.Schema.root().body);
      const repairTrack = (flatCur, flatMax, key, defCur, defMax) => {
        const t = b[key] && typeof b[key] === 'object' && !Array.isArray(b[key]) ? b[key] : {};
        let max = Number(t.max != null ? t.max : (b[flatMax] != null ? b[flatMax] : defMax));
        let cur = Number(t.current != null ? t.current : (b[flatCur] != null ? b[flatCur] : defCur));
        if (!Number.isFinite(max) || max <= 0) { max = defMax; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.' + key + '.max was invalid and reset to ' + defMax + '.'); }
        if (!Number.isFinite(cur)) { cur = max; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.' + key + '.current was invalid and reset safely.'); }
        if (cur > max) { cur = max; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.' + key + '.current was greater than max and was clamped; this is structural repair, not healing.'); }
        if (cur < 0) { cur = 0; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.' + key + '.current was below zero and was clamped; this is structural repair, not healing.'); }
        b[key] = { current: cur, max: max };
        b[flatCur] = cur;
        b[flatMax] = max;
      };
      repairTrack('hp','maxHp','hpTrack',100,100);
      repairTrack('mp','maxMp','mpTrack',50,50);
      repairTrack('ep','maxEp','epTrack',100,100);
      ['conditions','injuries','statusNotes','bodyTraits','bodyWarnings'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(b[k])) { b[k] = []; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.' + k + ' was missing/malformed and reset to an empty array.'); } });
      if (!['normal','tired','fatigued','exhausted'].includes(String(b.fatigue || 'normal'))) { b.fatigue = 'normal'; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.fatigue invalid enum reset to normal.'); }
      if (!['alive','unconscious','dead','unknown'].includes(String(b.deathBoundary || 'alive'))) { b.deathBoundary = 'alive'; this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: body.deathBoundary invalid enum reset to alive.'); }
      return b;
    },
    ensureProgression(root) {
      const p = this.ensureBucket(root, 'progression', () => WL.Schema.root().progression);
      if (typeof p.levelXp !== 'number') p.levelXp = 0;
      if (typeof p.abilityXp !== 'number') p.abilityXp = 0;
      if (!p.domainXp || typeof p.domainXp !== 'object') p.domainXp = {};
      if (!p.statXp || typeof p.statXp !== 'object') p.statXp = { atk: 0, def: 0, spd: 0, int: 0, lck: 0 };
      ['atk','def','spd','int','lck'].forEach(k => { p.statXp[k] = WL.U.clamp(p.statXp[k] || 0, 0, 999999999); });
      if (!Array.isArray(p.recent)) p.recent = [];
      return p;
    },
    ensureInventory(root) {
      const inv = this.ensureBucket(root, 'inventory', () => WL.Schema.root().inventory);
      if (!inv.itemsById || typeof inv.itemsById !== 'object' || Array.isArray(inv.itemsById)) inv.itemsById = {};
      ['ownedIds','visibleSceneItems','availableSceneItems','sceneItems','log'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(inv[k])) inv[k] = []; });
      if (!inv.sceneItems.length && inv.visibleSceneItems.length) inv.sceneItems = inv.visibleSceneItems.slice(0, 20);
      if (!inv.equipment || typeof inv.equipment !== 'object' || Array.isArray(inv.equipment)) inv.equipment = this.clone(WL.Schema.root().inventory.equipment);
      if (!Array.isArray(inv.equipment.pack)) inv.equipment.pack = [];
      if (!inv.currency || typeof inv.currency !== 'object' || Array.isArray(inv.currency)) inv.currency = { copper: 0, silver: 0, gold: 0 };
      ['copper','silver','gold'].forEach(k => { inv.currency[k] = WL.U.clamp(inv.currency[k] || 0, 0, 999999999); });
      if (!inv.custody || typeof inv.custody !== 'object' || Array.isArray(inv.custody)) inv.custody = {};
      return inv;
    },
    ensureAbilities(root) {
      const a = this.ensureBucket(root, 'abilities', () => WL.Schema.root().abilities);
      ['candidatesById','abilitiesById'].forEach(k => { if (!a[k] || typeof a[k] !== 'object' || Array.isArray(a[k])) a[k] = {}; });
      ['candidateIds','abilityIds','denied','recent'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(a[k])) a[k] = []; });
      if (!Array.isArray(a.statuses)) a.statuses = ['candidate','unstable','stable'];
      ['candidate','stable'].forEach(v => { if (a.statuses.indexOf(v) < 0) a.statuses.push(v); });
      return a;
    },
    ensureQuests(root) {
      const q = this.ensureBucket(root, 'quests', () => WL.Schema.root().quests);
      if (!q.questsById || typeof q.questsById !== 'object' || Array.isArray(q.questsById)) q.questsById = {};
      ['activeIds','offeredIds','completedIds','archivedIds','rewardPromises','rewardPayments'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(q[k])) q[k] = []; });
      if (!Array.isArray(q.rewardStatuses)) q.rewardStatuses = ['promised','paid'];
      ['promised','paid'].forEach(v => { if (q.rewardStatuses.indexOf(v) < 0) q.rewardStatuses.push(v); });
      return q;
    },
    ensureActors(root) {
      const a = this.ensureBucket(root, 'actors', () => WL.Schema.root().actors);
      if (!a.actorsById || typeof a.actorsById !== 'object' || Array.isArray(a.actorsById)) a.actorsById = {};
      ['presentIds','referencedIds','companionIds','recent'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(a[k])) a[k] = []; });
      if (!Array.isArray(a.presenceStates)) a.presenceStates = ['present','elsewhere','absent'];
      ['elsewhere','absent'].forEach(v => { if (a.presenceStates.indexOf(v) < 0) a.presenceStates.push(v); });
      return a;
    },
    ensurePlaces(root) {
      const p = this.ensureBucket(root, 'places', () => WL.Schema.root().places);
      if (!p.placesById || typeof p.placesById !== 'object' || Array.isArray(p.placesById)) p.placesById = {};
      ['referencedIds','recent'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(p[k])) p[k] = []; });
      if (p.currentId == null) p.currentId = '';
      if (p.routeId == null) p.routeId = '';
      if (p.currentLocation == null) p.currentLocation = p.currentId || '';
      if (p.travelIntent == null) p.travelIntent = p.routeId || '';
      return p;
    },
    ensureTime(root) {
      const t = this.ensureBucket(root, 'time', () => WL.Schema.root().time);
      t.day = WL.U.clamp(t.day || 1, 1, 999999);
      t.hour = WL.U.clamp(t.hour == null ? 8 : t.hour, 0, 23);
      t.minute = WL.U.clamp(t.minute || 0, 0, 59);
      ['notes','deadlines'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(t[k])) t[k] = []; });
      return t;
    },
    ensureCards(root) {
      const c = this.ensureBucket(root, 'cards', () => WL.Schema.root().cards);
      c.protectedTitles = WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.slice();
      ['dirtyTargets','dryRunWrites','warnings'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(c[k])) c[k] = []; });
      c.generatedDisabledReason = 'Generated cards are disabled in Code Pass 1.';
      c.generatedCardsEnabled = false;
      return c;
    },
    ensureOperationLedger(root) {
      const led = this.ensureBucket(root, 'operationLedger', () => WL.Schema.root().operationLedger);
      ['permanentKeys','rewardKeys','cardKeys','sourceUseKeys'].forEach(k => {
        if (Array.isArray(led[k])) {
          const obj = {};
          led[k].forEach(x => { const key = WL.OperationLedger ? WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey(x) : WL.U.slug(x); if (key) obj[key] = { turn: 0, migratedFromArray: true }; });
          led[k] = obj;
          this.addRepairWarning(root, 'State repair: operationLedger.' + k + ' array was converted into keyed object without deleting entries.');
        }
        if (!led[k] || typeof led[k] !== 'object') led[k] = {};
      });
      ['recentKeys','duplicateSkips'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(led[k])) led[k] = []; });
      return led;
    },
    ensureAudits(root) {
      const a = this.ensureBucket(root, 'audits', () => WL.Schema.root().audits);
      ['debug','warnings','parser','proposals','validations','reducers','cards','context','cleanup','migration','releasecheck','lastTurnSummary','recentProposals','recentValidationResults','debugMessages','runtimeWarnings','runtimeErrors','duplicateAudit','cardAudit','contextAudit','cleanupAudit','migrationAudit','repairAudit','auditLog'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(a[k])) a[k] = []; });
      return a;
    },
    ensureMigration(root) {
      const m = this.ensureBucket(root, 'migration', () => WL.Schema.root().migration);
      if (typeof m.schema !== 'number') m.schema = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      if (!m.migrationStatus) m.migrationStatus = 'checked';
      ['warnings','repairs','quarantined','migrationOperationKeys'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(m[k])) m[k] = []; });
      if (typeof m.lastRunTurn !== 'number') m.lastRunTurn = 0;
      if (typeof m.unresolvedCount !== 'number') m.unresolvedCount = 0;
      return m;
    },
    ensureQuarantine(root) {
      const q = this.ensureBucket(root, 'quarantine', () => WL.Schema.root().quarantine);
      if (!Array.isArray(q.records)) q.records = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.notes)) q.notes = [];
      if (typeof q.unresolvedCount !== 'number') q.unresolvedCount = q.records.filter(r => r && r.unresolved !== false).length;
      return q;
    },
    ensureDirty(root) {
      const d = this.ensureBucket(root, 'dirty', () => WL.Schema.root().dirty);
      if (!Array.isArray(d.targets)) d.targets = [];
      if (d.lastReason == null) d.lastReason = '';
      return d;
    },
    ensureCleanup(root) {
      const c = this.ensureBucket(root, 'cleanup', () => WL.Schema.root().cleanup);
      if (typeof c.lastRunTurn !== 'number') c.lastRunTurn = 0;
      if (typeof c.capsApplied !== 'number') c.capsApplied = 0;
      if (!Array.isArray(c.notes)) c.notes = [];
      return c;
    },
    repair(root) {
      return this.init({ [WL.ROOT_KEY]: root });
    },
    repairKnownFieldTypes(root) {
      if (WL.CommandOutputFormatLock) WL.CommandOutputFormatLock.ensure(root);
      if (WL.StateSchemaLock) WL.StateSchemaLock.ensureLockedShapes(root);
      this.ensurePlayer(root);
      this.ensureBody(root);
      this.ensureInventory(root);
      this.ensureAbilities(root);
      this.ensureQuests(root);
      this.ensureActors(root);
      this.ensurePlaces(root);
      return root;
    },
    migrateIfNeeded(root) {
      this.ensureMigration(root);
      if (root.migration.schema !== WL.SCHEMA_VERSION) {
        root.migration.migratedFrom = String(root.migration.schema || 'unknown');
        root.migration.schema = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
        root.migration.migrationStatus = 'shell_only';
        this.addRepairWarning(root, 'Migration shell: schema marker updated without importing old prose or Story Card text.');
      }
      root.features.generatedCardsEnabled = false;
      root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled = false;
      root.features.cardSyncDryRun = true;
      root.features.cardSyncSafeMode = true;
      return root;
    },
    quarantine(root, record) {
      this.ensureQuarantine(root);
      this.ensureMigration(root);
      const rec = Object.assign({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), source: 'unknown', sourceKey: '', originalPreview: '', reason: 'unspecified', severity: 'info', suggestedAction: 'manual review', unresolved: true }, record || {});
      const key = WL.U.slug((rec.source || '') + '|' + (rec.sourceKey || '') + '|' + (rec.reason || ''));
      for (let i = 0; i < root.quarantine.records.length; i++) {
        const r = root.quarantine.records[i];
        const rk = WL.U.slug((r.source || '') + '|' + (r.sourceKey || '') + '|' + (r.reason || ''));
        if (rk === key) { r.turn = rec.turn; r.originalPreview = rec.originalPreview || r.originalPreview; return r; }
      }
      root.quarantine.records.push(rec);
      root.migration.quarantined.push({ turn: rec.turn, source: rec.source, sourceKey: rec.sourceKey, reason: rec.reason, severity: rec.severity });
      root.quarantine.unresolvedCount = root.quarantine.records.filter(r => r && r.unresolved !== false).length;
      root.migration.unresolvedCount = root.quarantine.unresolvedCount;
      WL.U.capArray(root.quarantine.records, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.quarantine);
      WL.U.capArray(root.migration.quarantined, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.migration);
      return rec;
    },
    addRepairWarning(root, message) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return;
      const msg = WL.U.norm(message);
      if (!msg) return;
      if (!root.audits || typeof root.audits !== 'object') root.audits = WL.Schema.root().audits;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.audits.warnings)) root.audits.warnings = [];
      root.audits.warnings.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), subsystem: 'StateManager', message: msg });
      if (!Array.isArray(root.audits.debugMessages)) root.audits.debugMessages = [];
      root.audits.debugMessages.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), message: msg });
      if (!root.migration || typeof root.migration !== 'object') root.migration = WL.Schema.root().migration;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.migration.warnings)) root.migration.warnings = [];
      root.migration.warnings.push(msg);
      if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime && root.runtime.runtimeWarnings)) {
        if (!root.runtime || typeof root.runtime !== 'object') root.runtime = WL.Schema.root().runtime;
        root.runtime.runtimeWarnings = [];
      }
      root.runtime.runtimeWarnings.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), message: msg });
      this.applySafeCaps(root);
    },
    markGameplayStarted(root) {
      this.ensureStartup(root);
      root.startup.gameplayStarted = true;
      root.startup.firstGameplayInputSeen = true;
      this.lockStartup(root, 'gameplay started');
      return true;
    },
    lockStartup(root, reason) {
      this.ensureStartup(root);
      root.startup.startupLocked = true;
      root.startup.setupStatus = 'locked';
      root.startup.setupLockedTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      root.startup.lockReason = WL.U.norm(reason || 'locked');
      ['player.name','player.raceSpecies','player.age','player.appearance','inventory.startingItems','abilities.startingAbilities','places.currentLocation','actors.startingCompanion','quests.startingQuest'].forEach(k => WL.U.uniquePush(root.startup.lockedFields, k, 30));
      return true;
    },
    isSetupLocked(root) {
      return !!(root && root.startup && (root.startup.setupStatus === 'locked' || root.startup.gameplayStarted || root.startup.startupLocked));
    },
    validateIntegrity(root) {
      const warnings = [], errors = [], repaired = [];
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') errors.push('root missing');
      else {
        const all = WL.ROOT_BUCKETS_ALL || [];
        for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i++) if (!root[all[i]] || typeof root[all[i]] !== 'object') errors.push('missing bucket: ' + all[i]);
        if (root.schemaVersion !== WL.SCHEMA_VERSION) errors.push('schema version mismatch');
        if (!root.features) errors.push('feature flags missing');
        if (!root.runtime || typeof root.runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash === 'undefined' || typeof root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput === 'undefined') errors.push('runtime output/command suppression fields missing');
        if (!root.player || !root.player.id || typeof root.player.name === 'undefined' || typeof root.player.raceSpecies === 'undefined') errors.push('player protected identity fields missing');
        const b = root.body || {};
        if (!b.hpTrack || !b.mpTrack || !b.epTrack) errors.push('resource tracks missing');
        if (!(b.hp >= 0 && b.hp <= b.maxHp)) errors.push('HP invariant broken');
        if (!(b.mp >= 0 && b.mp <= b.maxMp)) errors.push('MP invariant broken');
        if (!(b.ep >= 0 && b.ep <= b.maxEp)) errors.push('EP invariant broken');
        if (!root.inventory || !Array.isArray(root.inventory.sceneItems) || !Array.isArray(root.inventory.visibleSceneItems) || !Array.isArray(root.inventory.availableSceneItems)) errors.push('inventory scene item separation missing');
        if (!root.abilities || !Array.isArray(root.abilities.statuses) || root.abilities.statuses.indexOf('candidate') < 0 || root.abilities.statuses.indexOf('stable') < 0) errors.push('ability status registry missing candidate/stable');
        if (!root.quests || !Array.isArray(root.quests.rewardStatuses) || root.quests.rewardStatuses.indexOf('promised') < 0 || root.quests.rewardStatuses.indexOf('paid') < 0) errors.push('quest reward status registry missing promised/paid');
        if (!root.actors || !Array.isArray(root.actors.presenceStates) || root.actors.presenceStates.indexOf('elsewhere') < 0 || root.actors.presenceStates.indexOf('absent') < 0) errors.push('actor presence states missing elsewhere/absent');
        if (!root.places || typeof root.places.currentLocation === 'undefined' || typeof root.places.travelIntent === 'undefined') errors.push('place currentLocation/travelIntent separation missing');
        if (!root.cards || root.cards.generatedCardsEnabled !== false || !root.features || root.features.generatedCardsEnabled !== false) errors.push('generated cards not disabled');
        if (!root.operationLedger || !root.operationLedger.permanentKeys || !root.operationLedger.rewardKeys || !root.operationLedger.cardKeys || !root.operationLedger.sourceUseKeys) errors.push('operation ledger buckets missing');
        if (!root.audits) errors.push('audits bucket missing');
        if (!root.migration || !root.quarantine) errors.push('migration/quarantine missing');
        if (!root.dirty || !root.cleanup) errors.push('dirty/cleanup missing');
        if (root.features && root.features.cardSyncDryRun !== true) warnings.push('CardSync dry-run was not true');
        if (root.migration && root.migration.warnings) repaired.push.apply(repaired, root.migration.warnings.slice(-8));
      }
      return { ok: errors.length === 0, warnings, errors, repaired };
    },
    applySafeCaps(root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return root;
      const cap = WL.U.capArray;
      if (root.runtime) {
        cap(root.runtime.hookTrace || (root.runtime.hookTrace = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.hookTrace);
        cap(root.runtime.runtimeWarnings || (root.runtime.runtimeWarnings = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings);
        cap(root.runtime.runtimeErrors || (root.runtime.runtimeErrors = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors);
        cap(root.runtime.warnings || (root.runtime.warnings = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings);
        cap(root.runtime.errors || (root.runtime.errors = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors);
      }
      if (root.audits) ['debug','warnings','parser','proposals','validations','reducers','cards','context','cleanup','migration','releasecheck','lastTurnSummary','recentProposals','recentValidationResults','debugMessages','runtimeWarnings','runtimeErrors','duplicateAudit','cardAudit','contextAudit','cleanupAudit','migrationAudit','repairAudit','auditLog'].forEach(k => cap(root.audits[k] || (root.audits[k] = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.diagnostics));
      if (root.operationLedger) { cap(root.operationLedger.recentKeys || (root.operationLedger.recentKeys = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.recentLedger); cap(root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips || (root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips = []), 30); }
      if (root.dirty) cap(root.dirty.targets || (root.dirty.targets = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets);
      if (root.cards) cap(root.cards.dryRunWrites || (root.cards.dryRunWrites = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dryRunWrites);
      if (root.quarantine) cap(root.quarantine.records || (root.quarantine.records = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.quarantine);
      if (root.migration) { cap(root.migration.warnings || (root.migration.warnings = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.migration); cap(root.migration.repairs || (root.migration.repairs = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.migration); }
      return root;
    },
    clampLogs(root) { return this.applySafeCaps(root); },
    protectOldState(root) {
      try {
        const st = globalThis.state || {};
        const legacyKeys = ['aidrpg','tasis','aidrpgOld','cardSync','patchData'];
        for (let i = 0; i < legacyKeys.length; i++) {
          const k = legacyKeys[i];
          if (st[k] && !root.startup.oldStateQuarantined) {
            this.quarantine(root, { source: 'state.' + k, sourceKey: 'legacy_root_' + k, originalPreview: '[legacy root present]', reason: 'Legacy-looking state root detected; full migration is deferred.', severity: 'medium', suggestedAction: 'Review during a later migration build. Do not import as canon now.' });
            root.startup.oldStateQuarantined = true;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    },
    getStateSummary(root) {
      const i = this.validateIntegrity(root);
      return {
        ok: i.ok,
        build: WL.BUILD_VERSION,
        schema: root && root.schemaVersion,
        warnings: i.warnings.length,
        errors: i.errors.length,
        repairs: i.repaired.length,
        items: root && root.inventory ? Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById || {}).length : 0,
        quests: root && root.quests ? Object.keys(root.quests.questsById || {}).length : 0,
        abilities: root && root.abilities ? Object.keys(root.abilities.abilitiesById || {}).length : 0,
        quarantine: root && root.quarantine ? root.quarantine.records.length : 0
      };
    },
    runBC5SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      let st, r;
      st = {}; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-001', !!(st[WL.ROOT_KEY] && r.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false && r.inventory), 'fresh root creation and safe flags');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = { schemaVersion: WL.SCHEMA_VERSION, player: { name: 'Arin' } }; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-002', r.player.name === 'Arin' && !!r.inventory, 'missing bucket repair preserves player');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = { schemaVersion: WL.SCHEMA_VERSION, body: { hp: 44, maxHp: 100, mp: 20, maxMp: 50 } }; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-003', r.body.hp === 44 && r.body.ep === 100 && r.body.mp === 20, 'missing EP repaired without body reset');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].body.hpTrack = { current: 150, max: 100 }; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-004', r.body.hp === 100, 'invalid high HP clamped');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].body.mpTrack = { current: -10, max: 100 }; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-005', r.body.mp === 0, 'negative MP clamped');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].features.generatedCardsEnabled = true; st[WL.ROOT_KEY].features.craftingEnabled = true; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-006', r.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false && r.features.craftingEnabled === false, 'unsafe flags forced safe');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].operationLedger.rewardKeys = ['quest.reward.old_bren.15_gold']; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-007', !!r.operationLedger.rewardKeys[WL.U.slug('quest.reward.old_bren.15_gold')], 'operation ledger reward key preserved');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].quarantine.records.push({ source: 'old_card', sourceKey: 'crown', reason: 'Player owns Crown of Dawn', originalPreview: 'Player owns Crown of Dawn.' }); r = this.init(st); add('BC5-008', r.quarantine.records.length === 1 && !r.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'quarantine remains inactive');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = 'broken'; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-009', !!(r && r.quarantine.records.length >= 1), 'malformed root fail-soft quarantine');
      st = {}; r = this.init(st); this.markGameplayStarted(r); add('BC5-010', r.startup.gameplayStarted === true && r.startup.setupStatus === 'locked' && r.startup.setupLockedTurn >= 0, 'startup lock helper works');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].player.name = 'Kael'; st[WL.ROOT_KEY].player.raceSpecies = 'elf'; r = this.init(st); add('BC5-011', r.player.name === 'Kael' && r.player.raceSpecies === 'elf', 'defaults do not overwrite identity');
      st = {}; st[WL.ROOT_KEY] = WL.Schema.root(); st[WL.ROOT_KEY].audits.debugMessages = new Array(1000).fill(0).map((_,i)=>({i})); r = this.init(st); add('BC5-012', r.audits.debugMessages.length <= WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.diagnostics, 'audits capped safely');
      st = {}; r = this.init(st); this.quarantine(r, { source: 'old_card', sourceKey: 'old_inventory_card', originalPreview: 'Player owns Crown of Dawn.', reason: 'old card prose claim', severity: 'medium' }); add('BC5-013', !r.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'old card prose not imported');
      st = {}; r = this.init(st); const integ = this.validateIntegrity(r); add('BC5-014', integ.ok, 'required integrity fields exist after init');
      add('BC5-015', true, 'StateManager call graph remains foundational; parser/reducer/CardSync are not called by StateManager self-tests');
      return results;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 08. OperationLedger
  // ============================================================
  WL.OperationLedger = {
    bucketAliases: Object.freeze({
      permanent: 'permanentKeys', permanentKeys: 'permanentKeys',
      recent: 'recentKeys', recentKeys: 'recentKeys',
      reward: 'rewardKeys', rewards: 'rewardKeys', rewardKeys: 'rewardKeys',
      card: 'cardKeys', cards: 'cardKeys', cardKeys: 'cardKeys',
      source: 'sourceUseKeys', sourceUse: 'sourceUseKeys', sourceUseKeys: 'sourceUseKeys'
    }),
    normalizeKey(value) {
      const key = WL.U.slug(String(value == null ? '' : value)).slice(0, 180);
      return key || 'empty_key';
    },
    makeKey(parts) {
      const key = (parts || []).map(p => this.normalizeKey(p)).filter(Boolean).join('|').slice(0, 260);
      return key || 'empty_key';
    },
    ensure(root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return null;
      if (!root.operationLedger || typeof root.operationLedger !== 'object' || Array.isArray(root.operationLedger)) root.operationLedger = {};
      const led = root.operationLedger;
      ['permanentKeys','rewardKeys','cardKeys','sourceUseKeys'].forEach(k => {
        if (Array.isArray(led[k])) {
          const obj = {};
          led[k].forEach(x => { obj[this.normalizeKey(x)] = { turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), migratedFromArray: true }; });
          led[k] = obj;
        }
        if (!led[k] || typeof led[k] !== 'object') led[k] = {};
      });
      if (!Array.isArray(led.recentKeys)) led.recentKeys = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(led.duplicateSkips)) led.duplicateSkips = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(led.ledgerWarnings)) led.ledgerWarnings = [];
      led.maxRecentKeys = WL.U.clamp(led.maxRecentKeys || WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.recentLedger || 180, 20, 1000);
      led.ledgerLockVersion = WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION;
      // Do not call compactRecent() here; compactRecent() itself needs ledger access.
      while (led.recentKeys.length > led.maxRecentKeys) led.recentKeys.shift();
      return led;
    },
    bucketName(name) { return this.bucketAliases[name] || name || 'recentKeys'; },
    bucket(root, name) {
      const led = this.ensure(root);
      const bucket = this.bucketName(name);
      if (bucket === 'recentKeys') return led.recentKeys;
      if (!led[bucket] || typeof led[bucket] !== 'object' || Array.isArray(led[bucket])) led[bucket] = {};
      return led[bucket];
    },
    has(root, bucket, key) {
      key = this.normalizeKey(key);
      const b = this.bucketName(bucket);
      if (b === 'recentKeys') return this.hasRecent(root, key);
      return !!this.bucket(root, b)[key];
    },
    hasPermanent(root, key) { return !!this.bucket(root, 'permanentKeys')[this.normalizeKey(key)]; },
    hasRecent(root, key) { return this.bucket(root, 'recentKeys').indexOf(this.normalizeKey(key)) >= 0; },
    hasReward(root, key) { return !!this.bucket(root, 'rewardKeys')[this.normalizeKey(key)]; },
    hasCard(root, key) { return !!this.bucket(root, 'cardKeys')[this.normalizeKey(key)]; },
    hasSourceUse(root, key) { return !!this.bucket(root, 'sourceUseKeys')[this.normalizeKey(key)]; },
    hasAny(root, key) {
      key = this.normalizeKey(key);
      return this.hasPermanent(root, key) || this.hasRecent(root, key) || this.hasReward(root, key) || this.hasCard(root, key) || this.hasSourceUse(root, key);
    },
    add(root, bucket, key, data) {
      key = this.normalizeKey(key);
      const b = this.bucketName(bucket);
      if (b === 'recentKeys') return this.addRecent(root, key, data);
      const obj = this.bucket(root, b);
      if (obj[key]) return false;
      obj[key] = Object.assign({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), bucket: b }, (data && typeof data === 'object') ? data : { reason: data || '' });
      return true;
    },
    addPermanent(root, key, data) { return this.add(root, 'permanentKeys', key, data); },
    addRecent(root, key, data) {
      const led = this.ensure(root);
      key = this.normalizeKey(key);
      if (led.recentKeys.indexOf(key) >= 0) return false;
      led.recentKeys.push(key);
      this.compactRecent(root);
      return true;
    },
    addReward(root, key, data) { return this.add(root, 'rewardKeys', key, data); },
    addCard(root, key, data) { return this.add(root, 'cardKeys', key, data); },
    addSourceUse(root, key, data) { return this.add(root, 'sourceUseKeys', key, data); },
    auditDuplicate(root, key, reason) {
      const led = this.ensure(root);
      const rec = { turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), key: this.normalizeKey(key), reason: WL.AuditLogger ? WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(reason || 'duplicate key skipped', 200) : String(reason || 'duplicate key skipped') };
      led.duplicateSkips.push(rec);
      led.lastDuplicate = rec;
      WL.U.capArray(led.duplicateSkips, 50);
      if (WL.AuditLogger) WL.AuditLogger.duplicate(root, rec.key, rec.reason);
      return rec;
    },
    tryAdd(root, bucketName, key, reason) {
      const bucket = this.bucketName(bucketName);
      const normalized = this.normalizeKey(key);
      if (this.has(root, bucket, normalized)) {
        this.auditDuplicate(root, normalized, (reason && reason.reason) || reason || 'duplicate ' + bucket + ' key');
        return { applied: false, duplicate: true, key: normalized, bucket };
      }
      this.add(root, bucket, normalized, (reason && typeof reason === 'object') ? reason : { reason: reason || '' });
      return { applied: true, duplicate: false, key: normalized, bucket };
    },
    compactRecent(root) {
      const led = this.ensure(root);
      const seen = {};
      const compact = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < led.recentKeys.length; i++) {
        const k = this.normalizeKey(led.recentKeys[i]);
        if (!seen[k]) { seen[k] = true; compact.push(k); }
      }
      while (compact.length > led.maxRecentKeys) compact.shift();
      led.recentKeys = compact;
      return compact.length;
    },
    summary(root) {
      const led = this.ensure(root);
      return [
        'lock=' + (led.ledgerLockVersion || 'none'),
        'recent=' + led.recentKeys.length + '/' + led.maxRecentKeys,
        'permanent=' + Object.keys(led.permanentKeys).length,
        'rewards=' + Object.keys(led.rewardKeys).length,
        'cards=' + Object.keys(led.cardKeys).length,
        'sources=' + Object.keys(led.sourceUseKeys).length,
        'duplicateSkips=' + led.duplicateSkips.length
      ].join(', ');
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const led = this.ensure(root);
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(led.ledgerLockVersion === WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'OperationLedger lock present: ' + WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'OperationLedger lock missing' });
      rows.push(led.permanentKeys && typeof led.permanentKeys === 'object' && !Array.isArray(led.permanentKeys) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'permanentKeys object present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'permanentKeys invalid' });
      rows.push(Array.isArray(led.recentKeys) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'recentKeys array present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'recentKeys invalid' });
