      rows.push(led.rewardKeys && typeof led.rewardKeys === 'object' && !Array.isArray(led.rewardKeys) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'rewardKeys object present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'rewardKeys invalid' });
      rows.push(led.cardKeys && typeof led.cardKeys === 'object' && !Array.isArray(led.cardKeys) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'cardKeys object present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'cardKeys invalid' });
      rows.push(led.sourceUseKeys && typeof led.sourceUseKeys === 'object' && !Array.isArray(led.sourceUseKeys) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'sourceUseKeys object present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'sourceUseKeys invalid' });
      rows.push(led.recentKeys.length <= led.maxRecentKeys ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'recent key cap enforced' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'recent key cap exceeded' });
      return rows;
    },
    runBC7SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const st = {}; const r = WL.StateManager.init(st);
      const k = this.makeKey(['output', 1, 'item.acquire', 'iron sword']);
      add('BC7-L01', !!k && k.indexOf('iron_sword') >= 0, 'makeKey normalizes parts');
      add('BC7-L02', this.addRecent(r, k) === true && this.hasRecent(r, k), 'recent add/has works');
      add('BC7-L03', this.tryAdd(r, 'recentKeys', k, 'repeat recent').duplicate === true, 'recent duplicate skipped');
      add('BC7-L04', r.operationLedger.duplicateSkips.length >= 1 && r.audits.duplicateAudit.length >= 1, 'duplicate skip audited');
      add('BC7-L05', this.addReward(r, 'quest_reward_15_gold') === true && this.hasReward(r, 'quest_reward_15_gold'), 'reward keys stored separately');
      add('BC7-L06', this.addCard(r, 'card_worldledger_player') === true && this.hasCard(r, 'card_worldledger_player'), 'card keys stored separately');
      add('BC7-L07', this.addSourceUse(r, 'source_output_hash') === true && this.hasSourceUse(r, 'source_output_hash'), 'source-use keys stored separately');
      for (let i = 0; i < 250; i++) this.addRecent(r, 'spam_' + i);
      add('BC7-L08', r.operationLedger.recentKeys.length <= r.operationLedger.maxRecentKeys, 'recent keys compacted');
      add('BC7-L09', !r.inventory.itemsById.quest_reward_15_gold, 'OperationLedger does not mutate gameplay');
      return results;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 07. Runtime Hook Contract
  // ============================================================
  WL.Runtime = {
    safeText(value) {
      if (value == null) return '';
      if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
      if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean') return String(value);
      try { return String(value); } catch (e) { return ''; }
    },
    hashText(text) { return WL.U.hash(this.safeText(text)); },
    makeReturn(text) {
      const safe = this.safeText(text);
      return safe || ' ';
    },
    validateHookInput(hookName, text, rawState) {
      const h = WL.U.slug(hookName || 'unknown');
      const okHook = h === 'input' || h === 'context' || h === 'output';
      return { ok: okHook, hook: okHook ? h : 'unknown', text: this.safeText(text), rawStateOk: !rawState || typeof rawState === 'object' };
    },
    pushHookTrace(root, record) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return null;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.hookTrace)) root.runtime.hookTrace = [];
      const rec = Object.assign({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn || 0, traceId: 'trace_' + WL.U.hash(String(root.runtime.currentTurn || 0) + '|' + String(root.runtime.hookTrace.length) + '|' + String(record && record.hook || 'hook')), status: 'started' }, record || {});
      root.runtime.hookTrace.push(rec);
      root.runtime.activeTraceId = rec.traceId;
      this.capRuntime(root);
      return rec;
    },
    updateTrace(root, traceId, status, notes) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime || !Array.isArray(root.runtime.hookTrace)) return;
      for (let i = root.runtime.hookTrace.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const rec = root.runtime.hookTrace[i];
        if (!traceId || rec.traceId === traceId) {
          rec.status = status || rec.status;
          rec.endedAt = WL.U.hash(String(status || '') + '|' + String(notes || '') + '|' + String(root.runtime.currentTurn || 0));
          if (notes) rec.notes = WL.U.norm(notes).slice(0, 160);
          return;
        }
      }
    },
    recordWarning(root, message, data) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.runtimeWarnings)) root.runtime.runtimeWarnings = [];
      const row = { turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), message: WL.U.norm(message), data: data || null };
      root.runtime.runtimeWarnings.push(row);
      if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.warnings)) root.runtime.warnings = [];
      root.runtime.warnings.push(row);
      this.capRuntime(root);
      try { WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'runtimeWarnings', { message: row.message, data: data || null }, 30); } catch (e) {}
    },
    recordError(root, hookName, phase, message, safeTextReturned, partialCommitBlocked) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.runtimeErrors)) root.runtime.runtimeErrors = [];
      const row = { turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), hook: hookName || 'unknown', phase: phase || 'unknown', message: WL.U.norm(message), safeTextReturned: !!safeTextReturned, partialCommitBlocked: partialCommitBlocked !== false };
      root.runtime.runtimeErrors.push(row);
      if (!Array.isArray(root.runtime.errors)) root.runtime.errors = [];
      root.runtime.errors.push(row);
      this.capRuntime(root);
      try { WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'runtimeWarnings', { message: 'ERROR ' + row.hook + '/' + row.phase + ': ' + row.message }, 30); } catch (e) {}
    },
    capRuntime(root) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return;
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.hookTrace || (root.runtime.hookTrace = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.hookTrace);
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.runtimeWarnings || (root.runtime.runtimeWarnings = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings);
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.runtimeErrors || (root.runtime.runtimeErrors = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors);
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.warnings || (root.runtime.warnings = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings);
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.errors || (root.runtime.errors = []), WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors);
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.suppressionAudit || (root.runtime.suppressionAudit = []), 30);
      WL.U.capArray(root.runtime.returnAudit || (root.runtime.returnAudit = []), 30);
    },
    guardReentry(root, hookName) {
      const depth = Number(root && root.runtime && root.runtime.hookDepth || 0);
      if (depth > 0) {
        root.runtime.reentryBlocked = true;
        root.runtime.mutationBlocked = true;
        this.recordWarning(root, 'Hook reentry detected in ' + hookName + '; gameplay mutation blocked for safety.', { depth });
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    },
    beginHook(root, hookName, text) {
      const validation = this.validateHookInput(hookName, text, globalThis.state);
      const hook = validation.hook;
      const safe = validation.text;
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return { hook, text: safe, hash: this.hashText(safe), traceId: '' };
      this.guardReentry(root, hook);
      root.runtime.hookDepth = WL.U.clamp((root.runtime.hookDepth || 0) + 1, 0, 5);
      root.runtime.currentHook = hook;
      root.runtime.runtimeLockVersion = WL.RUNTIME_HOOK_CONTRACT_VERSION;
      root.startup.firstHookSeen = true;
      const actionCount = WL.U.getInfoActionCount();
      if (typeof actionCount === 'number') root.runtime.currentTurn = actionCount;
      else if (typeof root.runtime.currentTurn !== 'number') root.runtime.currentTurn = root.meta.currentTurn || 0;
      root.meta.currentTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      const hash = this.hashText(safe);
      const trace = this.pushHookTrace(root, { turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, hook, textHash: hash, startedAt: hash, status: 'started', notes: 'Runtime hook begin' });
      if (hook === 'input') this.markInput(root, safe, true);
      if (hook === 'context') this.markContext(root, safe);
      if (hook === 'output') this.markOutput(root, safe);
      return { hook, text: safe, hash, traceId: trace && trace.traceId || '' };
    },
    endHook(root, hookName, resultText, status) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return this.makeReturn(resultText);
      const safe = this.makeReturn(resultText);
      root.runtime.lastReturnStatus = status || 'completed';
      root.runtime.lastReturnShape = 'string';
      root.runtime.returnAudit.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), hook: hookName || root.runtime.currentHook || 'unknown', status: root.runtime.lastReturnStatus, chars: safe.length });
      this.updateTrace(root, root.runtime.activeTraceId, status || 'completed', 'Runtime hook end');
      root.runtime.hookDepth = Math.max(0, Number(root.runtime.hookDepth || 1) - 1);
      if (root.runtime.hookDepth === 0) root.runtime.currentHook = 'none';
      this.capRuntime(root);
      if (WL.StateManager && WL.StateManager.clampLogs) WL.StateManager.clampLogs(root);
      return safe;
    },
    begin(root, hook, text) { return this.beginHook(root, hook, text); },
    end(root, hook, resultText, status) { return this.endHook(root, hook || (root && root.runtime && root.runtime.currentHook), resultText || '', status || 'completed'); },
    markInput(root, text, duringBegin) {
      const safe = this.safeText(text);
      const hash = this.hashText(safe);
      root.runtime.lastInputHash = hash;
      if (!duringBegin) root.runtime.lastInputText = safe;
      return hash;
    },
    markContext(root, text) {
      const hash = this.hashText(text);
      root.runtime.lastContextHash = hash;
      return hash;
    },
    markOutput(root, text) {
      const hash = this.hashText(text);
      root.runtime.lastOutputHash = hash;
      return hash;
    },
    isDuplicateOutput(root, outputHashOrText) {
      const h = /^[a-f0-9]{8}$/i.test(String(outputHashOrText || '')) ? String(outputHashOrText) : this.hashText(outputHashOrText);
      return !!h && root.runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash === h;
    },
    markOutputApplied(root, outputHashOrText) {
      const h = /^[a-f0-9]{8}$/i.test(String(outputHashOrText || '')) ? String(outputHashOrText) : this.hashText(outputHashOrText);
      root.runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash = h;
      root.runtime.outputReplayDetected = false;
      return h;
    },
    detectCommandOutputReplay(root, text) {
      const safe = this.safeText(text);
      const hash = this.hashText(safe);
      if (/^\s*\[(?:WorldLedger|AIDRPG)\]/i.test(safe)) return true;
      if (root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput && root.runtime.lastCommandOutputHash && hash === root.runtime.lastCommandOutputHash) return true;
      if (root.runtime.commandActive && root.runtime.lastCommandTurn === root.runtime.currentTurn) return true;
      return false;
    },
    setCommandSuppression(root, commandName, commandOutput) {
      const output = this.safeText(commandOutput);
      const hash = this.hashText(output);
      root.runtime.commandActive = true;
      root.runtime.commandSuppressed = true;
      root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput = true;
      root.runtime.lastCommandName = WL.U.slug(commandName || 'command');
      root.runtime.lastCommandHash = hash;
      root.runtime.lastCommandOutputHash = hash;
      root.runtime.lastCommandTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn || root.meta.currentTurn || 0;
      root.runtime.lastSuppressionReason = 'command_output';
      root.runtime.suppressionAudit.push({ turn: root.runtime.lastCommandTurn, command: root.runtime.lastCommandName, hash, reason: 'command output suppressed from story parsing' });
      this.capRuntime(root);
    },
    setCommand(root, name) {
      root.runtime.commandActive = true;
      root.runtime.commandSuppressed = true;
      root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput = true;
      root.runtime.lastCommandName = WL.U.slug(name || 'command');
      root.runtime.lastCommand = name || '';
      root.runtime.lastCommandTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn || 0;
    },
    clearCommandSuppression(root) {
      if (!root || !root.runtime) return;
      root.runtime.commandActive = false;
      root.runtime.commandSuppressed = false;
      root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput = false;
      root.runtime.lastSuppressionReason = '';
    },
    shouldSuppressEventScan(root, text) {
      const safe = this.safeText(text);
      if (this.detectCommandOutputReplay(root, safe)) {
        root.runtime.lastSuppressionReason = 'command_output_or_command_prefix';
        root.runtime.suppressionAudit.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), command: root.runtime.lastCommandName || '', hash: this.hashText(safe), reason: root.runtime.lastSuppressionReason });
        this.recordWarning(root, 'Command output suppression triggered; output was not scanned as story.', { command: root.runtime.lastCommandName || '' });
        this.capRuntime(root);
        return true;
      }
      if (WL.EventScannerLite && WL.EventScannerLite.unsafeSource('output', safe)) {
        root.runtime.lastSuppressionReason = 'unsafe_output_source';
        this.recordWarning(root, 'Unsafe output source suppressed from event scanning.', { prefix: safe.slice(0, 40) });
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    },
    failSoft(root, hookName, phaseOrError, errorOrOriginal, maybeOriginal, isCommand) {
      let phase = 'unknown', err = phaseOrError, originalText = errorOrOriginal;
      if (typeof phaseOrError === 'string' && arguments.length >= 5) { phase = phaseOrError; err = errorOrOriginal; originalText = maybeOriginal; }
      const message = String(err && err.message || err || 'unknown error');
      const commandFailure = !!isCommand || (root && root.runtime && root.runtime.commandActive);
      let safe = commandFailure ? '[WorldLedger] /error — Command Error\nStatus: ERROR\nMode: inspect-only; command output is non-canon and suppressed from story parsing.\nFormat: ' + WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION + '\n\nThe command failed safely. Gameplay state was not intentionally changed. Run /debuglast or /stateaudit for details.\n' : this.makeReturn(originalText);
      try {
        if (root && root.runtime) {
          root.runtime.failSoftCount = WL.U.clamp((root.runtime.failSoftCount || 0) + 1, 0, 999999);
          root.runtime.lastFailSoftHook = hookName || root.runtime.currentHook || 'unknown';
          root.runtime.lastFailSoftPhase = phase;
          this.recordError(root, hookName || root.runtime.currentHook || 'unknown', phase, message, true, true);
          this.updateTrace(root, root.runtime.activeTraceId, 'fail_soft', phase + ': ' + message.slice(0, 120));
          root.runtime.hookDepth = 0;
          root.runtime.currentHook = 'none';
          this.capRuntime(root);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      return this.makeReturn(safe);
    },
    runBC6SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        let before, after, out, root;
        out = WL.onInput('I look around.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-001', typeof out === 'string' && !!root.runtime.lastInputHash && Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById).length === 0, 'Input hook returns text and does not commit gameplay from intent alone');
        const turnBefore = root.runtime.currentTurn;
        out = WL.onInput('/help'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-002', typeof out === 'string' && /^\[WorldLedger\]/.test(out) && root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput === true && root.runtime.currentTurn === turnBefore, 'Command handled in Input and suppression set without gameplay turn advance');
        WL.onOutput(out + '\nOwned: Rusty Dagger'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-003', !root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger && root.runtime.suppressionAudit.length > 0, 'Command output not scanned as story');
        before = JSON.stringify({ inv: root.inventory, body: root.body, quests: root.quests, actors: root.actors, places: root.places });
        WL.onContext('Memory: The Iron Sword is in Mara custody.'); root = WL.StateManager.root();
        after = JSON.stringify({ inv: root.inventory, body: root.body, quests: root.quests, actors: root.actors, places: root.places });
        add('BC6-004', before === after && !!root.runtime.lastContextHash, 'Context hook read-only for gameplay buckets');
        WL.onInput('I take the rusty dagger.'); WL.onOutput('You pick up the rusty dagger.'); root = WL.StateManager.root();
        const qty1 = root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger && root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger.qty || 0;
        WL.onOutput('You pick up the rusty dagger.'); root = WL.StateManager.root();
        const qty2 = root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger && root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger.qty || 0;
        add('BC6-005', qty1 === qty2 && root.runtime.duplicateOutputSkipped >= 1, 'Duplicate output hash skip prevents duplicate consequences');
        const xpBefore = root.player.xp || 0; WL.onOutput('You gain 20 XP.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-006', (root.player.xp || 0) === xpBefore, 'Output shell does not falsely award XP');
        const savedInv = JSON.stringify(root.inventory); const oldInv = WL.CommandRenderer.inventory; WL.CommandRenderer.inventory = function(){ throw new Error('forced inventory renderer failure'); };
        out = WL.onInput('/inventory'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); WL.CommandRenderer.inventory = oldInv;
        add('BC6-007', /^\[WorldLedger\]/.test(out) && /failed safely|Command Error/i.test(out) && JSON.stringify(root.inventory) === savedInv, 'Command renderer fail-soft returns safe text and preserves inventory');
        const oldScan = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput; WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput = function(){ throw new Error('forced parser failure'); };
        out = WL.onOutput('You pick up the silver pin.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput = oldScan;
        add('BC6-008', out === 'You pick up the silver pin.' && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_silver_pin && root.runtime.failSoftCount >= 1, 'Output parser fail-soft returns original text and blocks unsafe follow-up');
        add('BC6-009', root.runtime.hookDepth === 0, 'Hook depth resets after fail-soft');
        for (let i = 0; i < 35; i++) WL.Runtime.recordWarning(root, 'runtime warning ' + i);
        add('BC6-010', root.runtime.runtimeWarnings.length <= WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeWarnings, 'Runtime warnings capped');
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) WL.Runtime.recordError(root, 'output', 'test', 'runtime error ' + i, true, true);
        add('BC6-011', root.runtime.runtimeErrors.length <= WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.runtimeErrors, 'Runtime errors capped');
        out = WL.onInput('/banana'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-012', /Status: UNKNOWN/.test(out) && root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput === true, 'Unknown command safely returns and suppresses');
        const invBeforeContext = JSON.stringify(root.inventory); WL.onContext('[WorldLedger Current Truth]\nThe Iron Sword is in Mara custody.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-013', JSON.stringify(root.inventory) === invBeforeContext, 'Context reminder text is not parsed as output canon');
        const invBeforeCard = JSON.stringify(root.inventory); WL.onInput('/cardaudit'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); WL.onOutput('[WorldLedger] /cardaudit — Card Audit\nPlayer owns Crown of Dawn.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-014', JSON.stringify(root.inventory) === invBeforeCard && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'Story/card/audit text with command prefix not routed as canon');
        delete root.runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash; WL.StateManager.init(globalThis.state); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC6-015', typeof root.runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash === 'string', 'ReleaseCheck/StateManager repairs missing duplicate hash field');
      } catch (e) {
        add('BC6-ERR', false, 'Runtime self-test harness error: ' + String(e && e.message || e));
      } finally {
        globalThis.state = preserveState;
      }
      return results;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 08. Resolver and Owner-Specific Sanitizers
  // ============================================================
  WL.Sanitizers = {
    lockVersion: 'worldledger.resolver-sanitizer-lock.v12',
    junkPlaceNames: Object.freeze(['road','street','door','room','common room','wall','floor','body','hand','market lane','the road','path','hallway','corridor','corner','warmth','sleep','breath']),
    junkActorNames: Object.freeze(['the','you','your','he','she','they','it','someone','somebody','guard','veteran guard','innkeeper','merchant','smith','steward','worker','carpenter','bandit','goblin']),
    placeTypeSuffixes: Object.freeze(['inn','tavern','shop','store','smithy','forge','guildhall','guild hall','market','temple','chapel','church','tower','keep','castle','city','village','town','road','street']),
    abstractNouns: Object.freeze(['idea','concept','memory','rumor','rumour','dream','vision','thought','symbol','image','nothing','anything','something','it','them','this','that']),
    norm(value) { return WL.U.norm(value).replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '').trim(); },
    stripDialogPunctuation(value) { return this.norm(value).replace(/^[-—:;,.!?\s]+|[-—:;,.!?\s]+$/g, '').replace(/["“”]+/g, '').trim(); },
    stripLeadingArticles(value) { return this.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some|your|my|his|her|their|our)\s+/i, '').trim(); },
    stripPossessive(value) { return this.norm(value).replace(/\b(?:your|my|his|her|their|our)\s+/ig, '').trim(); },
    stripUnsafeTail(value) {
      return this.stripDialogPunctuation(value)
        .replace(/\s+(?:to|by|while|when|as|because|before|after|until|with|without|near|from|inside|outside|towards?|against|for|into|onto|through|under|over|beside|between|where)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, '')
        .trim();
    },
    hasAbstractPrefix(value) { return /\b(?:idea of|concept of|thought of|memory of|rumou?r of|dream of|vision of|image of|symbol of)\b/i.test(this.norm(value)); },
    isAbstractName(value) {
      const v = WL.U.slug(value);
      if (!v) return true;
      for (let i = 0; i < this.abstractNouns.length; i++) if (v === WL.U.slug(this.abstractNouns[i])) return true;
      return false;
    },
    removeEmbeddedActionNoise(value) {
      return this.norm(value)
        .replace(/\b(?:you|he|she|they|mara|the guard)\s+(?:pick up|take|grab|accept|refuse|drop|hand|hands|give|gives|place|places)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .trim();
    },
    item(value) {
      let v = this.stripUnsafeTail(value);
      v = this.stripLeadingArticles(v);
      v = v.replace(/\b(?:in your hand|into your hand|to you)$/i, '').trim();
      v = v.replace(/\b(?:is|are|was|were)\s+(?:on|in|near|beside|under)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      v = v.replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, '').trim();
      const l = WL.U.lower(v);
      if (!l || l.length > 70) return '';
      if (this.hasAbstractPrefix(l) || this.isAbstractName(l)) return '';
      if (/\b(?:visible|nearby|shelf|floor|ground|road|street|door|room|common room|memory|rumou?r|dream|vision|quote|says?)\b$/i.test(l)) return '';
      if (/\b(?:quest|mission|task|job|xp|level|gold|silver|copper)$/.test(l) && !/\d/.test(l)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(v);
    },
    itemCategory(name) {
      const l = WL.U.lower(name);
      if (/\b(?:potion|elixir|draught|salve|tonic)\b/.test(l)) return 'consumable';
      if (/\b(?:sword|dagger|blade|axe|mace|bow|staff|spear|wand|shield)\b/.test(l)) return 'equipment';
      if (/\b(?:key|map|letter|note|ring|gem|crown|relic|token)\b/.test(l)) return 'important';
      if (/\b(?:ore|wood|hide|cloth|herb|crystal|ingot|thread)\b/.test(l)) return 'material';
      if (/\b(?:gold|silver|copper)\b/.test(l)) return 'currency';
      return 'item';
    },
    ability(value) {
      const raw = this.norm(value);
      const m = raw.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}){0,3}\s+(?:Ward|Slash|Strike|Bolt|Sense|Sight|Step|Stance|Form|Art|Barrier|Shield|Control|Pulse|Veil|Blade|Guard|Guarding|Flame|Spark|Flow|Grip|Awareness|Thread|Mark|Echo|Breath|Skin|Claw))\b/);
      let v = m ? m[1] : raw;
      v = this.stripUnsafeTail(v).replace(/\bGuarding$/i, 'Guard');
      if (!/[A-Z]/.test(v)) return '';
      if (/\b(?:travel|smithy|door|key|inspect|open|road|street|inventory|quest|gold|copper|silver|room|inn|tavern)\b/i.test(v)) return '';
      if (v.split(/\s+/).length > 5) return '';
      return WL.U.title(v);
    },
    actor(value) {
      let v = this.stripUnsafeTail(value);
      v = v.replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/\b(?:the\s+)?(?:innkeeper|merchant|smith|guard|steward|foreman|captain|elder|apprentice)\b$/i, '').trim() || this.stripUnsafeTail(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').trim();
      v = v.replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, '').trim();
      const slug = WL.U.slug(v);
      if (!v || v.length > 70) return '';
      for (let i = 0; i < this.junkActorNames.length; i++) if (slug === WL.U.slug(this.junkActorNames[i])) return '';
      if (!/^[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,3}$/.test(v)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(v);
    },
    actorRole(value) {
      const l = WL.U.lower(value);
      const m = l.match(/\b(innkeeper|merchant|smith|steward|foreman|guard|captain|elder|apprentice|priest|scholar|hunter|worker|carpenter|healer)\b/);
      return m ? m[1] : '';
    },
    placeDisplay(value) {
      let v = this.stripUnsafeTail(value);
      const m = v.match(/\b(?:enter|entered|arrive at|arrived at|arrive in|arrived in|reach|reached|inside|step inside|walk into|return to|go inside|head into)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’ -]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) v = m[1];
      v = v.replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, '').trim();
      if (!v || v.length > 90) return '';
      const slug = WL.U.slug(v.replace(/^the\s+/i, ''));
      for (let i = 0; i < this.junkPlaceNames.length; i++) if (slug === WL.U.slug(this.junkPlaceNames[i])) return '';
      if (/^(?:road|street|door|room|wall|floor|body|hand|common room|market lane)$/i.test(v)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(v.replace(/^the\s+/i, ''));
    },
    placeCanonical(value) {
      let d = this.placeDisplay(value);
      if (!d) return '';
      let base = d.replace(/^the\s+/i, '').trim();
      for (let i = 0; i < this.placeTypeSuffixes.length; i++) {
        const suffix = this.placeTypeSuffixes[i].replace(/\s+/g, '\\s+');
        const re = new RegExp('\\s+' + suffix + '$', 'i');
        const stripped = base.replace(re, '').trim();
        if (stripped && stripped.length >= 3 && stripped !== base) { base = stripped; break; }
      }
      return WL.U.title(base);
    },
    place(value) { return this.placeCanonical(value); },
    placeType(value) {
      const l = WL.U.lower(value);
      for (let i = 0; i < this.placeTypeSuffixes.length; i++) {
        const suffix = this.placeTypeSuffixes[i];
        if (new RegExp('\\b' + suffix.replace(/\s+/g, '\\s+') + '\\b', 'i').test(l)) return suffix;
      }
      return '';
    },
    quest(value) {
      let v = this.stripDialogPunctuation(value).replace(/^to\s+/i, '');
      v = v.replace(/\b(?:reward|payment|paid|promised)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').replace(/[.;]+$/g, '').trim();
      if (!v || v.length < 3 || v.length > 120) return '';
      if (/^(?:quest|task|job|mission)$/i.test(v)) return '';
      return v.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + v.slice(1);
    },
    reward(value) {
      const v = this.norm(value).match(/(\d{1,6})\s*(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (!v) return null;
      return { amount: Number(v[1]), coin: v[2].toLowerCase(), display: Number(v[1]) + ' ' + v[2].toLowerCase() };
    },
    timeAmount(value) {
      const map = { one:1,two:2,three:3,four:4,five:5,six:6,seven:7,eight:8,nine:9,ten:10,eleven:11,twelve:12,thirteen:13,fourteen:14,fifteen:15,sixteen:16,seventeen:17,eighteen:18,nineteen:19,twenty:20 };
      const m = this.norm(value).match(/\b(\d{1,3}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?|weeks?|months?|years?)\b/i);
      if (!m) return null;
      const n = /^\d+$/.test(m[1]) ? Number(m[1]) : (map[m[1].toLowerCase()] || 0);
      return { amount: n, unit: m[2].toLowerCase().replace(/s$/, '') };
    },
    cardTrigger(value) {
      const v = this.stripDialogPunctuation(value);
      if (!v || /^(?:worldledger|aidrpg|system|settings|debug|street|door|room|common room)$/i.test(v)) return '';
      return v.slice(0, 80);
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(this.lockVersion === WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Sanitizer lock present: ' + this.lockVersion } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Sanitizer lock mismatch' });
      rows.push(this.item('the red healing potion in your hand') === 'Red Healing Potion' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'item sanitizer strips articles and custody/location tail' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'item sanitizer failed red healing potion' });
      rows.push(this.item('idea of a brass key') === '' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'abstract item sanitizer rejects idea/concept claims' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'abstract item sanitizer failed' });
      rows.push(this.ability('I try Ember Ward, but nothing happens.') === 'Ember Ward' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ability sanitizer preserves named candidate' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ability sanitizer failed Ember Ward' });
      rows.push(this.actor('Mara') === 'Mara' && this.actor('the guard') === '' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'actor sanitizer keeps names and rejects generic roles' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'actor sanitizer generic-name guard failed' });
      rows.push(this.place('The Sturdy Oak inn') === 'Sturdy Oak' && this.place('Sturdy Oak tavern') === 'Sturdy Oak' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'place aliases canonicalize across article/type suffixes' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'place alias canonicalization failed' });
      rows.push(this.place('the road') === '' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'junk place fragment rejected' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'junk place fragment accepted' });
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.Resolver = {
    lockVersion: 'worldledger.resolver-sanitizer-lock.v12',
    ownerKinds: Object.freeze({ inventory:'item', ability:'ability', actor:'actor', place:'place', quest:'quest', reward:'reward', body:'body', time:'time', progression:'progression' }),
    canonicalId(kind, name) {
      const k = WL.U.slug(kind || 'target');
      const n = WL.U.slug(name || 'unknown');
      return k + '_' + n;
    },
    familyKind(family, type) {
      if (/^currency\./.test(type || '') || family === 'reward') return 'reward';
      return this.ownerKinds[family] || WL.U.slug(family || 'target');
    },
    sanitizeByFamily(family, value, hit) {
      const type = hit && hit.type || '';
      if (family === 'inventory') return /^currency\./.test(type) ? (WL.Sanitizers.reward(value) ? WL.Sanitizers.reward(value).display : '') : WL.Sanitizers.item(value);
      if (family === 'ability') return WL.Sanitizers.ability(value);
      if (family === 'actor') return WL.Sanitizers.actor(value);
      if (family === 'place') return WL.Sanitizers.place(value);
      if (family === 'quest') return /^reward\./.test(type) ? (WL.Sanitizers.reward(value) ? WL.Sanitizers.reward(value).display : '') : WL.Sanitizers.quest(value);
      if (family === 'reward') return WL.Sanitizers.reward(value) ? WL.Sanitizers.reward(value).display : '';
      if (family === 'time') return WL.Sanitizers.timeAmount(value) ? WL.U.norm(value) : WL.U.norm(value || 'time');
      if (family === 'body') return WL.U.norm(value || 'body');
      if (family === 'progression') return WL.U.norm(value || 'progression');
      return WL.U.norm(value);
    },
    displayForFamily(family, raw, hit) {
      const clean = this.sanitizeByFamily(family, raw, hit);
      if (family === 'place' && clean) return clean;
      return clean;
    },
    aliasesForBinding(family, display, raw, hit) {
      const out = [];
      const add = v => { v = WL.U.title(WL.U.norm(v)); if (v && out.indexOf(v) < 0 && v !== display) out.push(v); };
      if (family === 'place') {
        const original = WL.Sanitizers.placeDisplay(raw);
        add(original);
        if (display) {
          add('The ' + display);
          const type = WL.Sanitizers.placeType(raw || original);
          if (type) { add(display + ' ' + type); add('The ' + display + ' ' + type); }
        }
      } else if (family === 'actor') {
        const role = WL.Sanitizers.actorRole(raw);
        if (role && display) add(display + ' the ' + role);
      }
      return out.slice(0, 8);
    },
    buildTargetBinding(hit) {
      const h = hit || {};
      const family = h.family || 'unknown';
      const type = h.type || '';
      let name = h.name || h.target || h.item || h.ability || h.place || h.actor || h.objective || '';
      if (!name && h.reward && h.reward.display) name = h.reward.display;
      const display = this.displayForFamily(family, name, h);
      const kind = this.familyKind(family, type);
      const id = display ? this.canonicalId(kind, display) : '';
      const aliases = this.aliasesForBinding(family, display, name, h);
      const binding = {
        raw: name,
        display,
        id,
        kind,
        family,
        type,
        canonicalName: display || '',
        sourceName: name || '',
        aliases,
        ambiguous: !display,
        safeForCommit: !!display || family === 'body' || family === 'time' || family === 'progression',
        sanitizerLockVersion: WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION,
        resolverLockVersion: WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION,
        bindingVersion: WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION,
        notes: []
      };
      if (!display && family !== 'body' && family !== 'time' && family !== 'progression') binding.notes.push('target could not be sanitized for owner family');
      if (family === 'place' && display && aliases.length) binding.notes.push('place alias-safe canonicalization applied');
      if (family === 'inventory' && display) binding.category = WL.Sanitizers.itemCategory(display);
      if ((family === 'quest' || family === 'reward' || /^currency\./.test(type)) && WL.Sanitizers.reward(name)) binding.reward = WL.Sanitizers.reward(name);
      return binding;
    },
    bindLocalClause(hit) {
      return {
        sourceHook: hit && hit.sourceHook || '',
        sourceHash: hit && hit.sourceInfo && hit.sourceInfo.textHash || '',
        localClausePreview: hit && hit.localClausePreview || hit && hit.sourceText || '',
        clauseHash: WL.U.hash(hit && (hit.localClausePreview || hit.sourceText || '') || '')
      };
    },
    mergePlaceAliases(root, display) {
      // Resolver may compute alias keys for future owner reducers, but may not mutate place state in Code Pass 1.
      if (!display) return { wouldMerge: [], mutates: false };
      const aliases = this.aliasesForBinding('place', display, display, { family: 'place' });
      return { wouldMerge: aliases.map(a => this.canonicalId('place', a)), mutates: false };
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(this.lockVersion === WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Resolver lock present: ' + this.lockVersion } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Resolver lock mismatch' });
      rows.push(WL.Sanitizers && WL.Sanitizers.lockVersion === WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Sanitizers share resolver lock' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Sanitizer lock mismatch' });
      const b1 = this.buildTargetBinding({ family:'place', type:'place.current', name:'The Sturdy Oak inn' });
      const b2 = this.buildTargetBinding({ family:'place', type:'place.current', name:'Sturdy Oak tavern' });
      rows.push(b1.id && b1.id === b2.id ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'place aliases resolve to one canonical id' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'place alias id mismatch' });
      const item = this.buildTargetBinding({ family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', name:'idea of a brass key' });
      rows.push(item.ambiguous && !item.safeForCommit ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'abstract item binding blocked' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'abstract item binding unsafe' });
      const actor = this.buildTargetBinding({ family:'actor', type:'actor.present', name:'Mara' });
      rows.push(actor.id === 'actor_mara' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'actor canonical id stable' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'actor canonical id unstable' });
      const ability = this.buildTargetBinding({ family:'ability', type:'ability.evidence', name:'Ember Ward' });
      rows.push(ability.id === 'ability_ember_ward' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ability canonical id stable' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ability canonical id unstable' });
      const before = root ? WL.U.safeJson({ inv:root.inventory, abilities:root.abilities, actors:root.actors, places:root.places, quests:root.quests }) : '';
      this.buildTargetBinding({ family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', name:'red healing potion' });
      const after = root ? WL.U.safeJson({ inv:root.inventory, abilities:root.abilities, actors:root.actors, places:root.places, quests:root.quests }) : '';
      rows.push(!root || before === after ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Resolver/Sanitizers do not mutate gameplay buckets' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Resolver/Sanitizers mutated gameplay buckets' });
      return rows;
    },
    render(root) {
      const rows = this.inspect(root);
      const fail = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      const lines = [
        'Resolver/Sanitizer Lock: ' + WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION + (fail ? ' / FAILURES: ' + fail : ' / locked'),
        'Purpose: normalize owner-specific targets before ProposalBuilder, CanonValidator, and reducers use them.',
        'Rules:',
        '- Shared cleanup is conservative; owner-specific sanitizers decide item/ability/actor/place/quest forms.',
        '- Canonical ids are stable machine ids; display names are human-readable only.',
        '- Place aliases strip leading articles and common type suffixes for matching while preserving display aliases.',
        '- Abstract, quoted, generic, or junk fragments are refused as safe targets.',
        '- Resolver/Sanitizers never mutate gameplay state.',
        'Audit:'
      ];
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return lines;
    },
    runBC12SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        let root = WL.StateManager.init({});
        add('BC12-001', WL.Sanitizers.item('the red healing potion in your hand') === 'Red Healing Potion', 'item sanitizer strips article and location tail');
        add('BC12-002', WL.Sanitizers.item('idea of a brass key') === '', 'abstract item rejected');
        add('BC12-003', WL.Sanitizers.ability('You attempt Ember Ward, but nothing happens.') === 'Ember Ward', 'ability candidate name preserved');
        add('BC12-004', WL.Sanitizers.actor('Mara') === 'Mara' && WL.Sanitizers.actor('the guard') === '', 'actor name guard works');
        add('BC12-005', WL.Sanitizers.place('The Sturdy Oak inn') === 'Sturdy Oak' && WL.Sanitizers.place('Sturdy Oak tavern') === 'Sturdy Oak', 'place aliases collapse to one display');
        add('BC12-006', this.buildTargetBinding({family:'place', type:'place.current', name:'The Sturdy Oak inn'}).id === this.buildTargetBinding({family:'place', type:'place.current', name:'Sturdy Oak inn'}).id, 'place aliases collapse to one canonical id');
        add('BC12-007', this.buildTargetBinding({family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', name:'red healing potion'}).id === 'item_red_healing_potion', 'item canonical id stable');
        add('BC12-008', this.buildTargetBinding({family:'ability', type:'ability.evidence', name:'Mana Slash'}).id === 'ability_mana_slash', 'ability canonical id stable');
        add('BC12-009', this.buildTargetBinding({family:'actor', type:'actor.present', name:'Mara'}).id === 'actor_mara', 'actor canonical id stable');
        add('BC12-010', this.buildTargetBinding({family:'place', type:'place.reference', name:'the road'}).safeForCommit === false, 'junk place fragment not safe for commit');
        add('BC12-011', this.buildTargetBinding({family:'inventory', type:'currency.gain', name:'15 gold'}).reward.amount === 15, 'currency reward binding carries reward packet');
        add('BC12-012', this.mergePlaceAliases(root, 'Sturdy Oak').mutates === false, 'alias merge planner is read-only in Code Pass 1');
        const before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:root.inventory, body:root.body, abilities:root.abilities, quests:root.quests, actors:root.actors, places:root.places });
        this.buildTargetBinding({family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', name:'Crown of Dawn'});
        WL.Sanitizers.place('The Sturdy Oak inn');
        const after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:root.inventory, body:root.body, abilities:root.abilities, quests:root.quests, actors:root.actors, places:root.places });
        add('BC12-013', before === after, 'Resolver/Sanitizers do not mutate gameplay buckets');
        globalThis.state = {};
        WL.onOutput('You enter The Sturdy Oak inn.');
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        add('BC12-014', !!root.places.placesById.place_sturdy_oak && root.places.currentId === 'place_sturdy_oak', 'pipeline uses resolver canonical place id');
        globalThis.state = {};
        WL.onOutput('You enter Sturdy Oak tavern.');
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        add('BC12-015', !!root.places.placesById.place_sturdy_oak && Object.keys(root.places.placesById).length === 1, 'alternate place alias does not fragment place ids');
        globalThis.state = {};
        WL.onOutput('You pick up the idea of a brass key.');
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        add('BC12-016', Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById).length === 0, 'abstract item does not enter inventory through pipeline');
        globalThis.state = {};
        WL.onOutput('Mara refuses the crown but hands you a red healing potion in your hand.');
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        add('BC12-017', !!root.inventory.itemsById.item_red_healing_potion && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown, 'mixed denial/handoff keeps only sanitized confirmed item');
        globalThis.state = {};
        const out = WL.onInput('/resolver');
        add('BC12-018', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/resolver/.test(out) && /Resolver\/Sanitizer Lock/.test(out), '/resolver renders lock report');
      } catch (err) {
        add('BC12-ERR', false, 'Resolver self-test exception: ' + (err && err.message || err));
      } finally {
        globalThis.state = preserveState;
      }
      return results;
    }
  };


  // ============================================================
  // 09. Command Output Format Lock
  // ============================================================
  WL.CommandOutputFormatLock = {
    statusLabels: Object.freeze({ ACTIVE: 'ACTIVE', DEFERRED: 'DEFERRED', UNKNOWN: 'UNKNOWN', ERROR: 'ERROR' }),
    noInstallPattern: /\b(?:paste\s+.*(?:input|context|output)|install\s+instructions?|wrapper\s+tabs?|put\s+this\s+in\s+(?:input|context|output)|globalThis\.WorldLedger\.onInput\s*\()\b/i,
    ensure(root) {
      if (!root.commandFormat || typeof root.commandFormat !== 'object' || Array.isArray(root.commandFormat)) {
        const fresh = WL.Schema.root().commandFormat;
        root.commandFormat = fresh;
      }
      const cf = root.commandFormat;
      cf.formatVersion = WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION;
      cf.locked = true;
      cf.prefix = '[WorldLedger]';
      if (!Array.isArray(cf.warnings)) cf.warnings = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(cf.rules)) cf.rules = WL.Schema.root().commandFormat.rules.slice();
      return cf;
    },
    stripOldHeader(raw) {
      const lines = String(raw == null ? '' : raw).replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n');
      if (lines[0] && /^\[WorldLedger\]/.test(lines[0])) lines.shift();
      return lines.join('\n').trim();
    },
    titleFromRaw(name, raw) {
      const first = String(raw || '').split(/\n/)[0] || '';
      const m = first.match(/^\[WorldLedger\]\s*(.*)$/);
      const title = WL.U.norm(m ? m[1] : '');
      if (title) return title;
      return '/' + WL.U.norm(name || 'command');
    },
    enforce(root, name, raw, status) {
      const cf = this.ensure(root);
      name = WL.U.slug(name || 'command');
      status = this.statusLabels[status] || status || 'ACTIVE';
      let body = this.stripOldHeader(raw);
      if (!body) body = 'No details available.';
      if (this.noInstallPattern.test(body)) {
        cf.warnings.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), command: name, issue: 'hook_wrapper_text_removed' });
        WL.U.capArray(cf.warnings, 20);
        body = body.replace(this.noInstallPattern, '[removed hook-wrapper snippet]').trim() || 'Output contained blocked hook-wrapper wording and was removed.';
      }
      const title = this.titleFromRaw(name, raw).replace(/^\//, '');
      const out = [
        '[WorldLedger] /' + name + ' — ' + title,
        'Status: ' + status,
        'Mode: inspect-only; command output is non-canon and suppressed from story parsing.',
        'Format: ' + WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION,
        '',
        body
      ].join('\n').replace(/\n{4,}/g, '\n\n\n').trim() + '\n';
      cf.lastCommandRendered = name;
      cf.lastStatus = status;
      cf.lastLength = out.length;
      return out || '[WorldLedger] /' + name + ' — Empty command\nStatus: ERROR\nMode: inspect-only; command output is non-canon.\nFormat: ' + WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION + '\n\nEmpty command output repaired.\n';
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const cf = this.ensure(root);
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(cf.formatVersion === WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'command format version locked: ' + WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'command format version mismatch' });
      rows.push(cf.locked === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'command format locked' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'command format unlocked' });
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.commandFormatLockEnabled === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'command format feature enabled' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'command format feature disabled' });
      rows.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('commandformat') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: '/commandformat registered' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: '/commandformat missing' });
      rows.push(this.noInstallPattern.test(WL.SourceText || '') ? { status: 'FAIL', label: 'source contains hook-wrapper snippets' } : { status: 'PASS', label: 'no hook-wrapper snippets detected by format lock' });
      const sample = this.enforce(root, 'format_sample', '[WorldLedger] Format Sample\nSample body.', 'ACTIVE');
      rows.push(/^\[WorldLedger\] \/format_sample/.test(sample) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'formatted sample uses locked header' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'formatted sample header failed' });
      rows.push(/Status: ACTIVE/.test(sample) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'formatted sample includes status' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'formatted sample missing status' });
      rows.push(/non-canon/.test(sample) ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'formatted sample includes non-canon warning' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'formatted sample missing non-canon warning' });
      return rows;
    },
    render(root) {
      const cf = this.ensure(root);
      const rows = this.inspect(root);
      const fail = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      const lines = [
        'Command Output Format Lock: ' + WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION + (fail ? ' / FAILURES: ' + fail : ' / locked'),
        'Prefix: ' + cf.prefix,
        'Mode line: ' + cf.modeLine,
        'Last command rendered: ' + (cf.lastCommandRendered || 'none'),
        'Rules:'
      ];
      (cf.rules || []).forEach(r => lines.push('- ' + r));
      lines.push('Audit:');
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 09. Command Router and Renderers
  // ============================================================
  WL.CommandRouter = {

    outputCaps: Object.freeze({

    activeDispatch: Object.freeze({

    }),
    disabledFamilies: Object.freeze({
      shop: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], buy: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], sell: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], economy: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], price: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], barter: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], debt: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], money: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'], coins: ['economy', 'Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec required.'],
      craft: ['crafting', 'Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec required.'], crafting: ['crafting', 'Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec required.'], recipes: ['crafting', 'Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec required.'], materials: ['crafting', 'Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec required.'], forge: ['crafting', 'Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec required.'], alchemy: ['crafting', 'Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec required.'],
      cases: ['investigation', 'Investigation / Evidence Mini-Spec required.'], case: ['investigation', 'Investigation / Evidence Mini-Spec required.'], clues: ['investigation', 'Investigation / Evidence Mini-Spec required.'], clue: ['investigation', 'Investigation / Evidence Mini-Spec required.'], evidence: ['investigation', 'Investigation / Evidence Mini-Spec required.'], investigation: ['investigation', 'Investigation / Evidence Mini-Spec required.'],
      reputation: ['reputation', 'Reputation / Faction / Titles Mini-Spec required.'], rep: ['reputation', 'Reputation / Faction / Titles Mini-Spec required.'], factions: ['reputation', 'Reputation / Faction / Titles Mini-Spec required.'], faction: ['reputation', 'Reputation / Faction / Titles Mini-Spec required.'], titles: ['reputation', 'Reputation / Faction / Titles Mini-Spec required.'],
      domain: ['domain/labor', 'Domain / Labor Mini-Spec required.'], workers: ['domain/labor', 'Domain / Labor Mini-Spec required.'], labor: ['domain/labor', 'Domain / Labor Mini-Spec required.'], projects: ['domain/labor', 'Domain / Labor Mini-Spec required.'], base: ['domain/labor', 'Domain / Labor Mini-Spec required.'], settlement: ['domain/labor', 'Domain / Labor Mini-Spec required.'],

      followers: ['party advanced', 'Party / Followers / Summons / Multi-Protagonist Spec required.'], summons: ['party advanced', 'Party / Followers / Summons / Multi-Protagonist Spec required.'], summon: ['party advanced', 'Party / Followers / Summons / Multi-Protagonist Spec required.'], protagonists: ['multi-protagonist', 'Party / Followers / Summons / Multi-Protagonist Spec required.'], players: ['multi-protagonist', 'Party / Followers / Summons / Multi-Protagonist Spec required.'], groups: ['scale', 'Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'],
      scale: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], warfare: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], battlefield: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], army: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], armies: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], groupcombat: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], threat: ['scale/warfare', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'], combat: ['combat', 'Combat/Progression v0.2 or Scale / Group Combat Spec required.'],
      cardsync: ['card sync live/generated', 'Story Card API Adapter tests and Generated CardSync Beta Spec required.'], generatecards: ['card sync live/generated', 'Story Card API Adapter tests and Generated CardSync Beta Spec required.'], cardlive: ['card sync live/generated', 'Story Card API Adapter tests and Generated CardSync Beta Spec required.'],
      additem: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], removeitem: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], sethp: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], setmp: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], setep: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], setlevel: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], addxp: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], setability: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], completequest: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], payreward: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], setactor: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], setlocation: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], settime: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], importcard: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.'], resolvequarantine: ['manual mutation', 'Manual Override Spec required.']
    }),
    isCommand(text) { return /^\s*\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+/.test(String(text || '')); },
    normalizeCommandName(name) { return WL.U.slug(String(name || '').replace(/^\/+/, '')).replace(/_/g, ''); },
    parse(text) {
      const rawText = String(text == null ? '' : text);
      const m = rawText.match(/^\s*\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)(?:\s+([\s\S]*))?$/);
      const commandName = m ? m[1] : '';
      const argsText = m && m[2] ? WL.U.norm(m[2]) : '';
      const normalizedName = this.normalizeCommandName(commandName);
      const resolvedName = this.resolveAlias(normalizedName);
      return {
        rawText, commandName, normalizedName: resolvedName, argsText,
        args: argsText ? argsText.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean) : [],
        isCommand: !!m,
        aliasOf: resolvedName !== normalizedName ? normalizedName : '',
        routeStatus: this.isActiveCommand(resolvedName) ? 'active' : (this.isDisabledCommand(resolvedName) ? 'disabled' : (m ? 'unknown' : 'error')),
        notes: []
      };
    },
    resolveAlias(commandName) { return this.aliases[commandName] || commandName; },
    isActiveCommand(commandName) { return !!this.activeDispatch[commandName]; },
    isDisabledCommand(commandName) { return !!this.disabledFamilies[commandName]; },
    getDisabledInfo(commandName) {
      const d = this.disabledFamilies[commandName];
      if (!d) return null;
      return { commandName, family: d[0], reason: d[1], status: 'DEFERRED' };
    },
    markCommandAudit(root, parsed, outcome, detail) {
      try {
        if (!root || !parsed) return;
        const msg = '/' + (parsed.aliasOf || parsed.normalizedName || parsed.commandName || 'command') + (parsed.aliasOf ? ' -> /' + parsed.normalizedName : '') + ' ' + outcome;
        WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'auditLog', { subsystem: 'CommandRouter', command: parsed.normalizedName || parsed.commandName, aliasOf: parsed.aliasOf || '', outcome, message: msg, detail: detail || '' }, 80);
      } catch (e) {}
    },
    dispatchActive(root, parsed) {
      const rendererName = this.activeDispatch[parsed.normalizedName];
      const fn = WL.CommandRenderer[rendererName];
      if (typeof fn !== 'function') return { raw: WL.CommandRenderer.unknown(parsed.normalizedName), status: 'UNKNOWN' };
      const raw = fn.call(WL.CommandRenderer, root, parsed.argsText, parsed);
      return { raw, status: 'ACTIVE' };
    },
    dispatchDisabled(root, parsed, info) {
      return { raw: WL.CommandRenderer.disabled(parsed.normalizedName, info.reason, info.family, parsed), status: 'DEFERRED' };
    },
    renderUnknown(root, parsed) { return { raw: WL.CommandRenderer.unknown(parsed.commandName || parsed.normalizedName || 'unknown', parsed), status: 'UNKNOWN' }; },
    truncateOutput(text, commandName, status) {
      const cap = this.outputCaps[commandName] || this.outputCaps[String(status || '').toLowerCase()] || this.outputCaps.default;
      let out = String(text == null ? '' : text);
      if (out.length > cap) out = out.slice(0, Math.max(0, cap - 80)) + '\n[WorldLedger command output trimmed; omitted ' + (out.length - cap + 80) + ' characters.]\n';
      return out;
    },
    route(root, text) { return this.safeRoute(root, text); },
    safeRoute(root, text) {
      let parsed = this.parse(text), packet, formatted, raw, status;
      try {
        if (!parsed.isCommand) parsed.routeStatus = 'unknown';
        if (parsed.routeStatus === 'active') packet = this.dispatchActive(root, parsed);
        else if (parsed.routeStatus === 'disabled') packet = this.dispatchDisabled(root, parsed, this.getDisabledInfo(parsed.normalizedName));
        else packet = this.renderUnknown(root, parsed);
        raw = packet.raw;
        if (parsed.aliasOf && raw) raw += '\nAlias: /' + parsed.aliasOf + ' -> /' + parsed.normalizedName + '\n';
        status = packet.status || 'ACTIVE';
        formatted = WL.CommandOutputFormatLock.enforce(root, parsed.normalizedName || parsed.commandName || 'help', raw, status);
        formatted = this.truncateOutput(formatted, parsed.normalizedName || 'default', status);
        WL.Runtime.setCommandSuppression(root, parsed.normalizedName || parsed.commandName || 'command', formatted);
        this.markCommandAudit(root, parsed, status.toLowerCase(), 'routed safely');
        return formatted || '[WorldLedger] /command — Command Error\nStatus: ERROR\nMode: inspect-only; command output is non-canon.\n\nEmpty command output repaired.\n';
      } catch (err) {
        this.markCommandAudit(root, parsed || { normalizedName: 'command' }, 'error', String(err && err.message || err));
        return WL.Runtime.failSoft(root, 'input', 'CommandRouter.safeRoute:' + ((parsed && parsed.normalizedName) || 'unknown'), err, text, true);
      }
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push({ status: 'PASS', label: 'CommandRouter lock present: ' + this.lockVersion });
      rows.push(this.isCommand('/help') ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'slash command detection works' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'slash command detection failed' });
      rows.push(!this.isCommand('Mara says /help as a joke.') ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'non-leading slash is not a command' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'non-leading slash routed as command' });
      rows.push(this.parse('   /Inventory list').normalizedName === 'inventory' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'leading whitespace and case normalization work' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'command normalization failed' });
      rows.push(this.parse('/questlog active').normalizedName === 'quests' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'alias /questlog -> /quests works' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'questlog alias failed' });
      rows.push(this.isDisabledCommand('craft') && this.isDisabledCommand('buy') && this.isDisabledCommand('cardsync') && this.isDisabledCommand('additem') ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'disabled command families recognized' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'disabled family recognition incomplete' });
      rows.push(root && root.runtime && typeof root.runtime.lastCommandOutputHash !== 'undefined' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Runtime command suppression fields available' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Runtime command suppression field missing' });
      rows.push(WL.CommandRenderer && typeof WL.CommandRenderer.help === 'function' && typeof WL.CommandRenderer.disabled === 'function' && typeof WL.CommandRenderer.unknown === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'required renderer functions available' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'renderer functions missing' });
      return rows;
    },
    runBC8SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      const preserveBc8Flag = globalThis.__WL_BC8_RUNNING;
      globalThis.__WL_BC8_RUNNING = true;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        let root, out, before, after;
        add('BC8-001', this.parse('/help').isCommand && this.parse('/help').normalizedName === 'help', 'slash command detected and normalized');
        add('BC8-002', !this.parse('Mara says /help as a joke.').isCommand, 'non-leading slash not detected as command');
        out = WL.onInput('   /inventory'); add('BC8-003', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/inventory/.test(out), 'leading whitespace command routed');
        out = WL.onInput('/ReLeAsEcHeCk'); add('BC8-004', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/releasecheck/.test(out), 'case-insensitive command routed');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); before = JSON.stringify(root.quests); out = WL.onInput('/questlog active'); after = JSON.stringify(WL.StateManager.root().quests); add('BC8-005', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/quests/.test(out) && /Alias: \/questlog -> \/quests/.test(out) && before === after, 'alias /questlog maps to /quests without quest mutation');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); before = JSON.stringify({ inv: root.inventory, quests: root.quests, body: root.body }); out = WL.onInput('/finalcheck'); after = JSON.stringify({ inv: WL.StateManager.root().inventory, quests: WL.StateManager.root().quests, body: WL.StateManager.root().body }); add('BC8-006', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/releasecheck/.test(out) && before === after, 'alias /finalcheck maps to /releasecheck without gameplay mutation');
        globalThis.state = {}; out = WL.onInput('/banana'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-007', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/banana/.test(out) && /Status: UNKNOWN/.test(out), 'unknown command safe');
        globalThis.state = {}; out = WL.onInput('/craft iron sword'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-008', /Status: DEFERRED/.test(out) && root.features.craftingEnabled === false && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_iron_sword, 'disabled crafting safe');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.inventory.currency.copper = 10; out = WL.onInput('/buy potion'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-009', /Status: DEFERRED/.test(out) && root.inventory.currency.copper === 10 && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_potion, 'disabled economy safe');
        globalThis.state = {}; out = WL.onInput('/cardsync live on'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-010', /Status: DEFERRED/.test(out) && root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false && root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled === false, 'disabled generated/live cards safe');
        globalThis.state = {}; out = WL.onInput('/additem Crown of Dawn'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-011', /Status: DEFERRED/.test(out) && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'disabled manual mutation safe');
        globalThis.state = {}; out = WL.onInput('/help'); add('BC8-012', /Deferred:/i.test(out) && !/economy.*active/i.test(out) && !/generated cards.*active/i.test(out), '/help is honest');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger = { id:'item_rusty_dagger', name:'Rusty Dagger', qty:1 }; root.inventory.ownedIds.push('item_rusty_dagger'); before = JSON.stringify(root.inventory); out = WL.onInput('/inventory'); after = JSON.stringify(WL.StateManager.root().inventory); add('BC8-013', before === after && /Rusty Dagger/.test(out), '/inventory non-mutating');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.quests.rewardPromises.push({ text:'15 gold promised', paid:false }); before = JSON.stringify({ q: root.quests, cur: root.inventory.currency }); out = WL.onInput('/quests'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); after = JSON.stringify({ q: root.quests, cur: root.inventory.currency }); add('BC8-014', before === after && root.inventory.currency.gold === 0, '/quests does not pay promised reward');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.abilities.candidatesById.ability_shadow_step = { id:'ability_shadow_step', name:'Shadow Step', stage:'candidate' }; root.abilities.candidateIds.push('ability_shadow_step'); before = JSON.stringify(root.abilities); out = WL.onInput('/abilities'); after = JSON.stringify(WL.StateManager.root().abilities); add('BC8-015', before === after && /Shadow Step/.test(out), '/abilities does not stabilize candidate');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.actors.actorsById.actor_mara = { id:'actor_mara', name:'Mara', presence:'elsewhere', present:false }; root.actors.referencedIds.push('actor_mara'); before = JSON.stringify(root.actors); out = WL.onInput('/actors'); after = JSON.stringify(WL.StateManager.root().actors); add('BC8-016', before === after, '/actors does not make elsewhere actor present');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.places.placesById.place_old_mill = { id:'place_old_mill', name:'Old Mill' }; root.places.currentId = 'place_old_mill'; root.places.travelIntent = 'chapel'; before = JSON.stringify(root.places); out = WL.onInput('/location'); after = JSON.stringify(WL.StateManager.root().places); add('BC8-017', before === after && /Old Mill/.test(out), '/location does not move player');
        const active = ['help','version','sheet','status','inventory','abilities','quests','actors','location','time','debuglast','cardaudit','contextaudit','stateaudit','ledger','dirty','migration','quarantine','releasecheck','startupaudit'];
        let allPrefixed = true; for (let i=0;i<active.length;i++) { out = WL.onInput('/' + active[i]); if (!/^\[WorldLedger\]/.test(out)) { allPrefixed = false; break; } }
        add('BC8-018', allPrefixed, 'active command outputs start with [WorldLedger]');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); for (let i=0;i<120;i++){ const id='item_test_'+i; root.inventory.itemsById[id]={id, name:'Test Item '+i, qty:1}; root.inventory.ownedIds.push(id); } out = WL.onInput('/inventory'); add('BC8-019', out.length <= (this.outputCaps.inventory + 200), 'command output cap works for large inventory');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.abilities.candidateIds = 'broken'; out = WL.onInput('/abilities'); add('BC8-020', /^\[WorldLedger\]/.test(out), 'renderer handles malformed bucket safely through StateManager repair');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); const oldInv = WL.CommandRenderer.inventory; WL.CommandRenderer.inventory = function(){ throw new Error('BC8 forced renderer error'); }; before = JSON.stringify(root.inventory); out = WL.onInput('/inventory'); WL.CommandRenderer.inventory = oldInv; after = JSON.stringify(WL.StateManager.root().inventory); add('BC8-021', /Command Error|Status: ERROR/.test(out) && before === after, 'renderer error fail-soft');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger = { id:'item_rusty_dagger', name:'Rusty Dagger', qty:1 }; root.inventory.ownedIds.push('item_rusty_dagger'); out = WL.onInput('/inventory'); WL.onOutput(out + '\nOwned: Rusty Dagger'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-022', Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById).length === 1 && root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger.qty === 1, 'command output suppression blocks parser scan');
        globalThis.state = {}; for (let i=0;i<120;i++) WL.onInput('/help'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('BC8-023', (root.audits.auditLog || []).length <= (WL.AuditLogger.caps.auditLog || 80), 'command audit capped');
        globalThis.state = {}; root = WL.StateManager.root(); root.features.manualMutationCommandsEnabled = true; out = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run(root).join('\n'); add('BC8-024', /FAIL: manual mutation commands enabled/.test(out), '/releasecheck catches command hazards before StateManager repair can mask unsafe flags');
      } catch (err) { add('BC8-ERR', false, 'self-test exception: ' + (err && err.message || err)); }
      finally { globalThis.state = preserveState; globalThis.__WL_BC8_RUNNING = preserveBc8Flag; }
      return results;
    }
  };

  WL.CommandRenderer = {
    header(title) { return '[WorldLedger] ' + title + '\n'; },
    noneIfEmpty(list, formatter, maxItems) {
      if (!Array.isArray(list) || !list.length) return 'none';
      const max = WL.U.clamp(maxItems || 12, 1, 50);
      const rows = list.slice(0, max).map((x, i) => formatter ? formatter(x, i) : String(x));
      if (list.length > max) rows.push('... +' + (list.length - max) + ' more');
      return rows.filter(Boolean).join(', ') || 'none';
    },
    truncateOutput(text, maxChars) {
      const cap = WL.U.clamp(maxChars || 1800, 500, 5000);
      let out = String(text == null ? '' : text);
      if (out.length > cap) out = out.slice(0, cap - 80) + '\n[Command output trimmed; omitted ' + (out.length - cap + 80) + ' characters.]\n';
      return out;
    },
    disabled(name, reason, family) { return this.header('/' + name + ' disabled') + [
      'System: ' + (family || name),
      'Status: Disabled in WorldLedgerV0.1 / Code Pass 1.',
      (family || name) + ' is not active yet.',
      'Required before activation: ' + (reason || 'future mini-spec required.'),
      'No gameplay state was changed.'
    ].join('\n') + '\n'; },
    unknown(name) { return this.header('Unknown command') + [
      'Command: /' + WL.U.norm(name),
      'Status: Not recognized.',
      'Use /help to see active MVP commands.',
      'No gameplay state was changed.'
    ].join('\n') + '\n'; },
    help(root) {
      return this.header('Help') + [
        'WorldLedger is a canon/state engine for AI Dungeon. Player input is intent; AI output must confirm consequences.',
        'Commands inspect only and are non-canon. Command output is suppressed from story parsing.',
        'Active commands: ' + WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.map(c => '/' + c).join(', ') + '.',
        'Active limited reducers: inventory acquisition/handoff, ability candidate evidence, body condition/injury shell, quest lite, actor/place/time/progression shells.',
        'Deferred: generated cards, live card writes, full economy, crafting, investigation, reputation/factions, domain/labor, absorption/evolution, advanced combat, warfare, multi-protagonist, manual mutation commands.',
        'Disabled commands answer honestly with Status: DEFERRED; aliases cannot bypass gates.'
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    version(root) {
      return this.header('Version') + [
        'Project: ' + WL.PROJECT_NAME,
        'Build: ' + WL.BUILD_NAME,
        'Version: ' + WL.BUILD_VERSION,
        'Stage: ' + WL.BUILD_STAGE,
        'Schema: ' + WL.SCHEMA_VERSION,
        'Layout: ' + WL.LAYOUT_VERSION,
        'Schema Lock: ' + WL.SCHEMA_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Command Format Lock: ' + WL.COMMAND_FORMAT_LOCK_VERSION,
        'StateManager Lock: ' + WL.STATE_MANAGER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Runtime Hook Contract: ' + WL.RUNTIME_HOOK_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        'Audit/Ledger Lock: ' + WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'CommandRouter Lock: ' + WL.COMMAND_ROUTER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'EventScanner Lock: ' + WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'ProposalBuilder Lock: ' + WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'CanonValidator Lock: ' + WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Resolver/Sanitizer Lock: ' + WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'State root: state.' + WL.ROOT_KEY
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    layout(root) {
      return this.header('Layout Lock') + WL.LayoutLock.render(root).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    schema(root) {
      return this.header('Schema Lock') + WL.StateSchemaLock.render(root).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    schemaaudit(root) {
      const rows = WL.StateSchemaLock.inspect(root);
      return this.header('Schema Audit') + rows.map(r => r.status + ': ' + r.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    owners(root) {
      const lines = ['Root bucket owners:'];
      Object.keys(WL.SCHEMA_OWNER_MAP).forEach(k => lines.push('- ' + k + ': ' + WL.SCHEMA_OWNER_MAP[k]));
      return this.header('Owner Map') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    sheet(root) {
      const p = root.player;
      return this.header('Player Sheet') + [
        'Name: ' + p.name,
        'Species/Race: ' + (p.species || p.race || 'unknown'),
        'Age: ' + (p.age == null ? 'unknown' : p.age),
        'Level: ' + p.level,
        'XP: ' + p.xp + ' / ' + p.xpToNext,
        'Location: ' + (root.places.currentId && root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId] ? root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId].name : 'unknown')
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    status(root) {
      const b = root.body;
      return this.header('Status') + [
        'HP: ' + b.hp + '/' + b.maxHp,
        'MP: ' + b.mp + '/' + b.maxMp,
        'EP: ' + b.ep + '/' + b.maxEp,
        'Conditions: ' + (b.conditions.length ? b.conditions.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Injuries: ' + (b.injuries.length ? b.injuries.join(', ') : 'none')
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    inventory(root) {
      const inv = root.inventory;
      const items = inv.ownedIds.map(id => inv.itemsById[id] && inv.itemsById[id].name).filter(Boolean);
      return this.header('Inventory') + [
        'Currency: ' + inv.currency.gold + ' gold, ' + inv.currency.silver + ' silver, ' + inv.currency.copper + ' copper',
        'Owned: ' + (items.length ? items.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Visible scene items: ' + (inv.visibleSceneItems.length ? inv.visibleSceneItems.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Available scene items: ' + (inv.availableSceneItems.length ? inv.availableSceneItems.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Main hand: ' + (inv.equipment.mainHand || 'empty')
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    abilities(root) {
      const a = root.abilities;
      const c = a.candidateIds.map(id => a.candidatesById[id] && a.candidatesById[id].name).filter(Boolean);
      const stable = a.abilityIds.map(id => a.abilitiesById[id] && a.abilitiesById[id].name).filter(Boolean);
      return this.header('Abilities') + [
        'Candidates: ' + (c.length ? c.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Stable abilities: ' + (stable.length ? stable.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Note: candidates are not reliable usable abilities yet.'
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    quests(root) {
      const q = root.quests;
      const active = q.activeIds.map(id => q.questsById[id] && q.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean);
      const offered = q.offeredIds.map(id => q.questsById[id] && q.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean);
      return this.header('Quests') + [
        'Offered: ' + (offered.length ? offered.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Active: ' + (active.length ? active.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Completed: ' + q.completedIds.length,
        'Reward promises: ' + (q.rewardPromises.length ? q.rewardPromises.map(r => r.text).join('; ') : 'none')
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    actors(root) {
      const a = root.actors;
      const present = a.presentIds.map(id => a.actorsById[id] && a.actorsById[id].name).filter(Boolean);
      const comps = a.companionIds.map(id => a.actorsById[id] && a.actorsById[id].name).filter(Boolean);
      return this.header('Actors') + ['Present: ' + (present.length ? present.join(', ') : 'none'), 'Companions: ' + (comps.length ? comps.join(', ') : 'none')].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    location(root) {
      const p = root.places.currentId ? root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId] : null;
      return this.header('Location') + ['Current: ' + (p ? p.name : 'unknown'), 'Route/interstitial: ' + (root.places.routeId || 'none')].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    time(root) { return this.header('Time') + 'Day ' + root.time.day + ', ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0') + '\n'; },
    debuglast(root) {
      return this.header('Debug Last') + [
        'Parser hits: ' + (root.pending.lastParserHits || []).length,
        'Proposals: ' + (root.pending.lastProposals || []).length,
        'Validations: ' + (root.pending.lastValidations || []).map(v => v.result + ':' + v.type).join(', '),
        'Reducer results: ' + (root.pending.lastReducerResults || []).map(r => r.status + ':' + r.type).join(', '),
        'Last summary: ' + ((root.audits.lastTurnSummary.slice(-1)[0] || {}).message || 'none')
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    ledger(root) {
      const rows = WL.OperationLedger.inspect(root);
      return this.header('Operation Ledger') + WL.OperationLedger.summary(root) + '\n' + rows.map(r => r.status + ': ' + r.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    ledgercheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.OperationLedger.runBC7SelfTests();
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('LedgerCheck') + 'BC7 ledger self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    audit(root) {
      const rows = WL.AuditLogger.inspect(root);
      return this.header('Audit') + 'Audit/Ledger Lock: ' + WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION + '\n' + rows.map(r => r.status + ': ' + r.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    auditlog(root) {
      const last = (root.audits.auditLog || []).slice(-10);
      const dup = (root.audits.duplicateAudit || []).slice(-5);
      const turns = (root.audits.lastTurnSummary || []).slice(-5);
      const lines = ['Recent audit log entries: ' + (last.length || 0), 'Duplicate skips: ' + dup.length, 'Turn summaries: ' + turns.length];
      turns.forEach(r => lines.push('Turn ' + r.turn + ': ' + ((r.message || (r.notes && r.notes[0]) || 'summary'))));
      dup.forEach(r => lines.push('Duplicate: ' + (r.sourceEventKey || r.key || 'unknown') + ' — ' + (r.reason || 'duplicate')));
      return this.header('Audit Log') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    dirty(root) { return this.header('Dirty Queue') + (root.dirty.targets.length ? root.dirty.targets.join(', ') : 'empty') + '\n'; },
    migration(root) { return this.header('Migration') + 'Schema: ' + root.migration.schema + '\nRepairs: ' + root.migration.repairs.length + '\nWarnings: ' + root.migration.warnings.length + '\n'; },
    quarantine(root) { return this.header('Quarantine') + (root.quarantine.records.length ? root.quarantine.records.map(r => r.kind + ': ' + (r.note || '')).join('\n') : 'empty') + '\n'; },
    startupaudit(root) { return this.header('Startup Audit') + ['firstHookSeen=' + root.startup.firstHookSeen, 'firstGameplayInputSeen=' + root.startup.firstGameplayInputSeen, 'startupLocked=' + root.startup.startupLocked, 'oldStateQuarantined=' + root.startup.oldStateQuarantined].join('\n') + '\n'; },
    cardaudit(root) { return this.header('Card Audit') + WL.CardSyncLite.audit(root).join('\n') + '\n'; },
    contextaudit(root) { return this.header('Context Audit') + (root.audits.context.length ? root.audits.context.slice(-5).map(r => r.message || WL.U.safeJson(r)).join('\n') : 'No context packets recorded yet.') + '\n'; },
    commandformat(root) { return this.header('Command Format') + WL.CommandOutputFormatLock.render(root).join('\n') + '\n'; },
    commands(root) {
      return this.header('Commands') + [
