        'Active inspect-only commands: ' + WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.map(c => '/' + c).join(', '),
        'Deferred command groups answer with Status: DEFERRED and do not mutate state.',
        'Unknown commands answer with Status: UNKNOWN and suggest /help.',
        'All command output is non-canon and suppressed from story parsing.'
      ].join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    commandrouter(root) {
      const rows = WL.CommandRouter.inspect(root);
      const disabled = Object.keys(WL.CommandRouter.disabledFamilies || {}).sort();
      const aliases = Object.keys(WL.CommandRouter.aliases || {}).sort().map(k => '/' + k + ' -> /' + WL.CommandRouter.aliases[k]);
      const lines = [
        'CommandRouter Lock: ' + WL.COMMAND_ROUTER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'EventScanner Lock: ' + WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'ProposalBuilder Lock: ' + WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Doctrine: commands inspect, explain, route, suppress themselves, and never create gameplay truth.',
        'Active command count: ' + Object.keys(WL.CommandRouter.activeDispatch || {}).length,
        'Alias count: ' + aliases.length,
        'Disabled command count: ' + disabled.length,
        'Aliases: ' + (aliases.length ? aliases.join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Disabled command families include: ' + disabled.slice(0, 30).map(x => '/' + x).join(', ') + (disabled.length > 30 ? ', ... +' + (disabled.length - 30) + ' more' : ''),
        'Audit:'
      ];
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return this.header('CommandRouter') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    commandcheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.CommandRouter.runBC8SelfTests();
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('CommandCheck') + 'BC8 command self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    parser(root) {
      const rows = WL.EventScannerLite.inspect(root);
      const lastOut = root.pending.lastParserHits || [];
      const lastIn = root.pending.lastInputParserHits || [];
      const lines = [
        'EventScanner Lock: ' + WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'ProposalBuilder Lock: ' + WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Doctrine: parser detects possible events only; ParserHits are not canon and cannot commit directly.',
        'Allowed sources: input, output.',
        'Rejected sources: command_output, context, story_card, protected_card, generated_card, debug, audit, help, releasecheck, migration_log, archive.',
        'Last input hits: ' + lastIn.length,
        'Last output hits: ' + lastOut.length,
        'Last output families: ' + (lastOut.length ? lastOut.map(h => h.family + ':' + h.type).join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Audit:'
      ];
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return this.header('Parser') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    parsercheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.EventScannerLite.runBC9SelfTests();
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('ParserCheck') + 'BC9 parser self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    proposal(root) {
      const rows = WL.ProposalBuilder.inspect(root);
      const last = root.pending.lastProposals || [];
      const lines = [
        'ProposalBuilder Lock: ' + WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Doctrine: ParserHits become EventProposals only; proposals do not validate, reduce, or mutate canon.',
        'Last proposals: ' + last.length,
        'Last proposal types: ' + (last.length ? last.map(p => p.type + ':' + (p.target && p.target.display || 'none')).join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Audit:'
      ];
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return this.header('ProposalBuilder') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    proposalcheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC10SelfTests();
      const bc14 = WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC14SelfTests ? WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC14SelfTests() : [];
      const controlTests = WL.LegacySalvageLedger ? WL.LegacySalvageLedger.runBC10ControlSelfTests() : [];
      const all = tests.concat(bc14).concat(controlTests);
      const pass = all.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('ProposalCheck') + 'BC10/BC14 proposal + control-map self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + all.length + ' pass\n' + all.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    validator(root) {
      const rows = WL.CanonValidator.inspect(root);
      const last = root.pending.lastValidations || [];
      const lines = [
        'CanonValidator Lock: ' + WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Resolver/Sanitizer Lock: ' + WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Doctrine: mentioned/attempted/quoted/dreamed/rumored/denied/visible/promised/input-only text is not canon until validated.',
        'Last validation results: ' + last.length,
        'Last statuses: ' + (last.length ? last.map(v => (v.status || v.result) + ':' + (v.eventType || v.type)).join(', ') : 'none'),
        'Audit:'
      ];
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return this.header('CanonValidator') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    validatorcheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.CanonValidator.runBC11SelfTests().concat(WL.CanonValidator.runBC15SelfTests ? WL.CanonValidator.runBC15SelfTests() : []);
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('ValidatorCheck') + 'BC11/BC15 CanonValidator self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    resolver(root) {
      return this.header('Resolver / Sanitizers') + WL.Resolver.render(root).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    resolvercheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.Resolver.runBC12SelfTests();
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('ResolverCheck') + 'BC12 Resolver/Sanitizer self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    statemanager(root) {
      const summary = WL.StateManager.getStateSummary(root);
      const tests = WL.StateManager.runBC5SelfTests();
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      const lines = [
        'StateManager Lock: ' + WL.STATE_MANAGER_LOCK_VERSION,
        'Doctrine: create structure, repair structure, preserve truth, quarantine unsafe data, never invent gameplay.',
        'Integrity: ' + (summary.ok ? 'OK' : 'CHECK ERRORS'),
        'Warnings: ' + summary.warnings + ', Errors: ' + summary.errors + ', Recent repairs: ' + summary.repairs,
        'Quarantine records: ' + summary.quarantine,
        'BC5 self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass'
      ];
      tests.forEach(t => lines.push((t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label));
      return this.header('StateManager') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    integrity(root) {
      const r = WL.StateManager.validateIntegrity(root);
      const lines = ['OK: ' + r.ok, 'Errors: ' + (r.errors.length ? r.errors.join('; ') : 'none'), 'Warnings: ' + (r.warnings.length ? r.warnings.join('; ') : 'none'), 'Recent repairs: ' + (r.repaired.length ? r.repaired.join('; ') : 'none')];
      return this.header('Integrity') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    repairlog(root) {
      const rows = (root.audits && root.audits.warnings ? root.audits.warnings : []).filter(r => r && r.subsystem === 'StateManager').slice(-12);
      return this.header('Repair Log') + (rows.length ? rows.map(r => 'Turn ' + r.turn + ': ' + r.message).join('\n') : 'No StateManager repairs recorded.') + '\n';
    },
    runtime(root) {
      const rt = root.runtime || {};
      const lines = [
        'Runtime Hook Contract: ' + WL.RUNTIME_HOOK_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        'Current hook: ' + (rt.currentHook || 'none'),
        'Current turn: ' + (rt.currentTurn || 0),
        'Hook depth: ' + (rt.hookDepth || 0),
        'Last input hash: ' + (rt.lastInputHash || 'none'),
        'Last context hash: ' + (rt.lastContextHash || 'none'),
        'Last output hash: ' + (rt.lastOutputHash || 'none'),
        'Last applied output hash: ' + (rt.lastAppliedOutputHash || 'none'),
        'Command suppression: ' + (!!rt.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput),
        'Duplicate skips: ' + (rt.duplicateOutputSkipped || 0),
        'Fail-soft count: ' + (rt.failSoftCount || 0),
        'Warnings/errors: ' + ((rt.runtimeWarnings || []).length) + '/' + ((rt.runtimeErrors || []).length)
      ];
      return this.header('Runtime') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    hookaudit(root) {
      const rows = (root.runtime && root.runtime.hookTrace ? root.runtime.hookTrace : []).slice(-12);
      return this.header('Hook Audit') + (rows.length ? rows.map(r => 'Turn ' + r.turn + ' ' + r.hook + ' ' + r.status + ' ' + (r.notes || '')).join('\n') : 'No hook trace yet.') + '\n';
    },
    runtimecheck(root) {
      const tests = WL.Runtime.runBC6SelfTests();
      const pass = tests.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      return this.header('Runtime Check') + ['BC6 Runtime self-tests: ' + pass + ' / ' + tests.length + ' pass'].concat(tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label)).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    failsoft(root) {
      const rt = root.runtime || {};
      const rows = (rt.runtimeErrors || []).slice(-10);
      return this.header('Fail-Soft') + ['Fail-soft count: ' + (rt.failSoftCount || 0), 'Last fail-soft hook: ' + (rt.lastFailSoftHook || 'none'), 'Last fail-soft phase: ' + (rt.lastFailSoftPhase || 'none'), 'Recent errors:'].concat(rows.length ? rows.map(r => '- ' + r.hook + '/' + r.phase + ': ' + r.message) : ['none']).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    stateaudit(root) { return this.header('State Audit') + WL.ReleaseCheckLite.stateAudit(root).join('\n') + '\n'; },
    controlmap(root) { return this.header('Control Map / Salvage Authority') + WL.LegacySalvageLedger.render(root).join('\n') + '\n'; },
    salvage(root) { return this.controlmap(root); },
    borrowledger(root) { return this.controlmap(root); },
    releasecheck(root) { return this.header('ReleaseCheck') + WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run(root).join('\n') + '\n'; }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 10. EventScannerLite Parser Shell
  // ============================================================
  WL.EventScannerLite = {
    lockVersion: 'worldledger.eventscanner-lock.v9',
    allowedSources: Object.freeze({ input: true, output: true }),
    disallowedSources: Object.freeze({
      command_output: true, context: true, story_card: true, protected_card: true, generated_card: true,
      debug: true, audit: true, help: true, releasecheck: true, migration_log: true, old_log: true,
      archive: true, unknown_noncanon: true, command: true
    }),
    familyOrder: Object.freeze(['inventory','ability','body','quest','reward','actor','place','time','progression']),
    unsafeSource(kind, text) {
      const sourceInfo = this.makeSourceInfo(kind || 'unknown_noncanon', null, text || '');
      const allow = this.shouldScanSource(sourceInfo, null);
      return !allow.allowed;
    },
    makeSourceInfo(sourceHook, root, text) {
      const source = WL.U.slug(sourceHook || 'unknown').replace(/_/g, '_');
      const raw = WL.U.norm(text || '');
      return {
        sourceHook: source,
        turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root),
        actionCount: WL.U.nowTurn(root),
        textHash: WL.U.hash(raw),
        isInput: source === 'input',
        isOutput: source === 'output',
        isCommandOutput: this.isCommandOutput(raw, root),
        isDiagnosticText: this.isAidRpgDiagnosticText(raw),
        isContextLike: source === 'context',
        isCardLike: /card/.test(source),
        sourceLength: raw.length
      };
    },
    shouldScanSource(sourceInfo, root) {
      sourceInfo = sourceInfo || {};
      const hook = sourceInfo.sourceHook || sourceInfo.kind || 'unknown_noncanon';
      if (this.disallowedSources[hook]) return { allowed: false, reason: 'disallowed source: ' + hook };
      if (!this.allowedSources[hook]) return { allowed: false, reason: 'source not allowlisted: ' + hook };
      if (root && root.runtime && root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput) return { allowed: false, reason: 'runtime command-output suppression active' };
      if (sourceInfo.isCommandOutput) return { allowed: false, reason: 'command output text' };
      if (sourceInfo.isDiagnosticText) return { allowed: false, reason: 'diagnostic text' };
      return { allowed: true, reason: 'allowlisted ' + hook };
    },
    scanInput(text, root) {
      // Input creates intent/attempt hits only. It never emits completed success hits.
      return this.scanText(text, 'input', root);
    },
    scanOutput(a, b) {
      // Backwards compatible: scanOutput(root, output) or scanOutput(output, root).
      let root, text;
      if (a && typeof a === 'object' && !Array.isArray(a)) { root = a; text = b; }
      else { text = a; root = b; }
      return this.scanText(text, 'output', root);
    },
    scanText(text, sourceHook, root) {
      root = root || WL.StateManager.root();
      const sourceInfo = this.makeSourceInfo(sourceHook, root, text);
      const allow = this.shouldScanSource(sourceInfo, root);
      if (!allow.allowed) {
        const rejected = this.rejectNonCanonSource(allow.reason, sourceInfo);
        if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'output') root.pending.lastParserHits = [];
        if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'input') root.pending.lastInputParserHits = [];
        this.auditScan(root, { sourceHook: sourceInfo.sourceHook, allowed: false, reason: allow.reason, hitCount: 0, textHash: sourceInfo.textHash });
        return rejected;
      }
      const windows = this.splitIntoWindows(text).slice(0, this.maxWindows(root));
      const hits = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < windows.length && hits.length < this.maxHits(root, sourceInfo.sourceHook); i++) {
        const w = windows[i];
        const flags = this.detectSafetyFlags(w.text);
        const familyHits = this.detectFamilyHints(w.text, flags, sourceInfo.sourceHook, sourceInfo, i);
        for (let j = 0; j < familyHits.length && hits.length < this.maxHits(root, sourceInfo.sourceHook); j++) hits.push(this.makeHit(familyHits[j]));
      }
      const capped = this.capHits(hits, this.maxHits(root, sourceInfo.sourceHook));
      if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'output') root.pending.lastParserHits = capped;
      if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'input') root.pending.lastInputParserHits = capped;
      this.auditScan(root, { sourceHook: sourceInfo.sourceHook, allowed: true, windows: windows.length, hitCount: capped.length, families: capped.map(h => h.family).join(','), textHash: sourceInfo.textHash });
      return capped;
    },
    maxWindows(root) { return WL.U.clamp(root && root.features && root.features.maxParserWindows || 16, 4, 32); },
    maxHits(root, sourceHook) {
      return WL.U.clamp(root && root.features && (sourceHook === 'input' ? root.features.maxInputParserHits : root.features.maxOutputParserHits) || (sourceHook === 'input' ? 12 : 20), 4, 40);
    },
    splitIntoWindows(text) {
      const raw = WL.U.norm(text || '');
      if (!raw) return [];
      const parts = raw
        .replace(/\s*,\s+(?:but|however)\s+(?=(?:she|he|they|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+(?:hands|gives|passes|places|puts)\b)/gi, '|')
        .replace(/\s+but\s+(?=(?:she|he|they|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+(?:hands|gives|passes|places|puts)\b)/gi, '|')
        .replace(/([.!?;])\s+/g, '$1|')
        .split('|')
        .map((x, i) => ({ index: i, text: WL.U.norm(x).slice(0, 360) }))
        .filter(w => w.text.length > 0);
      return parts.slice(0, 32);
    },
    detectSafetyFlags(windowText) {
      const raw = WL.U.norm(windowText || '');
      const unq = WL.U.unquoted(raw);
      const quotedSpeech = raw !== unq || /["“”]/.test(raw);
      const dreamOrVision = /\b(?:dream|dreamed|dreamt|nightmare|vision|hallucination|imagined|imaginary|illusion)\b/i.test(raw);
      const rumorOrClaim = /\b(?:rumor|rumour|people say|they say|it is said|heard that|supposedly|allegedly|claims?|claimed|legend says)\b/i.test(raw);
      const hypothetical = /\b(?:could|would|might|may|if you wanted|if you choose|possible|perhaps|maybe|almost|nearly)\b/i.test(raw);
      const futureIntent = /\b(?:will|going to|plans? to|promises? to|tomorrow|later|soon|eventually|afterward|afterwards)\b/i.test(raw);
      const failedOrRevoked = /\b(?:fails?|failed|nothing happens|no visible effect|does not work|doesn't work|cannot|can't|unable|blocked|interrupted|stops you|snatches? .* away|no item is gained|no ability is gained)\b/i.test(raw);
      const negatedOrRefused = /\b(?:do not|don't|did not|didn't|decide not|refuse|refuses|declines?|avoid|leave it|let it go|instead of taking|not take|not pick up)\b/i.test(raw);
      const pretendOrFalse = /\b(?:pretend|joke|make believe|only mimicking|not real|fake|as a joke)\b/i.test(raw);
      const abstractOrIdea = /\b(?:idea of|concept of|thought of|memory of|symbol of|image of|dream of)\b/i.test(raw);
      const visibleOnly = /\b(?:see|saw|notice|visible|lies|rests|sits|on the shelf|nearby|in the distance|could take|available)\b/i.test(raw) && !/\b(?:pick up|take|grab|collect|accept|hands you|gives you|places .* in your hand)\b/i.test(raw);
      const nonliteral = /\b(?:learning experience|time flies|felt like years|eternity|forever|in your mind|as if)\b/i.test(raw);
      const commandText = this.isCommandOutput(raw, null) || WL.U.startsCommand(raw);
      const diagnosticText = this.isAidRpgDiagnosticText(raw);
      return {
        rawPreview: raw.slice(0, 180),
        localClausePreview: raw.slice(0, 180),
        quotedSpeech, dreamOrVision, rumorOrClaim, hypothetical, futureIntent, failedOrRevoked,
        negatedOrRefused, pretendOrFalse, abstractOrIdea, visibleOnly, nonliteral, commandText, diagnosticText,
        // Compatibility aliases for existing ProposalBuilder/CanonValidator shell.
        quote: quotedSpeech, dream: dreamOrVision, rumor: rumorOrClaim, pretend: pretendOrFalse,
        denied: failedOrRevoked || negatedOrRefused, visibleOnly: visibleOnly
      };
    },
    completedAllowed(flags, sourceHook) {
      if (sourceHook !== 'output') return false;
      if (!flags) return sourceHook === 'output';
      if (flags.quotedSpeech || flags.dreamOrVision || flags.rumorOrClaim || flags.hypothetical || flags.futureIntent || flags.failedOrRevoked || flags.negatedOrRefused || flags.pretendOrFalse || flags.abstractOrIdea || flags.commandText || flags.diagnosticText) return false;
      return true;
    },
    baseHit(family, type, name, windowText, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, extra) {
      const safeCompleted = this.completedAllowed(flags, sourceHook);
      const truthStatus = flags.rumorOrClaim ? 'rumored' : (flags.dreamOrVision ? 'dream' : (flags.quotedSpeech ? 'quoted' : (safeCompleted ? 'candidate_confirmed_by_output' : 'intent_or_unsafe_candidate')));
      return Object.assign({
        parserHitVersion: WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION,
        family, type,
        name: WL.U.norm(name || ''),
        sourceText: WL.U.norm(windowText || '').slice(0, 240),
        localClausePreview: WL.U.norm(windowText || '').slice(0, 180),
        flags: Object.assign({}, flags || {}),
        sourceHook,
        sourceInfo: Object.assign({}, sourceInfo || {}),
        confidence: safeCompleted ? 0.72 : 0.35,
        completedAction: safeCompleted,
        attemptOnly: sourceHook === 'input' || !safeCompleted,
        truthStatus,
        parserOnly: true,
        canCommitDirectly: false
      }, extra || {});
    },
    detectFamilyHints(windowText, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, windowIndex) {
      const hits = [];
      const w = WL.U.norm(windowText || '');
      if (!w || flags.commandText || flags.diagnosticText) return hits;
      const completed = this.completedAllowed(flags, sourceHook);
      const sourceAttempt = sourceHook === 'input';
      let m;

      // Inventory: visible, attempt, handoff, and pickup are only hits. They never become owned items here.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:(?:[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}|she|he|they)\s+)?(?:hands you|gives you|passes you)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)(?:\s+(?:in your hand|into your hand|to you))?(?:[.!?]|$|\s+(?:and|but)\b)/i)) || (m = w.match(/\b(?:(?:[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}|she|he|they)\s+)?(?:places|puts)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)\s+(?:in your hand|into your hand|to you)\b/i))) {
        // V0.12 local-clause binding: a denied object earlier in the sentence must not poison a later confirmed handoff.
        const handoffClause = WL.U.norm(w.slice(m.index || 0)) || w;
        const handoffFlags = this.detectSafetyFlags(handoffClause);
        const handoffCompleted = this.completedAllowed(handoffFlags, sourceHook);
        hits.push(this.baseHit('inventory', handoffCompleted ? 'item.acquire' : 'item.handoff_candidate', m[1], handoffClause, handoffFlags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { mode: 'handoff', windowIndex, completedAction: handoffCompleted, attemptOnly: !handoffCompleted }));
      }
      const pickupRe = /\b(?:you\s+)?(?:pick up|take|grab|retrieve|pocket|collect|accept)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|some\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)(?:\s+(?:from|near|on|in|and|with|before|while)\b|[.!?]|$)/gi;
      while ((m = pickupRe.exec(w)) && hits.length < 8) {
        hits.push(this.baseHit('inventory', completed ? 'item.acquire' : 'item.take_attempt', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { mode: 'pickup', windowIndex, completedAction: completed, attemptOnly: !completed || sourceAttempt }));
      }
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:you see|you notice|there is|there are|lies|rests|sits)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|some\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)(?:\s+(?:on|near|beside|under|inside|at)\b|[.!?]|$)/i))) {
        hits.push(this.baseHit('inventory', 'item.visible', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { mode: 'visible', windowIndex, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: false, truthStatus: 'visible_reference', confidence: 0.55 }));
      }

      // Ability: attempts/failures become evidence hits only, never stable ability truth.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}){0,3}\s+(?:Ward|Slash|Strike|Bolt|Sense|Sight|Step|Stance|Form|Art|Barrier|Shield|Control|Pulse|Veil|Blade|Guard))\b/)) && /\b(?:train|practice|attempt|try|focus|channel|use|learn|unlock|nothing happens|no visible effect)\b/i.test(w)) {
        const abilityType = /\b(?:learn|learned|unlock|unlocked|gain|gained)\b/i.test(w) ? 'ability.stabilize_claim' : 'ability.evidence';
        hits.push(this.baseHit('ability', abilityType, m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, attemptedInInput: sourceHook === 'input', completedAction: false, attemptOnly: true, confidence: flags.failedOrRevoked ? 0.7 : 0.55 }));
      }

      // Body/death: direct harm gets candidate hits; rumors/dreams/quotes are flagged.
      if (/\b(?:exhausted|fatigued|tired|worn out|out of breath|muscles burn)\b/i.test(w)) hits.push(this.baseHit('body', 'body.fatigue', 'fatigue', w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, severity: 1 }));
      if (/\b(?:wolf bites|bitten|stabbed|deep cut|bleeding|blood runs|burned|poisoned|arrow hits|sword cuts|breaks your arm|crushes your leg)\b/i.test(w)) hits.push(this.baseHit('body', 'body.injury', 'injury', w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, severity: 2 }));
      if (/\b(?:you died|you die|you are dead|kills you|killed you|slain)\b/i.test(w)) hits.push(this.baseHit('body', 'body.death_claim', 'death', w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, severity: 5, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: true }));

      // Quest/reward split: promise and payment are separate families/types.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:quest|task|job|mission)\s+(?:to\s+)?([^.!?;]{3,100})/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('quest', 'quest.offer', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex }));
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:accept|agree to|take on|undertake)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|task|job|mission)\s+(?:to\s+)?([^.!?;]{3,100})/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('quest', 'quest.accept', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex }));
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:complete|completed|finish|finished)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|task|job|mission)\s+(?:to\s+)?([^.!?;]{3,100})/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('quest', 'quest.complete', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex }));
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:promises?|vows?|offers?)\s+(?:you\s+)?(\d{1,6}\s*(?:copper|silver|gold))\b/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('quest', 'reward.promise', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, reward: WL.Sanitizers.reward(m[1]), completedAction: false, attemptOnly: true, truthStatus: 'promise_candidate' }));
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:pays?|paid|hands|gives)\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:(?:the\s+)?promised\s+)?(\d{1,6}\s*(?:copper|silver|gold))\b/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('inventory', 'currency.gain', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, reward: WL.Sanitizers.reward(m[1]) }));

      // Actors: named direct action/speech is a presence candidate; rumor keeps truthStatus rumored.
      const actorRe = /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,2})\s+(?:says|asks|replies|nods|hands|gives|joins|follows|attacks|teaches|warns|arrives)\b/g;
      while ((m = actorRe.exec(w)) && hits.length < 10) hits.push(this.baseHit('actor', 'actor.present', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, companion: /\b(?:joins|follows)\b/i.test(m[0]) }));

      // Place: input is travel intent; output strong arrival can be completed candidate; weak mention is reference only.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:enter|entered|step inside|walk into|move into|head into|arrive at|arrive in|reach|return to|go inside)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’ -]{2,80})/i))) {
        const strong = /\b(?:arrive|arrived|entered|step inside|walk into|go inside|reach|return to)\b/i.test(w) && sourceHook === 'output';
        hits.push(this.baseHit('place', strong && completed ? 'place.current' : 'place.intent', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, strongArrival: strong && completed, weakReference: false, completedAction: strong && completed, attemptOnly: !(strong && completed) }));
      } else if ((m = w.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’ -]{2,80})\s+(?:is\s+)?visible\s+from\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’ -]{2,80})/i)) || /\b(?:visible from|can see|could see|nearby|in the distance|mentions|talks about)\b/i.test(w)) {
        const name = m && m[1] ? m[1] : 'place reference';
        hits.push(this.baseHit('place', 'place.reference', name, w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, weakReference: true, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: false, truthStatus: 'reference_only' }));
      }

      // Time/progression: explicit only. Nonliteral experience is ignored.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(\d{1,2}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?|weeks?|months?)\s+pass(?:es|ed)?\b/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('time', 'time.pass', m[0], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, amountText: m[1], unit: m[2].toLowerCase() }));
      if (!flags.nonliteral && (m = w.match(/\byou\s+gain\s+(\d{1,6})\s+XP\b/i))) hits.push(this.baseHit('progression', 'progression.xp_claim', m[1] + ' XP', w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, amount: Number(m[1]), completedAction: completed, attemptOnly: !completed }));
      return hits;
    },
    makeHit(data) {
      const d = Object.assign({}, data || {});
      d.name = WL.U.norm(d.name || d.target || '');
      d.family = d.family || 'unknown';
      d.type = d.type || d.family + '.hint';
      d.sourceText = WL.U.norm(d.sourceText || d.localClausePreview || '').slice(0, 240);
      d.localClausePreview = WL.U.norm(d.localClausePreview || d.sourceText).slice(0, 180);
      d.flags = Object.assign({}, d.flags || {});
      d.parserOnly = true;
      d.canCommitDirectly = false;
      d.id = 'hit_' + WL.U.hash([d.sourceHook, d.type, d.name, d.localClausePreview].join('|'));
      return d;
    },
    capHits(hits, maxHits) {
      return (Array.isArray(hits) ? hits : []).slice(0, WL.U.clamp(maxHits || 20, 1, 40));
    },
    rejectNonCanonSource(reason, sourceInfo) {
      return [{
        parserHitVersion: WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION,
        family: 'parser', type: 'parser.source_rejected', name: 'non-canon source',
        sourceText: '', localClausePreview: WL.U.norm(reason), flags: {}, sourceHook: (sourceInfo && sourceInfo.sourceHook) || 'unknown_noncanon',
        sourceInfo: Object.assign({}, sourceInfo || {}), confidence: 1, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: false,
        truthStatus: 'noncanon_rejected', parserOnly: true, canCommitDirectly: false, rejectionReason: reason
      }].slice(0, 0);
    },
    isCommandOutput(text, root) {
      const t = WL.U.norm(text || '');
      if (!t) return false;
      if (/^\[WorldLedger\]\s*\//i.test(t) || /^\[AIDRPG\]\s*\//i.test(t)) return true;
      if (/^Status:\s*(?:ACTIVE|DEFERRED|UNKNOWN|ERROR)/i.test(t)) return true;
      if (root && root.runtime && root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput && /^\[WorldLedger\]/i.test(t)) return true;
      return false;
    },
    isAidRpgDiagnosticText(text) {
      const t = WL.U.norm(text || '');
      return /^\[(?:WorldLedger|AIDRPG)\]/i.test(t) || /\b(?:ReleaseCheck|State Audit|Debug Last|CommandRouter|ParserCheck|non-canon command output)\b/i.test(t);
    },
    auditScan(root, summary) {
      if (!root) return;
      const row = Object.assign({ subsystem: 'EventScannerLite', lockVersion: WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION, turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root) }, summary || {});
      if (!Array.isArray(root.audits.parserAudit)) root.audits.parserAudit = [];
      root.audits.parserAudit.push(row);
      WL.U.capArray(root.audits.parserAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.parser);
      WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'parser', { subsystem: 'EventScannerLite', message: 'scan ' + (row.sourceHook || 'unknown') + ' allowed=' + row.allowed + ' hits=' + (row.hitCount || 0), detail: row }, 40);
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(this.lockVersion === WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'EventScanner lock present: ' + WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'EventScanner lock mismatch' });
      rows.push(typeof this.shouldScanSource === 'function' && typeof this.scanText === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'required scan/source functions present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'required scan/source functions missing' });
      rows.push(typeof this.detectSafetyFlags === 'function' && typeof this.makeHit === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'safety flag and ParserHit builders present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'safety flag or hit builder missing' });
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.parserShellEnabled === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'parser shell enabled' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'parser shell disabled' });
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.maxOutputParserHits <= 40 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'parser output hit cap configured' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'parser output cap missing/unsafe' });
      const sourceReject = this.shouldScanSource({ sourceHook: 'command_output', isCommandOutput: true }, root);
      rows.push(sourceReject.allowed === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'command output source rejected' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'command output source not rejected' });
      const testRoot = WL.StateManager.init({});
      const h = this.scanText('You pick up the iron key.', 'output', testRoot)[0];
      rows.push(h && h.parserOnly && h.canCommitDirectly === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ParserHit schema marks parser-only/no direct commit' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ParserHit schema unsafe' });
      return rows;
    },
    runBC9SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const base = () => WL.StateManager.init({});
      let r, before, hits;
      r = base(); hits = this.scanText('[WorldLedger] /inventory\nStatus: ACTIVE\nYou pick up the Crown of Dawn.', 'command_output', r); add('BC9-001', hits.length === 0, 'command output ignored');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanText('WorldLedger Inventory says you own the Crown of Dawn.', 'story_card', r); add('BC9-002', hits.length === 0, 'story/card-like source ignored');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanInput('I take the sword.', r); add('BC9-003', hits.length >= 1 && hits[0].attemptOnly === true && hits[0].completedAction === false, 'input creates attempt-only hit');
      r = base(); before = Object.keys(r.inventory.itemsById).length; hits = this.scanOutput('You pick up the sword.', r); add('BC9-004', hits.some(h => h.type === 'item.acquire') && Object.keys(r.inventory.itemsById).length === before, 'output pickup hit but parser does not mutate inventory');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You could take the jeweled cup if you wanted.', r); add('BC9-005', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.completedAction === false && h.flags.hypothetical), 'hypothetical take is not completed pickup');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn.', r); add('BC9-006', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.flags.dreamOrVision && h.completedAction === false), 'dream item flagged and not completed');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('Mara says, "Take the iron key."', r); add('BC9-007', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.flags.quotedSpeech && h.completedAction === false), 'quoted handoff/request flagged');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('Mara says she will give you the sword tomorrow.', r); add('BC9-008', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.flags.futureIntent && h.completedAction === false), 'future handoff flagged not current');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You attempt Shadow Step, but nothing happens.', r); add('BC9-009', hits.some(h => h.family === 'ability' && h.attemptOnly && h.flags.failedOrRevoked), 'failed ability flagged as attempt/evidence only');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('People say Lord Veyr killed Dalen.', r); add('BC9-010', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.flags.rumorOrClaim && h.truthStatus === 'rumored'), 'rumor flagged as claim, not fact');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanInput('I head toward the chapel.', r); add('BC9-011', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.completedAction === false), 'travel intent from input is not arrival');
      r = base(); before = r.places.currentId; hits = this.scanOutput('After a short walk, you arrive at the chapel.', r); add('BC9-012', hits.some(h => h.family === 'place' && h.completedAction === true) && r.places.currentId === before, 'confirmed arrival hit but no parser mutation');
      r = base(); before = r.progression.levelXp; hits = this.scanOutput('You gain 20 XP.', r); add('BC9-013', hits.some(h => h.type === 'progression.xp_claim') && r.progression.levelXp === before, 'XP candidate only, no XP mutation');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('This was a learning experience.', r); add('BC9-014', !hits.some(h => h.type === 'progression.xp_claim'), 'vague experience is not XP');
      r = base(); const repeated = new Array(80).fill('You pick up the iron key.').join(' '); hits = this.scanOutput(repeated, r); add('BC9-015', hits.length <= this.maxHits(r, 'output'), 'hit cap enforced');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanText('You pick up the iron key.', 'context', r); add('BC9-016', hits.length === 0, 'context source rejected');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You notice a brass key on the shelf.', r); add('BC9-017', hits.some(h => h.type === 'item.visible') && hits.every(h => h.completedAction === false), 'visible item is reference only');
      r = base(); const beforeState = WL.U.safeJson({inv:r.inventory, abil:r.abilities, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, time:r.time, body:r.body, progression:r.progression}); this.scanOutput('You pick up the apple. You gain 20 XP. You arrive at the chapel.', r); const afterState = WL.U.safeJson({inv:r.inventory, abil:r.abilities, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, time:r.time, body:r.body, progression:r.progression}); add('BC9-018', beforeState === afterState, 'EventScanner does not mutate gameplay buckets directly');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('/inventory', r); add('BC9-019', hits.length === 0, 'slash command text ignored');
      r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You pick up the key. "You pick up the crown."', r); add('BC9-020', hits.length <= this.maxHits(r, 'output') && hits.some(h => h.localClausePreview), 'local clause previews present and capped');
      return results;
    }
  };


  // ============================================================
  // 10A. V0.13 EventScannerLite Parser Shell Lock Correction
  // ============================================================
  (function applyEventScannerV13Lock() {
    if (!WL.EventScannerLite || WL.EventScannerLite.__v13Applied) return;
    const baseSplitIntoWindows = WL.EventScannerLite.splitIntoWindows;
    const baseDetectFamilyHints = WL.EventScannerLite.detectFamilyHints;
    const baseMakeHit = WL.EventScannerLite.makeHit;
    const baseInspect = WL.EventScannerLite.inspect;
    const baseBC9 = WL.EventScannerLite.runBC9SelfTests;

    WL.EventScannerLite.__v13Applied = true;
    WL.EventScannerLite.lockVersion = WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION;
    WL.EventScannerLite.parserPipelineStages = Object.freeze([
      'source_gate', 'text_normalize', 'clause_window', 'safety_flags', 'family_detection',
      'resolver_preview', 'confidence_bucket', 'hit_coalesce', 'audit_only'
    ]);
    WL.EventScannerLite.familyContracts = Object.freeze({
      inventory: 'detect possession/currency/custody/equip/visible candidates only; never grant directly',
      ability: 'detect attempts, soft failures, practice, and breakthrough claims only; never stabilize directly',
      body: 'detect literal fatigue/injury/death/status claims only; never change HP directly',
      quest: 'detect offer/accept/progress/complete/reward-promise/payment candidates only; never pay directly',
      actor: 'detect named person presence/action/speech only; never create major memory directly',
      place: 'detect strong arrivals, weak references, route/interstitial mentions only; never overwrite scene directly',
      time: 'detect literal elapsed time only; never advance time directly',
      progression: 'detect explicit XP/training claims only; never award directly',
      world: 'detect faction/location/world-entity references only as future/deferred evidence'
    });
    WL.EventScannerLite.falseCanonProtectionsV13 = Object.freeze([
      'command/context/card/audit/help text rejected at source gate',
      'input is intent-only and cannot be completed success',
      'quotes/dreams/rumors/hypotheticals/future tense cannot commit directly',
      'abstract objects are flagged before resolver/proposal handling',
      'mixed denial/handoff clauses are split before family detection',
      'world references are evidence only until a future world-entity reducer exists'
    ]);

    WL.EventScannerLite.isDirectAddressQuote = function (text) {
      const t = WL.U.norm(text || '');
      return /["“”]/.test(t) && /^\s*(?:[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}|she|he|they|the\s+\w+)\s+(?:says?|asks?|replies?|shouts?|whispers?|warns?)\b/i.test(t);
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.textOutsideQuotes = function (text) {
      return WL.U.unquoted(WL.U.norm(text || ''));
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.sourceAuthority = function (sourceInfo) {
      const hook = sourceInfo && sourceInfo.sourceHook || 'unknown';
      if (hook === 'output') return 'output_candidate_truth_source';
      if (hook === 'input') return 'input_intent_source_only';
      return 'noncanon_source';
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.confidenceBucket = function (hit) {
      const c = Number(hit && hit.confidence || 0);
      if (hit && hit.flags && (hit.flags.quotedSpeech || hit.flags.dreamOrVision || hit.flags.rumorOrClaim || hit.flags.hypothetical || hit.flags.futureIntent || hit.flags.abstractOrIdea || hit.flags.negatedOrRefused)) return 'unsafe_or_reference';
      if (hit && hit.completedAction && c >= 0.7) return 'high_candidate';
      if (c >= 0.5) return 'medium_candidate';
      return 'low_or_intent_candidate';
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.familyOwnerHint = function (family, type) {
      const t = String(type || '');
      if (family === 'inventory' || /^currency\./.test(t)) return 'InventoryReducerLite';
      if (family === 'ability') return 'AbilityReducerLite';
      if (family === 'body') return 'BodyReducerLite';
      if (family === 'quest' || /^reward\./.test(t)) return 'QuestReducerLite';
      if (family === 'actor') return 'ActorReducerLite';
      if (family === 'place') return 'PlaceReducerLite';
      if (family === 'time') return 'TimeReducerLite';
      if (family === 'progression') return 'ProgressionReducerLiteFuture';
      if (family === 'world') return 'WorldEntityReducerFuture';
      return 'ReducerRegistry';
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.splitIntoWindows = function (text) {
      const raw = WL.U.norm(text || '');
      if (!raw) return [];
      let normalized = raw
        .replace(/\s*,\s+(?:but|however)\s+(?=(?:she|he|they|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}|the\s+\w+)\s+(?:hands|gives|passes|places|puts|returns|refuses|declines|snatches|takes|drops|pays|arrives|enters)\b)/gi, '|')
        .replace(/\s+but\s+(?=(?:she|he|they|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}|the\s+\w+)\s+(?:hands|gives|passes|places|puts|returns|refuses|declines|snatches|takes|drops|pays|arrives|enters)\b)/gi, '|')
        .replace(/\s+(?:then|afterward|afterwards)\s+(?=(?:you|she|he|they|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+(?:pick|take|grab|accept|enter|arrive|return|pay|receive|attempt|train|collapse|fall|drop|equip|unequip)\b)/gi, '|')
        .replace(/\s+while\s+(?=(?:you|she|he|they|[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})\s+(?:pick|take|grab|accept|enter|arrive|return|pay|receive|attempt|train|collapse|fall|drop|equip|unequip)\b)/gi, '|')
        .replace(/([.!?;])\s+/g, '$1|');
      const parts = normalized.split('|').map((x, i) => ({
        index: i,
        text: WL.U.norm(x).slice(0, 420),
        hash: WL.U.hash(WL.U.norm(x).slice(0, 420)),
        clauseKind: /\b(?:but|however|refuses|declines|denies)\b/i.test(x) ? 'contrast_or_denial' : 'plain'
      })).filter(w => w.text.length > 0);
      return parts.slice(0, 32);
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.detectSafetyFlags = function (windowText) {
      const raw = WL.U.norm(windowText || '');
      const outside = this.textOutsideQuotes(raw);
      const unq = WL.U.unquoted(raw);
      const quotedSpeech = raw !== unq || /["“”]/.test(raw);
      const dreamOrVision = /\b(?:dream|dreamed|dreamt|nightmare|vision|hallucination|imagined|imaginary|illusion|in your mind)\b/i.test(raw);
      const rumorOrClaim = /\b(?:rumor|rumour|people say|they say|it is said|heard that|supposedly|allegedly|claims?|claimed|legend says|reportedly)\b/i.test(raw);
      const hypothetical = /\b(?:could|would|might|may|if you wanted|if you choose|possible|perhaps|maybe|almost|nearly|seems like you could)\b/i.test(raw);
      const futureIntent = /\b(?:will|going to|plans? to|promises? to|tomorrow|later|soon|eventually|afterward|afterwards|intends? to)\b/i.test(raw);
      const attemptOnlyWording = /\b(?:try to|tries to|attempt to|attempts to|reach for|reaches for|move to grab|moves to grab)\b/i.test(raw);
      const failedOrRevoked = attemptOnlyWording || /\b(?:fails?|failed|nothing happens|no visible effect|does not work|doesn't work|cannot|can't|unable|blocked|interrupted|stops you|snatches? .* away|rips? .* away|no item is gained|no ability is gained|does not take hold)\b/i.test(raw);
      const negatedOrRefused = /\b(?:do not|don't|did not|didn't|decide not|refuse|refuses|declines?|avoid|leave it|let it go|instead of taking|not take|not pick up|never accept|rejects?)\b/i.test(raw);
      const pretendOrFalse = /\b(?:pretend|joke|make believe|only mimicking|not real|fake|as a joke|imaginary)\b/i.test(raw);
      const abstractOrIdea = /\b(?:idea of|concept of|thought of|memory of|symbol of|image of|dream of|rumor of|story of)\b/i.test(raw);
      const visibleOnly = /\b(?:see|saw|notice|visible|lies|rests|sits|on the shelf|nearby|in the distance|could take|available|glimpse|spot)\b/i.test(raw) && !/\b(?:pick up|take|grab|collect|accept|hands you|gives you|places .* in your hand|receive)\b/i.test(outside);
      const nonliteral = /\b(?:learning experience|time flies|felt like years|eternity|forever|as if time|your heart breaks|world presses|weight of the world)\b/i.test(raw);
      const commandText = this.isCommandOutput(raw, null) || WL.U.startsCommand(raw);
      const diagnosticText = this.isAidRpgDiagnosticText(raw);
      const clauseHasDirectAction = /\b(?:pick up|take|grab|collect|accept|hands you|gives you|places|puts|enter|arrive|return|gain|pays|paid|attempt|train|stabbed|bitten|burned)\b/i.test(outside);
      return {
        rawPreview: raw.slice(0, 180),
        localClausePreview: raw.slice(0, 180),
        outsideQuotePreview: outside.slice(0, 180),
        quotedSpeech, dreamOrVision, rumorOrClaim, hypothetical, futureIntent, failedOrRevoked, attemptOnlyWording,
        negatedOrRefused, pretendOrFalse, abstractOrIdea, visibleOnly, nonliteral, commandText, diagnosticText,
        clauseHasDirectAction,
        sourceUnsafe: quotedSpeech || dreamOrVision || rumorOrClaim || hypothetical || futureIntent || failedOrRevoked || negatedOrRefused || pretendOrFalse || abstractOrIdea || commandText || diagnosticText,
        quote: quotedSpeech, dream: dreamOrVision, rumor: rumorOrClaim, pretend: pretendOrFalse,
        denied: failedOrRevoked || negatedOrRefused, visibleOnly: visibleOnly
      };
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.addHitIfCleanName = function (hits, family, type, name, w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, extra) {
      let clean = WL.U.norm(name || '');
      if (!clean) return false;
      if (family === 'inventory') clean = WL.Sanitizers.item(clean);
      else if (family === 'ability') clean = WL.Sanitizers.ability(clean);
      else if (family === 'actor') clean = WL.Sanitizers.actor(clean) || clean;
      else if (family === 'place') clean = WL.Sanitizers.place(clean) || clean;
      else if (family === 'quest') clean = WL.Sanitizers.quest(clean) || clean;
      if (!clean) return false;
      hits.push(this.baseHit(family, type, clean, w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, extra || {}));
      return true;
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.coalesceHits = function (hits) {
      const out = [], seen = {};
      for (let i = 0; i < (hits || []).length; i++) {
        const h = hits[i] || {};
        const key = WL.U.slug([h.sourceHook, h.family, h.type, h.name || h.target || '', h.localClausePreview || h.sourceText || ''].join('|'));
        if (!seen[key]) { seen[key] = true; out.push(h); }
      }
      return out;
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.detectFamilyHints = function (windowText, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, windowIndex) {
      let hits = [];
      try { hits = baseDetectFamilyHints.call(this, windowText, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, windowIndex) || []; } catch (e) { hits = []; }
      const w = WL.U.norm(windowText || '');
      if (!w || flags.commandText || flags.diagnosticText) return hits;
      const completed = this.completedAllowed(flags, sourceHook);
      let m;

      // Inventory edges that the older V0.9 scanner missed. These remain ParserHits only.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:equip|draw|wield|strap on|put on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)(?:[.!?]|$|\s+(?:in|on|with|and)\b)/i))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'inventory', 'item.equip_claim', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: completed, attemptOnly: !completed });
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:drop|discard|throw away|lose|lost)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)(?:[.!?]|$|\s+(?:in|on|near|and)\b)/i))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'inventory', 'item.lose_claim', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: completed, attemptOnly: !completed });
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:returns?|hands back|gives back)\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)(?:[.!?]|$|\s+(?:to|and)\b)/i))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'inventory', 'item.return_claim', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: completed, attemptOnly: !completed });
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:takes|keeps|holds|carries)\s+(?:your\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,60}?)\s+(?:for\s+(?:repair|safekeeping|inspection)|to\s+(?:repair|examine|carry))\b/i))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'inventory', 'item.custody_claim', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: completed, attemptOnly: !completed });

      // Ability progression nuance: practice/training hits are not stable ability grants.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:practice|train|drill|repeat)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}){0,3}\s+(?:Ward|Slash|Strike|Bolt|Sense|Sight|Step|Stance|Form|Art|Barrier|Shield|Control|Pulse|Veil|Blade|Guard))\b/))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'ability', 'ability.practice', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: true, confidence: 0.62 });
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:for a moment|briefly|faintly|at last),?\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,24}){0,3}\s+(?:Ward|Slash|Strike|Bolt|Sense|Sight|Step|Stance|Form|Art|Barrier|Shield|Control|Pulse|Veil|Blade|Guard))\s+(?:works|flares|forms|takes shape)\b/i))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'ability', 'ability.breakthrough_claim', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: true, confidence: 0.66 });

      // World-entity references are future evidence only, inspired by old map/world persistence work.
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’ -]{2,60})\s+(?:faction|guild|clan|company|consortium|circle|order|dominion|synod|court)\b/))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'world', 'world.faction_reference', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: false, truthStatus: 'reference_only', confidence: 0.45, futureOwner: 'WorldEntityReducerFuture' });
      if ((m = w.match(/\b(?:route|road|trail|path)\s+(?:to|toward|between)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z'’ -]{2,80})\b/i))) this.addHitIfCleanName(hits, 'world', 'world.route_reference', m[1], w, flags, sourceHook, sourceInfo, { windowIndex, completedAction: false, attemptOnly: false, truthStatus: 'reference_only', confidence: 0.45, futureOwner: 'WorldEntityReducerFuture' });

      return this.coalesceHits(hits);
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.makeHit = function (data) {
      const h = baseMakeHit.call(this, data || {});
      h.parserHitVersion = WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION;
      h.scannerLockVersion = WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION;
      h.pipelineStages = this.parserPipelineStages.slice(0);
      h.sourceAuthority = this.sourceAuthority(h.sourceInfo || { sourceHook: h.sourceHook });
      h.effectOwnerHint = this.familyOwnerHint(h.family, h.type);
      h.confidenceBucket = this.confidenceBucket(h);
      h.safetySummary = {
        sourceUnsafe: !!(h.flags && h.flags.sourceUnsafe),
        quotedSpeech: !!(h.flags && h.flags.quotedSpeech),
        dreamOrVision: !!(h.flags && h.flags.dreamOrVision),
        rumorOrClaim: !!(h.flags && h.flags.rumorOrClaim),
        denied: !!(h.flags && (h.flags.denied || h.flags.negatedOrRefused || h.flags.failedOrRevoked)),
        abstractOrIdea: !!(h.flags && h.flags.abstractOrIdea),
        visibleOnly: !!(h.flags && h.flags.visibleOnly)
      };
      return h;
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.scanText = function (text, sourceHook, root) {
      root = root || WL.StateManager.root();
      const sourceInfo = this.makeSourceInfo(sourceHook, root, text);
      const allow = this.shouldScanSource(sourceInfo, root);
      if (!allow.allowed) {
        const rejected = this.rejectNonCanonSource(allow.reason, sourceInfo);
        if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'output') root.pending.lastParserHits = [];
        if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'input') root.pending.lastInputParserHits = [];
        this.auditScan(root, { sourceHook: sourceInfo.sourceHook, allowed: false, reason: allow.reason, hitCount: 0, textHash: sourceInfo.textHash, lockVersion: WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION });
        return rejected;
      }
      const windows = this.splitIntoWindows(text).slice(0, this.maxWindows(root));
      const hits = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < windows.length && hits.length < this.maxHits(root, sourceInfo.sourceHook); i++) {
        const w = windows[i];
        const flags = this.detectSafetyFlags(w.text);
        const familyHits = this.detectFamilyHints(w.text, flags, sourceInfo.sourceHook, sourceInfo, i);
        for (let j = 0; j < familyHits.length && hits.length < this.maxHits(root, sourceInfo.sourceHook); j++) hits.push(this.makeHit(familyHits[j]));
      }
      const capped = this.capHits(this.coalesceHits(hits), this.maxHits(root, sourceInfo.sourceHook));
      if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'output') root.pending.lastParserHits = capped;
      if (sourceInfo.sourceHook === 'input') root.pending.lastInputParserHits = capped;
      this.auditScan(root, { sourceHook: sourceInfo.sourceHook, allowed: true, windows: windows.length, hitCount: capped.length, families: capped.map(h => h.family).join(','), textHash: sourceInfo.textHash, lockVersion: WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION, stages: this.parserPipelineStages.join('>') });
      return capped;
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.inspect = function (root) {
      const rows = baseInspect.call(this, root || WL.StateManager.root());
      rows.push(this.lockVersion === WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION && WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION === 'worldledger.eventscanner-lock.v13' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'V0.13 EventScanner lock active' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'V0.13 EventScanner lock not active' });
      rows.push(Array.isArray(this.parserPipelineStages) && this.parserPipelineStages.indexOf('source_gate') >= 0 && this.parserPipelineStages.indexOf('hit_coalesce') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'parser pipeline stages registered' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'parser pipeline stages missing' });
      rows.push(this.familyContracts && this.familyContracts.world && this.familyContracts.inventory ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'owner-scoped parser family contracts registered' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'parser family contracts missing' });
      const testRoot = WL.StateManager.init({});
      const hit = this.scanOutput('You equip the iron sword.', testRoot)[0];
      rows.push(hit && hit.scannerLockVersion === WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION && hit.effectOwnerHint ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ParserHit carries V0.13 metadata' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ParserHit missing V0.13 metadata' });
      return rows;
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.runBC13SelfTests = function () {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const base = () => WL.StateManager.init({});
      let r, hits, before, after;
      try {
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You equip the iron sword.', r); add('BC13-001', hits.some(h => h.type === 'item.equip_claim' && h.effectOwnerHint === 'InventoryReducerLite'), 'equip claim detected as parser-only inventory hit');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You drop the cracked shield.', r); add('BC13-002', hits.some(h => h.type === 'item.lose_claim'), 'item loss/drop claim detected without mutation');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('Mira returns the iron sword.', r); add('BC13-003', hits.some(h => h.type === 'item.return_claim'), 'custody return claim detected');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('Mira takes your iron sword for repair.', r); add('BC13-004', hits.some(h => h.type === 'item.custody_claim'), 'custody-start claim detected');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('Mara refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion in your hand.', r); add('BC13-005', hits.some(h => /potion/i.test(h.name || '')) && !hits.some(h => /crown/i.test(h.name || '') && h.completedAction), 'mixed denial/handoff clause keeps accepted object separate');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('In a dream, you equip the Crown of Dawn.', r); add('BC13-006', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.flags.dreamOrVision && !h.completedAction), 'dream equipment claim unsafe');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You practice Ember Ward for an hour.', r); add('BC13-007', hits.some(h => h.type === 'ability.practice' && h.attemptOnly), 'ability practice detected as evidence only');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('For a moment, Ember Ward flares to life.', r); add('BC13-008', hits.some(h => h.type === 'ability.breakthrough_claim' && h.attemptOnly), 'ability breakthrough claim detected without stabilization');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('The Iron Dominion faction tightens its grip on the road to Obel City.', r); add('BC13-009', hits.some(h => h.family === 'world' && h.type === 'world.faction_reference' && h.effectOwnerHint === 'WorldEntityReducerFuture'), 'world/faction reference detected as future evidence only');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanText('You pick up the crown.', 'context', r); add('BC13-010', hits.length === 0, 'context source still rejected');
        r = base(); before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, abil:r.abilities, places:r.places, actors:r.actors, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, prog:r.progression }); this.scanOutput('You equip the iron sword. You practice Ember Ward. The Iron Dominion faction watches.', r); after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, abil:r.abilities, places:r.places, actors:r.actors, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, prog:r.progression }); add('BC13-011', before === after, 'V0.13 scanner does not mutate gameplay buckets');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key. You pick up the iron key.', r); add('BC13-012', hits.filter(h => h.type === 'item.acquire').length === 1, 'hit coalescing prevents duplicate identical ParserHits');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('You equip the idea of a brass key.', r); add('BC13-013', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.flags.abstractOrIdea && !h.completedAction), 'abstract item/equip claim is unsafe');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanOutput('[WorldLedger] /inventory\nStatus: ACTIVE\nYou equip the iron sword.', r); add('BC13-014', hits.length === 0, 'command diagnostic output still ignored');
        r = base(); hits = this.scanInput('I equip the iron sword.'); add('BC13-015', hits.length === 0 || hits.every(h => h.attemptOnly && !h.completedAction), 'input equipment action remains intent-only');
        r = base(); const old = baseBC9.call(this); const oldPass = old.filter(t => t.ok).length; add('BC13-016', old.length >= 20 && oldPass === old.length, 'legacy BC9 scanner tests still pass under V0.13 lock');
      } catch (e) { add('BC13-ERR', false, 'BC13 self-test exception: ' + String(e && e.message || e)); }
      return results;
    };

    WL.EventScannerLite.runBC9SelfTests = function () {
      return baseBC9.call(this).concat(this.runBC13SelfTests());
    };
  })();


  // ============================================================
  // 10B. Legacy Salvage Ledger / Control Map Guard
  // ============================================================
  WL.LegacySalvageLedger = {
    directSources() { return WL.DIRECT_FOUNDATION_SOURCES.slice(); },
    rules() { return WL.LEGACY_SALVAGE_RULES.slice(); },
    entries() { return WL.LEGACY_SALVAGE_LEDGER.slice(); },
    forbiddenPatchNames() {
      return ['CatRegressionRepairSystem','LongRunPolishSystem','FinalGameplayPolishSystem','RandomGameplayRepair','PatchPileSystem','ManagedQuarantineCardGenerator','DuplicateReviewCardGenerator'];
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(WL.CONTROL_MAP_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'control map lock present: ' + WL.CONTROL_MAP_LOCK_VERSION } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'control map lock missing' });
      rows.push(WL.LEGACY_SALVAGE_LEDGER && WL.LEGACY_SALVAGE_LEDGER.length >= 6 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'legacy salvage ledger has direct script and old-version evidence entries' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'legacy salvage ledger too small' });
      rows.push(WL.DIRECT_FOUNDATION_SOURCES.indexOf('TAS / True Auto Stats by @Yi1i1i') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'TAS credited as direct functional inspiration' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'TAS direct inspiration missing' });
      rows.push(WL.DIRECT_FOUNDATION_SOURCES.indexOf('Auto-Cards by @LewdLeah') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Auto-Cards credited as direct functional inspiration' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Auto-Cards direct inspiration missing' });
      rows.push(WL.DIRECT_FOUNDATION_SOURCES.indexOf('Inner Self by @LewdLeah') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'Inner Self credited as direct functional inspiration' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'Inner Self direct inspiration missing' });
      rows.push(WL.DIRECT_FOUNDATION_SOURCES.indexOf('RPGUI by @RonenNess') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'RPGUI credited as direct presentation inspiration' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'RPGUI direct inspiration missing' });
      rows.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('controlmap') >= 0 && WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('borrowledger') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: '/controlmap and /borrowledger registered as inspect-only commands' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'control/salvage commands missing' });
      rows.push(WL.ProposalBuilder && typeof WL.ProposalBuilder.fromHits === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ProposalBuilder exists after salvage/control map correction' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ProposalBuilder missing after control map correction' });
      rows.push(WL.CanonValidator ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'CanonValidator remains separate from ProposalBuilder' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'CanonValidator missing' });
      rows.push(WL.ReducerRegistry ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ReducerRegistry remains separate from ProposalBuilder' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ReducerRegistry missing' });
      const src = String(WL.SourceText || '');
      const forbidden = this.forbiddenPatchNames();
      for (let i = 0; i < forbidden.length; i++) {
        rows.push(src.indexOf(forbidden[i]) < 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'forbidden patch layer absent: ' + forbidden[i] } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'forbidden patch layer present: ' + forbidden[i] });
      }
      rows.push(root && root.features && root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'generated cards still disabled despite Auto-Cards inspiration' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'generated cards enabled too early' });
      rows.push(root && root.features && root.features.economyEnabled === false && root.features.craftingEnabled === false && root.features.domainEnabled === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'old advanced systems remain deferred, not faked' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'deferred old systems enabled too early' });
      return rows;
    },
    render(root) {
      const lines = [];
      const rows = this.inspect(root);
      const fail = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      lines.push('Control Map Lock: ' + WL.CONTROL_MAP_LOCK_VERSION + (fail ? ' / FAILURES: ' + fail : ' / locked'));
      lines.push('Purpose: prove WorldLedgerV0.10 is guided by the updated package and prior working evidence, not a random rebuild.');
      lines.push('Direct foundation sources:');
      this.directSources().forEach(s => lines.push('- ' + s));
      lines.push('Salvage rules:');
      this.rules().forEach(r => lines.push('- ' + r));
      lines.push('Borrow / salvage ledger:');
      this.entries().forEach(e => lines.push('- ' + e.source + ' (' + e.creator + '): ' + e.usefulRule + ' -> ' + e.absorbedInto + ' [' + e.status + ']'));
      lines.push('Audit:');
      rows.forEach(r => lines.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      return lines;
    },
    runBC10ControlSelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      let root;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        let out = WL.onInput('/controlmap');
        add('BC10C-001', /^\[WorldLedger\] \/controlmap/.test(out) && /Control Map Lock/.test(out), '/controlmap renders control map lock');
        out = WL.onInput('/borrowledger');
        add('BC10C-002', /TAS \/ True Auto Stats/.test(out) && /Auto-Cards/.test(out) && /Inner Self/.test(out) && /RPGUI/.test(out), '/borrowledger names direct functional inspirations');
        const before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv: root.inventory, abil: root.abilities, quests: root.quests, body: root.body });
        WL.onInput('/salvage');
        const after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv: WL.StateManager.root().inventory, abil: WL.StateManager.root().abilities, quests: WL.StateManager.root().quests, body: WL.StateManager.root().body });
        add('BC10C-003', before === after, 'salvage/control commands do not mutate gameplay state');
        const rows = this.inspect(WL.StateManager.root());
        add('BC10C-004', rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'control map inspect clean');
        const rc = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run(WL.StateManager.root()).join('\n');
        add('BC10C-005', /Legacy salvage control map audit clean/.test(rc), 'releasecheck includes salvage/control gate');
      } catch (e) { add('BC10C-ERR', false, String(e && e.message || e)); }
      return results;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 14. ProposalBuilder Shell / EventProposal Lock
  // ============================================================
  WL.ProposalBuilder = {
    lockVersion: 'worldledger.proposalbuilder-lock.v14',
    proposalSchemaVersion: 'worldledger.eventproposal.schema.v14',
    knownFamilies: Object.freeze(['inventory','ability','body','quest','reward','actor','place','time','progression','card','migration','unknown']),
    maxProposals(root) {
      return WL.U.clamp(root && root.features && root.features.maxProposalsPerHook || 20, 4, 40);
    },
    // Supports fromHits(root, hits, sourceKind) and fromHits(hits, root, sourceKind).
    fromHits(a, b, c) {
      let root, hits, sourceKind;
      if (Array.isArray(a)) { hits = a; root = b || WL.StateManager.root(); sourceKind = c || 'output'; }
      else { root = a || WL.StateManager.root(); hits = Array.isArray(b) ? b : []; sourceKind = c || 'output'; }
      root = root || WL.StateManager.root();
      const source = WL.U.slug(sourceKind || 'output') || 'output';
      const proposals = [];
      const rejectedHits = [];
      const seen = {};
      const max = this.maxProposals(root);
      for (let i = 0; i < hits.length; i++) {
        if (proposals.length >= max) { rejectedHits.push(this.rejectMalformedHit(hits[i], 'proposal cap reached')); continue; }
        const p = this.fromHit(root, hits[i], source, i);
        if (!p) continue;
        if (p.status === 'malformed' || p.status === 'blocked_prevalidation') { rejectedHits.push(p); continue; }
        const sig = this.signature(p);
        if (seen[sig]) { rejectedHits.push(this.rejectMalformedHit(hits[i], 'duplicate parser hit coalesced')); continue; }
        seen[sig] = true;
        proposals.push(p);
      }
      root.pending.lastProposals = proposals;
      this.auditProposals(root, proposals, rejectedHits, { sourceKind: source, hitCount: hits.length, omitted: Math.max(0, hits.length - proposals.length - rejectedHits.length) });
      return proposals;
    },
    // Supports fromHit(root, hit, sourceKind, index). Kept broad for future local tests.
    fromHit(a, b, c, d) {
      let root, hit, sourceKind, index;
      if (a && typeof a === 'object' && a.schemaVersion) { root = a; hit = b; sourceKind = c || 'output'; index = d || 0; }
      else { hit = a; root = b || WL.StateManager.root(); sourceKind = c || 'output'; index = d || 0; }
      if (!hit || typeof hit !== 'object') return null;
      if (hit.family === 'parser' || hit.type === 'parser.source_rejected') return null;
      if (hit.canCommitDirectly === true) return this.rejectMalformedHit(hit, 'ParserHit claimed direct commit path');
      const sourceCheck = this.sourceAllowed(hit, sourceKind, root);
      if (!sourceCheck.allowed) return null;
      const normalized = this.normalizeHit(hit, sourceKind, index);
      if (!normalized) return null;
      return this.buildBaseProposal(normalized, root);
    },
    sourceAllowed(hit, sourceKind, root) {
      const sourceHook = WL.U.slug(hit.sourceHook || sourceKind || 'unknown_noncanon');
      const text = WL.U.norm(hit.sourceText || hit.localClausePreview || '');
      const blocked = { command:1, command_output:1, context:1, story_card:1, protected_card:1, generated_card:1, debug:1, audit:1, help:1, releasecheck:1, migration_log:1, old_log:1, archive:1, unknown_noncanon:1 };
      if (blocked[sourceHook]) return { allowed:false, reason:'blocked non-canon source: ' + sourceHook };
      if (WL.EventScannerLite && WL.EventScannerLite.isCommandOutput && WL.EventScannerLite.isCommandOutput(text, root)) return { allowed:false, reason:'command output text' };
      if (/^\s*\[(?:WorldLedger|AIDRPG)\]/i.test(text)) return { allowed:false, reason:'diagnostic prefix' };
      if (sourceHook !== 'input' && sourceHook !== 'output' && sourceHook !== 'setup' && sourceHook !== 'migration' && sourceHook !== 'manual') return { allowed:false, reason:'source not allowlisted: ' + sourceHook };
      return { allowed:true, reason:'allowlisted source' };
    },
    normalizeHit(hit, sourceKind, index) {
      const sourceHook = WL.U.slug(hit.sourceHook || sourceKind || 'output') || 'output';
      const sourceText = WL.U.norm(hit.sourceText || hit.localClausePreview || '').slice(0, 320);
      const localClausePreview = WL.U.norm(hit.localClausePreview || sourceText).slice(0, 240);
      const flags = this.copySafetyFlags(hit);
      const family = this.classifyProposalFamily(hit);
      const eventType = this.classifyProposalType(hit, family);
      return {
        parserHitId: hit.id || hit.parserHitId || 'hit_' + WL.U.hash([sourceHook, hit.type, hit.name, localClausePreview, index].join('|')),
        parserHitVersion: hit.parserHitVersion || hit.scannerLockVersion || '',
        scannerLockVersion: hit.scannerLockVersion || hit.parserHitVersion || '',
        family,
        eventType,
        type: eventType,
        rawType: hit.type || '',
        name: WL.U.norm(hit.name || hit.target || ''),
        sourceText,
        localClausePreview,
        flags,
        sourceHook,
        sourceInfo: Object.assign({}, hit.sourceInfo || {}),
        confidence: WL.U.clamp(hit.confidence == null ? 0.25 : hit.confidence, 0, 1),
        confidenceBucket: hit.confidenceBucket || this.confidenceBucket(hit.confidence == null ? 0.25 : hit.confidence),
        completedAction: !!hit.completedAction && sourceHook === 'output' && !flags.attemptOnly && !flags.sourceUnsafe,
        attemptOnly: !!hit.attemptOnly || sourceHook === 'input' || !!flags.attemptOnly,
        truthStatus: hit.truthStatus || 'unknown_candidate',
        parserOnly: hit.parserOnly !== false,
        canCommitDirectly: false,
        raw: hit,
        hitIndex: index || 0
      };
    },
    buildBaseProposal(hit, root) {
      const localClause = this.bindLocalClause(hit, root);
      const target = this.bindTarget(hit, hit.family, root);
      const secondaryTargets = this.bindSecondaryTargets(hit, target, localClause, root);
      const safety = this.copySafetyFlags(hit);
      const riskFlags = this.computeRiskFlags({ hit, target, safety, localClause });
      const owner = this.assignOwner(hit.family, hit.eventType);
      const source = this.buildProposalSource(hit, root);
      const evidence = this.buildEvidence(hit, target, source.textHash, localClause, safety);
      const proposedChange = this.buildProposedChange(hit, target, riskFlags);
      const operation = this.suggestOperationKey({ hit, target, proposedChange, localClause, source }, root);
      const idSeed = [hit.eventType, target.targetId || target.id || target.canonicalKey || target.rawTargetText || 'none', source.textHash || '', localClause.localClauseHash, hit.sourceHook].join('|');
      const proposalId = 'prop_' + WL.U.hash(idSeed);
      const status = (riskFlags.indexOf('source_unsafe') >= 0 || safety.commandOutput || safety.contextText || safety.storyCardText || safety.debugOrAuditText) ? 'blocked_prevalidation' : 'proposed';
      const proposal = {
        proposalVersion: WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        eventProposalSchemaVersion: this.proposalSchemaVersion,
        proposalId,
        id: proposalId,
        source,
        family: hit.family,
        eventType: hit.eventType,
        type: hit.eventType,
        ownerModule: owner,
        effectOwner: owner,
        reducerHint: owner,
        localClause,
        localClausePreview: localClause.localClauseText,
        target,
        secondaryTargets,
        safety,
        flags: Object.assign({}, safety),
        evidence,
        proposedChange,
        operation,
        opKey: operation.suggestedKey,
        sourceUseKey: WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['source', hit.sourceHook, source.textHash, hit.eventType, target.targetId || target.id || target.rawTargetText || 'none']),
        dedupeKey: WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['dedupe', hit.eventType, target.targetId || target.id || target.rawTargetText || 'none', source.textHash, localClause.localClauseHash]),
        confidence: evidence.proposalConfidence,
        truthStatus: hit.truthStatus,
        riskFlags,
        validatorRequired: true,
        reducerRequired: owner !== 'NoActiveOwner' && owner !== 'CardSyncLite' && owner !== 'MigrationManager',
        validationReadiness: this.validationReadiness(hit, riskFlags),
        status,
        proposalNotes: this.proposalNotes(hit, target, safety, riskFlags),
        commitPrecondition: 'CanonValidator must accept this proposal and OperationLedger/ReducerRegistry must allow it; ProposalBuilder never commits canon.',
        proposalOnly: true,
        canCommitDirectly: false,
        sourceKind: hit.sourceHook,
        sourceInfo: hit.sourceInfo,
        sourceText: hit.sourceText,
        data: this.dataPayload(hit, target, proposedChange, operation)
      };
      return proposal;
    },
    buildProposalSource(hit, root) {
      const textHash = (hit.sourceInfo && hit.sourceInfo.textHash) || WL.U.hash(hit.sourceText || hit.localClausePreview || '');
      return {
        sourceHook: hit.sourceHook,
        parserHitId: hit.parserHitId,
        inputHash: root && root.runtime ? root.runtime.lastInputHash || '' : '',
        outputHash: root && root.runtime ? root.runtime.lastOutputHash || '' : '',
        textHash,
        turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root),
        commandSuppressed: !!(root && root.runtime && root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput),
        duplicateOutput: !!(root && root.runtime && root.runtime.outputReplayDetected),
        nonCanonSource: false,
        sourceLabel: hit.sourceHook + ':' + textHash
      };
    },
    bindLocalClause(hit, root) {
      const full = WL.U.norm(hit.sourceText || hit.localClausePreview || '').slice(0, 320);
      const local = WL.U.norm(hit.localClausePreview || full).slice(0, 240);
      const beforeAfter = this.beforeAfter(full, local);
      const clauseRole = hit.flags && (hit.flags.quotedSpeech || hit.flags.quote) ? 'quoted' : (hit.flags && hit.flags.hypothetical ? 'conditional' : 'main');
      const denialScope = /\b(?:refuse|refuses|never|not|do not|don't|didn't|decide not|snatches?.*away|fails?|failed|empty-handed)\b/i.test(local) ? 'local_clause' : '';
      const revocationScope = /\b(?:snatches?.*away|dissolves|vanishes|taken back|revoked|lost|drop|drops|breaks|destroyed)\b/i.test(local) ? 'local_clause' : '';
      return {
        fullWindowPreview: full,
        localClauseText: local,
        localClauseHash: WL.U.hash(local),
        clauseIndex: Number(hit.raw && hit.raw.windowIndex || hit.hitIndex || 0),
        sentenceIndex: Number(hit.raw && hit.raw.windowIndex || hit.hitIndex || 0),
        beforeTextPreview: beforeAfter.before,
        afterTextPreview: beforeAfter.after,
        matchedText: WL.U.norm(hit.name || '').slice(0, 120),
        matchedTextHash: WL.U.hash(hit.name || ''),
        clauseRole,
        denialScope,
        revocationScope,
        targetScope: hit.name ? 'matched_target' : 'unknown',
        notes: []
      };
    },
    beforeAfter(full, local) {
      const idx = full.indexOf(local);
      if (idx < 0 || !local) return { before:'', after:'' };
      return { before: full.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 80), idx).trim(), after: full.slice(idx + local.length, idx + local.length + 80).trim() };
    },
    bindTarget(hit, family, root) {
      const binding = WL.Resolver.buildTargetBinding(Object.assign({}, hit, { family: this.familyForResolver(family || hit.family, hit.eventType) }));
      const raw = hit.name || binding.raw || '';
      const display = binding.display || '';
      const targetFamily = this.targetFamilyFor(family || hit.family, hit.eventType);
      const status = display ? (binding.ambiguous ? 'ambiguous' : 'new_candidate') : (this.targetNotNeeded(family || hit.family, hit.eventType) ? 'known' : 'unknown');
      const id = display ? (binding.id || WL.Resolver.canonicalId(targetFamily, display)) : '';
      return Object.assign({}, binding, {
        targetFamily,
        rawTargetText: raw,
        cleanedTargetText: display,
        canonicalKey: id || WL.U.slug(raw),
        displayName: display,
        display,
        targetId: id,
        id,
        targetStatus: status,
        targetConfidence: display ? 0.82 : (this.targetNotNeeded(family || hit.family, hit.eventType) ? 0.55 : 0.1),
        sanitizerUsed: targetFamily + ' sanitizer',
        resolverNotes: binding.notes || [],
        canonicalName: display,
        sourceName: raw,
        kind: family || hit.family || 'unknown',
        safeForCommit: !!display || this.targetNotNeeded(family || hit.family, hit.eventType),
        bindingVersion: WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION
      });
    },
    bindSecondaryTargets(hit, target, localClause, root) {
      const out = [];
      const text = WL.U.norm(localClause && localClause.localClauseText || hit.sourceText || '');
      const actor = text.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}){0,2})\s+(?:hands|gives|takes|returns|pays|refuses)\b/);
      if (actor && hit.family !== 'actor') {
        const clean = WL.Sanitizers.actor(actor[1]);
