        if (clean) out.push({ targetFamily:'actor', rawTargetText:actor[1], cleanedTargetText:clean, displayName:clean, display:clean, canonicalKey:WL.Resolver.canonicalId('actor', clean), targetId:WL.Resolver.canonicalId('actor', clean), targetStatus:'candidate', targetConfidence:0.55, sanitizerUsed:'actor sanitizer', resolverNotes:['secondary source actor hint'] });
      }
      return out.slice(0, 3);
    },
    familyForResolver(family, eventType) {
      if (/^reward\./.test(eventType || '')) return 'quest';
      if (/^currency\./.test(eventType || '')) return 'inventory';
      if (family === 'body_status' || family === 'body_injury') return 'body';
      if (family === 'place_scene') return 'place';
      return family || 'unknown';
    },
    targetFamilyFor(family, eventType) {
      if (/^item\.|^currency\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'inventory') return /^currency\./.test(eventType || '') ? 'reward' : 'item';
      if (/^ability\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'ability') return 'ability';
      if (/^quest\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'quest') return 'quest';
      if (/^reward\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'reward') return 'reward';
      if (/^actor\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'actor') return 'actor';
      if (/^place\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'place') return 'place';
      if (/^time\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'time') return 'time';
      if (/^body\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'body') return 'body';
      if (/^progression\./.test(eventType || '') || family === 'progression') return 'progression';
      return 'unknown';
    },
    targetNotNeeded(family, eventType) {
      return /^body\.|^time\.|^progression\.xp_claim$/.test(eventType || '') || family === 'body' || family === 'time';
    },
    classifyProposalFamily(hit) {
      const type = hit && hit.type || '';
      if (/^currency\./.test(type) && /\b(?:promises?|offers?|vows?)\b/i.test(hit && (hit.sourceText || hit.localClausePreview || '') || '')) return 'reward';
      if (/^currency\./.test(type)) return 'inventory';
      if (/^reward\./.test(type)) return 'reward';
      if (/^place\./.test(type)) return 'place';
      if (/^body\./.test(type)) return 'body';
      if (/^item\./.test(type)) return 'inventory';
      return (hit && hit.family) || 'unknown';
    },
    classifyProposalType(hit, family) {
      let t = hit && hit.type || (family || 'unknown') + '.hint';
      const text = WL.U.norm(hit && (hit.sourceText || hit.localClausePreview) || '');
      if (/^currency\.gain$/.test(t) && /\b(?:promises?|offers?|vows?)\b/i.test(text)) t = 'reward.promise';
      const flags = hit && hit.flags || {};
      const sourceHook = WL.U.slug(hit && hit.sourceHook || 'output');
      if (sourceHook === 'input' && hit && hit.completedAction !== true) {
        if (family === 'inventory' && /^item\./.test(t)) t = 'item.intent';
        else if (family === 'place' && /^place\./.test(t)) t = 'place.intent';
        else if (family === 'quest' && /^quest\./.test(t)) t = 'quest.intent';
        else if (family === 'actor' && /^actor\./.test(t)) t = 'actor.intent';
        else if (family === 'progression') t = 'progression.intent';
        else if (family === 'ability' && t !== 'ability.evidence') t = 'ability.intent';
      }
      if (family === 'inventory' && /^item\.acquire$/.test(t) && (flags.negatedOrRefused || flags.failedOrRevoked || flags.denied)) t = 'item.denied_candidate';
      if (family === 'inventory' && /^item\.acquire$/.test(t) && flags.abstractOrIdea) t = 'item.abstract_reference';
      if (family === 'ability' && /^ability\.stabilize_claim$/.test(t) && sourceHook === 'output') t = 'ability.output_only_learning_claim';
      return t;
    },
    assignOwner(family, eventType) {
      if (/^item\.|^currency\./.test(eventType) || family === 'inventory') return 'InventoryReducerLite';
      if (/^ability\./.test(eventType) || family === 'ability') return 'AbilityReducerLite';
      if (/^body\./.test(eventType) || family === 'body') return 'BodyReducer';
      if (/^quest\.|^reward\./.test(eventType) || family === 'quest' || family === 'reward') return 'QuestReducerLite';
      if (/^actor\./.test(eventType) || family === 'actor') return 'ActorReducerLite';
      if (/^place\./.test(eventType) || family === 'place') return 'PlaceReducerLite';
      if (/^time\./.test(eventType) || family === 'time') return 'TimeReducerLite';
      if (/^progression\./.test(eventType) || family === 'progression') return 'ProgressionReducerLiteFuture';
      if (/^card\./.test(eventType) || family === 'card') return 'CardSyncLite';
      if (/^migration\./.test(eventType) || family === 'migration') return 'MigrationManager';
      return 'NoActiveOwner';
    },
    copySafetyFlags(hit) {
      const f = Object.assign({}, hit && (hit.flags || hit.safety) || {});
      const text = WL.U.norm((hit && (hit.localClausePreview || hit.sourceText)) || '');
      const sourceHook = WL.U.slug(hit && hit.sourceHook || 'output');
      const out = {
        negated: !!(f.negated || f.negatedOrRefused) || /\b(?:do not|don't|did not|didn't|never|not take|decide not|refuse|refuses|declines?)\b/i.test(text),
        hypothetical: !!f.hypothetical,
        quotedSpeech: !!(f.quotedSpeech || f.quote),
        dreamOrVision: !!(f.dreamOrVision || f.dream),
        rumorOrClaim: !!(f.rumorOrClaim || f.rumor),
        failedOrRevoked: !!(f.failedOrRevoked || f.failed || f.revoked || f.denied) || /\b(?:fails?|failed|nothing happens|snatches?.*away|dissolves|vanishes|empty-handed|collapses|cannot|can't|unable)\b/i.test(text),
        conditional: !!f.conditional || /\bif\b/i.test(text),
        futureIntent: !!f.futureIntent,
        attemptOnly: !!(f.attemptOnly || (hit && hit.attemptOnly) || sourceHook === 'input'),
        commandOutput: !!(f.commandOutput || f.commandText),
        contextText: !!f.contextText,
        storyCardText: !!f.storyCardText,
        debugOrAuditText: !!(f.debugOrAuditText || f.diagnosticText),
        abstractOrMetaphorical: !!(f.abstractOrMetaphorical || f.abstractOrIdea || f.nonliteral) || /\b(?:idea of|concept of|thought of|symbol of|metaphor|as if|felt like)\b/i.test(text),
        visibleOnly: !!f.visibleOnly,
        availableOnly: !!f.availableOnly,
        deniedByLocalClause: /\b(?:refuse|refuses|denies|won't give|will not give|do not|don't|not take|decide not)\b/i.test(text),
        revokedByLocalClause: /\b(?:snatches?.*away|taken back|revoked|dissolves|vanishes|lost|breaks|destroyed)\b/i.test(text),
        nonliteral: !!f.nonliteral,
        sourceUnsafe: !!(f.commandText || f.diagnosticText || f.contextText || f.storyCardText),
        confidencePenaltyReasons: []
      };
      if (out.negated) out.confidencePenaltyReasons.push('negated');
      if (out.quotedSpeech) out.confidencePenaltyReasons.push('quoted speech');
      if (out.dreamOrVision) out.confidencePenaltyReasons.push('dream/vision');
      if (out.rumorOrClaim) out.confidencePenaltyReasons.push('rumor/claim');
      if (out.failedOrRevoked) out.confidencePenaltyReasons.push('failed/revoked');
      // Compatibility aliases used by older validator shell.
      out.quote = out.quotedSpeech; out.dream = out.dreamOrVision; out.rumor = out.rumorOrClaim;
      out.denied = out.negated || out.failedOrRevoked || out.deniedByLocalClause || out.revokedByLocalClause;
      out.pretend = !!(f.pretend || f.pretendOrFalse);
      out.abstractOrIdea = out.abstractOrMetaphorical;
      out.negatedOrRefused = out.negated;
      return out;
    },
    computeRiskFlags(ctx) {
      const hit = ctx.hit || {}, target = ctx.target || {}, s = ctx.safety || {}, lc = ctx.localClause || {};
      const flags = [];
      const add = v => { if (v && flags.indexOf(v) < 0) flags.push(v); };
      if (!target.display && !this.targetNotNeeded(hit.family, hit.eventType)) add('ambiguous_target');
      if (target.targetStatus === 'generic') add('generic_target');
      if (s.quotedSpeech) add('quote_present');
      if (s.dreamOrVision) add('dream');
      if (s.rumorOrClaim) add('rumor');
      if (s.pretend) add('pretend');
      if (s.negated || s.failedOrRevoked || s.deniedByLocalClause || s.revokedByLocalClause) add('denied');
      if (s.visibleOnly) add('visible_only');
      if (s.availableOnly) add('available_only');
      if (s.hypothetical) add('hypothetical');
      if (s.futureIntent) add('future_intent');
      if (s.abstractOrMetaphorical) add('abstract_or_idea');
      if (s.nonliteral) add('nonliteral');
      if (s.commandOutput) add('command_text');
      if (s.contextText || s.storyCardText || s.debugOrAuditText || s.sourceUnsafe) add('source_unsafe');
      if (hit.sourceHook === 'input') add('unconfirmed_input');
      if (hit.attemptOnly || s.attemptOnly) add('attempt_only');
      if (hit.completedAction !== true) add('not_confirmed_completed');
      if (/^reward\.promise/.test(hit.eventType || '')) add('promise_not_payment');
      if (/^reward\.payment|^currency\.gain/.test(hit.eventType || '')) add('repeat_sensitive_reward');
      if (/^ability\.output_only_learning_claim/.test(hit.eventType || '')) add('output_only_ability_claim');
      if (/^place\.reference|^place\.weak|^place\.visible/.test(hit.eventType || '') || s.visibleOnly) add('weak_scene_reference');
      if (lc.denialScope) add('local_denial_scope');
      if (lc.revocationScope) add('local_revocation_scope');
      return flags;
    },
    buildEvidence(hit, target, sourceHash, localClause, safety) {
      const support = [], blocking = [], ambiguity = [];
      const text = WL.U.norm(localClause && localClause.localClauseText || hit.localClausePreview || '');
      if (/\b(?:pick up|hands you|gives you|places .* hand|arrive at|enter|practice|train|completed|pays?)\b/i.test(text)) support.push('action wording');
      if (safety.negated || safety.failedOrRevoked || safety.quotedSpeech || safety.dreamOrVision || safety.rumorOrClaim || safety.abstractOrMetaphorical) blocking.push.apply(blocking, safety.confidencePenaltyReasons || []);
      if (!target || !target.display) ambiguity.push('target not cleanly bound');
      const srcConf = hit.sourceHook === 'output' ? 0.72 : 0.35;
      const penalty = Math.min(0.35, blocking.length * 0.08 + ambiguity.length * 0.05);
      const propConf = WL.U.clamp((hit.confidence || 0.25) * 0.7 + srcConf * 0.3 - penalty, 0, 1);
      return {
        rawTextPreview: WL.U.norm(hit.sourceText || '').slice(0, 240),
        normalizedTextPreview: WL.U.norm(hit.sourceText || '').slice(0, 240),
        matchedTextPreview: WL.U.norm(hit.name || '').slice(0, 120),
        evidenceType: hit.sourceHook === 'input' ? 'input_intent' : (hit.sourceHook === 'output' ? 'output_consequence' : 'parser_shell'),
        sourceConfidence: srcConf,
        parserConfidence: hit.confidence || 0.25,
        proposalConfidence: propConf,
        confidence: propConf,
        completedAction: hit.completedAction,
        attemptOnly: hit.attemptOnly,
        truthStatus: hit.truthStatus,
        parserHitId: hit.parserHitId,
        parserHitVersion: hit.parserHitVersion,
        sourceHook: hit.sourceHook,
        sourceHash,
        localClausePreview: localClause.localClauseText,
        targetDisplay: target && (target.display || target.displayName) || '',
        targetId: target && (target.id || target.targetId) || '',
        supportPhrases: support,
        blockingPhrases: blocking,
        ambiguityPhrases: ambiguity,
        notes: []
      };
    },
    buildProposedChange(hit, target, riskFlags) {
      const t = hit.eventType || hit.type || '';
      let action = 'observe', direction = 'none', qty = 1;
      if (/acquire|gain|handoff|payment/.test(t)) { action = 'gain candidate'; direction = 'gain'; }
      else if (/drop|lose|destroy|consume/.test(t)) { action = 'loss candidate'; direction = 'loss'; }
      else if (/visible|reference|intent|evidence/.test(t)) { action = 'observe candidate'; direction = 'observe'; }
      else if (/status|injury|current|arrival|accept|complete|time\.pass/.test(t)) { action = 'update candidate'; direction = 'update'; }
      const reward = hit.raw && hit.raw.reward;
      if (reward && reward.amount) qty = reward.amount;
      return {
        action,
        targetFamily: target && target.targetFamily || 'unknown',
        targetKey: target && (target.targetId || target.id || target.canonicalKey) || '',
        targetDisplayName: target && (target.displayName || target.display || target.rawTargetText) || '',
        quantity: qty,
        direction,
        currentStateHint: 'validator/reducer must inspect current state',
        proposedStateHint: t,
        reversible: direction !== 'gain' && direction !== 'loss',
        repeatSensitive: this.isRepeatSensitive(t, hit.family),
        dirtyFamiliesIfAccepted: this.dirtyFamiliesFor(hit.family, t),
        notes: riskFlags.slice(0, 8)
      };
    },
    suggestOperationKey(ctx, root) {
      const hit = ctx.hit || {}, target = ctx.target || {}, change = ctx.proposedChange || {}, lc = ctx.localClause || {}, source = ctx.source || {};
      const t = hit.eventType || hit.type || '';
      const targetKey = target.targetId || target.id || target.canonicalKey || target.displayName || target.rawTargetText || 'none';
      let keyFamily = 'recent', durable = false, repeat = this.isRepeatSensitive(t, hit.family);
      if (/^reward\.|^currency\.gain/.test(t)) { keyFamily = 'reward'; durable = true; repeat = true; }
      else if (/^card\./.test(t)) { keyFamily = 'card'; durable = true; repeat = true; }
      else if (/^ability\.stabilize|^quest\.complete|^time\.pass|^item\.acquire|^item\.lose|^item\.drop|^item\.consume/.test(t)) { keyFamily = 'recent'; repeat = true; }
      else if (/intent|visible|reference|evidence/.test(t)) { keyFamily = 'sourceUse'; repeat = false; }
      const key = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([t, targetKey, hit.sourceHook || 'output', source.textHash || '', lc.localClauseHash || '']);
      return {
        suggestedKey: key,
        keyFamily,
        repeatSensitive: repeat,
        durable,
        reason: repeat ? 'repeat-sensitive proposed effect' : 'source-use/evidence suggestion only',
        requiredBeforeCommit: repeat,
        notes: [change.action || 'proposal']
      };
    },
    isRepeatSensitive(type, family) {
      return /^item\.acquire|^item\.lose|^item\.drop|^item\.consume|^currency\.gain|^reward\.payment|^quest\.complete|^time\.pass|^progression\.xp_claim|^ability\.stabilize/.test(type || '') || family === 'reward';
    },
    dirtyFamiliesFor(family, type) {
      const out = [];
      if (family === 'inventory' || /^item\.|^currency\./.test(type || '')) out.push('inventory');
      if (family === 'ability' || /^ability\./.test(type || '')) out.push('abilities');
      if (family === 'quest' || family === 'reward' || /^quest\.|^reward\./.test(type || '')) out.push('quests');
      if (family === 'body' || /^body\./.test(type || '')) out.push('body');
      if (family === 'actor' || /^actor\./.test(type || '')) out.push('actors');
      if (family === 'place' || /^place\./.test(type || '')) out.push('places');
      if (family === 'time' || /^time\./.test(type || '')) out.push('time');
      if (family === 'progression' || /^progression\./.test(type || '')) out.push('progression');
      return out.length ? out : ['audit'];
    },
    confidenceBucket(confidence) {
      const c = WL.U.clamp(confidence || 0, 0, 1);
      if (c >= 0.75) return 'high';
      if (c >= 0.5) return 'medium';
      if (c >= 0.25) return 'low';
      return 'trace';
    },
    validationReadiness(hit, riskFlags) {
      if (riskFlags.indexOf('source_unsafe') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('command_text') >= 0) return 'reject_source';
      if (riskFlags.indexOf('dream') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('quote_present') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('rumor') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('pretend') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('abstract_or_idea') >= 0) return 'unsafe_needs_reject_or_pending';
      if (riskFlags.indexOf('unconfirmed_input') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('attempt_only') >= 0 || riskFlags.indexOf('not_confirmed_completed') >= 0) return 'intent_or_pending_only';
      if (hit.completedAction && hit.confidence >= 0.6) return 'validator_ready';
      return 'low_confidence_pending';
    },
    proposalNotes(hit, target, safety, riskFlags) {
      const notes = ['ProposalBuilder output only; CanonValidator required.'];
      if (riskFlags.length) notes.push('Risk: ' + riskFlags.slice(0, 6).join(', '));
      if (!target || !target.display) notes.push('Target not cleanly bound.');
      if (safety.attemptOnly) notes.push('Attempt/intent only.');
      return notes;
    },
    signature(p) {
      return WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([p.eventType || p.type, p.target && (p.target.targetId || p.target.id || p.target.displayName || p.target.display || p.target.rawTargetText), p.source && p.source.sourceHook || p.sourceKind, p.source && p.source.textHash || p.evidence && p.evidence.sourceHash, p.localClause && p.localClause.localClauseHash || WL.U.hash(p.localClausePreview || '')]);
    },
    dataPayload(hit, target, proposedChange, operation) {
      const raw = Object.assign({}, hit.raw || {});
      delete raw.flags; delete raw.sourceInfo;
      return Object.assign({}, raw, {
        parserHitId: hit.parserHitId,
        truthStatus: hit.truthStatus,
        completedAction: hit.completedAction,
        attemptOnly: hit.attemptOnly,
        confidence: hit.confidence,
        targetDisplayName: target && (target.displayName || target.display) || '',
        proposedDirection: proposedChange && proposedChange.direction || 'none',
        repeatSensitive: operation && operation.repeatSensitive || false
      });
    },
    rejectMalformedHit(hit, reason) {
      const preview = hit && (hit.localClausePreview || hit.sourceText || hit.name) || '';
      return {
        proposalVersion: WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        eventProposalSchemaVersion: this.proposalSchemaVersion,
        id: 'malformed_prop_' + WL.U.hash(String(reason || '') + '|' + preview),
        proposalId: 'malformed_prop_' + WL.U.hash(String(reason || '') + '|' + preview),
        family: hit && hit.family || 'unknown',
        eventType: hit && hit.type || 'proposal.malformed',
        type: hit && hit.type || 'proposal.malformed',
        status: 'malformed',
        reason: WL.U.norm(reason || 'malformed hit'),
        proposalOnly: true,
        canCommitDirectly: false,
        validatorRequired: false,
        reducerRequired: false,
        riskFlags: ['malformed_hit'],
        proposalNotes: [WL.U.norm(reason || 'malformed hit')]
      };
    },
    capProposals(proposals, max) {
      return (Array.isArray(proposals) ? proposals : []).slice(0, WL.U.clamp(max || 20, 1, 40));
    },
    auditProposals(root, proposals, rejectedHits, summary) {
      if (!root) return;
      const row = Object.assign({ subsystem: 'ProposalBuilder', lockVersion: WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION, turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), proposalCount: proposals.length, rejectedCount: rejectedHits.length, proposalTypes: proposals.map(p => p.type).slice(0, 12).join(',') }, summary || {});
      if (!Array.isArray(root.audits.proposalAudit)) root.audits.proposalAudit = [];
      root.audits.proposalAudit.push(row);
      WL.U.capArray(root.audits.proposalAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.proposals);
      if (WL.AuditLogger) WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'proposals', { subsystem: 'ProposalBuilder', message: 'built proposals=' + proposals.length + ', rejected=' + rejectedHits.length, detail: row }, 40);
    },
    audit(root, summary) { return this.auditProposals(root, [], [], summary || {}); },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(this.lockVersion === WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'ProposalBuilder lock present: ' + WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'ProposalBuilder lock mismatch' });
      ['fromHits','fromHit','buildBaseProposal','bindLocalClause','bindTarget','classifyProposalFamily','classifyProposalType','assignOwner','copySafetyFlags','suggestOperationKey','computeRiskFlags','capProposals','rejectMalformedHit','auditProposals'].forEach(fn => rows.push(typeof this[fn] === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'function present: ' + fn } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'function missing: ' + fn }));
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.proposalBuilderLockEnabled === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'proposal builder lock feature enabled' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'proposal builder lock feature disabled' });
      rows.push(this.maxProposals(root) <= 40 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'proposal cap configured' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'proposal cap unsafe' });
      const testRoot = WL.StateManager.init({});
      const hit = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key.', testRoot)[0];
      const p = this.fromHits(testRoot, [hit], 'output')[0];
      rows.push(p && p.proposalOnly && p.canCommitDirectly === false && p.validatorRequired === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'EventProposal schema marks proposal-only/validator-required/no direct commit' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'EventProposal schema unsafe' });
      rows.push(p && p.localClause && p.target && p.safety && p.operation && p.proposedChange ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'localClause/target/safety/operation/proposedChange schema present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'EventProposal rich schema missing' });
      rows.push(p && p.opKey && p.sourceUseKey && p.dedupeKey ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'operation/source/dedupe keys present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'proposal keys missing' });
      return rows;
    },
    runBC10SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const base = () => WL.StateManager.init({});
      let r, hits, props, before, after;
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-001', props.length === 1 && props[0].type === 'item.acquire' && props[0].target.display === 'Iron Key', 'pickup hit becomes structured item.acquire proposal');
      add('BC10-002', props[0] && props[0].opKey && props[0].sourceUseKey && props[0].dedupeKey, 'proposal carries operation/source/dedupe keys');
      add('BC10-003', props[0] && props[0].proposalOnly === true && props[0].canCommitDirectly === false, 'proposal cannot commit directly');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanInput('I take the sword.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'input'); add('BC10-004', props.length >= 1 && props.every(p => p.sourceKind === 'input' && /intent$/.test(p.type) && p.validationReadiness === 'intent_or_pending_only'), 'input hits become intent-only proposals');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('Mara says, "Take the crown."', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-005', props.length === 0 || props.every(p => p.riskFlags.indexOf('quote_present') >= 0), 'quoted clauses preserve quote risk flag');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-006', props.length === 0 || props.every(p => p.riskFlags.indexOf('dream') >= 0 && p.validationReadiness === 'unsafe_needs_reject_or_pending'), 'dream proposals marked unsafe');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('People say Lord Veyr killed Dalen.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-007', props.length === 0 || props.every(p => p.riskFlags.indexOf('rumor') >= 0), 'rumor proposals marked rumor');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You see a brass key on the shelf.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-008', props.length >= 1 && props[0].type === 'item.visible' && props[0].riskFlags.indexOf('visible_only') >= 0, 'visible item remains visible-only proposal');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key. You pick up the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-009', props.length === 1, 'duplicate identical hits coalesce into one proposal');
      r = base(); before = WL.U.safeJson({inv:r.inventory, abil:r.abilities, quests:r.quests, body:r.body, actors:r.actors, places:r.places}); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the apple. You attempt Ember Ward, but nothing happens. Mara says hello.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); after = WL.U.safeJson({inv:r.inventory, abil:r.abilities, quests:r.quests, body:r.body, actors:r.actors, places:r.places}); add('BC10-010', before === after && props.length >= 2, 'ProposalBuilder does not mutate gameplay buckets');
      r = base(); hits = new Array(80).fill(0).map((_, i) => ({ family: 'inventory', type: 'item.acquire', name: 'Key ' + i, sourceHook: 'output', sourceText: 'You pick up key ' + i + '.', localClausePreview: 'You pick up key ' + i + '.', flags: {}, sourceInfo: { textHash: 'h' + i }, completedAction: true, confidence: 0.8, parserOnly: true, canCommitDirectly: false })); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-011', props.length <= this.maxProposals(r), 'proposal cap enforced');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); const vals = WL.CanonValidator.validateAll(r, props); add('BC10-012', vals.length === props.length && vals[0].type === 'item.acquire', 'existing CanonValidator remains compatible with new proposal schema');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); const p1 = props[0]; props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); const p2 = props[0]; add('BC10-013', p1 && p2 && p1.opKey === p2.opKey && p1.id === p2.id, 'same source hit creates stable proposal identity');
      r = base(); props = this.fromHits(r, [{ family:'parser', type:'parser.source_rejected', name:'non-canon source' }], 'command_output'); add('BC10-014', props.length === 0, 'rejected parser-source hits do not become proposals');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You gain 20 XP.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC10-015', props.length === 0 || props.every(p => p.effectOwner === 'ProgressionReducerLiteFuture'), 'future progression proposals are labeled future owner, not routed as current gameplay ownership');
      return results;
    },
    runBC14SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const base = () => WL.StateManager.init({});
      let r, hits, props, p, before, after;
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); p = props[0]; add('BC14-001', !!(p && p.eventProposalSchemaVersion === this.proposalSchemaVersion && p.localClause && p.target && p.safety && p.proposedChange && p.operation), 'rich EventProposal schema exists');
      add('BC14-002', !!(p && p.localClause.localClauseHash && p.localClause.localClauseText), 'local clause binding exists');
      add('BC14-003', !!(p && p.target.targetFamily === 'item' && p.target.targetId === 'item_iron_key'), 'target binding uses owner sanitizer/canonical id');
      add('BC14-004', !!(p && p.operation.suggestedKey && p.operation.repeatSensitive === true && p.operation.requiredBeforeCommit === true), 'repeat-sensitive operation suggestion exists');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanInput('I take the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'input'); add('BC14-005', props.length && props.every(x => x.source.sourceHook === 'input' && x.safety.attemptOnly && /intent$/.test(x.eventType)), 'input proposals are attempt/intent only');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('Mara refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); const crown = props.find(x => /crown/i.test(x.target.display || x.target.rawTargetText || '')); const potion = props.find(x => /potion/i.test(x.target.display || x.target.rawTargetText || '')); add('BC14-006', (!crown || crown.riskFlags.indexOf('denied') >= 0 || crown.eventType === 'item.denied_candidate') && !!potion && potion.localClause.localClauseText !== (crown && crown.localClause.localClauseText), 'mixed denial/handoff is locally separated');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-007', props.length === 0 || props.every(x => x.safety.dreamOrVision && x.riskFlags.indexOf('dream') >= 0), 'dream safety flag is sticky');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('Mara says, "Take the key."', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-008', props.length === 0 || props.every(x => x.safety.quotedSpeech && x.riskFlags.indexOf('quote_present') >= 0), 'quote safety flag is sticky');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the idea of a brass key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-009', props.length === 0 || props.every(x => x.riskFlags.indexOf('abstract_or_idea') >= 0 || x.status !== 'proposed'), 'abstract item risk preserved');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold. Old Bren pays you the promised 15 gold.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-010', props.some(x => /^reward\.promise/.test(x.eventType) && x.riskFlags.indexOf('promise_not_payment') >= 0) && props.some(x => /^currency\.gain|^reward\.payment/.test(x.eventType) && x.operation.keyFamily === 'reward'), 'promise and payment separate with reward key suggestion');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('After the road, you enter The Sturdy Oak inn.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); p = props.find(x => x.family === 'place'); add('BC14-011', !!(p && p.target.targetFamily === 'place' && p.target.targetId === 'place_sturdy_oak'), 'place aliases bind to canonical place id');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('The chapel is visible from the market lane.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-012', props.length === 0 || props.every(x => x.riskFlags.indexOf('weak_scene_reference') >= 0 || x.eventType === 'place.reference'), 'weak place references remain weak');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You learned Moonblade.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-013', props.length === 0 || props.every(x => x.riskFlags.indexOf('output_only_ability_claim') >= 0 || x.validationReadiness !== 'validator_ready'), 'output-only ability learning is risky');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('People say you died in the ruins.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); add('BC14-014', props.length === 0 || props.every(x => x.riskFlags.indexOf('rumor') >= 0), 'death rumor risk preserved');
      r = base(); hits = this.capProposals(new Array(80).fill(0).map((_,i)=>({ id:'p'+i })), 20); add('BC14-015', hits.length === 20, 'capProposals helper enforces caps');
      r = base(); before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, abilities:r.abilities, time:r.time }); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the apple. You arrive at the chapel. You attempt Ember Ward, but nothing happens.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, abilities:r.abilities, time:r.time }); add('BC14-016', before === after, 'ProposalBuilder does not mutate gameplay truth');
      r = base(); props = this.fromHits(r, [{ family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', name:'Crown of Dawn', sourceHook:'command_output', sourceText:'[WorldLedger] /inventory Crown of Dawn', localClausePreview:'[WorldLedger] /inventory Crown of Dawn', flags:{ commandText:true }, completedAction:true, parserOnly:true, canCommitDirectly:false }], 'command_output'); add('BC14-017', props.length === 0, 'non-canon command output blocked before proposal');
      r = base(); hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput('You pick up the iron key.', r); props = this.fromHits(r, hits, 'output'); const vals = WL.CanonValidator.validateAll(r, props); add('BC14-018', vals.length === props.length && vals[0].status === 'accepted', 'CanonValidator remains compatible with v14 proposal schema');
      return results;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 12. CanonValidator Lock
  // ============================================================
  WL.CanonValidator = {
    lockVersion: WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION,
    statuses: Object.freeze(['accepted','rejected','pending','deferred','blocked','malformed','duplicate_skipped']),
    disabledFamilies: Object.freeze({
      economy: true, shop: true, buy: true, sell: true, barter: true, debt: true,
      crafting: true, materials: true, recipes: true,
      investigation: true, cases: true, evidence: true,
      reputation: true, factions: true, domain: true, labor: true,
      absorption: true, evolution: true, advancedCombat: true, warfare: true,
      scale: true, partyAdvanced: true, multiProtagonist: true, generatedCards: true,
      manualMutation: true
    }),
    repeatSensitiveTypes: Object.freeze({
      'item.acquire': true, 'item.consume': true, 'item.destroy': true, 'item.transfer': true,
      'currency.gain': true, 'reward.payment': true, 'progression.xp_claim': true,
      'time.pass': true, 'ability.stabilize_claim': true, 'quest.complete': true,
      'card.write': true, 'startup.import': true, 'migration.import': true
    }),
    maxResults(root) { return WL.U.clamp(root && root.features && root.features.maxValidationResultsPerTurn || 25, 5, 50); },
    maxReasons(root) { return WL.U.clamp(root && root.features && root.features.maxRejectionReasonsPerProposal || 5, 2, 10); },
    maxPreview(root) { return WL.U.clamp(root && root.features && root.features.maxValidationAuditPreviewLength || 180, 60, 360); },
    // Supports both validateAll(root, proposals) and validateAll(proposals, root).
    validateAll(a, b) {
      let root, proposals;
      if (Array.isArray(a)) { proposals = a; root = b || WL.StateManager.root(); }
      else { root = a || WL.StateManager.root(); proposals = Array.isArray(b) ? b : []; }
      const out = [];
      const max = this.maxResults(root);
      for (let i = 0; i < proposals.length && out.length < max; i++) out.push(this.validate(proposals[i], root));
      const omitted = Math.max(0, proposals.length - out.length);
      if (omitted) out.push(this.pending({ id: 'validation_cap_' + WL.U.hash(String(omitted)), type: 'validator.cap', family: 'validator', target: {}, opKey: '', sourceText: '', sourceKind: 'validator', data: {} }, 'validation cap reached; omitted ' + omitted + ' low-priority proposals', ['cap']));
      root.pending.lastValidations = out;
      this.auditResults(root, out);
      return out;
    },
    validate(a, b) {
      let proposal, root;
      if (a && a.schemaVersion) { root = a; proposal = b; } else { proposal = a; root = b || WL.StateManager.root(); }
      const malformed = this.validateStructure(proposal, root);
      if (malformed) return malformed;
      const source = this.validateSource(proposal, root); if (source) return source;
      const clause = this.validateClause(proposal, root);
      const target = this.validateTarget(proposal, root); if (target) return target;
      const duplicate = this.checkDuplicateRisk(proposal, root); if (duplicate) return duplicate;
      const familyResult = this.validateFamily(proposal, root, clause);
      return familyResult || this.pending(proposal, 'no active Code Pass 1 family rule', ['family shell-only']);
    },
    validateStructure(p, root) {
      if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') return this.malformed({ id: 'missing_proposal', type: 'unknown', family: 'unknown', target: {}, opKey: '', sourceText: '', data: {} }, 'proposal missing or not an object');
      if (!p.type && !p.eventType) return this.malformed(p, 'proposal missing event type');
      if (!p.family) return this.malformed(p, 'proposal missing family');
      if (p.proposalOnly === false || p.canCommitDirectly === true) return this.block(p, 'proposal claims direct commit path; blocked', ['ProposalBuilder must not commit canon']);
      return null;
    },
    validateSource(p, root) {
      const src = WL.U.slug(p.sourceKind || p.sourceHook || (p.sourceInfo && p.sourceInfo.sourceHook) || 'unknown_noncanon');
      const text = WL.U.norm(p.sourceText || p.localClausePreview || '');
      const blocked = { command:1, command_output:1, context:1, story_card:1, protected_card:1, generated_card:1, debug:1, audit:1, help:1, releasecheck:1, old_log:1, migration_log:1, unknown_noncanon:1 };
      if (blocked[src]) return this.block(p, 'unsafe source blocked: ' + src, ['source gate']);
      if (/^\s*\[(?:WorldLedger|AIDRPG)\]/i.test(text)) return this.block(p, 'diagnostic/command-prefixed text cannot become canon', ['source gate']);
      if (src !== 'input' && src !== 'output' && src !== 'setup' && src !== 'migration' && src !== 'manual') return this.block(p, 'source not allowlisted: ' + src, ['source gate']);
      return null;
    },
    validateClause(p, root) {
      const risk = p.riskFlags || [];
      const flags = Object.assign({}, p.flags || {});
      const addFlag = (name, cond) => { if (cond) flags[name] = true; };
      const text = WL.U.lower((p.sourceText || '') + ' ' + (p.localClausePreview || ''));
      addFlag('quotedSpeech', risk.indexOf('quote_present') >= 0 || flags.quote);
      addFlag('dreamOrVision', risk.indexOf('dream') >= 0 || flags.dream);
      addFlag('rumorOrClaim', risk.indexOf('rumor') >= 0 || flags.rumor);
      addFlag('failedOrRevoked', risk.indexOf('denied') >= 0 || flags.denied || /\b(?:snatches? .* away|dissolves|wake empty-handed|nothing remains|no usable|collapses)\b/i.test(text));
      addFlag('negatedOrRefused', /\b(?:do not|don't|did not|didn't|decide not|refuse|refuses|never take|not take|not picked|leave it)\b/i.test(text));
      addFlag('hypothetical', risk.indexOf('hypothetical') >= 0 || /\b(?:could|would|might|if you wanted|possible|almost|nearly)\b/i.test(text));
      addFlag('futureIntent', risk.indexOf('future_intent') >= 0 || /\b(?:will|going to|plans? to|later|tomorrow|soon|eventually)\b/i.test(text));
      addFlag('attemptOnly', risk.indexOf('attempt_only') >= 0 || risk.indexOf('unconfirmed_input') >= 0 || p.sourceKind === 'input' || p.sourceHook === 'input');
      addFlag('abstractOrMetaphorical', risk.indexOf('abstract_or_idea') >= 0 || /\b(?:idea of|concept of|thought of|memory of|symbol of|felt like|as if)\b/i.test(text));
      addFlag('visibleOnly', risk.indexOf('visible_only') >= 0 || flags.visibleOnly);
      addFlag('nonliteral', risk.indexOf('nonliteral') >= 0 || /\b(?:practicing narration|no time passes|felt like years|time flies|dead tired)\b/i.test(text));
      addFlag('pretendOrFalse', risk.indexOf('pretend') >= 0 || flags.pretend || /\b(?:pretend|fake|not real|as a joke|only mimicking)\b/i.test(text));
      return flags;
    },
    validateTarget(p, root) {
      const target = p.target || {};
      const noTargetNeeded = /^body\.|^time\.|^validator\./.test(p.type || p.eventType || '');
      if (!noTargetNeeded && (!target.display && !target.id && !target.raw)) return this.malformed(p, 'target missing or unbound');
      if ((p.riskFlags || []).indexOf('ambiguous_target') >= 0 && !noTargetNeeded) return this.malformed(p, 'target ambiguous or sanitizer rejected target');
      if (/^(?:thing|stuff|something|anything|it|them|unknown|place reference)$/i.test(target.display || target.raw || '') && !/^quest\./.test(p.type || '')) return this.pending(p, 'generic target cannot become durable canon', ['target gate']);
      return null;
    },
    checkDuplicateRisk(p, root) {
      const t = p.type || p.eventType || '';
      if (!this.repeatSensitiveTypes[t] && p.family !== 'reward') return null;
      const key = this.duplicateKeyFor(p);
      if (!key) return null;
      let dup = false;
      if (t === 'currency.gain' || t === 'reward.payment') dup = WL.OperationLedger.hasReward(root, key) || WL.OperationLedger.has(root, 'recentKeys', p.opKey || key);
      else if (/^card\./.test(t)) dup = WL.OperationLedger.hasCard(root, key);
      else dup = WL.OperationLedger.has(root, 'recentKeys', p.opKey || key) || WL.OperationLedger.hasPermanent(root, key);
      if (dup) {
        if (WL.AuditLogger) WL.AuditLogger.duplicate(root, key, 'CanonValidator duplicate skip for ' + t);
        return this.duplicate(p, 'operation key already used', ['duplicate gate'], key);
      }
      return null;
    },
    duplicateKeyFor(p) {
      const t = p.type || p.eventType || '';
      const reward = p.data && p.data.reward;
      if ((t === 'currency.gain' || t === 'reward.payment') && reward) return WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['reward_payment', reward.amount, reward.coin, WL.U.hash(p.sourceText || '')]);
      return p.opKey || p.dedupeKey || WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['validation', t, p.target && (p.target.id || p.target.display || p.target.raw), WL.U.hash(p.sourceText || '')]);
    },
    validateFamily(p, root, safety) {
      if (this.isDeferredProposal(p)) return this.defer(p, 'system is deferred in Code Pass 1: ' + (p.family || p.type), ['deferred gate']);
      const family = p.family || 'unknown';
      if (family === 'inventory') return this.validateInventory(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'ability') return this.validateAbility(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'body') return this.validateBody(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'quest' || family === 'reward') return this.validateQuest(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'actor') return this.validateActor(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'place') return this.validatePlaceScene(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'time') return this.validateTime(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'progression') return this.validateProgression(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'card') return this.validateCardMirror(p, root, safety);
      if (family === 'migration') return this.validateMigration(p, root, safety);
      return this.pending(p, 'family has no active Code Pass 1 validator: ' + family, ['family shell-only']);
    },
    isDeferredProposal(p) {
      const s = WL.U.lower((p.family || '') + ' ' + (p.type || '') + ' ' + (p.effectOwner || '') + ' ' + (p.reducerHint || ''));
      return /\b(?:economy|shop|buy|sell|craft|crafting|material|recipe|investigation|case|reputation|faction|domain|labor|worker|absorption|evolution|warfare|scale|multi.?protagonist|generated.?card|manual.?mutation)\b/.test(s);
    },
    unsafeRealCanon(s) { return !!(s.quotedSpeech || s.dreamOrVision || s.rumorOrClaim || s.pretendOrFalse || s.commandText || s.diagnosticText); },
    deniedOrRevoked(s) { return !!(s.failedOrRevoked || s.negatedOrRefused); },
    validateInventory(p, root, s) {
      const t = p.type || '';
      if (t === 'item.intent' || p.sourceKind === 'input') return this.pending(p, 'input item action is intent only, not acquisition', ['input intent']);
      if (t === 'item.visible') return this.pending(p, 'visible/available item is not owned inventory', ['visible is not owned']);
      if ((t === 'item.take_attempt' || t === 'item.handoff_candidate' || t === 'item.acquire') && (this.deniedOrRevoked(s) || s.abstractOrMetaphorical || s.dreamOrVision || s.quotedSpeech || s.rumorOrClaim || s.pretendOrFalse)) return this.reject(p, 'inventory acquisition blocked by local clause safety flags', ['inventory safety']);
      if (t !== 'item.acquire' && t !== 'item.handoff_candidate' && t !== 'currency.gain') return this.pending(p, 'inventory event is shell-only: ' + t, ['inventory shell']);
      if (t === 'currency.gain') {
        if (/\b(?:promise|promises|will pay|offers? to pay|later|if you)\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '')) return this.reject(p, 'reward promise is not payment', ['promise/payment split']);
        if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || this.deniedOrRevoked(s) || s.futureIntent || s.hypothetical) return this.reject(p, 'unsafe currency source blocked', ['currency safety']);
        return this.accept(p, 'direct currency payment confirmed', ['payment gate']);
      }
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || this.deniedOrRevoked(s) || s.futureIntent || s.hypothetical || s.abstractOrMetaphorical || s.visibleOnly || s.attemptOnly) return this.reject(p, 'inventory acquisition blocked by local clause safety flags', ['inventory safety']);
      return this.accept(p, 'confirmed output acquisition/handoff', ['inventory acquisition']);
    },
    validateAbility(p, root, s) {
      const t = p.type || '';
      if (s.quotedSpeech || s.dreamOrVision || s.pretendOrFalse) return this.reject(p, 'fake/quoted/dream ability cannot become canon', ['ability safety']);
      if (t === 'ability.intent') return this.pending(p, 'input ability mention is intent only', ['ability intent']);
      if (t === 'ability.stabilize_claim') return this.pending(p, 'output-only ability learning claim lacks validated source/training', ['candidate is not stable']);
      if (t === 'ability.evidence') {
        if (s.failedOrRevoked || s.negatedOrRefused || /\b(?:nothing happens|no visible effect|collapses|no usable technique|fails?)\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '')) return this.accept(p, 'failed/soft ability evidence preserved; no stable ability', ['soft_fail']);
        return this.accept(p, 'ability training evidence allowed as candidate/evidence', ['ability evidence']);
      }
      return this.pending(p, 'ability event requires future specific validator: ' + t, ['ability shell']);
    },
    validateBody(p, root, s) {
      const t = p.type || '';
      if (t === 'body.death_claim') {
        if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || s.nonliteral || /\b(?:people say|rumor|dead tired|as if dead)\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '')) return this.reject(p, 'death/body boundary blocked by rumor/quote/dream/metaphor', ['death boundary']);
        return this.pending(p, 'direct death claim requires later high-safety death boundary gate', ['death boundary']);
      }
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || s.nonliteral) return this.reject(p, 'body/status change blocked by non-canon source or metaphor', ['body safety']);
      if (t === 'body.fatigue') return this.accept(p, /\bexhausted|barely stand|drained completely\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '') ? 'exhaustion wording accepted distinctly from fatigue' : 'fatigue wording accepted distinctly; no severe status upgrade', ['body status']);
      if (t === 'body.injury') return this.accept(p, 'direct literal injury/status wording accepted', ['body injury']);
      return this.pending(p, 'body event pending future body validator: ' + t, ['body shell']);
    },
    validateQuest(p, root, s) {
      const t = p.type || '';
      if (t === 'reward.promise') {
        if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s)) return this.reject(p, 'unsafe reward promise source', ['reward safety']);
        return this.accept(p, 'reward promise tracked as promise only; not paid', ['promise is not payment']);
      }
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || s.hypothetical) return this.reject(p, 'unsafe quest source', ['quest safety']);
      if (t === 'quest.offer') return this.pending(p, 'quest offered/requested but not necessarily accepted', ['quest offer']);
      if (t === 'quest.accept') return s.attemptOnly && p.sourceKind === 'input' ? this.pending(p, 'input quest acceptance intent needs output/setup support', ['quest intent']) : this.accept(p, 'quest acceptance/start confirmed', ['quest acceptance']);
      if (t === 'quest.complete') return this.accept(p, 'quest completion claim accepted for owner reducer review', ['quest completion']);
      return this.pending(p, 'quest event shell-only: ' + t, ['quest shell']);
    },
    validateActor(p, root, s) {
      const text = WL.U.lower(p.sourceText || '');
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || /\b(?:not here|elsewhere|absent|only mentioned|named after|training dummy)\b/i.test(text)) return this.reject(p, 'actor presence blocked by absent/reference-only/non-canon clause', ['actor presence']);
      if ((p.target && p.target.display || '').toLowerCase() === 'guard' && /veteran guard/i.test(text)) return this.reject(p, 'short generic compound actor suppressed in favor of longer target', ['compound actor']);
      return this.accept(p, 'actor directly present/acting in scene', ['actor presence']);
    },
    validatePlaceScene(p, root, s) {
      const t = p.type || '';
      if (p.sourceKind === 'input') return this.pending(p, 'travel/place input is intent, not arrival', ['heading toward is not arrival']);
      if (t === 'place.reference' || s.visibleOnly || /\b(?:visible from|nearby|down the street|road to|mentions|talks about|consider going)\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '')) return this.pending(p, 'weak/visible place mention cannot overwrite current scene', ['weak scene reference']);
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || this.deniedOrRevoked(s) || s.futureIntent || s.hypothetical) return this.reject(p, 'place arrival blocked by unsafe clause', ['place safety']);
      if (t === 'place.current' || (t === 'place.intent' && /\b(?:enter|entered|step inside|walk into|move into|head into|arrive at|arrive in|reach|return to|go inside|descend into|push through)\b/i.test(p.sourceText || ''))) return this.accept(p, 'strong named arrival/current-scene confirmation accepted', ['place arrival']);
      if (t === 'place.intent') return this.pending(p, 'place intent lacks output arrival confirmation', ['place intent']);
      return this.pending(p, 'place event shell-only: ' + t, ['place shell']);
    },
    validateTime(p, root, s) {
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || s.nonliteral || this.deniedOrRevoked(s) || /\bno time passes\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '')) return this.reject(p, 'time/age mutation blocked by nonliteral/quoted/dream/negated clause', ['time safety']);
      if (/\byears?\b/i.test(p.sourceText || '')) return this.pending(p, 'literal long time passage accepted as time note but age policy is deferred', ['age policy deferred']);
      return this.accept(p, 'literal time passage accepted', ['time passage']);
    },
    validateProgression(p, root, s) {
      if (this.unsafeRealCanon(s) || s.nonliteral || !/\b\d{1,6}\s*XP\b/i.test(p.sourceText || p.target && p.target.display || '')) return this.reject(p, 'vague or unsafe XP/progression claim rejected', ['XP safety']);
      return this.defer(p, 'progression commit/formula support is deferred in this Code Pass 1 lock', ['progression deferred']);
    },
    validateCardMirror(p, root, s) { return this.defer(p, 'card mirror/live/generated card validation is deferred; cards never create gameplay canon', ['card mirror only']); },
    validateMigration(p, root, s) { return this.defer(p, 'migration may quarantine/repair structure only; old prose cannot become canon', ['migration safety']); },
    acceptedResult(result) { return result.status === 'accepted' && result.canonAllowed === true && result.reducerAllowed === true; },
    resultMap(status, notes) {
      if (status === 'accepted' && notes && notes.indexOf('soft_fail') >= 0) return 'soft_fail';
      if (status === 'accepted') return 'accept';
      if (status === 'rejected' || status === 'blocked' || status === 'malformed') return 'reject';
      if (status === 'duplicate_skipped') return 'duplicate_skip';
      if (status === 'deferred') return 'deferred';
      return 'pending';
    },
    requiredReducerFor(p, status, notes) {
      if (status !== 'accepted') return '';
      const t = p.type || '';
      if (/^item\.|^currency\./.test(t) || p.family === 'inventory') return 'InventoryReducerLite';
      if (/^ability\./.test(t) || p.family === 'ability') return 'AbilityReducerLite';
      if (/^body\./.test(t) || p.family === 'body') return 'BodyReducer';
      if (/^quest\.|^reward\./.test(t) || p.family === 'quest') return 'QuestReducerLite';
      if (/^actor\./.test(t) || p.family === 'actor') return 'ActorReducerLite';
      if (/^place\./.test(t) || p.family === 'place') return 'PlaceReducerLite';
      if (/^time\./.test(t) || p.family === 'time') return 'TimeReducerLite';
      return '';
    },
    makeResult(p, status, reason, notes, duplicateKey) {
      notes = Array.isArray(notes) ? notes : (notes ? [String(notes)] : []);
      const reasons = [WL.U.norm(reason || status)].concat(notes).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 8);
      const requiredReducer = this.requiredReducerFor(p, status, notes);
      const reducerAllowed = status === 'accepted' && !!requiredReducer;
      const canonAllowed = status === 'accepted';
      const source = { sourceKind: p.sourceKind || p.sourceHook || '', sourceHash: p.sourceInfo && p.sourceInfo.textHash || WL.U.hash(p.sourceText || '') };
      const localClause = { preview: WL.U.norm(p.localClausePreview || p.sourceText || '').slice(0, 180), riskFlags: (p.riskFlags || []).slice(0, 12) };
      const safety = this.validateClause(p, null);
      const result = {
        validationVersion: WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION,
        proposalId: p.id || '', family: p.family || '', eventType: p.type || p.eventType || '', type: p.type || p.eventType || '',
        ownerModule: p.effectOwner || p.reducerHint || '', targetKey: p.target && (p.target.id || p.target.display || p.target.raw) || '', targetDisplayName: p.target && (p.target.display || p.target.raw) || '',
        status, canonAllowed, reducerAllowed, operationKey: p.opKey || duplicateKey || '', duplicate: status === 'duplicate_skipped', reasons,
        reason: reasons[0], result: this.resultMap(status, notes), requiredReducer, dirtyFamiliesIfAccepted: reducerAllowed ? [p.family || 'unknown'] : [],
        auditLine: this.auditLine(status, p, reasons[0]), source, localClause, safety, notes,
        target: p.target || {}, opKey: p.opKey || duplicateKey || '', sourceText: p.sourceText || '', data: p.data || {}
      };
      return result;
    },
    auditLine(status, p, reason) {
      const target = p && p.target && (p.target.display || p.target.raw || p.target.id) || 'no target';
      return (status + ' ' + (p && (p.type || p.eventType) || 'unknown') + ' ' + target + ': ' + reason).slice(0, 220);
    },
    accept(p, reason, notes) { return this.makeResult(p, 'accepted', reason, notes); },
    reject(p, reason, notes) { return this.makeResult(p, 'rejected', reason, notes); },
    pending(p, reason, notes) { return this.makeResult(p, 'pending', reason, notes); },
    defer(p, reason, notes) { return this.makeResult(p, 'deferred', reason, notes); },
    block(p, reason, notes) { return this.makeResult(p, 'blocked', reason, notes); },
    malformed(p, reason, notes) { return this.makeResult(p || {}, 'malformed', reason, notes); },
    duplicate(p, reason, notes, key) { return this.makeResult(p, 'duplicate_skipped', reason, notes, key); },
    auditResults(root, results) {
      if (!root || !root.pending) return;
      const maxLines = WL.U.clamp(root.features && root.features.maxValidationAuditLinesPerTurn || 12, 4, 30);
      if (!Array.isArray(root.audits.validationAudit)) root.audits.validationAudit = [];
      const prioritized = (results || []).slice().sort((a,b) => {
        const rank = { accepted:0, duplicate_skipped:1, blocked:2, malformed:3, rejected:4, deferred:5, pending:6 };
        return (rank[a.status] || 9) - (rank[b.status] || 9);
      }).slice(0, maxLines);
      for (let i=0;i<prioritized.length;i++) {
        const line = prioritized[i].auditLine || this.auditLine(prioritized[i].status, prioritized[i], prioritized[i].reason);
        root.audits.validationAudit.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), line: line.slice(0, this.maxPreview(root)), status: prioritized[i].status, type: prioritized[i].eventType || prioritized[i].type });
        WL.AuditLogger.validation(root, { family: prioritized[i].family, result: prioritized[i].status, reason: prioritized[i].reason, preview: line.slice(0, this.maxPreview(root)) });
      }
      WL.U.capArray(root.audits.validationAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.validations || 40);
    },
    inspect(root) {
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION === this.lockVersion ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'CanonValidator lock present: ' + this.lockVersion } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'CanonValidator lock mismatch' });
      ['validateAll','validate','validateSource','validateClause','validateTarget','validateFamily','checkDuplicateRisk','auditResults'].forEach(fn => rows.push(typeof this[fn] === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'function present: ' + fn } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'function missing: ' + fn }));
      rows.push(root.features && root.features.canonValidatorLockEnabled === true ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'canon validator lock feature enabled' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'canon validator lock feature missing' });
      const testRoot = WL.StateManager.init({});
      const p = { id:'inspect', family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', sourceKind:'command_output', sourceText:'[WorldLedger] /inventory\nYou pick up Crown.', target:{display:'Crown', id:'item_crown'}, opKey:'inspect', riskFlags:[], flags:{}, data:{}, proposalOnly:true, canCommitDirectly:false };
      const r = this.validate(p, testRoot);
      rows.push(r.status === 'blocked' && r.reducerAllowed === false ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'unsafe source blocked before reducer' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'unsafe source did not block' });
      const before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv: testRoot.inventory, body: testRoot.body, quests: testRoot.quests, actors: testRoot.actors, places: testRoot.places, time: testRoot.time, abilities: testRoot.abilities });
      this.validate({ id:'inspect2', family:'inventory', type:'item.acquire', sourceKind:'output', sourceText:'You pick up the apple.', target:{display:'Apple', id:'item_apple'}, opKey:'inspect2', riskFlags:[], flags:{}, data:{}, proposalOnly:true, canCommitDirectly:false }, testRoot);
      const after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv: testRoot.inventory, body: testRoot.body, quests: testRoot.quests, actors: testRoot.actors, places: testRoot.places, time: testRoot.time, abilities: testRoot.abilities });
      rows.push(before === after ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'CanonValidator does not mutate gameplay buckets' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'CanonValidator mutated gameplay buckets' });
      return rows;
    },
    makeTestProposal(root, type, family, sourceText, opts) {
      opts = opts || {};
      const display = opts.targetDisplay || opts.name || 'Test Target';
      return {
        id: opts.id || 'test_' + WL.U.hash([type, family, sourceText, display].join('|')),
        proposalOnly: true, canCommitDirectly: false, proposalVersion: WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION,
        type, family, effectOwner: opts.effectOwner || '', reducerHint: opts.reducerHint || '',
        sourceKind: opts.sourceKind || 'output', sourceText: sourceText || '', localClausePreview: (opts.localClausePreview || sourceText || '').slice(0,180),
        sourceInfo: { textHash: WL.U.hash(sourceText || '') }, flags: Object.assign({}, opts.flags || {}), riskFlags: (opts.riskFlags || []).slice(),
        target: opts.target || { display, raw: display, id: (family || 'target') + '_' + WL.U.slug(display), safeForCommit: true },
        evidence: { sourceHash: WL.U.hash(sourceText || ''), localClausePreview: sourceText || '', completedAction: opts.completedAction !== false, attemptOnly: !!opts.attemptOnly },
        opKey: opts.opKey || WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['test', type, display, WL.U.hash(sourceText || '')]),
        dedupeKey: opts.dedupeKey || '', sourceUseKey: opts.sourceUseKey || '', data: Object.assign({}, opts.data || {})
      };
    },
    fromText(root, text, sourceKind) {
      const hits = sourceKind === 'input' ? WL.EventScannerLite.scanInput(text, root) : WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput(text, root);
      return WL.ProposalBuilder.fromHits(root, hits, sourceKind || 'output');
    },
    runBC11SelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const base = () => WL.StateManager.init({});
      let r, p, v, props, before, after, key;
      try {
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r, 'item.acquire','inventory','[AIDRPG] Inventory says you pick up Crown.', { sourceKind:'command_output', targetDisplay:'Crown' }); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-001', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'command output blocked');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r, 'item.acquire','inventory','Context says you own Crown.', { sourceKind:'context', targetDisplay:'Crown' }); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-002', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'context text blocked');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r, 'item.acquire','inventory','Story Card says you own Crown.', { sourceKind:'story_card', targetDisplay:'Crown' }); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-003', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'Story Card text blocked');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'I take the jeweled crown.', 'input'); v = this.validate(props[0], r); add('BC11-004', v && v.status !== 'accepted' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'input item attempt not acquisition');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'You decide not to take the rusted iron key.', 'output'); v = props[0] ? this.validate(props[0], r) : this.validate(this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You decide not to take the rusted iron key.',{targetDisplay:'Rusted Iron Key', riskFlags:['denied']}), r); add('BC11-005', v.status === 'rejected', 'denied item rejected');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'You try to grab the rusted iron key, but the goblin snatches it away first.', 'output'); v = props[0] ? this.validate(props[0], r) : this.validate(this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You try to grab the rusted iron key, but the goblin snatches it away first.',{targetDisplay:'Rusted Iron Key', riskFlags:['denied']}), r); add('BC11-006', v.status === 'rejected', 'failed grab rejected');
        r = base(); const crown = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.',{targetDisplay:'Jeweled Crown', riskFlags:['denied']}); const potion = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','Mira hands you the red healing potion.',{targetDisplay:'Red Healing Potion'}); add('BC11-007', this.validate(crown,r).status === 'rejected' && this.validate(potion,r).status === 'accepted', 'mixed clause: crown rejected, potion accepted');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the idea of a brass key.',{targetDisplay:'Brass Key', riskFlags:['abstract_or_idea']}); add('BC11-008', this.validate(p,r).status === 'rejected', 'abstract item rejected');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','In a dream, you receive Crown of Dawn, then wake empty-handed.',{targetDisplay:'Crown of Dawn', riskFlags:['dream','denied']}); add('BC11-009', this.validate(p,r).status === 'rejected', 'dream item rejected');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat.', 'input'); v = props[0] ? this.validate(props[0], r) : this.validate(this.makeTestProposal(r,'ability.evidence','ability','I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat.',{targetDisplay:'Ember Ward', sourceKind:'input', riskFlags:['unconfirmed_input','attempt_only']}), r); add('BC11-010', v.status === 'accepted' && v.result === 'accept', 'ability training allowed as evidence, not stable mastery');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'ability.stabilize_claim','ability','You learned Moonblade.',{targetDisplay:'Moonblade'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-011', v.status !== 'accepted' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'output-only learned ability not stable');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'ability.stabilize_claim','ability','You attempt Void Lantern Veil, but the veil collapses and no usable technique remains.',{targetDisplay:'Void Lantern Veil', riskFlags:['denied']}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-012', v.status !== 'accepted' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'failed ability rejected as stable');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'body.fatigue','body','Your legs shake and you are exhausted by the climb.',{targetDisplay:'fatigue'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-013', v.status === 'accepted' && /exhaustion/.test(v.reason), 'exhausted accepted distinctly');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'body.fatigue','body','You are fatigued and worn out.',{targetDisplay:'fatigue'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-014', v.status === 'accepted' && !/exhaustion/.test(v.reason), 'fatigue does not auto-exhaust');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'body.death_claim','body','People say you died in the ruins.',{targetDisplay:'death', riskFlags:['rumor']}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-015', v.status === 'rejected', 'death rumor rejected');
