        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'You enter the Chapel.', 'output'); v = props[0] ? this.validate(props[0],r) : this.validate(this.makeTestProposal(r,'place.current','place','You enter the Chapel.',{targetDisplay:'Chapel'}), r); add('BC11-017', v.status === 'accepted', 'strong arrival accepted');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.', 'output'); v = props[0] ? this.validate(props[0],r) : this.validate(this.makeTestProposal(r,'place.current','place','After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.',{targetDisplay:'Drunken Huntsman'}), r); add('BC11-018', v.status === 'accepted' && !/road$/i.test(v.targetDisplayName), 'named arrival beats road');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'time.pass','time','You say, "Twenty years pass," while practicing narration. No time passes.',{targetDisplay:'time', riskFlags:['quote_present','nonliteral','denied']}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-019', v.status === 'rejected', 'nonliteral time rejected');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'time.pass','time','Twenty years pass in exile.',{targetDisplay:'time'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-020', v.status === 'pending' || v.status === 'accepted', 'literal time pending/accepted, not duplicate');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold if you clear the wolves.', 'output'); v = props.find ? (props.map(x=>this.validate(x,r)).find(x=>x.eventType==='reward.promise') || this.validate(this.makeTestProposal(r,'reward.promise','quest','Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold if you clear the wolves.',{targetDisplay:'15 gold', data:{reward:{amount:15, coin:'gold'}}}),r)) : null; add('BC11-021', v.status === 'accepted' && v.result === 'accept' && /promise/.test(v.reason), 'reward promise not payment');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'currency.gain','inventory','Old Bren pays you the promised 15 gold.',{targetDisplay:'15 gold', data:{reward:{amount:15, coin:'gold'}}}); key = this.duplicateKeyFor(p); WL.OperationLedger.addReward(r, key, { test:true }); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-022', v.status === 'duplicate_skipped' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'reward duplicate skipped');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'actor.present','actor','The goblin is definitely not here.',{targetDisplay:'Goblin'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-023', v.status === 'rejected', 'absent actor not present');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'actor.present','actor','A veteran guard stands nearby.',{targetDisplay:'Veteran Guard'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-024', v.status === 'accepted' && /Veteran Guard/.test(v.targetDisplayName), 'compound actor preserved');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'progression.xp_claim','progression','This was a learning experience.',{targetDisplay:'experience'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-025', v.status === 'rejected', 'vague XP rejected');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'crafting.project','crafting','You craft an iron sword.',{targetDisplay:'Iron Sword', effectOwner:'CraftingReducer'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC11-026', v.status === 'deferred' || v.status === 'blocked', 'deferred crafting blocked');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You decide not to take the rusted iron key.',{targetDisplay:'Rusted Iron Key', riskFlags:['denied']}); v = this.validate(p,r); this.auditResults(r,[v]); const audit = (r.audits.validationAudit || [])[0]; add('BC11-027', !!audit && audit.line && audit.line.length <= this.maxPreview(r), 'validation audit compact');
        r = base(); before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, time:r.time, abilities:r.abilities }); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the apple.',{targetDisplay:'Apple'}); this.validate(p,r); after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, time:r.time, abilities:r.abilities }); add('BC11-028', before === after, 'CanonValidator never mutates gameplay buckets');
      } catch (e) { add('BC11-ERR', false, 'self-test exception: ' + (e && e.message || e)); }
      return results;
    }
  };


  // ============================================================
  // 12B. CanonValidator V0.15 Shell Lock Correction
  // ============================================================
  (function () {
    const CV = WL.CanonValidator;
    if (!CV) return;
    CV.lockVersion = WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION;
    CV.validationResultSchemaVersion = 'worldledger.validation-result.schema.v15';
    CV.decisionStages = Object.freeze(['structure','source','clause','target','owner','duplicate','family','result']);
    CV.familyOwnerContracts = Object.freeze({
      inventory: 'InventoryReducerLite', ability: 'AbilityReducerLite', body: 'BodyReducer', quest: 'QuestReducerLite', reward: 'QuestReducerLite',
      actor: 'ActorReducerLite', place: 'PlaceReducerLite', time: 'TimeReducerLite', progression: 'ProgressionReducerLiteFuture',
      card: 'CardSyncLite', migration: 'MigrationManager', world: 'WorldEntityReducerFuture'
    });
    CV.validationDoctrine = Object.freeze([
      'CanonValidator is the only truth gate between EventProposal and reducer commit.',
      'Validation may accept, reject, pending, defer, block, mark malformed, or duplicate-skip; it may not mutate gameplay state.',
      'Input intent, context reminders, command output, Story Cards, audit text, dreams, rumors, quotes, and metaphors cannot become direct canon.',
      'Owner hints are checked before reducers; a proposal cannot route inventory truth to an actor/body/place owner.',
      'Repeat-sensitive events must carry ledger-safe operation keys before reducer ownership can apply them.'
    ]);

    CV.expectedOwnerFor = function (p) {
      const type = p && (p.type || p.eventType) || '';
      const family = p && p.family || '';
      if (/^item\.|^currency\./.test(type) || family === 'inventory') return 'InventoryReducerLite';
      if (/^ability\./.test(type) || family === 'ability') return 'AbilityReducerLite';
      if (/^body\./.test(type) || family === 'body') return 'BodyReducer';
      if (/^quest\.|^reward\./.test(type) || family === 'quest' || family === 'reward') return 'QuestReducerLite';
      if (/^actor\./.test(type) || family === 'actor') return 'ActorReducerLite';
      if (/^place\./.test(type) || family === 'place') return 'PlaceReducerLite';
      if (/^time\./.test(type) || family === 'time') return 'TimeReducerLite';
      if (/^progression\./.test(type) || family === 'progression') return 'ProgressionReducerLiteFuture';
      if (/^card\./.test(type) || family === 'card') return 'CardSyncLite';
      if (/^migration\./.test(type) || family === 'migration') return 'MigrationManager';
      if (family === 'world') return 'WorldEntityReducerFuture';
      return '';
    };
    CV.ownerAliases = function (owner) {
      const o = WL.U.norm(owner || '');
      const aliases = {
        InventoryReducerLite: ['InventoryReducerLite', 'InventoryReducer'],
        AbilityReducerLite: ['AbilityReducerLite', 'AbilityReducer'],
        BodyReducer: ['BodyReducer', 'BodyReducerLite'],
        QuestReducerLite: ['QuestReducerLite', 'QuestReducer'],
        ActorReducerLite: ['ActorReducerLite', 'ActorReducer'],
        PlaceReducerLite: ['PlaceReducerLite', 'PlaceReducer'],
        TimeReducerLite: ['TimeReducerLite', 'TimeReducer'],
        ProgressionReducerLiteFuture: ['ProgressionReducerLiteFuture', 'ProgressionReducerLite', 'ProgressionReducer'],
        CardSyncLite: ['CardSyncLite'],
        MigrationManager: ['MigrationManager'],
        WorldEntityReducerFuture: ['WorldEntityReducerFuture', 'NoActiveOwner']
      };
      return aliases[o] || (o ? [o] : []);
    };
    CV.ownerProvidedFor = function (p) { return WL.U.norm(p && (p.effectOwner || p.ownerModule || p.reducerHint) || ''); };
    CV.ownerMatches = function (provided, expected) {
      if (!provided || !expected) return true;
      const allowed = this.ownerAliases(expected);
      return allowed.indexOf(provided) >= 0;
    };
    CV.validateOwnerBoundary = function (p, root) {
      if (!p || this.isDeferredProposal(p)) return null;
      const expected = this.expectedOwnerFor(p);
      const provided = this.ownerProvidedFor(p);
      if (provided && expected && !this.ownerMatches(provided, expected)) {
        return this.block(p, 'owner boundary mismatch: ' + provided + ' cannot own ' + (p.type || p.eventType || p.family) + '; expected ' + expected, ['owner gate']);
      }
      return null;
    };
    CV.enrichResult = function (res, p, path) {
      if (!res || typeof res !== 'object') return res;
      const expected = this.expectedOwnerFor(p || res);
      const provided = this.ownerProvidedFor(p || res);
      res.validationVersion = WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION;
      res.validatorLockVersion = WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION;
      res.validationResultSchemaVersion = this.validationResultSchemaVersion;
      res.decisionPath = Array.isArray(path) ? path.slice(0, 12) : [];
      res.decisionStages = this.decisionStages.slice(0);
      res.ownerExpected = expected;
      res.ownerProvided = provided;
      res.ownerBoundaryOk = this.ownerMatches(provided, expected);
      res.truthGate = res.status === 'accepted' ? 'canon_allowed_for_reducer' : (res.status === 'duplicate_skipped' ? 'side_effect_already_seen' : 'not_canon');
      res.reducerAllowed = res.status === 'accepted' && !!res.requiredReducer && res.ownerBoundaryOk !== false;
      res.canonAllowed = res.status === 'accepted';
      res.validatorNotes = (res.validatorNotes || []).concat(['v15 schema lock applied']).slice(0, 8);
      return res;
    };
    CV.validateV14Base = CV.validateV14Base || CV.validate;
    CV.validate = function (a, b) {
      let proposal, root;
      if (a && a.schemaVersion) { root = a; proposal = b; } else { proposal = a; root = b || WL.StateManager.root(); }
      const path = [];
      const finish = (res, stage) => { if (stage) path.push(stage); return this.enrichResult(res, proposal || res, path); };
      path.push('structure');
      const malformed = this.validateStructure(proposal, root); if (malformed) return finish(malformed, 'result');
      path.push('source');
      const source = this.validateSource(proposal, root); if (source) return finish(source, 'result');
      path.push('clause');
      const clause = this.validateClause(proposal, root);
      path.push('target');
      const target = this.validateTarget(proposal, root); if (target) return finish(target, 'result');
      path.push('owner');
      const owner = this.validateOwnerBoundary(proposal, root); if (owner) return finish(owner, 'result');
      path.push('duplicate');
      const duplicate = this.checkDuplicateRisk(proposal, root); if (duplicate) return finish(duplicate, 'result');
      path.push('family');
      const familyResult = this.validateFamily(proposal, root, clause);
      return finish(familyResult || this.pending(proposal, 'no active Code Pass 1 family rule', ['family shell-only']), 'result');
    };

    CV.inspectV14Base = CV.inspectV14Base || CV.inspect;
    CV.inspect = function (root) {
      const rows = this.inspectV14Base(root);
      rows.push(this.validationResultSchemaVersion === 'worldledger.validation-result.schema.v15' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'validation result schema locked: ' + this.validationResultSchemaVersion } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'validation result schema missing' });
      rows.push(this.lockVersion === WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'V0.15 lock bound to global constant' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'V0.15 lock/global mismatch' });
      rows.push(typeof this.validateOwnerBoundary === 'function' && typeof this.expectedOwnerFor === 'function' ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'owner boundary gate present' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'owner boundary gate missing' });
      rows.push(Array.isArray(this.decisionStages) && this.decisionStages.indexOf('owner') >= 0 && this.decisionStages.indexOf('duplicate') >= 0 ? { status: 'PASS', label: 'decision stages include owner and duplicate gates' } : { status: 'FAIL', label: 'decision stages incomplete' });
      return rows;
    };

    CV.runBC15SelfTests = function () {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const base = () => WL.StateManager.init({});
      let r, p, v, props, before, after, key;
      try {
        r = base(); add('BC15-001', WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION === 'worldledger.canonvalidator-lock.v15' && this.lockVersion === WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION, 'CanonValidator V0.15 lock is active');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the apple.',{targetDisplay:'Apple'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-002', v.validationResultSchemaVersion === 'worldledger.validation-result.schema.v15' && v.validationVersion === WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION, 'ValidationResult schema/version fields present');
        add('BC15-003', Array.isArray(v.decisionPath) && v.decisionPath.indexOf('owner') >= 0 && v.decisionPath.indexOf('duplicate') >= 0, 'decision path records owner and duplicate gates');
        add('BC15-004', v.ownerExpected === 'InventoryReducerLite' && v.reducerAllowed === true && v.truthGate === 'canon_allowed_for_reducer', 'accepted inventory proposal routes only after truth gate');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the apple.',{targetDisplay:'Apple', effectOwner:'ActorReducerLite'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-005', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.reducerAllowed && /owner boundary/.test(v.reason), 'owner mismatch blocks reducer route');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'actor.present','actor','Mara enters the room.',{targetDisplay:'Mara', effectOwner:'InventoryReducerLite'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-006', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.canonAllowed, 'actor truth cannot be owned by inventory reducer');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','[WorldLedger] /inventory says you pick up a crown.',{sourceKind:'command_output', targetDisplay:'Crown'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-007', v.status === 'blocked' && v.truthGate === 'not_canon', 'command output blocked before reducer');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','Story Card says you own Crown of Dawn.',{sourceKind:'story_card', targetDisplay:'Crown of Dawn'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-008', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'Story Card prose blocked before reducer');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'I take the jeweled crown.', 'input'); v = this.validate(props[0], r); add('BC15-009', v.status !== 'accepted' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'input intent cannot become direct acquisition');
        r = base(); props = this.fromText(r, 'Mara refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.', 'output'); const vals = this.validateAll(r, props); const crown = vals.find(x => /crown/i.test(x.targetDisplayName || '')); const potion = vals.find(x => /potion/i.test(x.targetDisplayName || '')); add('BC15-010', (!crown || crown.status !== 'accepted') && potion && potion.status === 'accepted', 'mixed local clauses keep refusal and handoff separate');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'ability.stabilize_claim','ability','You learned Moonblade.',{targetDisplay:'Moonblade'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-011', v.status !== 'accepted' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'output-only ability learning still cannot stabilize ability');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'ability.evidence','ability','You attempt Ember Ward, but nothing happens.',{targetDisplay:'Ember Ward'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-012', v.status === 'accepted' && v.requiredReducer === 'AbilityReducerLite', 'failed/practice ability evidence can pass as candidate evidence only');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'body.death_claim','body','People say you died in the ruins.',{targetDisplay:'death', riskFlags:['rumor']}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-013', v.status === 'rejected' && !v.canonAllowed, 'death rumor rejected');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'place.current','place','The chapel is visible from the market lane.',{targetDisplay:'Chapel', riskFlags:['visible_only']}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-014', v.status !== 'accepted', 'weak place reference cannot overwrite current location');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'time.pass','time','You say, "Twenty years pass," while practicing narration. No time passes.',{targetDisplay:'time', riskFlags:['quote_present','nonliteral','denied']}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-015', v.status === 'rejected', 'quoted/nonliteral time passage rejected');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'currency.gain','inventory','Old Bren pays you the promised 15 gold.',{targetDisplay:'15 gold', data:{reward:{amount:15, coin:'gold'}}}); key = this.duplicateKeyFor(p); WL.OperationLedger.addReward(r, key, { test:true }); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-016', v.status === 'duplicate_skipped' && v.duplicate === true && !v.reducerAllowed, 'repeat-sensitive reward payment duplicate skipped before reducer');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'crafting.project','crafting','You craft an iron sword.',{targetDisplay:'Iron Sword', effectOwner:'CraftingReducer'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-017', v.status === 'deferred' || v.status === 'blocked', 'deferred crafting remains blocked/deferred honestly');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the Crown of Dawn.',{targetDisplay:'Crown of Dawn'}); p.proposalOnly = false; p.canCommitDirectly = true; v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-018', v.status === 'blocked' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'direct-commit proposal claim blocked');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the apple.',{targetDisplay:'Apple'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-019', v.auditLine.length <= 220 && v.localClause.preview.length <= 180, 'validation audit line and local clause preview remain compact');
        r = base(); before = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, time:r.time, abilities:r.abilities, progression:r.progression }); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'item.acquire','inventory','You pick up the apple.',{targetDisplay:'Apple'}); this.validate(p,r); after = WL.U.safeJson({ inv:r.inventory, body:r.body, quests:r.quests, actors:r.actors, places:r.places, time:r.time, abilities:r.abilities, progression:r.progression }); add('BC15-020', before === after, 'CanonValidator V0.15 remains read-only for gameplay buckets');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'progression.xp_claim','progression','You gain 20 XP.',{targetDisplay:'20 XP', effectOwner:'ProgressionReducerLiteFuture'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-021', v.status === 'deferred' && !v.reducerAllowed, 'progression XP formula/commit remains deferred in Code Pass 1');
        r = base(); p = this.makeTestProposal(r,'world.faction_reference','world','The Iron Dominion tightens its grip on the road to Obel City.',{targetDisplay:'Iron Dominion', effectOwner:'WorldEntityReducerFuture'}); v = this.validate(p,r); add('BC15-022', v.status === 'pending' || v.status === 'deferred', 'world/faction references remain evidence/pending, not gameplay mutation');
      } catch (e) { add('BC15-ERR', false, 'self-test exception: ' + (e && e.message || e)); }
      return results;
    };
  })();

  // ============================================================
  // 13. ReducerRegistry and Limited Reducers
  // ============================================================
  WL.ReducerRegistry = {
    applyAcceptedBatch(root, validations) {
      const results = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < validations.length; i++) {
        const v = validations[i];
        if (v.reducerAllowed === false || v.canonAllowed === false || (v.status && v.status !== 'accepted')) { results.push({ type: v.type || v.eventType, status: 'skipped', reason: v.status || v.result }); continue; }
        if (['accept','soft_fail','hard_deny'].indexOf(v.result) < 0) { results.push({ type: v.type || v.eventType, status: 'skipped', reason: v.result }); continue; }
        const led = WL.OperationLedger.tryAdd(root, 'recentKeys', v.opKey || v.operationKey, { type: v.type || v.eventType, result: v.result });
        if (!led.applied) { results.push({ type: v.type, status: 'duplicate_skip', reason: v.reason }); continue; }
        let r;
        if (v.family === 'inventory') r = WL.InventoryReducer.apply(root, v);
        else if (v.family === 'ability') r = WL.AbilityReducer.apply(root, v);
        else if (v.family === 'body') r = WL.BodyReducer.apply(root, v);
        else if (v.family === 'quest') r = WL.QuestReducerLite.apply(root, v);
        else if (v.family === 'actor') r = WL.ActorReducerLite.apply(root, v);
        else if (v.family === 'place') r = WL.PlaceReducerLite.apply(root, v);
        else if (v.family === 'time') r = WL.TimeReducerLite.apply(root, v);
        else r = { type: v.type, status: 'no_owner', reason: 'No active reducer owner.' };
        results.push(r);
      }
      root.pending.lastReducerResults = results;
      WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'reducers', { message: 'reducer results=' + results.length, statuses: results.map(r => r.status).join(',') }, 30);
      return results;
    }
  };

  WL.InventoryReducer = {
    apply(root, v) {
      if (v.type === 'item.acquire') return this.acquire(root, v);
      if (v.type === 'item.visible') return this.visible(root, v);
      if (v.type === 'currency.gain') return this.currency(root, v);
      return { type: v.type, status: 'ignored', reason: 'Inventory reducer has no handler.' };
    },
    itemId(name) { return 'item_' + WL.U.slug(name); },
    acquire(root, v) {
      const name = v.target.display;
      const id = this.itemId(name);
      if (!root.inventory.itemsById[id]) root.inventory.itemsById[id] = { id, name, qty: 1, holder: 'player', owner: 'player', availability: 'available', createdTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root) };
      else root.inventory.itemsById[id].qty = WL.U.clamp((root.inventory.itemsById[id].qty || 1) + 1, 1, 9999);
      if (root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.push(id);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'inventory', 'item acquired: ' + name);
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: name };
    },
    visible(root, v) {
      const name = v.target.display;
      WL.U.uniquePush(root.inventory.visibleSceneItems, name, 20);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'inventory', 'visible item: ' + name);
      return { type: v.type, status: 'pending_visible_only', target: name };
    },
    currency(root, v) {
      const reward = v.data && v.data.reward || WL.Sanitizers.reward(v.target.raw || v.target.display || '');
      if (!reward) return { type: v.type, status: 'rejected', reason: 'No currency parsed.' };
      const payKey = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['reward', v.sourceText, reward.amount, reward.coin]);
      const led = WL.OperationLedger.tryAdd(root, 'rewardKeys', payKey, { reward });
      if (!led.applied) return { type: v.type, status: 'duplicate_skip', reason: 'Reward payment already applied.' };
      root.inventory.currency[reward.coin] = WL.U.clamp((root.inventory.currency[reward.coin] || 0) + reward.amount, 0, 999999999);
      root.quests.rewardPayments.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), amount: reward.amount, coin: reward.coin, source: v.sourceText });
      WL.U.capArray(root.quests.rewardPayments, 40);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'inventory', 'currency gain');
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: reward.amount + ' ' + reward.coin };
    }
  };

  WL.AbilityReducer = {
    apply(root, v) {
      if (v.type === 'ability.evidence') return this.evidence(root, v);
      if (v.type === 'ability.stabilize_claim') return { type: v.type, status: 'pending', reason: v.reason };
      return { type: v.type, status: 'ignored' };
    },
    evidence(root, v) {
      const name = v.target.display;
      const id = 'ability_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      if (v.result === 'hard_deny') {
        root.abilities.denied.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), name, reason: v.reason });
        WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.denied, 30);
        return { type: v.type, status: 'hard_denied', target: name };
      }
      let rec = root.abilities.candidatesById[id];
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { id, name, stage: 'candidate', attempts: 0, failures: 0, successes: 0, evidence: [], createdTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), lastTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root) };
        root.abilities.candidatesById[id] = rec;
        if (root.abilities.candidateIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.abilities.candidateIds.push(id);
      }
      rec.attempts += 1;
      rec.lastTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      if (v.result === 'soft_fail') rec.failures += 1;
      else rec.successes += 1;
      rec.evidence.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), result: v.result, source: v.sourceText.slice(0, 120) });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 12);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'abilities', 'ability evidence: ' + name);
      return { type: v.type, status: v.result === 'soft_fail' ? 'soft_fail_committed' : 'committed', target: name };
    }
  };

  WL.BodyReducer = {
    apply(root, v) {
      if (v.type === 'body.fatigue') {
        root.body.ep = WL.U.clamp(root.body.ep - 5, 0, root.body.maxEp);
        WL.U.uniquePush(root.body.conditions, 'fatigued', 20);
        WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'body', 'fatigue');
        return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: 'fatigue' };
      }
      if (v.type === 'body.injury') {
        root.body.hp = WL.U.clamp(root.body.hp - 5, 0, root.body.maxHp);
        WL.U.uniquePush(root.body.injuries, 'minor injury', 20);
        WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'body', 'injury');
        return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: 'injury' };
      }
      return { type: v.type, status: 'pending_or_rejected', reason: v.reason };
    }
  };

  WL.QuestReducerLite = {
    apply(root, v) {
      if (v.type === 'reward.promise') return this.promise(root, v);
      const title = v.target.display || 'Untitled objective';
      const id = 'quest_' + WL.U.slug(title);
      let q = root.quests.questsById[id];
      if (!q) {
        q = { id, title, status: 'offered', createdTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), lastTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), progress: [] };
        root.quests.questsById[id] = q;
        if (root.quests.offeredIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.quests.offeredIds.push(id);
      }
      q.lastTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      if (v.type === 'quest.accept') { q.status = 'active'; if (root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.quests.activeIds.push(id); }
      if (v.type === 'quest.complete') { q.status = 'completed'; WL.U.uniquePush(root.quests.completedIds, id, 80); root.quests.activeIds = root.quests.activeIds.filter(x => x !== id); }
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'quests', v.type + ': ' + title);
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: title };
    },
    promise(root, v) {
      const reward = v.data && v.data.reward;
      const text = reward ? (reward.amount + ' ' + reward.coin + ' promised') : WL.U.norm(v.target.raw || v.target.display) + ' promised';
      root.quests.rewardPromises.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), text, paid: false, source: v.sourceText });
      WL.U.capArray(root.quests.rewardPromises, 40);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'quests', 'reward promise');
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed_promise_only', target: text };
    }
  };

  WL.ActorReducerLite = {
    apply(root, v) {
      const name = v.target.display;
      const id = 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      let a = root.actors.actorsById[id];
      if (!a) a = root.actors.actorsById[id] = { id, name, stage: 'present', mentions: 0, present: true, createdTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), lastTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), notes: [] };
      a.mentions += 1; a.present = true; a.lastTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      if (root.actors.presentIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.actors.presentIds.push(id);
      if (v.data && v.data.companion && root.actors.companionIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.actors.companionIds.push(id);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'actors', 'actor present: ' + name);
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: name };
    }
  };

  WL.PlaceReducerLite = {
    apply(root, v) {
      if (v.type === 'place.reference') {
        const name = v.target.display; const id = 'place_' + WL.U.slug(name);
        if (name && root.places.referencedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.places.referencedIds.push(id);
        return { type: v.type, status: 'reference_only', target: name };
      }
      if (v.type === 'place.route') { root.places.routeId = 'route_' + WL.U.slug(v.target.display || 'route'); return { type: v.type, status: 'route_only' }; }
      const name = v.target.display;
      const id = 'place_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      let p = root.places.placesById[id];
      if (!p) p = root.places.placesById[id] = { id, name, visits: 0, createdTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), lastTurn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), notes: [] };
      p.visits += 1; p.lastTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      root.places.currentId = id;
      root.player.locationId = id;
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'places', 'current place: ' + name);
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: name };
    }
  };

  WL.TimeReducerLite = {
    textNum(value) {
      const m = { one:1, two:2, three:3, four:4, five:5, six:6, seven:7, eight:8, nine:9, ten:10, twenty:20 };
      const l = WL.U.lower(value);
      return m[l] || Number(value) || 0;
    },
    apply(root, v) {
      const n = this.textNum(v.data && v.data.amountText);
      const unit = String(v.data && v.data.unit || 'minutes');
      if (!n) return { type: v.type, status: 'rejected', reason: 'No amount.' };
      let minutes = n;
      if (/hour/.test(unit)) minutes = n * 60;
      if (/day/.test(unit)) minutes = n * 1440;
      root.time.minute += minutes;
      while (root.time.minute >= 60) { root.time.minute -= 60; root.time.hour += 1; }
      while (root.time.hour >= 24) { root.time.hour -= 24; root.time.day += 1; }
      root.time.notes.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), text: v.sourceText });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.notes, 20);
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'time', 'time pass');
      return { type: v.type, status: 'committed', target: n + ' ' + unit };
    }
  };


  WL.Reducers = {
    inventory: 'InventoryReducer',
    ability: 'AbilityReducer',
    body: 'BodyReducer',
    quest: 'QuestReducerLite',
    actor: 'ActorReducerLite',
    place: 'PlaceReducerLite',
    time: 'TimeReducerLite'
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 14. DirtyQueue / ContextBuilderLite / CardSyncLite
  // ============================================================
  WL.DirtyQueue = {
    add(root, target, reason) {
      const k = WL.U.slug(target);
      if (root.dirty.targets.indexOf(k) < 0) root.dirty.targets.push(k);
      root.dirty.lastReason = WL.U.norm(reason);
      WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.targets, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets);
    }
  };

  WL.ContextBuilderLite = {
    build(root, baseText) {
      const parts = [];
      const b = root.body;
      parts.push('[WorldLedger Current Truth]');
      parts.push('Player: ' + root.player.name + ' | Level ' + root.player.level + ' | HP ' + b.hp + '/' + b.maxHp + ' MP ' + b.mp + '/' + b.maxMp + ' EP ' + b.ep + '/' + b.maxEp);
      if (root.places.currentId && root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId]) parts.push('Current place: ' + root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId].name);
      const items = root.inventory.ownedIds.map(id => root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].name).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 8);
      if (items.length) parts.push('Inventory: ' + items.join(', '));
      const cands = root.abilities.candidateIds.map(id => root.abilities.candidatesById[id] && root.abilities.candidatesById[id].name + ' candidate').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6);
      if (cands.length) parts.push('Ability development: ' + cands.join(', ') + '. Candidates are not stable abilities yet.');
      const quests = root.quests.activeIds.map(id => root.quests.questsById[id] && root.quests.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 5);
      if (quests.length) parts.push('Active quests: ' + quests.join('; '));
      if (root.body.conditions.length) parts.push('Conditions: ' + root.body.conditions.join(', '));
      if (root.body.injuries.length) parts.push('Injuries: ' + root.body.injuries.join(', '));
      parts.push('Rules: player input is intent; output confirms consequences; rumors, dreams, quotes, commands, and Story Card text are not canon by themselves.');
      let packet = parts.join('\n');
      const cap = WL.U.clamp(root.features.contextBudget || 1800, 400, 5000);
      if (packet.length > cap) packet = packet.slice(0, cap - 20) + '\n[trimmed]';
      WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'context', { message: 'Context packet built, chars=' + packet.length }, 30);
      if (!baseText) return packet;
      return String(baseText) + '\n\n' + packet;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 20. CardApiAdapter Shell
  // ============================================================
  WL.CardApiAdapter = {
    available() { return typeof globalThis.storyCards !== 'undefined' || typeof globalThis.addStoryCard === 'function' || typeof globalThis.updateStoryCard === 'function'; },
    safeFindByTitle(title) {
      const wanted = WL.U.lower(title);
      const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
      for (let i = 0; i < cards.length; i++) {
        const c = cards[i] || {};
        if (WL.U.lower(c.title || c.name || '') === wanted) return { ok: true, card: c, index: i };
      }
      return { ok: false, card: null, index: -1 };
    },
    dryRunOnly() { return true; }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 21. CardSyncLite Dry-Run Protected Shell
  // ============================================================
  WL.CardSyncLite = {
    protectedRegistry(root) { return root.cards.protectedTitles || []; },
    audit(root) {
      const lines = [];
      const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
      lines.push('Mode: dry-run=' + !!root.features.cardSyncDryRun + ', protectedWrites=' + !!root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled + ', generated=false');
      lines.push('Visible Story Cards detected: ' + cards.length);
      const titles = this.protectedRegistry(root);
      for (let i = 0; i < titles.length; i++) lines.push('Protected: ' + titles[i]);
      lines.push('Generated CardSync is disabled in Code Pass 1. Cards never create canon.');
      return lines;
    },
    dryRun(root) {
      if (!root.features.cardSyncLiteEnabled) return 0;
      const targets = root.dirty.targets.slice(0, 4);
      for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) root.cards.dryRunWrites.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), target: targets[i], mode: 'dry-run' });
      WL.U.capArray(root.cards.dryRunWrites, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dryRunWrites);
      if (targets.length) WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'cards', { message: 'CardSync dry-run targets=' + targets.join(',') }, 30);
      return targets.length;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 15. Migration / Quarantine / Cleanup
  // ============================================================
  WL.MigrationManager = {
    run(root) {
      if (root.migration.schema !== WL.SCHEMA_VERSION) {
        root.quarantine.records.push({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), kind: 'schema_mismatch', note: 'Future/unknown schema found. Repaired conservatively.' });
        root.migration.schema = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      }
      root.migration.lastRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
    }
  };

  WL.CleanupManagerLite = {
    run(root) {
      WL.StateManager.clampLogs(root);
      root.cleanup.lastRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
      root.cleanup.capsApplied += 1;
      return true;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 16. ReleaseCheckLite Foundation
  // ============================================================
  WL.ReleaseCheckLite = {
    pass(label) { return 'PASS: ' + label; },
    warn(label) { return 'WARN: ' + label; },
    fail(label) { return 'FAIL: ' + label; },
    run(root) {
      const out = [];
      out.push(this.pass('build identity present: ' + WL.BUILD_VERSION));
      out.push(root.schemaVersion === WL.SCHEMA_VERSION ? this.pass('schema version matches') : this.fail('schema version mismatch'));
      out.push(root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false ? this.pass('generated cards disabled') : this.fail('generated cards enabled too early'));
      out.push(root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled === false ? this.pass('live protected card writes disabled by default') : this.warn('protected card writes enabled'));
      out.push(root.features.manualMutationCommandsEnabled === false ? this.pass('manual mutation commands disabled') : this.fail('manual mutation commands enabled'));
      const deferred = ['economyEnabled','craftingEnabled','investigationEnabled','reputationEnabled','domainEnabled','absorptionEnabled','advancedCombatEnabled','warfareEnabled','multiProtagonistEnabled'];
      for (let i = 0; i < deferred.length; i++) out.push(root.features[deferred[i]] === false ? this.pass(deferred[i] + ' disabled') : this.fail(deferred[i] + ' enabled too early'));
      out.push(root.operationLedger && Array.isArray(root.operationLedger.recentKeys) ? this.pass('operation ledger buckets present') : this.fail('operation ledger missing'));
      out.push(WL.EventScannerLite && WL.ProposalBuilder && WL.CanonValidator ? this.pass('parser/proposal/validator shells present') : this.fail('pipeline shell missing'));
      out.push(WL.ReducerRegistry ? this.pass('reducer registry present') : this.fail('reducer registry missing'));
      out.push(root.dirty && Array.isArray(root.dirty.targets) ? this.pass('dirty queue present') : this.fail('dirty queue missing'));
      out.push(root.features.contextBuilderLiteEnabled ? this.pass('context builder lite enabled') : this.warn('context builder disabled'));
      out.push(root.features.cardSyncDryRun ? this.pass('CardSync dry-run mode active') : this.fail('CardSync dry-run not active'));
      out.push(root.features.layoutLockEnabled ? this.pass('layout lock enabled') : this.fail('layout lock disabled'));
      out.push(WL.SECTION_ORDER && WL.SECTION_ORDER.length === 25 ? this.pass('25-section layout order registered') : this.fail('layout order not registered'));
      out.push(WL.LayoutLock ? this.pass('LayoutLock inspector present') : this.fail('LayoutLock inspector missing'));
      out.push(WL.StateSchemaLock ? this.pass('StateSchemaLock inspector present') : this.fail('StateSchemaLock inspector missing'));
      out.push(root.meta && root.meta.schemaId === WL.SCHEMA_ID ? this.pass('locked schema metadata present') : this.fail('locked schema metadata missing'));
      out.push(WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS && WL.CANONICAL_ROOT_BUCKETS.length === 20 ? this.pass('20 canonical state buckets registered') : this.fail('canonical bucket registry mismatch'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('schema') >= 0 ? this.pass('/schema command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/schema command missing'));
      const layoutRows = WL.LayoutLock ? WL.LayoutLock.inspect(root) : [];
      const layoutFails = layoutRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(layoutFails === 0 ? this.pass('module boundary/layout audit clean') : this.fail('module boundary/layout audit failures: ' + layoutFails));
      const schemaRows = WL.StateSchemaLock ? WL.StateSchemaLock.inspect(root) : [];
      const schemaFails = schemaRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(schemaFails === 0 ? this.pass('state schema audit clean') : this.fail('state schema audit failures: ' + schemaFails));
      out.push(WL.CommandOutputFormatLock ? this.pass('CommandOutputFormatLock present') : this.fail('CommandOutputFormatLock missing'));
      const fmtRows = WL.CommandOutputFormatLock ? WL.CommandOutputFormatLock.inspect(root) : [];
      const fmtFails = fmtRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(fmtFails === 0 ? this.pass('command output format audit clean') : this.fail('command output format audit failures: ' + fmtFails));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('commandformat') >= 0 ? this.pass('/commandformat command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/commandformat command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('commands') >= 0 ? this.pass('/commands command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/commands command missing'));
      out.push(WL.StateManager && WL.STATE_MANAGER_LOCK_VERSION ? this.pass('StateManager lock present: ' + WL.STATE_MANAGER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('StateManager lock missing'));
      const integrity = WL.StateManager.validateIntegrity(root);
      out.push(integrity.ok ? this.pass('StateManager integrity validation clean') : this.fail('StateManager integrity errors: ' + integrity.errors.join('; ')));
      const bc5 = WL.StateManager.runBC5SelfTests();
      const bc5Pass = bc5.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc5Pass === bc5.length ? this.pass('BC5 StateManager self-tests pass: ' + bc5Pass + '/' + bc5.length) : this.fail('BC5 StateManager self-tests failing: ' + bc5Pass + '/' + bc5.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('statemanager') >= 0 ? this.pass('/statemanager command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/statemanager command missing'));
      out.push(WL.Runtime && WL.RUNTIME_HOOK_CONTRACT_VERSION ? this.pass('Runtime hook contract present: ' + WL.RUNTIME_HOOK_CONTRACT_VERSION) : this.fail('Runtime hook contract missing'));
      out.push(root.runtime && typeof root.runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash === 'string' ? this.pass('duplicate output hash field present') : this.fail('duplicate output hash field missing'));
      out.push(root.runtime && typeof root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput === 'boolean' ? this.pass('command output suppression field present') : this.fail('command output suppression field missing'));
      out.push(root.runtime && Array.isArray(root.runtime.hookTrace) ? this.pass('hook trace present/capped') : this.fail('hook trace missing'));
      out.push(WL.Runtime && typeof WL.Runtime.makeReturn === 'function' ? this.pass('Runtime.makeReturn valid-return guard present') : this.fail('Runtime.makeReturn missing'));
      out.push(WL.Runtime && typeof WL.Runtime.failSoft === 'function' ? this.pass('Runtime fail-soft guard present') : this.fail('Runtime fail-soft guard missing'));
      out.push(WL.Runtime && typeof WL.Runtime.shouldSuppressEventScan === 'function' ? this.pass('command/story suppression check present') : this.fail('command/story suppression check missing'));
      const bc6 = WL.Runtime && WL.Runtime.runBC6SelfTests ? WL.Runtime.runBC6SelfTests() : [];
      const bc6Pass = bc6.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc6.length && bc6Pass === bc6.length ? this.pass('BC6 Runtime self-tests pass: ' + bc6Pass + '/' + bc6.length) : this.fail('BC6 Runtime self-tests failing: ' + bc6Pass + '/' + bc6.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('runtime') >= 0 ? this.pass('/runtime command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/runtime command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('hookaudit') >= 0 ? this.pass('/hookaudit command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/hookaudit command missing'));
      out.push(WL.AuditLogger && WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION ? this.pass('AuditLogger lock present: ' + WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('AuditLogger lock missing'));
      const auditRows = WL.AuditLogger ? WL.AuditLogger.inspect(root) : [];
      const auditFails = auditRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(auditFails === 0 ? this.pass('AuditLogger audit clean') : this.fail('AuditLogger audit failures: ' + auditFails));
      out.push(WL.OperationLedger && WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION ? this.pass('OperationLedger lock present: ' + WL.AUDIT_LEDGER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('OperationLedger lock missing'));
      const ledgerRows = WL.OperationLedger ? WL.OperationLedger.inspect(root) : [];
      const ledgerFails = ledgerRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(ledgerFails === 0 ? this.pass('OperationLedger audit clean') : this.fail('OperationLedger audit failures: ' + ledgerFails));
      const bc7Audit = WL.AuditLogger && WL.AuditLogger.runBC7SelfTests ? WL.AuditLogger.runBC7SelfTests() : [];
      const bc7Ledger = WL.OperationLedger && WL.OperationLedger.runBC7SelfTests ? WL.OperationLedger.runBC7SelfTests() : [];
      const bc7All = bc7Audit.concat(bc7Ledger);
      const bc7Pass = bc7All.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc7All.length && bc7Pass === bc7All.length ? this.pass('BC7 Audit/Ledger self-tests pass: ' + bc7Pass + '/' + bc7All.length) : this.fail('BC7 Audit/Ledger self-tests failing: ' + bc7Pass + '/' + bc7All.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('audit') >= 0 ? this.pass('/audit command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/audit command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('ledgercheck') >= 0 ? this.pass('/ledgercheck command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/ledgercheck command missing'));
      out.push(WL.CommandRouter && WL.COMMAND_ROUTER_LOCK_VERSION ? this.pass('CommandRouter lock present: ' + WL.COMMAND_ROUTER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('CommandRouter lock missing'));
      const cmdRows = WL.CommandRouter ? WL.CommandRouter.inspect(root) : [];
      const cmdFails = cmdRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(cmdFails === 0 ? this.pass('CommandRouter audit clean') : this.fail('CommandRouter audit failures: ' + cmdFails));
      const bc8 = (!globalThis.__WL_BC8_RUNNING && WL.CommandRouter && WL.CommandRouter.runBC8SelfTests) ? WL.CommandRouter.runBC8SelfTests() : [];
      const bc8Pass = bc8.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      if (globalThis.__WL_BC8_RUNNING) out.push(this.pass('BC8 command self-tests skipped inside nested self-test to prevent recursion'));
      else out.push(bc8.length >= 24 && bc8Pass === bc8.length ? this.pass('BC8 command self-tests pass: ' + bc8Pass + '/' + bc8.length) : this.fail('BC8 command self-tests failing: ' + bc8Pass + '/' + bc8.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('commandrouter') >= 0 ? this.pass('/commandrouter command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/commandrouter command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('commandcheck') >= 0 ? this.pass('/commandcheck command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/commandcheck command missing'));
      out.push(WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION === 'worldledger.resolver-sanitizer-lock.v12' ? this.pass('Resolver/Sanitizer lock present: ' + WL.RESOLVER_SANITIZER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('Resolver/Sanitizer lock missing'));
      out.push(WL.Resolver && WL.Sanitizers && typeof WL.Resolver.buildTargetBinding === 'function' && typeof WL.Sanitizers.item === 'function' && typeof WL.Sanitizers.placeCanonical === 'function' ? this.pass('Resolver/Sanitizer required functions present') : this.fail('Resolver/Sanitizer required functions missing'));

      out.push(resolverFails === 0 ? this.pass('Resolver/Sanitizer audit clean') : this.fail('Resolver/Sanitizer audit failures: ' + resolverFails));
      const bc12 = WL.Resolver && WL.Resolver.runBC12SelfTests ? WL.Resolver.runBC12SelfTests() : [];
      const bc12Pass = bc12.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc12.length >= 18 && bc12Pass === bc12.length ? this.pass('BC12 Resolver/Sanitizer self-tests pass: ' + bc12Pass + '/' + bc12.length) : this.fail('BC12 Resolver/Sanitizer self-tests failing: ' + bc12Pass + '/' + bc12.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('resolver') >= 0 ? this.pass('/resolver command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/resolver command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('resolvercheck') >= 0 ? this.pass('/resolvercheck command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/resolvercheck command missing'));
      out.push(WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION === 'worldledger.eventscanner-lock.v13' ? this.pass('EventScanner lock present: ' + WL.EVENT_SCANNER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('EventScanner lock missing'));
      out.push(WL.EventScannerLite && typeof WL.EventScannerLite.shouldScanSource === 'function' && typeof WL.EventScannerLite.scanText === 'function' ? this.pass('EventScanner required functions present') : this.fail('EventScanner required functions missing'));
      const parserRows = WL.EventScannerLite ? WL.EventScannerLite.inspect(root) : [];

      out.push(parserFails === 0 ? this.pass('parser source/schema audit clean') : this.fail('parser source/schema audit failures: ' + parserFails));
      const bc9 = WL.EventScannerLite && WL.EventScannerLite.runBC9SelfTests ? WL.EventScannerLite.runBC9SelfTests() : [];
      const bc9Pass = bc9.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc9.length && bc9Pass === bc9.length ? this.pass('BC9 parser self-tests pass: ' + bc9Pass + '/' + bc9.length) : this.fail('BC9 parser self-tests failing: ' + bc9Pass + '/' + bc9.length));
      const bc13 = WL.EventScannerLite && WL.EventScannerLite.runBC13SelfTests ? WL.EventScannerLite.runBC13SelfTests() : [];
      const bc13Pass = bc13.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc13.length >= 16 && bc13Pass === bc13.length ? this.pass('BC13 EventScanner lock self-tests pass: ' + bc13Pass + '/' + bc13.length) : this.fail('BC13 EventScanner lock self-tests failing: ' + bc13Pass + '/' + bc13.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('parser') >= 0 ? this.pass('/parser command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/parser command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('parsercheck') >= 0 ? this.pass('/parsercheck command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/parsercheck command missing'));
      out.push(WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION === 'worldledger.proposalbuilder-lock.v14' ? this.pass('ProposalBuilder lock present: ' + WL.PROPOSAL_BUILDER_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('ProposalBuilder lock missing'));
      out.push(WL.ProposalBuilder && typeof WL.ProposalBuilder.fromHit === 'function' && typeof WL.ProposalBuilder.bindTarget === 'function' && typeof WL.ProposalBuilder.buildBaseProposal === 'function' && typeof WL.ProposalBuilder.bindLocalClause === 'function' ? this.pass('ProposalBuilder required functions present') : this.fail('ProposalBuilder required functions missing'));
      const proposalRows = WL.ProposalBuilder && WL.ProposalBuilder.inspect ? WL.ProposalBuilder.inspect(root) : [];
      const proposalFails = proposalRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(proposalFails === 0 ? this.pass('ProposalBuilder audit clean') : this.fail('ProposalBuilder audit failures: ' + proposalFails));
      const bc10 = WL.ProposalBuilder && WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC10SelfTests ? WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC10SelfTests() : [];
      const bc10Pass = bc10.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc10.length && bc10Pass === bc10.length ? this.pass('BC10 ProposalBuilder self-tests pass: ' + bc10Pass + '/' + bc10.length) : this.fail('BC10 ProposalBuilder self-tests failing: ' + bc10Pass + '/' + bc10.length));
      const bc14 = WL.ProposalBuilder && WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC14SelfTests ? WL.ProposalBuilder.runBC14SelfTests() : [];
      const bc14Pass = bc14.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc14.length >= 18 && bc14Pass === bc14.length ? this.pass('BC14 ProposalBuilder lock self-tests pass: ' + bc14Pass + '/' + bc14.length) : this.fail('BC14 ProposalBuilder lock self-tests failing: ' + bc14Pass + '/' + bc14.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('proposal') >= 0 ? this.pass('/proposal command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/proposal command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('proposalcheck') >= 0 ? this.pass('/proposalcheck command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/proposalcheck command missing'));
      out.push(WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION === 'worldledger.canonvalidator-lock.v15' ? this.pass('CanonValidator lock present: ' + WL.CANON_VALIDATOR_LOCK_VERSION) : this.fail('CanonValidator lock missing'));
      out.push(WL.CanonValidator && typeof WL.CanonValidator.validateAll === 'function' && typeof WL.CanonValidator.validateClause === 'function' && typeof WL.CanonValidator.checkDuplicateRisk === 'function' ? this.pass('CanonValidator required functions present') : this.fail('CanonValidator required functions missing'));
      const validatorRows = WL.CanonValidator && WL.CanonValidator.inspect ? WL.CanonValidator.inspect(root) : [];
      const validatorFails = validatorRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(validatorFails === 0 ? this.pass('CanonValidator audit clean') : this.fail('CanonValidator audit failures: ' + validatorFails));
      const bc11 = WL.CanonValidator && WL.CanonValidator.runBC11SelfTests ? WL.CanonValidator.runBC11SelfTests() : [];
      const bc11Pass = bc11.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc11.length >= 28 && bc11Pass === bc11.length ? this.pass('BC11 CanonValidator self-tests pass: ' + bc11Pass + '/' + bc11.length) : this.fail('BC11 CanonValidator self-tests failing: ' + bc11Pass + '/' + bc11.length));
      const bc15 = WL.CanonValidator && WL.CanonValidator.runBC15SelfTests ? WL.CanonValidator.runBC15SelfTests() : [];
      const bc15Pass = bc15.filter(t => t.ok).length;
      out.push(bc15.length >= 20 && bc15Pass === bc15.length ? this.pass('BC15 CanonValidator lock self-tests pass: ' + bc15Pass + '/' + bc15.length) : this.fail('BC15 CanonValidator lock self-tests failing: ' + bc15Pass + '/' + bc15.length));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('validator') >= 0 ? this.pass('/validator command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/validator command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('validatorcheck') >= 0 ? this.pass('/validatorcheck command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/validatorcheck command missing'));
      out.push(WL.LegacySalvageLedger ? this.pass('LegacySalvageLedger/control map guard present') : this.fail('LegacySalvageLedger/control map guard missing'));
      const salvageRows = WL.LegacySalvageLedger ? WL.LegacySalvageLedger.inspect(root) : [];
      const salvageFails = salvageRows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length;
      out.push(salvageFails === 0 ? this.pass('Legacy salvage control map audit clean') : this.fail('Legacy salvage control map failures: ' + salvageFails));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('controlmap') >= 0 ? this.pass('/controlmap command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/controlmap command missing'));
      out.push(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('borrowledger') >= 0 ? this.pass('/borrowledger command registered as inspect-only') : this.fail('/borrowledger command missing'));
      out.push(this.warn('Code Pass 1 is foundation-limited; advanced systems are intentionally deferred.'));
      root.audits.releasecheck = out.map(x => ({ turn: WL.U.nowTurn(root), text: x }));
      return out;
    },
    stateAudit(root) {
      const out = [];
      out.push(root && typeof root === 'object' ? this.pass('root exists') : this.fail('root missing'));
      const buckets = WL.ROOT_BUCKETS_ALL || ['meta','features','layout','runtime','startup','player','body','progression','inventory','abilities','quests','actors','places','time','cards','operationLedger','audits','migration','quarantine','dirty','cleanup','pending'];
      for (let i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) out.push(root[buckets[i]] ? this.pass('bucket ' + buckets[i]) : this.fail('missing bucket ' + buckets[i]));
      if (WL.StateSchemaLock) WL.StateSchemaLock.inspect(root).forEach(r => out.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      if (WL.CommandOutputFormatLock) WL.CommandOutputFormatLock.inspect(root).forEach(r => out.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      const b = root.body;
      out.push(b.hp >= 0 && b.hp <= b.maxHp ? this.pass('HP invariant') : this.fail('HP invariant broken'));
      out.push(b.mp >= 0 && b.mp <= b.maxMp ? this.pass('MP invariant') : this.fail('MP invariant broken'));
      out.push(b.ep >= 0 && b.ep <= b.maxEp ? this.pass('EP invariant') : this.fail('EP invariant broken'));
      return out;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 17. Hook Orchestration
  // ============================================================
  WL.processOutput = function (root, output) {
    const safe = WL.Runtime.safeText(output);
    const outputHash = WL.Runtime.hashText(safe);
    if (WL.Runtime.shouldSuppressEventScan(root, safe)) {
      WL.AuditLogger.summarizeLastTurn(root, 'Output suppressed by Runtime; command/audit text was not scanned as story.');
      WL.Runtime.clearCommandSuppression(root);
      return safe;
    }
    if (WL.Runtime.isDuplicateOutput(root, outputHash)) {
      root.runtime.duplicateOutputSkipped += 1;
      root.runtime.outputReplayDetected = true;
      root.runtime.lastDuplicateOutputHash = outputHash;
      WL.Runtime.recordWarning(root, 'Duplicate output replay detected; consequence processing skipped.', { hash: outputHash });
      if (WL.AuditLogger) WL.AuditLogger.duplicate(root, outputHash, 'duplicate output replay skipped before reducer');
      WL.AuditLogger.summarizeLastTurn(root, 'Duplicate output hash skipped; no consequences applied.');
      return safe;
    }
    const hits = WL.EventScannerLite.scanOutput(root, safe);
    const proposals = WL.ProposalBuilder.fromHits(root, hits, 'output');
    const validations = WL.CanonValidator.validateAll(root, proposals);
    const results = WL.ReducerRegistry.applyAcceptedBatch(root, validations);
    WL.CardSyncLite.dryRun(root);
    WL.CleanupManagerLite.run(root);
    WL.Runtime.markOutputApplied(root, outputHash);
    WL.AuditLogger.summarizeLastTurn(root, 'Output processed: hits=' + hits.length + ', proposals=' + proposals.length + ', results=' + results.length + '.');
    return safe;
  };

  WL.onInput = function (text) {
    let root;
    try {
      root = WL.StateManager.root();
      const begin = WL.Runtime.beginHook(root, 'input', text);
      WL.MigrationManager.run(root);
      const raw = begin.text;
      if (WL.U.startsCommand(raw) && root.features.commandsEnabled) {
        const out = WL.CommandRouter.route(root, raw);
        return WL.Runtime.endHook(root, 'input', out, 'completed');
      }
      // Gameplay input after a slash command begins a fresh story turn; previous command-output suppression must not swallow the next AI result.
      WL.Runtime.clearCommandSuppression(root);
      root.runtime.lastInputText = raw;
      WL.Runtime.markInput(root, raw);
      root.startup.firstGameplayInputSeen = true;
      root.startup.startupLocked = true;
      root.startup.gameplayStarted = true;
      const actionCount = WL.U.getInfoActionCount();
      if (typeof actionCount !== 'number') {
        root.meta.currentTurn = (root.meta.currentTurn || 0) + 1;
        root.runtime.currentTurn = root.meta.currentTurn;
      }
      root.runtime.pendingInputIntentId = 'intent_' + WL.U.hash(raw + '|' + root.runtime.currentTurn);
      if (root.features.parserShellEnabled && WL.EventScannerLite) WL.EventScannerLite.scanInput(raw, root);
      return WL.Runtime.endHook(root, 'input', raw, 'completed');
    } catch (err) {
      return WL.Runtime.failSoft(root || { runtime: { errors: [] }, audits: { warnings: [] } }, 'input', 'onInput', err, text, false);
    }
  };

  WL.onContext = function (text) {
    let root;
    try {
      root = WL.StateManager.root();
      const begin = WL.Runtime.beginHook(root, 'context', text);
      const out = root.features.contextBuilderLiteEnabled ? WL.ContextBuilderLite.build(root, begin.text) : begin.text;
      return WL.Runtime.endHook(root, 'context', out, 'completed');
    } catch (err) {
      return WL.Runtime.failSoft(root || { runtime: { errors: [] }, audits: { warnings: [] } }, 'context', 'onContext', err, text, false);
    }
  };

  WL.onOutput = function (text) {
    let root;
    try {
      root = WL.StateManager.root();
      const begin = WL.Runtime.beginHook(root, 'output', text);
      const raw = begin.text;
      const out = WL.processOutput(root, raw);
      return WL.Runtime.endHook(root, 'output', out, 'completed');
    } catch (err) {
      return WL.Runtime.failSoft(root || { runtime: { errors: [] }, audits: { warnings: [] } }, 'output', 'onOutput', err, text, false);
    }
  };


  // ============================================================
  // 15R. V0.15.1 Review-Depth Correction Layer
  // ============================================================
  // This layer intentionally stays inside V0.15. It does not unlock new gameplay systems.
  // It fixes the review finding that V0.15 had the right architecture but shallow support depth.
  WL.V15_REVIEW_FIX_VERSION = 'worldledger.v15.review-depth-fix.v1';
  WL.AIDRPG_WORLDLEDGER_BRIDGE = Object.freeze({
    project: 'AIDRPG Core MVP',
    implementation: 'WorldLedger',
    note: 'WorldLedger is the script-engine implementation name for the AIDRPG Core MVP / Code Pass 1 rebuild.',
    activeCheckpoint: 'WorldLedgerV0.15.2 support-layer schema completion, not V0.16 and not final game engine.'
  });
  WL.SUPPORT_DEPTH_SECTION_MAP = Object.freeze({
    '16': 'ReducerRegistry',
    '17': 'Core Reducer Shells / Limited Reducers',
    '18': 'DirtyQueue',
    '19': 'ContextBuilderLite',
    '20': 'CardApiAdapter Shell',
    '21': 'CardSyncLite Dry-Run Protected Shell',
    '22': 'Migration and Quarantine Manager',
    '23': 'CleanupManagerLite',
    '24': 'Hook Orchestration',
    '25': 'Export / Platform Hook Binding'
  });

  WL.FoundationDepthReview = {
    forbiddenFlags(v) {
      v = v || {};
      const f = Object.assign({}, v.flags || {}, v.riskFlags || {}, v.safetyFlags || {});
      const bad = [];
      if (f.quotedSpeech || f.quote) bad.push('quoted');
      if (f.dreamOrVision || f.dream) bad.push('dream');
      if (f.rumorOrClaim || f.rumor) bad.push('rumor');
      if (f.hypothetical) bad.push('hypothetical');
      if (f.futureIntent) bad.push('future');
      if (f.failedOrRevoked || f.denied || f.negatedOrRefused) bad.push('failed_or_denied');
      if (f.pretendOrFalse || f.pretend) bad.push('pretend_or_false');
      if (f.abstractOrIdea) bad.push('abstract');
      if (f.commandText || f.diagnosticText) bad.push('diagnostic');
      if (v.sourceHook && v.sourceHook !== 'output' && v.type !== 'reward.promise') bad.push('not_confirmed_output');
      if (v.sourceAuthority && /noncanon|command|context|card|audit|debug/i.test(v.sourceAuthority)) bad.push('noncanon_source');
      return bad;
    },
    safeTarget(v) {
      const t = v && v.target || {};
      return WL.U.norm(t.display || t.name || t.raw || '');
    },
    ensureDepthSchemas(root) {
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return root;
      if (!root.dirty || typeof root.dirty !== 'object') root.dirty = { targets: [], lastReason: '' };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.dirty.targets)) root.dirty.targets = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.dirty.records)) root.dirty.records = [];
      if (!root.context || typeof root.context !== 'object') root.context = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.context.packetAudit)) root.context.packetAudit = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.context.lastPackets)) root.context.lastPackets = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.context.skippedPackets)) root.context.skippedPackets = [];
      if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== 'object') root.cards = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.protectedRegistry)) {
        root.cards.protectedRegistry = (root.cards.protectedTitles || WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS || []).map((title, idx) => ({
          id: 'protected_' + WL.U.slug(title), title: title, key: WL.U.slug(title), family: 'protected', singleton: true,
          priority: idx + 1, renderHash: '', lastDryRunTurn: 0, duplicatePolicy: 'audit_only_in_codepass1'
        }));
      }
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit)) root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.tombstones)) root.cards.tombstones = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.renderRecords)) root.cards.renderRecords = [];
      if (!root.migration || typeof root.migration !== 'object') root.migration = { schema: WL.SCHEMA_VERSION, warnings: [], repairs: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit)) root.migration.quarantineAudit = [];
      if (!root.quarantine || typeof root.quarantine !== 'object') root.quarantine = { records: [], notes: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.quarantine.records)) root.quarantine.records = [];
      return root;
    },
    inspect(root) {
      this.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
      const rows = [];
      rows.push(WL.BUILD_VERSION === '0.15.2-support-layer-schema-completion-codepass1' ? { status:'PASS', label:'V0.15.2 support-layer schema completion identity active' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'build identity not corrected for V0.15.2 support schema pass' });
      rows.push(WL.AIDRPG_WORLDLEDGER_BRIDGE && /AIDRPG/.test(WL.AIDRPG_WORLDLEDGER_BRIDGE.project) ? { status:'PASS', label:'AIDRPG ↔ WorldLedger naming bridge present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'AIDRPG ↔ WorldLedger bridge missing' });
      rows.push(WL.SUPPORT_DEPTH_SECTION_MAP && WL.SUPPORT_DEPTH_SECTION_MAP['18'] === 'DirtyQueue' && WL.SUPPORT_DEPTH_SECTION_MAP['23'] === 'CleanupManagerLite' ? { status:'PASS', label:'support section map aligns sections 16-25' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'support section map missing/misaligned' });
      rows.push(WL.ReducerRegistry && WL.ReducerRegistry.LEDGER_ORDER === 'check_mutate_record_dirty_audit' ? { status:'PASS', label:'ReducerRegistry ledger order is check -> mutate -> record -> dirty/audit' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'ReducerRegistry ledger order not locked' });
      rows.push(WL.InventoryReducer && WL.InventoryReducer.defensiveLocked && WL.AbilityReducer.defensiveLocked && WL.BodyReducer.defensiveLocked && WL.QuestReducerLite.defensiveLocked && WL.ActorReducerLite.defensiveLocked && WL.PlaceReducerLite.defensiveLocked && WL.TimeReducerLite.defensiveLocked ? { status:'PASS', label:'owner reducers advertise defensive guard lock' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'one or more reducer defensive guard locks missing' });
      rows.push(root.dirty && Array.isArray(root.dirty.records) ? { status:'PASS', label:'DirtyTarget record schema present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyTarget record schema missing' });
      rows.push(WL.DirtyQueue && typeof WL.DirtyQueue.addRecord === 'function' && typeof WL.DirtyQueue.inspect === 'function' ? { status:'PASS', label:'DirtyQueue rich schema helpers present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyQueue rich schema helpers missing' });
      rows.push(WL.ContextBuilderLite && typeof WL.ContextBuilderLite.buildPackets === 'function' && typeof WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect === 'function' ? { status:'PASS', label:'ContextBuilder packet/audit helpers present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'ContextBuilder packet/audit helpers missing' });
      rows.push(root.context && Array.isArray(root.context.packetAudit) ? { status:'PASS', label:'context packet audit bucket present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'context packet audit bucket missing' });
      rows.push(WL.CardSyncLite && typeof WL.CardSyncLite.auditProtectedSingletons === 'function' && typeof WL.CardSyncLite.renderProtectedRecord === 'function' ? { status:'PASS', label:'CardSync protected registry/duplicate helpers present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'CardSync protected registry helpers missing' });
      rows.push(root.cards && Array.isArray(root.cards.protectedRegistry) && Array.isArray(root.cards.tombstones) ? { status:'PASS', label:'CardSync protected registry/tombstone buckets present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'CardSync protected registry/tombstone buckets missing' });
      rows.push(WL.MigrationManager && typeof WL.MigrationManager.quarantineRecord === 'function' && typeof WL.MigrationManager.inspect === 'function' ? { status:'PASS', label:'Migration/Quarantine schema helpers present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'Migration/Quarantine schema helpers missing' });
      rows.push(root.quarantine && Array.isArray(root.quarantine.records) ? { status:'PASS', label:'QuarantineRecord bucket present' } : { status:'FAIL', label:'QuarantineRecord bucket missing' });
      return rows;
    },
    runSelfTests() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok: !!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        let root = WL.StateManager.root();
        this.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
        add('BC15R-001', /0\.15\.2-support-layer-schema-completion/.test(WL.BUILD_VERSION), 'header/build identity corrected to V0.15.2');
        add('BC15R-002', !!WL.AIDRPG_WORLDLEDGER_BRIDGE, 'AIDRPG ↔ WorldLedger bridge exists');
        let v = { type:'item.acquire', family:'inventory', result:'accept', status:'accepted', target:{display:'Dream Crown', raw:'Dream Crown'}, flags:{dreamOrVision:true}, sourceHook:'output', sourceText:'In a dream, you receive the Dream Crown.', opKey:'test_dream_crown' };
        const invBefore = WL.U.safeJson(root.inventory);
        const rr = WL.InventoryReducer.apply(root, v);
        add('BC15R-003', /rejected/.test(rr.status) && WL.U.safeJson(root.inventory) === invBefore, 'InventoryReducer defensively rejects dream/unsafe acquire even if called directly');
        v = { type:'ability.stabilize_claim', family:'ability', result:'accept', status:'accepted', target:{display:'Moonblade'}, flags:{}, sourceHook:'output', sourceText:'You learned Moonblade.', opKey:'test_moonblade' };
        const abBefore = WL.U.safeJson(root.abilities);
        const ar = WL.AbilityReducer.apply(root, v);
        add('BC15R-004', ar.status !== 'committed' && WL.U.safeJson(root.abilities) === abBefore, 'AbilityReducer does not stabilize output-only ability claim');
        v = { type:'body.injury', family:'body', result:'accept', status:'accepted', target:{display:'death'}, flags:{rumorOrClaim:true}, sourceHook:'output', sourceText:'Rumor says you died.', opKey:'test_death_rumor' };
        const hpBefore = root.body.hp;
        const br = WL.BodyReducer.apply(root, v);
        add('BC15R-005', /rejected/.test(br.status) && root.body.hp === hpBefore, 'BodyReducer defensively rejects rumor/dream/quote harm');
        root = WL.StateManager.init({});
        const okv = { type:'item.acquire', family:'inventory', result:'accept', status:'accepted', reducerAllowed:true, canonAllowed:true, target:{display:'Apple', raw:'Apple'}, flags:{}, sourceHook:'output', sourceText:'You pick up the apple.', opKey:'review_unique_apple' };
        WL.ReducerRegistry.applyAcceptedBatch(root, [okv, okv]);
        add('BC15R-006', root.inventory.itemsById.item_apple && root.inventory.itemsById.item_apple.qty === 1, 'ReducerRegistry duplicate protection still prevents repeated committed item');
        add('BC15R-007', root.operationLedger.recentKeys.indexOf(WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey('review_unique_apple')) >= 0, 'ReducerRegistry records operation key after successful reducer commit');
        const d = WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'inventory', 'test dirty', { sourceReducer:'InventoryReducer', sourceOperationKey:'review_unique_apple', priority:1 });
        add('BC15R-008', d && d.family === 'inventory' && d.status === 'queued' && Array.isArray(root.dirty.records), 'DirtyQueue creates DirtyTarget records');
        const packets = WL.ContextBuilderLite.buildPackets(root);
        add('BC15R-009', Array.isArray(packets) && packets.some(p => p.family === 'player') && Array.isArray(root.context.lastPackets), 'ContextBuilder builds packet records and audit state');
        const preg = WL.CardSyncLite.protectedRegistry(root);
        add('BC15R-010', Array.isArray(preg) && preg[0] && preg[0].id && preg[0].title, 'CardSync protected registry returns structured records');
        const qr = WL.MigrationManager.quarantineRecord(root, { source:'old_card', sourceKey:'WorldLedger Inventory copy', originalPreview:'Player owns Crown of Dawn.', reason:'old card prose claim', severity:'medium' });
        add('BC15R-011', qr && qr.status === 'quarantined' && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'Migration quarantine stores prose claims without importing canon');
        const rows = this.inspect(root);
        add('BC15R-012', rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'Foundation depth inspection clean');
      } catch (e) { add('BC15R-ERR', false, 'self-test exception: ' + (e && e.message || e)); }
      finally { globalThis.state = preserveState; }
      return results;
    }
  };

  // Patch StateManager so every hook enters with the deeper support schemas available.
  (function () {
    const oldInit = WL.StateManager.init;
    WL.StateManager.init = function (rawState) {
      const root = oldInit.call(WL.StateManager, rawState);
      WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
      return root;
    };
  })();

  // Re-lock ReducerRegistry so duplicate keys are checked before mutation and recorded only after a successful reducer commit.
  WL.ReducerRegistry.LEDGER_ORDER = 'check_mutate_record_dirty_audit';
  WL.ReducerRegistry.ownerForFamily = function (family) {
    return { inventory: WL.InventoryReducer, ability: WL.AbilityReducer, body: WL.BodyReducer, quest: WL.QuestReducerLite, actor: WL.ActorReducerLite, place: WL.PlaceReducerLite, time: WL.TimeReducerLite }[family] || null;
  };
  WL.ReducerRegistry.isCommitResult = function (result) {
    return !!(result && /committed|pending_visible_only|reference_only|route_only|soft_fail_committed|committed_promise_only/.test(String(result.status || '')));
  };
  WL.ReducerRegistry.applyAcceptedBatch = function (root, validations) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const results = [];
    validations = Array.isArray(validations) ? validations : [];
    for (let i = 0; i < validations.length; i++) {
      const v = validations[i] || {};
      const type = v.type || v.eventType || 'unknown';
      const key = WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey(v.opKey || v.operationKey || WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['validation', type, v.target && (v.target.id || v.target.display || v.target.raw), v.sourceText || '']));
      if (v.reducerAllowed === false || v.canonAllowed === false || (v.status && v.status !== 'accepted')) { results.push({ type, status:'skipped', reason:v.status || v.result || 'not accepted' }); continue; }
      if (['accept','soft_fail','hard_deny'].indexOf(v.result) < 0) { results.push({ type, status:'skipped', reason:v.result || 'non-commit validation result' }); continue; }
      if (WL.OperationLedger.has(root, 'recentKeys', key)) { WL.OperationLedger.auditDuplicate(root, key, 'ReducerRegistry duplicate pre-check for ' + type); results.push({ type, status:'duplicate_skip', reason:'operation already applied' }); continue; }
      const owner = this.ownerForFamily(v.family);
      if (!owner || typeof owner.apply !== 'function') { results.push({ type, status:'no_owner', reason:'No active reducer owner.' }); continue; }
      let r;
      try { r = owner.apply(root, v); }
      catch (e) { r = { type, status:'error', reason:'Reducer failed safely: ' + (e && e.message || e) }; }
      if (this.isCommitResult(r)) WL.OperationLedger.addRecent(root, key, { type, reducer:v.family, result:v.result, recordedAfterCommit:true });
      else if (/duplicate_skip/.test(String(r && r.status))) WL.OperationLedger.auditDuplicate(root, key, 'Reducer reported duplicate for ' + type);
      results.push(r || { type, status:'unknown', reason:'Reducer returned no result.' });
    }
    root.pending.lastReducerResults = results;
    WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'reducers', { message:'reducer results=' + results.length + ' order=' + WL.ReducerRegistry.LEDGER_ORDER, statuses:results.map(r => r.status).join(',') }, 40);
    return results;
  };

  // Defensive reducer guards. These are deliberately redundant with CanonValidator.
  (function () {
    const unsafe = v => WL.FoundationDepthReview.forbiddenFlags(v);
    const targetName = v => WL.FoundationDepthReview.safeTarget(v);

    WL.InventoryReducer.defensiveLocked = true;
    WL.InventoryReducer.guard = function (v, allowedTypes) {
      const bad = unsafe(v);
      if (bad.length) return { ok:false, reason:'unsafe reducer flags: ' + bad.join(', ') };
      if (allowedTypes && allowedTypes.indexOf(v.type) < 0) return { ok:false, reason:'unsupported inventory type' };
      const name = targetName(v);
      if (!name && v.type !== 'currency.gain') return { ok:false, reason:'empty inventory target' };
      if (/^(?:idea|concept|dream|rumor|memory|nothing|something|anything)$/i.test(name)) return { ok:false, reason:'abstract inventory target' };
      return { ok:true, name };
    };
    const invAcquire = WL.InventoryReducer.acquire, invVisible = WL.InventoryReducer.visible;
    WL.InventoryReducer.acquire = function (root, v) { const g=this.guard(v, ['item.acquire']); if(!g.ok) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:g.reason }; return invAcquire.call(this, root, v); };
    WL.InventoryReducer.visible = function (root, v) { if ((v.flags && (v.flags.commandText || v.flags.diagnosticText))) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'diagnostic visible item' }; return invVisible.call(this, root, v); };
    WL.InventoryReducer.currency = function (root, v) {
      const bad = unsafe(v); if (bad.length) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe currency flags: ' + bad.join(', ') };
      const reward = v.data && v.data.reward || WL.Sanitizers.reward(v.target && (v.target.raw || v.target.display) || '');
      if (!reward || !reward.amount || !reward.coin) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected', reason:'No currency parsed.' };
      const payKey = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['reward', v.sourceText || '', reward.amount, reward.coin]);
      if (WL.OperationLedger.hasReward(root, payKey)) { WL.OperationLedger.auditDuplicate(root, payKey, 'Reward payment already applied.'); return { type:v.type, status:'duplicate_skip', reason:'Reward payment already applied.' }; }
      root.inventory.currency[reward.coin] = WL.U.clamp((root.inventory.currency[reward.coin] || 0) + reward.amount, 0, 999999999);
      root.quests.rewardPayments.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), amount:reward.amount, coin:reward.coin, source:v.sourceText || '', opKey:payKey });
      WL.U.capArray(root.quests.rewardPayments, 40);
      WL.OperationLedger.addReward(root, payKey, { reward, recordedAfterCommit:true });
      WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'inventory', 'currency gain', { sourceReducer:'InventoryReducer', sourceOperationKey:payKey, priority:1 });
      return { type:v.type, status:'committed', target:reward.amount + ' ' + reward.coin };
    };

    WL.AbilityReducer.defensiveLocked = true;
    const abilEvidence = WL.AbilityReducer.evidence;
