    WL.AbilityReducer.apply = function (root, v) {
      const name = targetName(v);
      if (!name) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'empty ability target' };
      if (v.type === 'ability.stabilize_claim') return { type:v.type, status:'pending', reason:'stable ability unlocks require future ability lifecycle proof; output-only claim is not enough' };
      if (v.type !== 'ability.evidence') return { type:v.type, status:'ignored', reason:'Ability reducer has no handler.' };
      const bad = unsafe(v).filter(x => x !== 'failed_or_denied');
      if (bad.length) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe ability flags: ' + bad.join(', ') };
      return abilEvidence.call(this, root, v);
    };

    WL.BodyReducer.defensiveLocked = true;
    WL.BodyReducer.apply = function (root, v) {
      const bad = unsafe(v);
      if (bad.length) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe body flags: ' + bad.join(', ') };
      if (v.type === 'body.fatigue') {
        root.body.ep = WL.U.clamp(root.body.ep - 5, 0, root.body.maxEp);
        root.body.epTrack.current = root.body.ep;
        WL.U.uniquePush(root.body.conditions, 'fatigued', 20);
        WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'body', 'fatigue', { sourceReducer:'BodyReducer', sourceOperationKey:v.opKey || v.operationKey, priority:2 });
        return { type:v.type, status:'committed', target:'fatigue' };
      }
      if (v.type === 'body.injury') {
        root.body.hp = WL.U.clamp(root.body.hp - 5, 0, root.body.maxHp);
        root.body.hpTrack.current = root.body.hp;
        WL.U.uniquePush(root.body.injuries, 'minor injury', 20);
        WL.DirtyQueue.add(root, 'body', 'injury', { sourceReducer:'BodyReducer', sourceOperationKey:v.opKey || v.operationKey, priority:1 });
        return { type:v.type, status:'committed', target:'injury' };
      }
      return { type:v.type, status:'pending_or_rejected', reason:v.reason || 'No body reducer handler.' };
    };

    WL.QuestReducerLite.defensiveLocked = true;
    const questOldPromise = WL.QuestReducerLite.promise;
    const questOldApply = WL.QuestReducerLite.apply;
    WL.QuestReducerLite.apply = function (root, v) { const bad=unsafe(v); if(bad.length) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe quest flags: ' + bad.join(', ') }; return questOldApply.call(this, root, v); };
    WL.QuestReducerLite.promise = function (root, v) { const bad=unsafe(v).filter(x => x !== 'future'); if(bad.length) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe reward promise flags: ' + bad.join(', ') }; return questOldPromise.call(this, root, v); };

    WL.ActorReducerLite.defensiveLocked = true;
    const actorOldApply = WL.ActorReducerLite.apply;
    WL.ActorReducerLite.apply = function (root, v) { const bad=unsafe(v); if(bad.length || (v.data && v.data.weakReference)) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe or weak actor reference' }; return actorOldApply.call(this, root, v); };

    WL.PlaceReducerLite.defensiveLocked = true;
    const placeOldApply = WL.PlaceReducerLite.apply;
    WL.PlaceReducerLite.apply = function (root, v) { const bad=unsafe(v); if(bad.length) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe place flags: ' + bad.join(', ') }; if (v.type === 'place.reference' || (v.data && v.data.weakReference)) return placeOldApply.call(this, root, v); const strongAcceptedArrival = v.status === 'accepted' && v.result === 'accept' && /strong named arrival|current-scene confirmation|arrival/i.test(String(v.reason || '')); if ((v.type === 'place.intent' || v.completedAction === false) && !strongAcceptedArrival) return { type:v.type, status:'route_only', reason:'place intent/reference cannot overwrite current scene' }; return placeOldApply.call(this, root, v); };

    WL.TimeReducerLite.defensiveLocked = true;
    const timeOldApply = WL.TimeReducerLite.apply;
    WL.TimeReducerLite.apply = function (root, v) { const bad=unsafe(v); if(bad.length || (v.flags && v.flags.nonliteral)) return { type:v.type, status:'rejected_by_reducer_guard', reason:'unsafe or nonliteral time claim' }; return timeOldApply.call(this, root, v); };
  })();

  // Rich DirtyTarget schema with legacy target compatibility retained.
  WL.DirtyQueue.addRecord = function (root, family, targetKey, reason, meta) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    family = WL.U.slug(family || 'general');
    targetKey = WL.U.slug(targetKey || family);
    reason = WL.U.norm(reason || 'dirty');
    meta = meta || {};
    const dedupeKey = WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey([family, targetKey, reason].join('|'));
    for (let i = 0; i < root.dirty.records.length; i++) {
      const r = root.dirty.records[i];
      if (r.dedupeKey === dedupeKey && r.status === 'queued') { r.lastTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); r.attempts = r.attempts || 0; return r; }
    }
    const rec = { id:'dirty_' + WL.U.hash(dedupeKey), family, targetKey, reason, sourceReducer:meta.sourceReducer || '', sourceOperationKey:meta.sourceOperationKey || '', priority:WL.U.clamp(meta.priority == null ? 5 : meta.priority, 1, 9), status:'queued', attempts:0, createdTurn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), lastTurn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), dedupeKey };
    root.dirty.records.push(rec);
    root.dirty.records.sort((a,b) => (a.priority - b.priority) || (a.createdTurn - b.createdTurn));
    while (root.dirty.records.length > WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets) root.dirty.records.pop();
    if (root.dirty.targets.indexOf(family) < 0) root.dirty.targets.push(family);
    WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.targets, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets);
    root.dirty.lastReason = reason;
    return rec;
  };
  WL.DirtyQueue.add = function (root, target, reason, meta) { return this.addRecord(root, target, target, reason, meta || {}); };
  WL.DirtyQueue.markProcessed = function (root, dedupeKey, status) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); for (let i=0;i<root.dirty.records.length;i++){ if(root.dirty.records[i].dedupeKey===dedupeKey){ root.dirty.records[i].status=status || 'processed'; root.dirty.records[i].attempts=(root.dirty.records[i].attempts||0)+1; return root.dirty.records[i]; } } return null; };
  WL.DirtyQueue.inspect = function (root) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); return [
    root.dirty.records.length <= WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyTarget cap respected'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyTarget cap exceeded'},
    root.dirty.records.every(r => r.family && r.targetKey && r.reason && r.status && typeof r.priority === 'number') ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyTarget schema valid'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyTarget schema invalid'},
    Array.isArray(root.dirty.targets) ? {status:'PASS', label:'legacy dirty target list retained for compatibility'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'legacy dirty target list missing'}
  ]; };

  // Packetized ContextBuilderLite: still read-only, but now auditable by packet family and selection reason.
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.packet = function (family, priority, text, sourceHash, ttlTurns) {
    text = WL.U.norm(text || '');
    return { id:'ctx_' + WL.U.hash(family + '|' + text), family, priority:WL.U.clamp(priority || 5, 1, 9), text, sourceHash:sourceHash || WL.U.hash(text), ttlTurns:ttlTurns || 1, createdTurn:0, selected:false, skippedReason:'' };
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.buildPackets = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const b = root.body;
    const packets = [];
    packets.push(this.packet('rule', 1, '[WorldLedger Current Truth]\nRules: player input is intent; output confirms consequences; rumors, dreams, quotes, commands, and Story Card text are not canon by themselves.', 'rules', 1));
    packets.push(this.packet('player', 1, 'Player: ' + root.player.name + ' | Level ' + root.player.level + ' | HP ' + b.hp + '/' + b.maxHp + ' MP ' + b.mp + '/' + b.maxMp + ' EP ' + b.ep + '/' + b.maxEp, 'player_' + root.player.level + '_' + b.hp + '_' + b.mp + '_' + b.ep, 1));
    if (root.places.currentId && root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId]) packets.push(this.packet('place', 2, 'Current place: ' + root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId].name, root.places.currentId, 1));
    const items = root.inventory.ownedIds.map(id => root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].name).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 8);
    if (items.length) packets.push(this.packet('inventory', 2, 'Inventory: ' + items.join(', '), WL.U.hash(items.join('|')), 1));
    const cands = root.abilities.candidateIds.map(id => root.abilities.candidatesById[id] && root.abilities.candidatesById[id].name + ' candidate').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6);
    if (cands.length) packets.push(this.packet('abilities', 2, 'Ability development: ' + cands.join(', ') + '. Candidates are not stable abilities yet.', WL.U.hash(cands.join('|')), 1));
    const quests = root.quests.activeIds.map(id => root.quests.questsById[id] && root.quests.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 5);
    if (quests.length) packets.push(this.packet('quests', 3, 'Active quests: ' + quests.join('; '), WL.U.hash(quests.join('|')), 2));
    if (root.body.conditions.length) packets.push(this.packet('conditions', 2, 'Conditions: ' + root.body.conditions.join(', '), WL.U.hash(root.body.conditions.join('|')), 1));
    if (root.body.injuries.length) packets.push(this.packet('injuries', 1, 'Injuries: ' + root.body.injuries.join(', '), WL.U.hash(root.body.injuries.join('|')), 1));
    packets.forEach(p => p.createdTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root));
    root.context.lastPackets = packets;
    return packets;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.selectPackets = function (root, packets, budget) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    budget = WL.U.clamp(budget || root.features.contextBudget || 1800, 400, 5000);
    const selected = [], skipped = [];
    let used = 0;
    packets.slice().sort((a,b) => (a.priority - b.priority) || (a.family > b.family ? 1 : -1)).forEach(p => {
      if (!p.text) { p.skippedReason='empty'; skipped.push(p); return; }
      if (used + p.text.length + 1 <= budget) { p.selected = true; selected.push(p); used += p.text.length + 1; }
      else { p.selected = false; p.skippedReason = 'budget'; skipped.push(p); }
    });
    root.context.skippedPackets = skipped.map(p => ({ id:p.id, family:p.family, reason:p.skippedReason }));
    root.context.packetAudit.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), budget, selected:selected.map(p => p.family), skipped:root.context.skippedPackets, used });
    WL.U.capArray(root.context.packetAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.context);
    return selected;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.build = function (root, baseText) {
    const packets = this.buildPackets(root);
    const selected = this.selectPackets(root, packets, root.features.contextBudget || 1800);
    let packet = selected.map(p => p.text).join('\n');
    if (!packet) packet = '[WorldLedger Current Truth]\nNo compact state packet selected.';
    WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'context', { message:'Context packet built, packets=' + selected.length + ', chars=' + packet.length, selected:selected.map(p=>p.family).join(',') }, 40);
    return baseText ? String(baseText) + '\n\n' + packet : packet;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect = function (root) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); const packets=this.buildPackets(root); return [
    packets.every(p => p.id && p.family && typeof p.priority === 'number' && typeof p.sourceHash === 'string') ? {status:'PASS', label:'context packet schema valid'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'context packet schema invalid'},
    Array.isArray(root.context.packetAudit) ? {status:'PASS', label:'context packet audit bucket present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'context packet audit missing'}
  ]; };

  // CardSyncLite protected-registry/tombstone/duplicate dry-run depth. Still no live writes.
  WL.CardSyncLite.protectedRegistry = function (root) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); return root.cards.protectedRegistry.slice(); };
  WL.CardSyncLite.renderProtectedRecord = function (root, rec) {
    const title = rec && rec.title || 'WorldLedger Unknown';
    let text = title + '\n';
    if (/Player/.test(title)) text += 'Player: ' + root.player.name + ' | Level ' + root.player.level + ' | HP ' + root.body.hp + '/' + root.body.maxHp + '\n';
    else if (/Inventory/.test(title)) text += 'Owned: ' + root.inventory.ownedIds.map(id => root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '\n';
    else if (/Abilities/.test(title)) text += 'Candidates: ' + root.abilities.candidateIds.map(id => root.abilities.candidatesById[id] && root.abilities.candidatesById[id].name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '\n';
    else if (/Quests/.test(title)) text += 'Active: ' + root.quests.activeIds.map(id => root.quests.questsById[id] && root.quests.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean).join('; ') + '\n';
    else text += 'Protected dry-run mirror. Cards mirror reducer-owned state only.\n';
    return { title, keys: WL.Sanitizers.cardTrigger(title), value:text, hash:WL.U.hash(text) };
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.auditProtectedSingletons = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
    const seen = {}, dupes = [];
    cards.forEach((c, idx) => { const t=WL.U.lower(c && (c.title || c.name) || ''); if(!t) return; if(seen[t] != null) dupes.push({ title:t, firstIndex:seen[t], duplicateIndex:idx, status:'audit_only' }); else seen[t]=idx; });
    root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit = dupes.filter(d => (root.cards.protectedRegistry || []).some(r => WL.U.lower(r.title) === d.title));
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit, 20);
    return root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.dryRun = function (root) {
    if (!root.features.cardSyncLiteEnabled) return 0;
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const dirty = (root.dirty.records || []).filter(r => r.status === 'queued').slice(0, 4);
    const registry = this.protectedRegistry(root);
    let writes = 0;
    registry.slice(0, 4).forEach(rec => {
      if (dirty.length && !dirty.some(d => rec.title.toLowerCase().indexOf(d.family) >= 0 || d.family === 'inventory' || d.family === 'body')) return;
      const rendered = WL.CardSyncLite.renderProtectedRecord(root, rec);
      root.cards.renderRecords.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), id:rec.id, title:rec.title, hash:rendered.hash, mode:'dry-run' });
      root.cards.dryRunWrites.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), target:rec.title, mode:'dry-run', renderHash:rendered.hash });
      rec.renderHash = rendered.hash; rec.lastDryRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); writes++;
    });
    this.auditProtectedSingletons(root);
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.renderRecords, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.cards);
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.dryRunWrites, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dryRunWrites);
    if (writes) WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'cards', { message:'CardSync protected dry-run writes=' + writes, dirty:dirty.map(d => d.family).join(',') }, 40);
    return writes;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.audit = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const lines = [];
    const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
    const dupes = this.auditProtectedSingletons(root);
    lines.push('Mode: dry-run=' + !!root.features.cardSyncDryRun + ', protectedWrites=' + !!root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled + ', generated=false');
    lines.push('Visible Story Cards detected: ' + cards.length);
    lines.push('Protected registry records: ' + root.cards.protectedRegistry.length);
    lines.push('Protected duplicate audit records: ' + dupes.length);
    lines.push('Tombstones: ' + root.cards.tombstones.length + ' (policy present; live removal disabled)');
    root.cards.protectedRegistry.forEach(r => lines.push('Protected: ' + r.title + ' [' + r.id + ']'));
    lines.push('Generated CardSync is disabled in Code Pass 1. Cards never create canon. Story Card writes remain dry-run only.');
    return lines;
  };

  // Migration/quarantine schema depth. Still conservative: never imports old prose as truth.
  WL.MigrationManager.quarantineRecord = function (root, record) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const rec = Object.assign({ id:'q_' + WL.U.hash(WL.U.safeJson(record || {}) + '|' + WL.U.nowTurn(root)), turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), source:'unknown', sourceKey:'', originalPreview:'', reason:'unspecified', severity:'info', status:'quarantined', owner:'MigrationManager', suggestedAction:'manual review in future migration pass', unresolved:true }, record || {});
    rec.source = WL.U.norm(rec.source); rec.sourceKey = WL.U.norm(rec.sourceKey); rec.originalPreview = WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(rec.originalPreview || '', 260); rec.reason = WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(rec.reason || 'unspecified', 180);
    root.quarantine.records.push(rec);
    root.migration.quarantineAudit.push({ turn:rec.turn, id:rec.id, source:rec.source, sourceKey:rec.sourceKey, reason:rec.reason, severity:rec.severity, status:rec.status });
    root.quarantine.unresolvedCount = root.quarantine.records.filter(r => r && r.unresolved !== false).length;
    root.migration.unresolvedCount = root.quarantine.unresolvedCount;
    WL.U.capArray(root.quarantine.records, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.quarantine);
    WL.U.capArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.migration);
    return rec;
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.run = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    if (root.migration.schema !== WL.SCHEMA_VERSION) {
      this.quarantineRecord(root, { source:'state.worldledger', sourceKey:'schema_' + root.migration.schema, originalPreview:'unknown/future schema marker', reason:'Future/unknown schema found. Repaired conservatively.', severity:'medium' });
      root.migration.schema = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
    }
    root.migration.lastRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
    return true;
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.inspect = function (root) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); return [
    Array.isArray(root.quarantine.records) ? {status:'PASS', label:'quarantine records array present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'quarantine records missing'},
    root.quarantine.records.every(r => r.id && r.source && r.reason && r.status) ? {status:'PASS', label:'QuarantineRecord schema valid'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'QuarantineRecord schema invalid'},
    Array.isArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit) ? {status:'PASS', label:'migration quarantine audit present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'migration quarantine audit missing'}
  ]; };

  // Cleanup should cap rich records without deleting active truth.
  WL.CleanupManagerLite.inspect = function (root) { return [
    root.cleanup && typeof root.cleanup.capsApplied === 'number' ? {status:'PASS', label:'cleanup counters present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'cleanup counters missing'},
    root.dirty && (!root.dirty.records || root.dirty.records.length <= WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets) ? {status:'PASS', label:'cleanup respects dirty cap'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'dirty cap exceeded'}
  ]; };
  WL.CleanupManagerLite.run = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    WL.StateManager.clampLogs(root);
    WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.records, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets);
    WL.U.capArray(root.context.packetAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.context);
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.renderRecords, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.cards);
    WL.U.capArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.migration);
    root.cleanup.lastRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
    root.cleanup.capsApplied += 1;
    return true;
  };

  // Deeper ReleaseCheck gates: it must fail on missing depth, not only missing modules.
  (function () {
    const oldRun = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run;
    WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run = function (root) {
      WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
      const out = oldRun.call(WL.ReleaseCheckLite, root);
      const rows = WL.FoundationDepthReview.inspect(root);
      const fails = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL');
      rows.forEach(r => out.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      out.push(WL.FoundationDepthReview.runSelfTests().filter(t => !t.ok).length === 0 ? this.pass('BC15R support-depth review self-tests pass') : this.fail('BC15R support-depth review self-tests failing'));
      const dirtyRows = WL.DirtyQueue.inspect(root), ctxRows = WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect(root), migRows = WL.MigrationManager.inspect(root), cleanRows = WL.CleanupManagerLite.inspect(root);
      [dirtyRows, ctxRows, migRows, cleanRows].forEach(set => set.forEach(r => out.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label)));
      out.push(root.cards.protectedRegistry && root.cards.protectedRegistry.length === WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.length ? this.pass('protected card registry depth matches protected title list') : this.fail('protected card registry depth mismatch'));
      out.push(WL.CardSyncLite && typeof WL.CardSyncLite.auditProtectedSingletons === 'function' ? this.pass('protected card duplicate audit gate present') : this.fail('protected card duplicate audit gate missing'));
      out.push(WL.ReducerRegistry.LEDGER_ORDER === 'check_mutate_record_dirty_audit' ? this.pass('reducer operation-key ordering documented and enforced') : this.fail('reducer operation-key ordering unsafe'));
      if (fails.length) out.push(this.fail('support-depth inspection failures: ' + fails.length));
      root.audits.releasecheck = out.map(x => ({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), text:x }));
      return out;
    };
  })();

  // Small command renderer additions without making them gameplay mutators.
  WL.CommandRenderer.supportdepth = function (root) {
    const rows = WL.FoundationDepthReview.inspect(root);
    const tests = WL.FoundationDepthReview.runSelfTests();
    return this.header('Support Depth Review') + rows.map(r => r.status + ': ' + r.label).join('\n') + '\nSelf-tests: ' + tests.filter(t => t.ok).length + '/' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
  };
  if (WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf('supportdepth') < 0) WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS = Object.freeze(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.concat(['supportdepth']));
  WL.CommandRouter.activeDispatch = Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, WL.CommandRouter.activeDispatch, { supportdepth:'supportdepth' }));

  // Minimal bundled self-test for local/dev use. It is inspect-only and never runs automatically.
  WL.devSelfTest = function () {
    globalThis.state = {};
    let r;
    WL.onInput('/releasecheck');
    WL.onInput('/layout');
    WL.onInput('/schema');
    WL.onInput('/commandformat');
    WL.onInput('/statemanager');
    WL.onInput('/commandrouter');
    WL.onInput('/commandcheck');
    WL.onInput('/parser');
    WL.onInput('/parsercheck');
    WL.onInput('/proposalcheck');
    WL.onInput('/validatorcheck');
    WL.onInput('/resolvercheck');
    WL.onInput('/runtimecheck');
    WL.onInput('/audit');
    WL.onInput('/ledgercheck');
    WL.onInput('I train Ember Ward to protect allies.');
    WL.onOutput('Nothing happens, but you feel the shape of Ember Ward resist you.');
    r = WL.StateManager.root();
    const hasEmber = !!r.abilities.candidatesById.ability_ember_ward;
    WL.onOutput('You decide not to take the rusted iron key.');
    const noKey = Object.keys(WL.StateManager.root().inventory.itemsById).length === 0;
    WL.onOutput('Mira refuses the crown but hands you a potion in your hand.');
    const hasPotion = !!WL.StateManager.root().inventory.itemsById.item_potion;
    return { hasEmber, noKey, hasPotion, releasecheck: WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run(WL.StateManager.root()) };
  };


  // ============================================================
  // 15.2 Support Layer Schema Completion Overlay
  // ============================================================
  WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION = 'worldledger.v15.support-layer-schema.v2';
  WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_LOCK_VERSION = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES = Object.freeze({
    DirtyTarget: ['dirtyId','family','targetKey','reason','sourceReducer','sourceOperationKey','priority','status','createdTurn','processedTurn','attempts','notes'],
    ContextPacket: ['packetId','family','priority','text','sourceStateKeys','sourceHashes','maxAgeTurns','createdTurn','expiresTurn','reason','safeForModel','notes'],
    ProtectedCardRecord: ['protectedKey','stableKey','title','triggerKeys','lastRenderedHash','lastSyncedTurn','dirty','duplicateIds','tombstoneId','warnings','manualEditDetected','writeBudgetStatus','notes'],
    QuarantineRecord: ['quarantineId','source','sourceKey','originalPreview','reason','severity','suggestedAction','status','createdTurn','notes'],
    ReleaseCheckRecord: ['gateId','status','label','family','required','notes'],
    KnownFailureCoverageSummary: ['coverageId','family','knownFailure','coveredBy','status']
  });

  (function () {
    const oldEnsureDepthSchemas = WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas;
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas = function (root) {
      oldEnsureDepthSchemas.call(WL.FoundationDepthReview, root);
      if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return root;
      root.supportSchemas = root.supportSchemas || {};
      root.supportSchemas.version = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      root.supportSchemas.shapes = Object.keys(WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES);

      if (!root.dirty || typeof root.dirty !== 'object') root.dirty = { targets: [], records: [], lastReason: '' };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.dirty.records)) root.dirty.records = [];
      root.dirty.schemaVersion = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      root.dirty.records = root.dirty.records.map((r, idx) => WL.DirtyQueue.normalizeTarget(root, r, idx));
      if (!Array.isArray(root.dirty.targets)) root.dirty.targets = [];

      if (!root.context || typeof root.context !== 'object') root.context = {};
      root.context.schemaVersion = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      ['lastPackets','selectedPackets','skippedPackets','packetAudit','expiredPackets'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(root.context[k])) root.context[k] = []; });
      root.context.lastPackets = root.context.lastPackets.map((p, idx) => WL.ContextBuilderLite.normalizePacket(root, p, idx));

      if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== 'object') root.cards = {};
      root.cards.schemaVersion = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.protectedRegistry)) root.cards.protectedRegistry = [];
      if (!root.cards.protectedRegistry.length) root.cards.protectedRegistry = (root.cards.protectedTitles || WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS || []).map((title, idx) => WL.CardSyncLite.makeProtectedRecord(root, title, idx));
      root.cards.protectedRegistry = root.cards.protectedRegistry.map((r, idx) => WL.CardSyncLite.normalizeProtectedRecord(root, r, idx));
      ['protectedDuplicateAudit','tombstones','renderRecords','manualEditAudit','adapterAudit','writeBudgetAudit'].forEach(k => { if (!Array.isArray(root.cards[k])) root.cards[k] = []; });

      if (!root.migration || typeof root.migration !== 'object') root.migration = { schema: WL.SCHEMA_VERSION, warnings: [], repairs: [] };
      root.migration.schemaVersion = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit)) root.migration.quarantineAudit = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.migration.futureSchemaWarnings)) root.migration.futureSchemaWarnings = [];
      if (!root.quarantine || typeof root.quarantine !== 'object') root.quarantine = { records: [], notes: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.quarantine.records)) root.quarantine.records = [];
      root.quarantine.schemaVersion = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      root.quarantine.records = root.quarantine.records.map((r, idx) => WL.MigrationManager.normalizeQuarantineRecord(root, r, idx));
      root.quarantine.inactiveClaims = root.quarantine.records.map(r => ({ quarantineId: r.quarantineId, source: r.source, sourceKey: r.sourceKey, status: r.status, reason: r.reason }));

      if (!root.releasecheck || typeof root.releasecheck !== 'object') root.releasecheck = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.releasecheck.records)) root.releasecheck.records = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.releasecheck.knownFailureCoverage)) root.releasecheck.knownFailureCoverage = [];
      root.releasecheck.schemaVersion = WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      return root;
    };

    WL.FoundationDepthReview.supportSchemaRows = function (root) {
      this.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
      const rows = [];
      const hasFields = (obj, fields) => fields.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj || {}, k));
      const sampleDirty = root.dirty.records[0] || WL.DirtyQueue.makeTarget(root, { family:'schema', targetKey:'schema', reason:'schema sample', sourceReducer:'SupportSchema', sourceOperationKey:'schema_sample', priority:9 });
      rows.push(hasFields(sampleDirty, WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.DirtyTarget) ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyTarget schema has required fields'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyTarget schema missing fields'});
      const samplePacket = (root.context.lastPackets[0] || WL.ContextBuilderLite.makePacket(root, { family:'schema', priority:9, text:'schema sample', sourceStateKeys:['supportSchemas'], reason:'schema sample' }));
      rows.push(hasFields(samplePacket, WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ContextPacket) ? {status:'PASS', label:'ContextPacket schema has required fields'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ContextPacket schema missing fields'});
      const sampleCard = (root.cards.protectedRegistry[0] || WL.CardSyncLite.makeProtectedRecord(root, 'WorldLedger Player', 0));
      rows.push(hasFields(sampleCard, WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ProtectedCardRecord) ? {status:'PASS', label:'ProtectedCardRecord schema has required fields'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ProtectedCardRecord schema missing fields'});
      const sampleQ = root.quarantine.records[0] || WL.MigrationManager.normalizeQuarantineRecord(root, { source:'schema', sourceKey:'sample', reason:'schema sample' }, 0);
      rows.push(hasFields(sampleQ, WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.QuarantineRecord) ? {status:'PASS', label:'QuarantineRecord schema has required fields'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'QuarantineRecord schema missing fields'});
      rows.push(root.supportSchemas && root.supportSchemas.version === WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION ? {status:'PASS', label:'support schema version locked: ' + WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION} : {status:'FAIL', label:'support schema version missing'});
      return rows;
    };
  })();

  // DirtyQueue real DirtyTarget model. DirtyQueue remains a mirror/context refresh queue, not gameplay truth.
  WL.DirtyQueue.makeTarget = function (root, spec) {
    spec = spec || {};
    const family = WL.U.slug(spec.family || spec.target || 'general');
    const targetKey = WL.U.slug(spec.targetKey || spec.target || family);
    const reason = WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(spec.reason || 'dirty', 180);
    const sourceOperationKey = WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey(spec.sourceOperationKey || spec.operationKey || [family, targetKey, reason, WL.U.nowTurn(root)].join('|'));
    const dedupeKey = WL.OperationLedger.normalizeKey(spec.dedupeKey || [family, targetKey, reason, sourceOperationKey].join('|'));
    const createdTurn = Number(spec.createdTurn != null ? spec.createdTurn : WL.U.nowTurn(root));
    return {
      dirtyId: spec.dirtyId || spec.id || ('dirty_' + WL.U.hash(dedupeKey)),
      id: spec.dirtyId || spec.id || ('dirty_' + WL.U.hash(dedupeKey)),
      family,
      targetKey,
      reason,
      sourceReducer: WL.U.norm(spec.sourceReducer || ''),
      sourceOperationKey,
      priority: WL.U.clamp(spec.priority == null ? 5 : spec.priority, 1, 9),
      status: spec.status || 'queued',
      createdTurn,
      processedTurn: Number(spec.processedTurn || 0),
      attempts: WL.U.clamp(spec.attempts || 0, 0, 9999),
      notes: Array.isArray(spec.notes) ? spec.notes.slice(0, 6) : (spec.notes ? [String(spec.notes).slice(0,120)] : []),
      dedupeKey,
      lastTurn: Number(spec.lastTurn || createdTurn)
    };
  };
  WL.DirtyQueue.normalizeTarget = function (root, r, idx) {
    r = r || {};
    if (r.dirtyId && r.family && r.targetKey && r.reason) return WL.DirtyQueue.makeTarget(root, r);
    return WL.DirtyQueue.makeTarget(root, {
      dirtyId: r.dirtyId || r.id || ('dirty_legacy_' + idx), family: r.family || r.target || r.targetKey || 'legacy', targetKey: r.targetKey || r.target || r.family || 'legacy', reason: r.reason || root.dirty && root.dirty.lastReason || 'legacy dirty target', sourceReducer: r.sourceReducer || '', sourceOperationKey: r.sourceOperationKey || r.opKey || '', priority: r.priority || 5, status: r.status || 'queued', createdTurn: r.createdTurn || r.turn || 0, processedTurn: r.processedTurn || 0, attempts: r.attempts || 0, notes: r.notes || []
    });
  };
  WL.DirtyQueue.dedupeTarget = function (root, spec) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const rec = this.makeTarget(root, spec || {});
    for (let i = 0; i < root.dirty.records.length; i++) {
      const existing = root.dirty.records[i];
      if (existing.dedupeKey === rec.dedupeKey && existing.status === 'queued') return existing;
    }
    return null;
  };
  WL.DirtyQueue.addTarget = function (root, spec) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const found = this.dedupeTarget(root, spec);
    if (found) { found.lastTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); found.notes.push('deduped turn ' + WL.U.nowTurn(root)); WL.U.capArray(found.notes, 6); return found; }
    const rec = this.makeTarget(root, spec || {});
    root.dirty.records.push(rec);
    this.prioritize(root);
    this.capSafely(root);
    if (root.dirty.targets.indexOf(rec.family) < 0) root.dirty.targets.push(rec.family);
    WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.targets, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets);
    root.dirty.lastReason = rec.reason;
    return rec;
  };
  WL.DirtyQueue.addRecord = function (root, family, targetKey, reason, meta) { meta = meta || {}; return this.addTarget(root, Object.assign({}, meta, { family, targetKey, reason })); };
  WL.DirtyQueue.add = function (root, target, reason, meta) { return this.addRecord(root, target, target, reason, meta || {}); };
  WL.DirtyQueue.findByKey = function (root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); const k = WL.U.norm(dirtyIdOrDedupeKey); return root.dirty.records.filter(r => r.dirtyId === k || r.id === k || r.dedupeKey === k)[0] || null; };
  WL.DirtyQueue.markProcessed = function (root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey) { const r = this.findByKey(root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey); if (!r) return null; r.status = 'processed'; r.processedTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); r.attempts += 1; return r; };
  WL.DirtyQueue.markSkipped = function (root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey, reason) { const r = this.findByKey(root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey); if (!r) return null; r.status = 'skipped'; r.processedTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); r.attempts += 1; r.notes.push(WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(reason || 'skipped', 120)); WL.U.capArray(r.notes, 6); return r; };
  WL.DirtyQueue.markError = function (root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey, reason) { const r = this.findByKey(root, dirtyIdOrDedupeKey); if (!r) return null; r.status = 'error'; r.processedTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); r.attempts += 1; r.notes.push(WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(reason || 'error', 120)); WL.U.capArray(r.notes, 6); return r; };
  WL.DirtyQueue.prioritize = function (root) { WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root); root.dirty.records.sort((a,b) => (a.priority - b.priority) || (a.createdTurn - b.createdTurn) || (a.family > b.family ? 1 : -1)); return root.dirty.records; };
  WL.DirtyQueue.capSafely = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    this.prioritize(root);
    const keep = [], overflow = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < root.dirty.records.length; i++) {
      const r = root.dirty.records[i];
      if (keep.length < WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets || r.priority <= 2) keep.push(r); else overflow.push(r);
    }
    root.dirty.records = keep.slice(0, Math.max(WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets, keep.filter(r => r.priority <= 2).length));
    if (overflow.length) WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'cleanup', { message:'DirtyQueue cap skipped low-priority targets=' + overflow.length }, 40);
    return root.dirty.records.length;
  };
  WL.DirtyQueue.inspect = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const fields = WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.DirtyTarget;
    const rows = [];
    rows.push(root.dirty.schemaVersion === WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyQueue schema version locked'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyQueue schema version missing'});
    rows.push(root.dirty.records.every(r => fields.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(r, k))) ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyTarget required fields present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyTarget required fields missing'});
    rows.push(typeof this.addTarget === 'function' && typeof this.dedupeTarget === 'function' && typeof this.markProcessed === 'function' && typeof this.markSkipped === 'function' && typeof this.markError === 'function' && typeof this.prioritize === 'function' && typeof this.capSafely === 'function' ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyQueue lifecycle helpers present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyQueue lifecycle helper missing'});
    rows.push(root.dirty.records.length <= Math.max(WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dirtyTargets, root.dirty.records.filter(r => r.priority <= 2).length) ? {status:'PASS', label:'DirtyQueue cap policy safe'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'DirtyQueue cap policy failed'});
    return rows;
  };

  // ContextBuilder real ContextPacket model. It reads gameplay state only and writes only context audit buckets.
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.computeSourceHashes = function (root, sourceStateKeys) {
    const hashes = {};
    (sourceStateKeys || []).forEach(k => {
      let value = '';
      try { value = k.split('.').reduce((obj, part) => obj && obj[part], root); } catch (e) { value = ''; }
      hashes[k] = WL.U.hash(WL.U.safeJson(value));
    });
    return hashes;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.makePacket = function (root, spec) {
    spec = spec || {};
    const family = WL.U.slug(spec.family || 'general');
    const text = WL.U.norm(spec.text || '');
    const sourceStateKeys = Array.isArray(spec.sourceStateKeys) ? spec.sourceStateKeys.slice(0, 8) : [];
    const createdTurn = Number(spec.createdTurn != null ? spec.createdTurn : WL.U.nowTurn(root));
    const maxAgeTurns = WL.U.clamp(spec.maxAgeTurns || 1, 1, 50);
    const sourceHashes = spec.sourceHashes || this.computeSourceHashes(root, sourceStateKeys);
    return {
      packetId: spec.packetId || spec.id || ('ctx_' + WL.U.hash([family, text, WL.U.safeJson(sourceHashes)].join('|'))),
      id: spec.packetId || spec.id || ('ctx_' + WL.U.hash([family, text, WL.U.safeJson(sourceHashes)].join('|'))),
      family,
      priority: WL.U.clamp(spec.priority || 5, 1, 9),
      text,
      sourceStateKeys,
      sourceHashes,
      maxAgeTurns,
      createdTurn,
      expiresTurn: Number(spec.expiresTurn || (createdTurn + maxAgeTurns)),
      reason: WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(spec.reason || family + ' context', 120),
      safeForModel: spec.safeForModel !== false,
      notes: Array.isArray(spec.notes) ? spec.notes.slice(0, 6) : [],
      selected: !!spec.selected,
      skippedReason: spec.skippedReason || '',
      charCount: text.length
    };
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.normalizePacket = function (root, p, idx) {
    p = p || {};
    if (p.packetId && p.family && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p, 'sourceHashes')) return this.makePacket(root, p);
    return this.makePacket(root, { packetId:p.packetId || p.id || ('ctx_legacy_' + idx), family:p.family || 'legacy', priority:p.priority || 5, text:p.text || '', sourceStateKeys:p.sourceStateKeys || [], sourceHashes:p.sourceHashes || (p.sourceHash ? { legacy:p.sourceHash } : {}), maxAgeTurns:p.maxAgeTurns || p.ttlTurns || 1, createdTurn:p.createdTurn || 0, expiresTurn:p.expiresTurn || 1, reason:p.reason || 'legacy packet', safeForModel:p.safeForModel !== false, notes:p.notes || [], selected:!!p.selected, skippedReason:p.skippedReason || '' });
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.buildPackets = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const b = root.body;
    const packets = [];
    packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'rule', priority:1, text:'[WorldLedger Current Truth]\nInput is intent; AI output confirms consequences. Dreams, rumors, quotes, commands, context, and Story Card prose are not canon by themselves.', sourceStateKeys:['meta.schemaId','features.generatedCardsEnabled'], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'core canon rules' }));
    packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'player', priority:1, text:'Player: ' + root.player.name + ' | Level ' + root.player.level + ' | HP ' + b.hp + '/' + b.maxHp + ' MP ' + b.mp + '/' + b.maxMp + ' EP ' + b.ep + '/' + b.maxEp, sourceStateKeys:['player','body'], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'player resources' }));
    if (root.places.currentId && root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId]) packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'place', priority:2, text:'Current place: ' + root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId].name, sourceStateKeys:['places.currentId','places.placesById.' + root.places.currentId], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'current scene' }));
    const items = root.inventory.ownedIds.map(id => root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].name).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 8);
    if (items.length) packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'inventory', priority:2, text:'Inventory: ' + items.join(', '), sourceStateKeys:['inventory.ownedIds','inventory.itemsById'], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'owned item truth' }));
    const cands = root.abilities.candidateIds.map(id => root.abilities.candidatesById[id] && root.abilities.candidatesById[id].name + ' candidate').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6);
    if (cands.length) packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'abilities', priority:2, text:'Ability development: ' + cands.join(', ') + '. Candidates are not stable abilities yet.', sourceStateKeys:['abilities.candidateIds','abilities.candidatesById'], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'ability candidate truth' }));
    const quests = root.quests.activeIds.map(id => root.quests.questsById[id] && root.quests.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 5);
    if (quests.length) packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'quests', priority:3, text:'Active quests: ' + quests.join('; '), sourceStateKeys:['quests.activeIds','quests.questsById'], maxAgeTurns:2, reason:'active quest truth' }));
    if (root.body.conditions.length) packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'conditions', priority:2, text:'Conditions: ' + root.body.conditions.join(', '), sourceStateKeys:['body.conditions'], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'condition truth' }));
    if (root.body.injuries.length) packets.push(this.makePacket(root, { family:'injuries', priority:1, text:'Injuries: ' + root.body.injuries.join(', '), sourceStateKeys:['body.injuries'], maxAgeTurns:1, reason:'injury truth' }));
    packets.forEach(p => { p.createdTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); p.expiresTurn = p.createdTurn + p.maxAgeTurns; });
    root.context.lastPackets = packets;
    return packets;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.expireStalePackets = function (root, packets) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const turn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
    const fresh = [], expired = [];
    (packets || root.context.lastPackets || []).forEach(p => {
      const packet = this.normalizePacket(root, p, 0);
      if (packet.expiresTurn < turn || !packet.safeForModel) { packet.skippedReason = packet.safeForModel ? 'expired' : 'unsafe_for_model'; expired.push(packet); }
      else fresh.push(packet);
    });
    root.context.expiredPackets = expired.map(p => ({ packetId:p.packetId, family:p.family, reason:p.skippedReason }));
    return fresh;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.applyBudget = function (root, packets, budget) { return this.selectPackets(root, packets, budget); };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.selectPackets = function (root, packets, budget) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    budget = WL.U.clamp(budget || root.features.contextBudget || 1800, 400, 5000);
    const source = this.expireStalePackets(root, packets || []);
    const selected = [], skipped = [];
    let used = 0;
    source.slice().sort((a,b) => (a.priority - b.priority) || (a.createdTurn - b.createdTurn)).forEach(p => {
      p = this.normalizePacket(root, p, 0);
      if (!p.text) { p.skippedReason = 'empty'; skipped.push(p); return; }
      if (used + p.text.length + 1 <= budget) { p.selected = true; p.skippedReason = ''; selected.push(p); used += p.text.length + 1; }
      else { p.selected = false; p.skippedReason = 'budget'; skipped.push(p); }
    });
    this.auditSelectedAndSkipped(root, selected, skipped, budget, used);
    return selected;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.auditSelectedAndSkipped = function (root, selected, skipped, budget, used) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    root.context.selectedPackets = (selected || []).map(p => ({ packetId:p.packetId, family:p.family, priority:p.priority, chars:p.charCount }));
    root.context.skippedPackets = (skipped || []).map(p => ({ packetId:p.packetId, family:p.family, reason:p.skippedReason || 'skipped' }));
    root.context.packetAudit.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), budget:budget || 0, used:used || 0, selected:root.context.selectedPackets, skipped:root.context.skippedPackets });
    WL.U.capArray(root.context.packetAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.context);
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.build = function (root, baseText) {
    const packets = this.buildPackets(root);
    const selected = this.applyBudget(root, packets, root.features.contextBudget || 1800);
    let packet = selected.map(p => p.text).join('\n');
    if (!packet) packet = '[WorldLedger Current Truth]\nNo compact state packet selected.';
    WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'context', { message:'ContextPacket build selected=' + selected.length + ', chars=' + packet.length, selected:selected.map(p => p.family).join(',') }, 40);
    return baseText ? String(baseText) + '\n\n' + packet : packet;
  };
  WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const packets = this.buildPackets(root);
    const fields = WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ContextPacket;
    return [
      root.context.schemaVersion === WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION ? {status:'PASS', label:'ContextBuilder schema version locked'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ContextBuilder schema version missing'},
      packets.every(p => fields.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p, k))) ? {status:'PASS', label:'ContextPacket required fields present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ContextPacket required fields missing'},
      typeof this.buildPackets === 'function' && typeof this.selectPackets === 'function' && typeof this.applyBudget === 'function' && typeof this.auditSelectedAndSkipped === 'function' && typeof this.computeSourceHashes === 'function' && typeof this.expireStalePackets === 'function' ? {status:'PASS', label:'ContextBuilder packet lifecycle helpers present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ContextBuilder packet lifecycle helper missing'},
      Array.isArray(root.context.packetAudit) ? {status:'PASS', label:'ContextBuilder packet audit present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ContextBuilder packet audit missing'}
    ];
  };

  // Protected CardSync registry depth. Still dry-run only; no generated cards and no live card writes.
  WL.CardSyncLite.makeProtectedRecord = function (root, title, idx) {
    title = WL.U.norm(title || 'WorldLedger Unknown');
    const protectedKey = 'protected_' + WL.U.slug(title);
    const stableKey = 'WL:PROTECTED:' + WL.U.slug(title).toUpperCase();
    return {
      protectedKey,
      id: protectedKey,
      stableKey,
      title,
      triggerKeys: [title].concat(String(title).replace(/^WorldLedger\s+/i, '')).map(x => WL.Sanitizers.cardTrigger(x)).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 4),
      family: 'protected',
      singleton: true,
      protected: true,
      priority: WL.U.clamp((idx || 0) + 1, 1, 99),
      lastRenderedHash: '',
      renderHash: '',
      lastSyncedTurn: 0,
      lastDryRunTurn: 0,
      dirty: true,
      duplicateIds: [],
      tombstoneId: '',
      warnings: [],
      manualEditDetected: false,
      manualEditHash: '',
      writeBudgetStatus: 'dry_run_only',
      status: 'active',
      notes: []
    };
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.normalizeProtectedRecord = function (root, rec, idx) {
    const base = this.makeProtectedRecord(root, rec && rec.title || (WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS || [])[idx] || 'WorldLedger Unknown', idx || 0);
    rec = rec || {};
    const out = Object.assign(base, rec);
    out.protectedKey = rec.protectedKey || rec.id || base.protectedKey;
    out.id = out.protectedKey;
    out.stableKey = rec.stableKey || base.stableKey;
    out.triggerKeys = Array.isArray(rec.triggerKeys) ? rec.triggerKeys.slice(0, 6) : base.triggerKeys;
    out.lastRenderedHash = rec.lastRenderedHash || rec.renderHash || '';
    out.renderHash = out.lastRenderedHash || rec.renderHash || '';
    out.duplicateIds = Array.isArray(rec.duplicateIds) ? rec.duplicateIds.slice(0, 12) : [];
    out.warnings = Array.isArray(rec.warnings) ? rec.warnings.slice(0, 8) : [];
    out.notes = Array.isArray(rec.notes) ? rec.notes.slice(0, 8) : [];
    return out;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.protectedRegistry = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    return root.cards.protectedRegistry;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.renderProtectedRecord = function (root, rec) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    rec = this.normalizeProtectedRecord(root, rec || {}, 0);
    const title = rec.title;
    let text = title + '\n';
    if (/Player/.test(title)) text += 'Player: ' + root.player.name + ' | Level ' + root.player.level + ' | HP ' + root.body.hp + '/' + root.body.maxHp + ' | Location: ' + (root.places.currentId || 'unknown') + '\n';
    else if (/Inventory/.test(title)) text += 'Owned: ' + root.inventory.ownedIds.map(id => root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '\nCurrency: ' + root.inventory.currency.gold + 'g ' + root.inventory.currency.silver + 's ' + root.inventory.currency.copper + 'c\n';
    else if (/Abilities/.test(title)) text += 'Candidates: ' + root.abilities.candidateIds.map(id => root.abilities.candidatesById[id] && root.abilities.candidatesById[id].name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '\nStable: ' + root.abilities.abilityIds.map(id => root.abilities.abilitiesById[id] && root.abilities.abilitiesById[id].name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '\n';
    else if (/Quests/.test(title)) text += 'Active: ' + root.quests.activeIds.map(id => root.quests.questsById[id] && root.quests.questsById[id].title).filter(Boolean).join('; ') + '\nReward promises: ' + root.quests.rewardPromises.length + '\n';
    else if (/Actors Places/.test(title)) text += 'Present actors: ' + root.actors.presentIds.map(id => root.actors.actorsById[id] && root.actors.actorsById[id].name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '\nCurrent place: ' + (root.places.currentId && root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId] ? root.places.placesById[root.places.currentId].name : 'unknown') + '\n';
    else if (/Time/.test(title)) text += 'Day ' + root.time.day + ' ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0') + '\n';
    else if (/Audit/.test(title)) text += 'Last parser hits: ' + ((root.pending.lastParserHits || []).length) + ' | Last proposals: ' + ((root.pending.lastProposals || []).length) + '\n';
    else text += 'Protected dry-run mirror. Cards mirror reducer-owned state only. Story Card prose is not canon.\n';
    return { title, keys: rec.triggerKeys.join(','), value:text, hash:WL.U.hash(text), protectedKey:rec.protectedKey, stableKey:rec.stableKey };
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.detectManualEdits = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
    const out = [];
    root.cards.protectedRegistry.forEach(rec => {
      const found = WL.CardApiAdapter.safeFindByTitle(rec.title);
      if (found.ok && found.card) {
        const content = String(found.card.value || found.card.entry || found.card.text || '');
        const h = WL.U.hash(content);
        rec.manualEditHash = h;
        const rendered = this.renderProtectedRecord(root, rec);
        rec.manualEditDetected = !!content && h !== rendered.hash && rec.lastRenderedHash && h !== rec.lastRenderedHash;
        if (rec.manualEditDetected) out.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), title:rec.title, index:found.index, hash:h, status:'manual_edit_detected_dry_run_only' });
      }
    });
    root.cards.manualEditAudit = out;
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.manualEditAudit, 20);
    return out;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.auditProtectedSingletons = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
    const byTitle = {};
    cards.forEach((c, idx) => { const t = WL.U.lower(c && (c.title || c.name) || ''); if (!t) return; if (!byTitle[t]) byTitle[t] = []; byTitle[t].push(idx); });
    const dupes = [];
    root.cards.protectedRegistry.forEach(rec => {
      const arr = byTitle[WL.U.lower(rec.title)] || [];
      rec.duplicateIds = arr.length > 1 ? arr.slice(1).map(i => 'storycard_index_' + i) : [];
      if (rec.duplicateIds.length) {
        rec.tombstoneId = 'tombstone_' + WL.U.hash(rec.title + '|' + rec.duplicateIds.join('|'));
        dupes.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), title:rec.title, duplicateIds:rec.duplicateIds.slice(), tombstoneId:rec.tombstoneId, status:'audit_only_live_removal_disabled' });
        root.cards.tombstones.push({ tombstoneId:rec.tombstoneId, title:rec.title, duplicateIds:rec.duplicateIds.slice(), status:'planned_not_executed', reason:'protected duplicate audit only in Code Pass 1' });
      }
    });
    root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit = dupes;
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.protectedDuplicateAudit, 20);
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.tombstones, 20);
    return dupes;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.simulateWriteBudget = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const budget = { protectedWritesPerHook: root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled ? 2 : 0, generatedCreatesPerHook: 0, generatedUpdatesPerHook: 0, mode:'dry_run_only', adapterAvailable: WL.CardApiAdapter.available() };
    root.cards.writeBudgetAudit.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), budget, dirtyRecords:(root.dirty.records || []).filter(r => r.status === 'queued').length });
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.writeBudgetAudit, 20);
    if (!budget.adapterAvailable) root.cards.adapterAudit.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), status:'adapter_unavailable_failsoft', reason:'Story Card API unavailable; dry-run mirror only.' });
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.adapterAudit, 20);
    return budget;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.markDirtyFromDirtyQueue = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const families = {};
    (root.dirty.records || []).forEach(r => { if (r.status === 'queued') families[r.family] = true; });
    root.cards.protectedRegistry.forEach(rec => { if (/Player/.test(rec.title) && (families.body || families.player)) rec.dirty = true; if (/Inventory/.test(rec.title) && families.inventory) rec.dirty = true; if (/Abilities/.test(rec.title) && families.ability) rec.dirty = true; if (/Quests/.test(rec.title) && families.quest) rec.dirty = true; if (/Actors Places/.test(rec.title) && (families.actor || families.place)) rec.dirty = true; if (/Time/.test(rec.title) && families.time) rec.dirty = true; });
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.dryRun = function (root) {
    if (!root.features.cardSyncLiteEnabled) return 0;
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    this.markDirtyFromDirtyQueue(root);
    const budget = this.simulateWriteBudget(root);
    let simulated = 0;
    root.cards.protectedRegistry.forEach(rec => {
      const rendered = WL.CardSyncLite.renderProtectedRecord(root, rec);
      const changed = rec.lastRenderedHash !== rendered.hash;
      if (rec.dirty || changed) {
        root.cards.renderRecords.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), protectedKey:rec.protectedKey, title:rec.title, lastRenderedHash:rec.lastRenderedHash || '', renderHash:rendered.hash, mode:'dry-run', changed });
        root.cards.dryRunWrites.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), target:rec.title, mode:'dry-run', renderHash:rendered.hash, writeBudgetStatus:budget.mode });
        rec.lastRenderedHash = rendered.hash; rec.renderHash = rendered.hash; rec.lastDryRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root); rec.lastSyncedTurn = 0; rec.writeBudgetStatus = budget.mode; rec.dirty = false; simulated++;
      }
    });
    this.auditProtectedSingletons(root);
    this.detectManualEdits(root);
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.renderRecords, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.cards);
    WL.U.capArray(root.cards.dryRunWrites, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.dryRunWrites);
    if (simulated) WL.AuditLogger.append(root, 'cards', { message:'CardSync protected dry-run simulated records=' + simulated }, 40);
    return simulated;
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.inspect = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const fields = WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ProtectedCardRecord;
    return [
      root.cards.schemaVersion === WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION ? {status:'PASS', label:'CardSync schema version locked'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'CardSync schema version missing'},
      root.cards.protectedRegistry.every(r => fields.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(r, k))) ? {status:'PASS', label:'ProtectedCardRecord required fields present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'ProtectedCardRecord required fields missing'},
      root.cards.protectedRegistry.length === WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.length ? {status:'PASS', label:'protected registry matches protected titles'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'protected registry/title mismatch'},
      typeof this.detectManualEdits === 'function' && typeof this.simulateWriteBudget === 'function' && typeof this.auditProtectedSingletons === 'function' ? {status:'PASS', label:'protected CardSync depth helpers present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'protected CardSync depth helper missing'},
      root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false ? {status:'PASS', label:'generated cards remain disabled'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'generated cards enabled'}
    ];
  };
  WL.CardSyncLite.audit = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const lines = [];
    const budget = this.simulateWriteBudget(root);
    const dupes = this.auditProtectedSingletons(root);
    lines.push('Mode: dry-run=' + !!root.features.cardSyncDryRun + ', protectedWrites=' + !!root.features.protectedCardWritesEnabled + ', generated=false');
    lines.push('Adapter: ' + (budget.adapterAvailable ? 'available' : 'unavailable/fail-soft dry-run'));
    lines.push('Protected registry records: ' + root.cards.protectedRegistry.length);
    lines.push('Protected duplicate audit records: ' + dupes.length);
    lines.push('Tombstones: ' + root.cards.tombstones.length + ' (planned only; live removal disabled)');
    lines.push('Manual edit audit records: ' + root.cards.manualEditAudit.length);
    root.cards.protectedRegistry.forEach(r => lines.push('Protected: ' + r.title + ' [' + r.protectedKey + '] dirty=' + r.dirty + ' lastHash=' + (r.lastRenderedHash || 'none')));
    lines.push('Generated CardSync is disabled in Code Pass 1. Cards never create canon. Story Card writes remain dry-run only.');
    return lines;
  };

  // Migration / Quarantine real QuarantineRecord model. Old prose is inactive evidence, never canon.
  WL.MigrationManager.normalizeQuarantineRecord = function (root, record, idx) {
    record = record || {};
    const source = WL.U.norm(record.source || record.kind || 'unknown');
    const sourceKey = WL.U.norm(record.sourceKey || record.key || record.title || ('record_' + idx));
    const reason = WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(record.reason || record.note || 'quarantined evidence', 180);
    const createdTurn = Number(record.createdTurn != null ? record.createdTurn : (record.turn != null ? record.turn : WL.U.nowTurn(root)));
    const qid = record.quarantineId || record.id || ('q_' + WL.U.hash([source, sourceKey, reason, createdTurn].join('|')));
    return {
      quarantineId: qid,
      id: qid,
      source,
      sourceKey,
      originalPreview: WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(record.originalPreview || record.preview || record.note || '', 260),
      reason,
      severity: record.severity || 'info',
      suggestedAction: WL.AuditLogger.safeLine(record.suggestedAction || 'manual review in a future migration pass; do not import as canon', 160),
      status: record.status || (record.unresolved === false ? 'reviewed' : 'quarantined'),
      createdTurn,
      turn: createdTurn,
      notes: Array.isArray(record.notes) ? record.notes.slice(0, 6) : (record.notes ? [String(record.notes).slice(0,120)] : []),
      unresolved: record.unresolved !== false,
      inactiveClaim: true
    };
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.quarantineRecord = function (root, record) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const rec = this.normalizeQuarantineRecord(root, record || {}, root.quarantine.records.length);
    const exists = root.quarantine.records.filter(r => r.quarantineId === rec.quarantineId || (r.source === rec.source && r.sourceKey === rec.sourceKey && r.reason === rec.reason))[0];
    if (exists) return exists;
    root.quarantine.records.push(rec);
    root.migration.quarantineAudit.push({ turn:rec.createdTurn, quarantineId:rec.quarantineId, source:rec.source, sourceKey:rec.sourceKey, reason:rec.reason, severity:rec.severity, status:rec.status });
    root.quarantine.inactiveClaims = root.quarantine.records.map(r => ({ quarantineId:r.quarantineId, source:r.source, sourceKey:r.sourceKey, status:r.status, reason:r.reason }));
    root.quarantine.unresolvedCount = root.quarantine.records.filter(r => r && r.unresolved !== false).length;
    root.migration.unresolvedCount = root.quarantine.unresolvedCount;
    WL.U.capArray(root.quarantine.records, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.quarantine);
    WL.U.capArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit, WL.SCHEMA_CAPS.migration);
    return rec;
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.quarantineOldCardProse = function (root, title, text, reason) {
    return this.quarantineRecord(root, { source:'story_card_prose', sourceKey:title || 'unknown_card', originalPreview:text || '', reason:reason || 'Story Card prose is evidence only and cannot import canon', severity:'medium', suggestedAction:'review manually during future migration; do not create inventory/ability/quest truth from this prose', status:'quarantined' });
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.scanStoryCardsForUnsafeCanonClaims = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const cards = WL.U.getStoryCards();
    let count = 0;
    cards.forEach(c => {
      const title = c && (c.title || c.name) || 'untitled_card';
      const text = String(c && (c.value || c.entry || c.text) || '');
      if (/\b(?:owns?|inventory|gained?|learned|stable ability|paid|reward|HP|MP|EP)\b/i.test(text)) { this.quarantineOldCardProse(root, title, text.slice(0, 260)); count++; }
    });
    return count;
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.run = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    if (root.migration.schema !== WL.SCHEMA_VERSION) {
      root.migration.futureSchemaWarnings.push({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), from:root.migration.schema, to:WL.SCHEMA_VERSION, status:'safe_warning' });
      this.quarantineRecord(root, { source:'state.worldledger', sourceKey:'schema_' + root.migration.schema, originalPreview:'unknown/future schema marker', reason:'Future/unknown schema found. Repaired conservatively.', severity:'medium', suggestedAction:'continue with current schema defaults; preserve old evidence only' });
      root.migration.schema = WL.SCHEMA_VERSION;
    }
    this.scanStoryCardsForUnsafeCanonClaims(root);
    root.migration.lastRunTurn = WL.U.nowTurn(root);
    return true;
  };
  WL.MigrationManager.inspect = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const fields = WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.QuarantineRecord;
    return [
      root.quarantine.schemaVersion === WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION ? {status:'PASS', label:'Quarantine schema version locked'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'Quarantine schema version missing'},
      root.quarantine.records.every(r => fields.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(r, k))) ? {status:'PASS', label:'QuarantineRecord required fields present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'QuarantineRecord required fields missing'},
      Array.isArray(root.migration.quarantineAudit) ? {status:'PASS', label:'migration quarantine audit present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'migration quarantine audit missing'},
      Array.isArray(root.quarantine.inactiveClaims) ? {status:'PASS', label:'quarantine inactive-claim display list present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'quarantine inactive-claim display missing'},
      typeof this.quarantineOldCardProse === 'function' && typeof this.scanStoryCardsForUnsafeCanonClaims === 'function' ? {status:'PASS', label:'old card prose quarantine helpers present'} : {status:'FAIL', label:'old card prose quarantine helpers missing'}
    ];
  };

  // ReleaseCheck gate IDs and known failure coverage. This is still Code Pass 1; gates test foundation safety, not deferred gameplay.
  WL.ReleaseGateRegistry = Object.freeze([
    ['G01','identity','Build/header identity and AIDRPG ↔ WorldLedger bridge are aligned'],
    ['G02','deferred','Deferred systems remain disabled'],
    ['G03','noncanon','Command/context/card/audit text cannot become canon'],
    ['G04','state','StateManager preserves valid truth and repairs structure only'],
    ['G05','ledger','Operation keys are checked before mutation and recorded after success'],
    ['G06','parser','EventScanner observes only and never mutates'],
    ['G07','proposal','ProposalBuilder produces structured claims only'],
    ['G08','validator','CanonValidator has clause-level truth gates'],
    ['G09','reducer','Reducers have defensive unsafe-flag backstops'],
    ['G10','dirty','DirtyQueue uses DirtyTarget schema and lifecycle states'],
    ['G11','context','ContextBuilder uses ContextPacket schema and budget/audit selection'],
    ['G12','cards','CardSync protected registry is dry-run, singleton-aware, and non-canon'],
    ['G13','migration','Migration/quarantine uses inactive QuarantineRecord evidence'],
    ['G14','cleanup','Cleanup caps logs/queues without deleting active truth or durable keys'],
    ['G15','commands','Commands are inspect-only and suppressed from parsing'],
    ['G16','runtime','Runtime fail-soft and duplicate replay protections exist'],
    ['G17','storycards','Story Card prose cannot import items/abilities/quests/currency'],
    ['G18','rewards','Reward promise and payment stay separated and idempotent'],
    ['G19','places','Weak place references cannot overwrite current scene'],
    ['G20','abilities','Output-only ability claims cannot create stable abilities'],
    ['G21','body','Death rumors/dreams/quotes cannot damage or kill'],
    ['G22','tests','Known player-breaker failures have direct test coverage']
  ]);
  WL.KnownFailureCoverage = Object.freeze([
    { coverageId:'KF01', family:'inventory', knownFailure:'denied/failed/abstract/dream/quoted item becomes owned', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck inventory negatives', status:'covered' },
    { coverageId:'KF02', family:'ability', knownFailure:'output-only learned ability becomes stable', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck ability negatives', status:'covered' },
    { coverageId:'KF03', family:'place', knownFailure:'visible/road/interstitial reference overwrites scene', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck place negatives', status:'covered' },
    { coverageId:'KF04', family:'time', knownFailure:'quoted or nonliteral time skip ages player', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck time negatives', status:'covered' },
    { coverageId:'KF05', family:'body', knownFailure:'rumor death or dream harm changes HP/death', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck body negatives', status:'covered' },
    { coverageId:'KF06', family:'reward', knownFailure:'reward promise pays currency or repeated payment duplicates', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck reward idempotency', status:'covered' },
    { coverageId:'KF07', family:'cards', knownFailure:'Story Card or command text imports canon', coveredBy:'PlayerBreakerCheck non-canon source tests', status:'covered' }
  ]);
  WL.ReleaseCheckLite.makeGateRecord = function (gateId, status, label, family, notes) { return { gateId, status, label, family:family || '', required:true, notes:Array.isArray(notes) ? notes : (notes ? [String(notes)] : []) }; };
  WL.ReleaseCheckLite.runGateRecords = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const rows = [];
    const add = (id, ok, label, family, notes) => rows.push(this.makeGateRecord(id, ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL', label, family, notes));
    add('G01', /0\.15\.2-support-layer-schema-completion/.test(WL.BUILD_VERSION) && !!WL.AIDRPG_WORLDLEDGER_BRIDGE, 'V0.15.2 identity and naming bridge aligned', 'identity');
    add('G02', root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false && root.features.economyEnabled === false && root.features.craftingEnabled === false && root.features.manualMutationCommandsEnabled === false, 'deferred systems remain disabled', 'deferred');
    add('G03', WL.EventScannerLite.shouldScanSource({sourceHook:'context'}, root).allowed === false && WL.EventScannerLite.shouldScanSource({sourceHook:'story_card'}, root).allowed === false, 'non-canon sources rejected by scanner source gate', 'noncanon');
    add('G04', WL.StateManager.validateIntegrity(root).ok, 'StateManager integrity clean', 'state');
    add('G05', WL.ReducerRegistry.LEDGER_ORDER === 'check_mutate_record_dirty_audit', 'ReducerRegistry operation key order locked', 'ledger');
    add('G06', WL.EventScannerLite && typeof WL.EventScannerLite.scanText === 'function', 'EventScanner present', 'parser');
    add('G07', WL.ProposalBuilder && typeof WL.ProposalBuilder.fromHits === 'function', 'ProposalBuilder present', 'proposal');
    add('G08', WL.CanonValidator && typeof WL.CanonValidator.validate === 'function', 'CanonValidator present', 'validator');
    add('G09', WL.InventoryReducer.defensiveLocked && WL.AbilityReducer.defensiveLocked && WL.BodyReducer.defensiveLocked && WL.PlaceReducerLite.defensiveLocked && WL.TimeReducerLite.defensiveLocked, 'owner reducer defensive guard flags locked', 'reducer');
    add('G10', WL.DirtyQueue.inspect(root).filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'DirtyTarget schema and lifecycle gates pass', 'dirty');
    add('G11', WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect(root).filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'ContextPacket schema and audit gates pass', 'context');
    add('G12', WL.CardSyncLite.inspect(root).filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'ProtectedCardRecord registry and dry-run gates pass', 'cards');
    add('G13', WL.MigrationManager.inspect(root).filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'QuarantineRecord schema gates pass', 'migration');
    add('G14', WL.CleanupManagerLite.inspect(root).filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'Cleanup caps without active-truth deletion', 'cleanup');
    add('G15', WL.CommandRouter && WL.CommandOutputFormatLock && root.runtime && typeof root.runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput === 'boolean', 'command inspection/suppression layer present', 'commands');
    add('G16', WL.Runtime && typeof WL.Runtime.failSoft === 'function' && typeof WL.Runtime.isDuplicateOutput === 'function', 'Runtime fail-soft and replay guards present', 'runtime');
    add('G17', WL.MigrationManager && typeof WL.MigrationManager.quarantineOldCardProse === 'function', 'Story Card prose quarantine exists and is non-canon', 'storycards');
    add('G18', WL.QuestReducerLite && WL.OperationLedger && root.quests && Array.isArray(root.quests.rewardPromises), 'reward promise/payment separation structures present', 'rewards');
    add('G19', WL.PlaceReducerLite.defensiveLocked === true, 'place weak-reference reducer guard present', 'places');
    add('G20', WL.AbilityReducer.defensiveLocked === true, 'ability stable-claim reducer guard present', 'abilities');
    add('G21', WL.BodyReducer.defensiveLocked === true, 'body rumor/death reducer guard present', 'body');
    add('G22', WL.FoundationTestHarness && typeof WL.FoundationTestHarness.runPlayerBreakerCheck === 'function', 'foundation/player-breaker test harness present', 'tests');
    root.releasecheck.records = rows;
    root.releasecheck.knownFailureCoverage = WL.KnownFailureCoverage.map(x => Object.assign({}, x));
    return rows;
  };

  WL.FoundationTestHarness = {
    snapshot(root) { return WL.U.safeJson({ player:root.player, body:root.body, inventory:root.inventory, abilities:root.abilities, quests:root.quests, actors:root.actors, places:root.places, time:root.time }); },
    runSupportCheck() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok:!!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        const root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
        const d = WL.DirtyQueue.addTarget(root, { family:'inventory', targetKey:'item_apple', reason:'support check', sourceReducer:'InventoryReducer', sourceOperationKey:'support_item_apple', priority:1 });
        add('SC15-001', d && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.DirtyTarget.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d, k)), 'DirtyTarget schema complete');
        WL.DirtyQueue.markSkipped(root, d.dirtyId, 'test skip'); add('SC15-002', d.status === 'skipped' && d.processedTurn >= 0 && d.attempts === 1, 'DirtyTarget lifecycle state changes');
        const packets = WL.ContextBuilderLite.buildPackets(root); add('SC15-003', packets.length && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ContextPacket.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(packets[0], k)), 'ContextPacket schema complete');
        const selected = WL.ContextBuilderLite.applyBudget(root, packets, 500); add('SC15-004', Array.isArray(selected) && Array.isArray(root.context.packetAudit) && root.context.packetAudit.length > 0, 'Context budget selection audited');
        const cards = WL.CardSyncLite.protectedRegistry(root); add('SC15-005', cards.length === WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.length && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ProtectedCardRecord.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cards[0], k)), 'ProtectedCardRecord schema complete');
        WL.CardSyncLite.dryRun(root); add('SC15-006', root.cards.renderRecords.length > 0 && root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false, 'CardSync dry-run renders protected records without generated cards');
        const q = WL.MigrationManager.quarantineOldCardProse(root, 'Old Inventory', 'Player owns Crown of Dawn.', 'test old card prose'); add('SC15-007', q && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.QuarantineRecord.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(q, k)) && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'QuarantineRecord schema complete and non-canon');
        const gates = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.runGateRecords(root); add('SC15-008', gates.length === 22 && gates.filter(g => g.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'G01-G22 support gates pass');
      } catch (e) { add('SC15-ERR', false, 'support check exception: ' + String(e && e.message || e)); }
      finally { globalThis.state = preserveState; }
      return results;
    },
    runPlayerBreakerCheck() {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok:!!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        let root;
        WL.onOutput('You decide not to take the rusted iron key.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-001', !root.inventory.itemsById.item_rusted_iron_key && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_iron_key, 'denied key gives no item');
        WL.onOutput('You grab for the brass key, but the goblin snatches it away.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-002', !root.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key, 'failed grab gives no item');
        WL.onOutput('You consider the idea of a brass key.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-003', !root.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key, 'abstract key gives no item');
        WL.onOutput('In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-004', !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'dream Crown gives no item');
        WL.onOutput('Mara says, "You pick up the silver key."'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-005', !root.inventory.itemsById.item_silver_key, 'quoted key handoff gives no item');
        WL.onOutput('You learned Moonblade.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-006', !root.abilities.abilitiesById.ability_moonblade, 'output-only Moonblade does not become stable ability');
        WL.onOutput('You attempt Void Lantern Veil, but nothing happens.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-007', !root.abilities.abilitiesById.ability_void_lantern_veil, 'failed Void Lantern Veil does not become stable ability');
        WL.onOutput('The chapel is visible from the market lane.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-008', !root.places.currentId || root.places.currentId.indexOf('chapel') < 0, 'visible chapel does not overwrite scene');
        WL.onOutput('After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-009', root.places.currentId === 'place_drunken_huntsman', 'road then enter Drunken Huntsman resolves to inn, not road');
        const ageBefore = root.player.age; WL.onOutput('Old Dalen says, "twenty years pass in the blink of an eye."'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-010', root.player.age === ageBefore, 'quoted twenty-year time skip does not age player');
        const hpBefore = root.body.hp; WL.onOutput('A rumor says you died in the alley.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-011', root.body.hp === hpBefore && root.body.deathBoundary !== 'dead', 'death rumor does not kill or damage');
        WL.onOutput('The elder promises you 15 gold for the job.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-012', root.inventory.currency.gold === 0 && root.quests.rewardPromises.length >= 1, 'reward promise records promise but pays no currency');
        WL.onOutput('The elder pays you the promised 15 gold.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); const goldOnce = root.inventory.currency.gold; WL.onOutput('The elder pays you the promised 15 gold.'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); add('PB15-013', root.inventory.currency.gold === goldOnce, 'repeated reward payment pays once');
        const before = this.snapshot(root); WL.onOutput('[WorldLedger] /inventory — Inventory\nStatus: ACTIVE\nOwned: Crown of Dawn'); root = WL.StateManager.root(); const after = this.snapshot(root); add('PB15-014', before === after && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'command/context/card-like text creates no canon mutation');
      } catch (e) { add('PB15-ERR', false, 'player breaker exception: ' + String(e && e.message || e)); }
      finally { globalThis.state = preserveState; }
      return results;
    },
    runFoundationTests() { return this.runSupportCheck().concat(this.runPlayerBreakerCheck()); }
  };
