  // Command renderers for direct support/regression checks.
  WL.CommandRenderer.supportcheck = function (root) {
    const tests = WL.FoundationTestHarness.runSupportCheck();
    return this.header('Support Check') + 'Support schema tests: ' + tests.filter(t => t.ok).length + '/' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
  };
  WL.CommandRenderer.playerbreakercheck = function (root) {
    const tests = WL.FoundationTestHarness.runPlayerBreakerCheck();
    return this.header('Player Breaker Check') + 'Player-breaker tests: ' + tests.filter(t => t.ok).length + '/' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
  };
  WL.CommandRenderer.regressioncheck = function (root) {
    const tests = WL.FoundationTestHarness.runFoundationTests();
    return this.header('Regression Check') + 'Foundation regression tests: ' + tests.filter(t => t.ok).length + '/' + tests.length + ' pass\n' + tests.map(t => (t.ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ': ' + t.id + ' — ' + t.label).join('\n') + '\n';
  };
  WL.CommandRenderer.runfoundationtests = function (root) { return this.regressioncheck(root); };
  WL.CommandRenderer.supportschemas = function (root) {
    WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
    const rows = WL.FoundationDepthReview.supportSchemaRows(root).concat(WL.DirtyQueue.inspect(root), WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect(root), WL.CardSyncLite.inspect(root), WL.MigrationManager.inspect(root));
    return this.header('Support Schemas') + rows.map(r => r.status + ': ' + r.label).join('\n') + '\n';
  };
  ['supportcheck','playerbreakercheck','regressioncheck','runfoundationtests','supportschemas'].forEach(cmd => {
    if (WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.indexOf(cmd) < 0) WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS = Object.freeze(WL.MVP_ACTIVE_COMMANDS.concat([cmd]));
  });
  WL.CommandRouter.activeDispatch = Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, WL.CommandRouter.activeDispatch, { supportcheck:'supportcheck', playerbreakercheck:'playerbreakercheck', regressioncheck:'regressioncheck', runfoundationtests:'runfoundationtests', supportschemas:'supportschemas' }));

  // Extend /supportdepth and /releasecheck with V0.15.2 real schema gates.
  (function () {
    const oldSupportDepth = WL.CommandRenderer.supportdepth;
    WL.CommandRenderer.supportdepth = function (root) {
      WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
      const base = oldSupportDepth.call(this, root);
      const schemaRows = WL.FoundationDepthReview.supportSchemaRows(root);
      const gateRows = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.runGateRecords(root);
      return base + '\nV0.15.2 Support Schema Rows:\n' + schemaRows.map(r => r.status + ': ' + r.label).join('\n') + '\nGate rows: ' + gateRows.filter(g => g.status === 'PASS').length + '/' + gateRows.length + ' pass\n';
    };
    const prevRun = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run;
    WL.ReleaseCheckLite.run = function (root) {
      WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
      const out = prevRun.call(WL.ReleaseCheckLite, root);
      const rows = WL.FoundationDepthReview.supportSchemaRows(root).concat(WL.DirtyQueue.inspect(root), WL.ContextBuilderLite.inspect(root), WL.CardSyncLite.inspect(root), WL.MigrationManager.inspect(root));
      rows.forEach(r => out.push(r.status + ': ' + r.label));
      const gates = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.runGateRecords(root);
      gates.forEach(g => out.push(g.status + ': ' + g.gateId + ' ' + g.label));
      const supportTests = WL.FoundationTestHarness.runSupportCheck();
      const breakerTests = WL.FoundationTestHarness.runPlayerBreakerCheck();
      out.push(supportTests.every(t => t.ok) ? this.pass('V0.15.2 support schema tests pass: ' + supportTests.length + '/' + supportTests.length) : this.fail('V0.15.2 support schema tests failing'));
      out.push(breakerTests.every(t => t.ok) ? this.pass('V0.15.2 player-breaker tests pass: ' + breakerTests.length + '/' + breakerTests.length) : this.fail('V0.15.2 player-breaker tests failing'));
      const failCount = rows.filter(r => r.status === 'FAIL').length + gates.filter(g => g.status === 'FAIL').length + supportTests.filter(t => !t.ok).length + breakerTests.filter(t => !t.ok).length;
      if (failCount) out.push(this.fail('V0.15.2 support/depth gate failures: ' + failCount));
      root.audits.releasecheck = out.map(x => ({ turn:WL.U.nowTurn(root), text:x }));
      return out;
    };
  })();

  // Make /version visibly identify the support-schema checkpoint without adding install instructions.
  WL.CommandRenderer.version = (function (oldVersion) {
    return function (root) {
      return oldVersion.call(this, root) + 'Support Schema Lock: ' + WL.V15_SUPPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION + '\nAIDRPG bridge: ' + WL.AIDRPG_WORLDLEDGER_BRIDGE.note + '\n';
    };
  })(WL.CommandRenderer.version);


  // V0.15.2 hotfix: route reward.promise to QuestReducerLite and correct self-test assertions.
  (function () {
    const priorOwnerForFamily = WL.ReducerRegistry.ownerForFamily;
    WL.ReducerRegistry.ownerForFamily = function (family) {
      if (family === 'reward') return WL.QuestReducerLite;
      return priorOwnerForFamily.call(this, family);
    };

    WL.FoundationTestHarness.runSupportCheck = function () {
      const results = [];
      const add = (id, ok, label) => results.push({ id, ok:!!ok, label });
      const preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        const root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.FoundationDepthReview.ensureDepthSchemas(root);
        const d = WL.DirtyQueue.addTarget(root, { family:'inventory', targetKey:'item_apple', reason:'support check', sourceReducer:'InventoryReducer', sourceOperationKey:'support_item_apple', priority:1 });
        add('SC15-001', d && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.DirtyTarget.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d, k)), 'DirtyTarget schema complete');
        const skipped = WL.DirtyQueue.markSkipped(root, d.dirtyId, 'test skip');
        add('SC15-002', skipped && skipped.status === 'skipped' && skipped.processedTurn >= 0 && skipped.attempts === 1, 'DirtyTarget lifecycle state changes');
        const packets = WL.ContextBuilderLite.buildPackets(root); add('SC15-003', packets.length && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ContextPacket.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(packets[0], k)), 'ContextPacket schema complete');
        const selected = WL.ContextBuilderLite.applyBudget(root, packets, 500); add('SC15-004', Array.isArray(selected) && Array.isArray(root.context.packetAudit) && root.context.packetAudit.length > 0, 'Context budget selection audited');
        const cards = WL.CardSyncLite.protectedRegistry(root); add('SC15-005', cards.length === WL.PROTECTED_CARD_KEYS.length && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.ProtectedCardRecord.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cards[0], k)), 'ProtectedCardRecord schema complete');
        WL.CardSyncLite.dryRun(root); add('SC15-006', root.cards.renderRecords.length > 0 && root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false, 'CardSync dry-run renders protected records without generated cards');
        const q = WL.MigrationManager.quarantineOldCardProse(root, 'Old Inventory', 'Player owns Crown of Dawn.', 'test old card prose'); add('SC15-007', q && WL.SUPPORT_SCHEMA_SHAPES.QuarantineRecord.every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(q, k)) && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn, 'QuarantineRecord schema complete and non-canon');
        const gates = WL.ReleaseCheckLite.runGateRecords(root); add('SC15-008', gates.length === 22 && gates.filter(g => g.status === 'FAIL').length === 0, 'G01-G22 support gates pass');
      } catch (e) { add('SC15-ERR', false, 'support check exception: ' + String(e && e.message || e)); }
      finally { globalThis.state = preserveState; }
      return results;
    };
  })();

  WL.SourceText = '';
  globalThis.WorldLedger = WL;
})();
